# The UX baseline review: every reviewer finding, raw (2026-10-26)

The eleven reviewers' findings as they handed them back, before deduplication and verification. The verified, ranked
and grouped findings are in `designs/lane-b-ux-review.md`. Synthetic data only. Shot ids name the screenshots in
`designs/lane-b-ux-review/manifest.json`; the screenshots themselves are in the private gallery.

## a11y-other

### `a11y-other-01`: Every refused form throws away what the person typed, or puts back a different value beside the error

- **Flags:** blocking; size S
- **Screens:** `sign-in.error-wrong-password.1280`, `sign-in.error-locked.1280`, `request-audit.error.1280`, `console.support.error.1280`, `console.terms.error.1280`, `console.models.error.1280`, `console.compliance.error.1280`, `console.status.error.1280`, `console.organization.error.1280`, `console.practice.error.1280`, `settings.billing-org.error.1280`, `settings.users.error.1280`, `support.error.1280`
- **Routes:** `/sign-in`, `/request-audit`, `/console/support`, `/console/terms`, `/console/models`, `/console/compliance`, `/console/status`, `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`, `/console/practices/[practiceId]`, `/settings/billing/[organizationId]`, `/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users`, `/support`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/action-form.test.tsx (new)`
- **Evidence:** After any refusal the fields are empty or back at their defaults. sign-in: the email is gone and must be retyped (sign-in.error-wrong-password.1280, -locked). request-audit.error.1280: all eight fields of the public form are blank under 'organizationName: this form takes no patient information'. console.support.error.1280: Ticket is empty although it held a value to pass its required check; console.terms.error.1280: Document reference and SHA-256 (64 hex characters) are empty. console.models.error.1280: the error is 'value: a budget in whole cents' but Flag has gone back to 'Kill switch (every model feature off)' and Applies to 'Everyone', so typing 500 as the message asks submits a kill switch for everyone. console.practice.error.1280 and settings.billing-org.error.1280: the refused ZIP and routing number are replaced by the stored 27601 and 110000000, so a valid-looking value sits marked invalid. Cause: ActionForm passes the server action straight to <form action> (apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx:32,65), so React 19 resets every uncontrolled field when the action returns; ActionState carries no submitted values (apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts:124-129, runAction :148-172, runSignInStep :177-191). 112 forms use ActionForm. The screening upload form keeps its values (screening.error-no-phi.1280, screening.error-zip.1280) because it submits through onSubmit and startTransition (apps/web/src/components/screening/upload-form.tsx:118-141), so the product already does it right in one place.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.2 3.3.7 Redundant Entry (A, named by audit #103); WCAG 2.1 3.3.3 Error Suggestion (the person cannot see what was refused); PRD.md:256 every error with a next step; brief: no state a refresh loses
- **Best fix:** Stop the reset in ActionForm, client side: submit through onSubmit with event.preventDefault() and startTransition(() => formAction(new FormData(form))), so a refusal leaves every field exactly as typed and nothing is echoed back from the server (no PHI round trip). Clear only password fields after a refusal (the 3.3.7 security exception) and keep the email. On success the action already redirects, so the fresh page starts empty. Add a unit test that a refused ActionForm keeps its values and a sign-in test that the email survives a wrong password.
- **Notes:** One fix in one component repairs all 112 forms. The fix keeps what was typed in the person's own browser and echoes nothing back from the server, so it also suits the no-PHI refusals (support.error.1280, request-audit.error.1280), where the person needs to see what to remove. axe cannot see this: it checks pages as loaded, never after a refusal.

### `a11y-other-02`: A refused field is named by its code key and marked only by a red border; a refused select or text area is not marked at all

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `console.billing.error.1280`, `console.compliance.error.1280`, `console.models.error.1280`, `console.organizations.error.1280`, `console.organization.error.1280`, `console.practice.error.1280`, `console.support.error.1280`, `console.terms.error.1280`, `request-audit.error.1280`, `screening-result.error-name.1280`, `settings.billing-org.error.1280`, `settings.users.error.1280`, `sign-in-new-password.error-mismatch.1280`, `support.error.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/billing`, `/console/compliance`, `/console/models`, `/console/organizations`, `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`, `/console/practices/[practiceId]`, `/console/support`, `/console/terms`, `/request-audit`, `/screening/results/[resultId]`, `/settings/billing/[organizationId]`, `/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users`, `/sign-in/new-password`, `/support`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/field.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/components/input.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts`
- **Evidence:** The summary lists 'organizationId:', 'occurredOn:', 'value:', 'name:', 'email:', 'zip:', 'reason:', 'version:', 'organizationName:', 'contactName:', 'routingNumber:', 'confirm:' and 'subject:' where the field's label reads Organization, Date, Value, Name, Email, Billing ZIP, Reason, Version, Organization, Your name, Routing number, New password again and Subject. The field itself shows no message, only a red border (sign-in-new-password.error-mismatch.1280, console.terms.error.1280, request-audit.error.1280), so a person who cannot tell red from the grey #808996 outline has nothing to match the summary to. console.billing.error.1280: the refused Organization select carries no mark at all, only the focus ring. The summary sits under the fields yet says 'Check the fields marked below.' Causes: ActionForm prints labels[issue.field] ?? issue.field (apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx:76), and only 2 of 112 ActionForm uses pass labels (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/pricing/page.tsx:97,112); the summary renders after {children} (action-form.tsx:66-84) with the words from apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts:162; the invalid look is aria-invalid:border-destructive on Input only (packages/ui/src/components/input.tsx:11), and SelectField and TextAreaField have no invalid style (apps/web/src/components/field.tsx:75,106).
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 3.3.1 Error Identification (the item in error is identified and described in text); 1.4.1 Use of Color; 1.3.1 Info and Relationships; PRD.md:256 every error with a next step
- **Best fix:** In ActionForm: (1) name each issue by the control's own visible label, read from the DOM (the <label for> of form.elements.namedItem(issue.field)), so no page can forget a labels map, with the labels prop kept only as an override; (2) render each issue as text directly under its control (an 'Error:' prefix plus the message, linked by aria-describedby as now), so the field is identified in words, not colour; (3) move the summary above the fields, as a list of links that move focus to each field, and change the words to 'Check the fields marked with an error.'; (4) give SelectField and TextAreaField the same aria-invalid:border-destructive style and a thicker invalid border (2 px) so the change is not hue alone.
- **Notes:** axe misses all of it: it checks that a control has a name, not that an error names the control the person sees, and it never runs after a refusal. The words reviewer may also raise the raw keys; this finding owns the field-to-error link.

### `a11y-other-03`: An open account menu or practice switcher stays open and covers the next focused control, and the sticky header can hide focus

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `console.organizations.account-menu.1280`, `support.account-menu.1280`, `home.account-menu.1280`, `terms.account-menu.1280`, `console.organizations.help.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/organizations`, `/support`, `/`, `/terms`, `every page in the AppShell`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/brand.css`, `apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts`
- **Evidence:** console.organizations.account-menu.1280 and support.account-menu.1280: the open account menu (w-72, z-40) sits exactly over 'Help with this screen' at the top right of main. Both menus are <details> elements (apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx:55-75, 83-104) that close only when their summary is clicked again: Tab from 'Sign out' runs through the rail and lands on the Help summary (shell.tsx:335-336), which is wholly under the still-open menu, so the person cannot see where focus is. Escape does nothing, and the menu does not close when focus or a click leaves it. Separately the header is sticky, 56 px tall (shell.tsx:315: sticky top-0 z-30 h-14) and nothing sets scroll-padding-top (no scroll-padding or scroll-mt anywhere in apps/web/src), so on long pages (console.organization.default.1280 at 4,068 px, console.compliance.default.1280 at 9,515 px) Shift+Tab scrolls the focused control to the top edge, under the header.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.2 2.4.11 Focus Not Obscured (Minimum) (AA, named by audit #103, which asks for 'focus not hidden under the sticky header' in the keyboard test); WCAG 2.1 2.4.7 Focus Visible
- **Best fix:** Make the two header menus a small client component (still a disclosure: a button with aria-expanded and aria-controls, not role=menu): Escape closes it and returns focus to its button, and it closes on focusout to outside it and on a click outside. Add html { scroll-padding-top: 4.5rem } (the 56 px header plus room for the 2 px offset ring) in apps/web/src/app/brand.css. Add both cases to apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts's keyboard helper: after each Tab, assert the focused element's rectangle is not covered (document.elementFromPoint at its centre returns it or a descendant).
- **Notes:** axe-core does not test 2.4.11 at all, and tabTo (apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts:20-47) checks only that an outline style exists, not that the focused element is visible on screen. shell.tsx is in PR #42.

### `a11y-other-04`: The account menu and practice switcher are announced by names that drop their visible words

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `console.organizations.account-menu.1280`, `support.account-menu.1280`, `settings.users.default.1280`, `settings.billing.default.1280`, `home.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `every page in the AppShell`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The account summary shows the signed-in email (support@synthetic.test, platform-admin@synthetic.t...) but its accessible name is aria-label="Account menu" (apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx:84). The switcher shows 'Choose a practice' or the current practice's name (settings.users.default.1280, support.account-menu.1280) but is named aria-label="Practice switcher" (shell.tsx:56). A speech-input user who says 'click Choose a practice' or the email gets nothing, and a screen-reader user never hears which practice is current from the control itself.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 2.5.3 Label in Name (A); 4.1.2 Name, Role, Value
- **Best fix:** Drop both aria-labels and let the visible text be the name, adding context with visually hidden text after it: 'Choose a practice' or '<practice name>' followed by <span class="sr-only">, switch practice</span>; and the email followed by <span class="sr-only">, account and sign out</span>. Below the md breakpoint, where the email is hidden, keep the sr-only email in the name so it still starts with what a sighted user would say (the initial is aria-hidden today).
- **Notes:** axe has a label-content-name-mismatch rule, but it is tagged experimental and is not in the wcag2a/wcag2aa/wcag21a/wcag21aa tags apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts:12 runs, so the journeys never report this.

### `a11y-other-05`: The six-digit code refuses a pasted code with a space and gives no format hint

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `sign-in-mfa.default.1280`, `sign-in-mfa.error.1280`, `sign-in-enroll.default.1280`, `sign-in-enroll.error.1280`
- **Routes:** `/sign-in/mfa`, `/sign-in/enroll`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/sign-in/mfa/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/sign-in/enroll/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/schemas.ts`
- **Evidence:** Both code fields are <Field ... inputMode="numeric" autoComplete="one-time-code" pattern="[0-9]{6}" required /> with no hint (apps/web/src/app/sign-in/mfa/page.tsx:24, enroll/page.tsx:34). autocomplete="one-time-code" is right. But authenticator apps show and copy the code as '123 456'; pasted that way it fails the browser's pattern check with the browser's generic 'Please match the requested format.' (no title attribute gives the format), and if it reached the server, sixDigitCode only trims the ends (apps/web/src/lib/routes/schemas.ts:23: z.string().trim().regex(/^\d{6}$/)), so it would be refused there too. The label says 'Six-digit code' but nothing tells the person spaces are not allowed.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.2 3.3.8 Accessible Authentication (Minimum) (AA, named by audit #103: copy and paste must work so the code need not be transcribed); WCAG 2.1 3.3.3 Error Suggestion; 3.3.2 Labels or Instructions
- **Best fix:** Accept the code however it is pasted: remove the pattern attribute, strip spaces and hyphens in the field's onInput/onPaste (client) and in sixDigitCode before the regex (z.string().transform((v) => v.replace(/[\s-]/g, '')).pipe(z.string().regex(/^\d{6}$/, ...))), keep maxLength generous (9) for the spaced form, and add the hint '6 digits; spaces are fine' tied by aria-describedby through Field's hint prop. Keep autocomplete="one-time-code" and inputMode="numeric".
- **Notes:** axe cannot test paste or pattern messages. Add a sign-in e2e step that pastes '123 456'.

### `a11y-other-06`: Setting up two-step verification on a computer means typing a 32-character key into a phone: no QR code and no copy button

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `sign-in-enroll.default.1280`, `sign-in-enroll.error.1280`
- **Routes:** `/sign-in/enroll`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/sign-in/enroll/page.tsx`, `apps/web/package.json`
- **Evidence:** The enroll card offers the setup key as grouped text ('X3UZ 3MDW TUKI GIF2 YQOM 2CFM HECL BU7K') and the link 'Open in an authenticator app on this device' (apps/web/src/app/sign-in/enroll/page.tsx:25-31). The otpauth link only helps when the authenticator is on the same device; a person enrolling at a desk with the app on their phone has to read and type 32 characters, a transcription task, with no QR code to scan (no QR code exists anywhere in apps/web/src). Enrollment is mandatory for every customer account before anything else (enroll/page.tsx:12).
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.2 3.3.8 Accessible Authentication (Minimum) (AA): no step of the authentication process may require a cognitive function test such as transcription unless a mechanism helps; WCAG 2.1 1.3.1 for the key's text
- **Best fix:** Show a QR code of the otpauth URI beside the key, rendered on the server as an inline SVG by a vetted, pinned library (nothing loaded from a third party), with alt text 'QR code for your authenticator app; the setup key below is the same'. Keep the key as text, add a 'Copy the setup key' button that copies it without spaces and announces 'Copied' in a polite live region, and keep the otpauth link for phones.
- **Notes:** touches42 because apps/web/package.json is in PR #42's file list. The secret is already on the page, so a QR code of it adds no exposure.

### `a11y-other-07`: The hardware-key buttons go silent while waiting: focus is dropped and the waiting and failed states are not announced in order

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `sign-in-hardware-key.default.1280`, `sign-in-hardware-key.error-no-credential.1280`, `sign-in-hardware-key.error-locked.1280`, `sign-in-register-key.default.1280`, `sign-in-register-key.error.1280`
- **Routes:** `/sign-in/hardware-key`, `/sign-in/register-key`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/hardware-key.tsx`
- **Evidence:** HardwareKeySignIn and HardwareKeyRegistration disable their button while busy and swap its text to 'Waiting for the key…' (apps/web/src/components/hardware-key.tsx:83-85, 126-128). Disabling the focused button drops focus to the document body, and a disabled button's new text is not announced, so a screen-reader user hears nothing while the browser waits for the key. When the ceremony fails, the Problem alert appears (hardware-key.tsx:26-36) but focus is not put back on the button or the alert, so the next Tab starts from the top of the page. ActionForm already solves this for the password steps by moving focus to the summary (apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx:52); these two client forms do not.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 4.1.3 Status Messages (AA); 2.4.3 Focus Order (A)
- **Best fix:** Keep the button focusable while busy (aria-disabled="true" and ignore clicks, rather than the disabled attribute), and add a polite live region inside the card that says 'Waiting for your hardware key. Touch it, then confirm with its PIN or fingerprint.' while busy and is cleared after. On failure, move focus to the Problem alert (tabIndex=-1), as ActionForm does, and on success leave as now.
- **Notes:** The hardware key itself is the accessible-authentication alternative for staff (3.3.8 passes on it: no cognitive test, and the email field has autocomplete="username webauthn", hardware-key.tsx:79). Words: the registration failure is titled 'Not signed in' (hardware-key.tsx:32), which the words reviewer may raise. ActionForm's own comment records the same focus drop (apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx:12-13). The live Stripe payment-method form uses the same disabled-while-pending button (apps/web/src/components/billing/stripe-payment-method.tsx:66); it is not in these shots (the capture ran the billing simulator), but the same fix applies.

### `a11y-other-08`: Sign-in step refusals mark no field and name fields the step does not have; the password policy is not tied to the password field

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `sign-in-mfa.error.1280`, `sign-in-enroll.error.1280`, `sign-in-new-password.default.1280`, `sign-in-new-password.error-policy.1280`, `sign-in-new-password.error-mismatch.1280`
- **Routes:** `/sign-in/mfa`, `/sign-in/enroll`, `/sign-in/new-password`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/routes/sign-in.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/sign-in/new-password/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** sign-in-mfa.error.1280 and sign-in-enroll.error.1280: a wrong code returns only an error string (apps/web/src/lib/routes/sign-in.ts:44,46 'That did not match. Check the email, the password or the code, and try again.'), so ActionForm marks no control invalid and focuses the summary (apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx:42-52); on a step with one field, the code, the message sends the person to an email and a password they cannot see. sign-in-new-password.error-policy.1280: the policy refusal ('It needs an uppercase letter, a digit, a symbol.') arrives as problems (sign-in.ts:86), again with no field marked. sign-in-new-password.default.1280: the rules (12 characters, lower, upper, digit, symbol) are in the CardDescription (apps/web/src/app/sign-in/new-password/page.tsx:21-24), not in a hint on the field, so a screen-reader user tabbing into 'New password' does not hear them.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 3.3.1 Error Identification (A); 3.3.3 Error Suggestion (AA); 3.3.2 Labels or Instructions (A); 1.3.1 Info and Relationships (A)
- **Best fix:** Return each step's refusal as an issue on its own field: on the code steps { field: 'code', message: 'that code did not match; enter the code your app shows now' }, and on the password step { field: 'password', message: 'it needs an uppercase letter, a digit and a symbol' }, so ActionForm marks and describes the field it names (the first-step sign-in keeps its deliberately vague 'email or password' wording, which is right there). Move the policy into the New password field's hint prop so aria-describedby carries it, and keep it visible.
- **Notes:** The no-account and wrong-password cases must stay indistinguishable on the first step (account enumeration); this changes only the steps after the email is known. axe never runs on any sign-in page (no expectAccessible call in a sign-in journey).

### `a11y-other-09`: Problems with a checkbox group or a code list are attached to no control, and the split form's live problem list is never announced

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `console.status.error.1280`, `console.screening.error.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/status`, `/console/screening`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/screening/split-form.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/support.ts`
- **Evidence:** console.status.error.1280: opening an incident with no component ticked returns 'components: Invalid input: expected array, received undefined', a raw Zod type message, and nothing is marked: the checkboxes are named components[] (apps/web/src/app/console/status/page.tsx:119) while ActionForm looks the issue up with form.elements.namedItem('components') and handles only single inputs, selects and text areas (apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx:43-45), so the 'Components affected' fieldset gets no mark, no description and no focus. console.screening.error.1280: typing 22 in both lists shows 'Place of service 22 is listed as both facility and non-facility.' in red under the fields (apps/web/src/components/screening/split-form.tsx:109-115), but the list is not a live region, neither field gets aria-invalid or aria-describedby, and 'Approve this split' stays enabled and would send an empty settings value (split-form.tsx:94), which the server refuses against a hidden field nothing can mark.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 3.3.1 Error Identification (A); 4.1.3 Status Messages (AA); 1.3.1 Info and Relationships (A)
- **Best fix:** In ActionForm, when namedItem returns a RadioNodeList or the issue names a group (components or components[]), mark the enclosing fieldset: aria-invalid on its first input, the issue's id added to the fieldset's aria-describedby, and focus on that first checkbox; route schemas give a group's own wording ('choose at least one component') before the type check (z.array(...).min(1, ...) with a preprocess that turns a missing field into []). In ScreeningSplitForm, render the problem list in a role="status" region that exists from the first render, set aria-invalid and aria-describedby on the field each problem concerns, and on submit with problems prevent the post and move focus to the first offending field.
- **Notes:** The status route's components schema is near apps/web/src/lib/routes/support.ts:393-396 (per the image checker); the friendly rule is there but runs after the type check.

### `a11y-other-10`: Row controls repeat one name in every row, and the tables that hold them have no row headers, so a screen-reader user cannot tell which row a button acts on

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `console.support.default.1280`, `console.support.error.1280`, `settings.users.default.1280`, `settings.users.end-membership-ticked.1280`, `console.status.default.1280`, `console.prospects.default.1280`, `console.prospects.all.1280`, `console.tickets.default.1280`, `console.organization.default.1280`, `account.devices.default.1280`, `console.specialist-queue.default.1280`, `console.specialist-queue.all.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/support`, `/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users`, `/console/status`, `/console/prospects`, `/console/tickets`, `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`, `/account/devices`, `/console/specialist-queue`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/console/support/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/status/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/prospects/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/tickets/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/account/devices/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/field.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/specialist-queue/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** console.support.default.1280: thirteen buttons all named 'End now' (apps/web/src/app/console/support/page.tsx:99). settings.users.default.1280: nine selects all labelled 'Role', nine 'Change role', nine 'End membership' and nine checkboxes 'They lose access now' (apps/web/src/app/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users/page.tsx:74-85). console.status.default.1280: seven selects labelled 'Status' and seven 'Set' buttons (apps/web/src/app/console/status/page.tsx:68-71), in a table with no header row at all (:60-61). console.prospects.default.1280: 'Create practice group', 'Mark contacted', 'Why declined' and 'Decline' per request (apps/web/src/app/console/prospects/page.tsx:92-113). console.tickets.default.1280: 'Mark answered', 'Mark closed' (apps/web/src/app/console/tickets/page.tsx:82). console.specialist-queue.default.1280: six rows each with links named 'Open the practice’s abstraction', 'Regulations' and 'Ledger corrections' (apps/web/src/app/console/specialist-queue/page.tsx:109-117), the practice's name sitting in another, non-header cell. account.devices.default.1280: two cards both titled 'Chrome on Windows' with 'Revoke device'. Tabbing through these, a screen-reader or speech user hears 'Role, combobox, Analyst', 'End membership, button' with no member, and no table marks a cell as the row's header (every <th> is in <thead>), so table navigation cannot supply it either. The users table's fourth header cell is empty (users/page.tsx:57).
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 2.4.6 Headings and Labels (AA); 2.4.4 Link Purpose (In Context) (A) for the specialist queue's repeated links; 1.3.1 Info and Relationships (A); 4.1.2 Name, Role, Value (A)
- **Best fix:** Make each row's identifying cell a row header (<th scope="row"> for the ticket, email, organization, practice, component and grant ticket), which also gives the specialist queue's repeated links their context under 2.4.4, give the action column a visible or sr-only header ('Actions'), and give each repeated control a name that carries its row: visually hidden text after the visible words ('End now <span class=sr-only>, grant SYN-TOUR-4</span>', 'Change role <span class=sr-only>for analyst@synthetic.test</span>', a select label 'Role for analyst@synthetic.test' with the visible part kept as 'Role', 'Status of Web application'). Visible text stays first so Label in Name (2.5.3) holds. A small helper (rowLabel) in field.tsx keeps the pattern in one place.
- **Notes:** ActionForm's submit accepts a ReactNode (action-form.tsx:24-25), so sr-only text fits without a new prop. axe misses all of this: duplicate accessible names are not a WCAG-tagged axe rule, and empty-table-header is tagged best-practice, outside the tags a11y.ts:12 runs. console/support/page.tsx and the users page are in PR #42.

### `a11y-other-11`: The console's members table is broken by flex cells: the action buttons sit under 'Account' and the 'Actions' column is empty

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `console.organization.default.1280`, `console.organization.error.1280`, `console.organization.created.1280`, `console.organization.firm.1280`, `console.organization.suspended.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx`, `eslint.config.js`
- **Evidence:** console.organization.default.1280: in Members, each 'End membership' button is drawn under the 'active' badge in the Account column and the 'Actions' header has nothing under it. Cause: both cells are <td className="flex flex-wrap ..."> (apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx:147,153). display:flex takes a <td> out of table-cell layout, so the browser wraps the two adjacent flex boxes in one anonymous cell; what is drawn no longer matches the header cells, and some browsers (Safari's WebKit) drop the cell's table role, so a screen reader reports the button under the wrong header or outside the table.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 1.3.1 Info and Relationships (A); 1.3.2 Meaningful Sequence (A)
- **Best fix:** Keep the cells as table cells and put the flex layout on a wrapper inside them: <td className="py-1 pr-3"><div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">…</div></td> for Account and the same for Actions, as the users page already does (apps/web/src/app/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users/page.tsx:77-78). Add a lint rule (a no-restricted-syntax rule in the root eslint.config.js on a td or th whose className starts with flex or grid) so it cannot return.
- **Notes:** axe's td-headers-attr and th-has-data-cells rules do not catch a layout change made by CSS. Only this page has the pattern (grep '<td className="flex' in apps/web/src finds these two lines alone).

### `a11y-other-12`: Hashes are cut to twelve characters, with the full value only in a mouse-hover title

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `terms.default.1280`, `console.terms.default.1280`, `console.organization.default.1280`, `console.organization.firm.1280`
- **Routes:** `/terms`, `/console/terms`, `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/terms/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/terms/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The customer's terms page shows 'ca88437093319f39…' (terms.default.1280; apps/web/src/app/terms/page.tsx:32-34), console.terms.default.1280 shows 'ca8843709331…' and the Agreements table shows '33ae577937d1…' etc.; the full SHA-256 is only in title= (apps/web/src/app/console/terms/page.tsx:46-48; apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx:257-258). A title tooltip opens on mouse hover only: a keyboard or touch user, or a screen magnifier user, cannot read or copy the full hash that the page exists to record and compare.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 1.3.1 Info and Relationships (A) and 2.1.1 Keyboard (A): information available only on pointer hover
- **Best fix:** Show the whole hash, in the mono face, wrapping with break-all inside its cell (or in a <details> 'Show the full SHA-256' that a keyboard opens), and drop the title attribute. A 'Copy' button beside it, announcing 'Copied' politely, serves the comparison it is there for.
- **Notes:** The earlier tour's 390 px finding (the ROI statement's SHA-256 does not wrap) is the opposite failure on another screen; the same break-all treatment fits both.

### `a11y-other-13`: A permission-refused page has no heading at all, so it does not say which page refused

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `console.organizations.error-forbidden.1280`, `console.screening.error-forbidden.1280`, `console.specialist-queue.error-forbidden.1280`, `settings.billing-org.error-forbidden.1280`, `settings.users.error-forbidden.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/organizations`, `/console/screening`, `/console/specialist-queue`, `/settings/billing/[organizationId]`, `/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/states/permission-state.tsx`
- **Evidence:** console.organizations.error-forbidden.1280: under the shell there is only a bordered box 'Not available to your role / As Contract specialist, you cannot do this here (...)'; no h1, no h2, and the rail marks nothing as current. Forbidden renders AppShell with PermissionState alone (apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx:358-363); PermissionState is an Alert with role="note" and a styled div for its title (packages/ui/src/states/permission-state.tsx:15-16). A screen-reader user who lists headings finds none, and nothing on the page names the screen they asked for (the <title> does, but is not read again after a client navigation).
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 1.3.1 Info and Relationships (A: the visual title is not a heading); 2.4.6 Headings and Labels (AA); PRD.md section 7: a permission state that explains what the role cannot do
- **Best fix:** Give Forbidden a required title prop and render <h1> with the page's own name (the same words as its metadata title, e.g. 'Organizations and practices') above the PermissionState, keep the section so the rail marks where the person is, and render the PermissionState title as an <h2> ('Not available to your role'). Add a link onward from nextStep where one exists (Home, or Help and support).
- **Notes:** axe's page-has-heading-one is tagged best-practice, so the WCAG-tagged run in apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts:12 never reports it, and no journey runs axe on a forbidden state. shell.tsx is in PR #42. The flows and words reviewers may also raise the missing page name and the raw permission wording; this finding owns the heading. The global error page has the same gap: ErrorState is an alert with a div title and no heading (apps/web/src/app/error.tsx:13-24; packages/ui/src/states/error-state.tsx:17-18), so the same h1 rule should cover it.

### `a11y-other-14`: Scroll boxes on the console and the screening result are not reachable by keyboard and have no name

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `console.rule-releases.notes-open.1280`, `console.rule-releases.default.1280`, `screening-result.default.1280`, `console.screening.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/rule-releases`, `/screening/results/[resultId]`, `/console/screening`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/console/rule-releases/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/releases/note.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/screening/coverage.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/screening/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** console.rule-releases.notes-open.1280: the release notes open in a fixed-height box that stops at the 'rarc' row with its own scroll bar; it is <pre className="max-h-96 overflow-auto ..."> with no tabIndex, role or name (apps/web/src/app/console/rule-releases/page.tsx:213; the same at :195 for check problems and apps/web/src/components/releases/note.tsx:94). The screening result's coverage table sits in <div className="overflow-x-auto"> with no tabIndex or name (apps/web/src/components/screening/coverage.tsx:61), and so does the console split history (apps/web/src/app/console/screening/page.tsx:82). Where these overflow, a keyboard user in Safari and older Chrome cannot scroll them, and a screen-reader user is not told a region scrolls. The anomalies and top-rows tables on the same result page do it right (apps/web/src/components/screening/anomalies.tsx:286,359), as does ScrollTable (apps/web/src/components/scroll-table.tsx:11).
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 2.1.1 Keyboard (A); 4.1.2 Name, Role, Value (A); 1.3.1
- **Best fix:** Wrap each in ScrollTable (or give the pre the same treatment: role="region", aria-label naming it, e.g. 'The release's notes, scrolls', tabIndex=0, and the focus ring), and add a lint check that any overflow-auto or overflow-x-auto element in apps/web/src carries tabIndex. For the notes, also render the Markdown (headings, the table) rather than raw source, so it reads as structure (1.3.1); that is the words and brand reviewers' call on looks.
- **Notes:** axe's scrollable-region-focusable rule (wcag2a) would catch these only on a page it visits with the box open and overflowing: the journeys never open the notes, and the result page's coverage table does not overflow at the sizes they test. Nine more unnamed scroll wrappers sit on practice screens outside this set (grep overflow-x-auto without tabIndex); the practice-screens accessibility reviewer owns those.

### `a11y-other-15`: The screening's file field: a refused file is not tied to the field, a second press of 'Screen the file' says nothing, and 'Reading the file' is never announced

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `screening.default.1280`, `screening.error-dos.1280`, `screening.error-member-id.1280`, `screening.file-ready.1280`
- **Routes:** `/screening`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/screening/upload-form.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The file input itself is well built: a visible label 'File to screen', the no-PHI hint tied by aria-describedby, and an always-present role="status" region for the ready and left-out lines (apps/web/src/components/screening/upload-form.tsx:198-212, 225-248); the server refusals are shown under each field and the focus moves to the first (upload-form.tsx:88-95, 168-180), which is the pattern ActionForm should copy. Three gaps: (1) screening.error-dos.1280: the 'Not sent' alert (upload-form.tsx:249-262) is not referenced by the input's aria-describedby and the input is not marked invalid, so returning to 'File to screen' a screen-reader user hears only the hint; (2) pressing 'Screen the file' with a refused file returns silently (upload-form.tsx:126-128: 'Nothing is sent: the problems are this browser's to show'), with no focus move and no new announcement, so the press seems to do nothing; (3) 'Reading the file in this browser…' (upload-form.tsx:223) sits outside the live region, and the submit button is disabled meanwhile (:282) with no spoken reason.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 3.3.1 Error Identification (A); 4.1.3 Status Messages (AA); 1.3.1 Info and Relationships (A)
- **Best fix:** Give the 'Not sent' alert an id and add it to the file input's aria-describedby with aria-invalid="true" while it shows; on a submit with a refused or missing file, move focus to the file input (its description then reads the problems) instead of returning silently; put the 'Reading the file' line inside the existing role="status" region so it is announced, and keep the submit button enabled while reading, telling the person 'Wait until the file has been read' if pressed.
- **Notes:** upload-form.tsx is in PR #42, so this lands after it. axe runs on the screening page (apps/web/e2e/screening.spec.ts:237, 727) but only on the page as loaded and after a ZIP refusal, never after a refused file.

### `a11y-other-16`: Every success and information box is an assertive alert, because Alert defaults to role="alert"

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `account.notifications.saved.1280`, `settings.users.notice-invited.1280`, `support.ticket-opened.1280`, `request-audit.sent.1280`, `screening.sent-waiting.1280`, `screening.sent-minutes.1280`, `console.models.notice.1280`, `console.practice.saved.1280`, `console.rule-releases.prepared.1280`, `console.screening.approved.1280`, `console.compliance.sent.1280`, `terms.internal.1280`, `screening-result.converted.1280`
- **Routes:** `/account/notifications`, `/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users`, `/support`, `/request-audit`, `/screening`, `/console/models`, `/console/practices/[practiceId]`, `/console/rule-releases`, `/console/screening`, `/console/compliance`, `/terms`, `/screening/results/[resultId]`
- **Files:** `packages/ui/src/components/alert.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/components/components.test.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Alert sets role="alert" unless told otherwise (packages/ui/src/components/alert.tsx:18), and 43 non-error Alerts in apps/web/src take that default, among them 'Your preferences are saved.' (apps/web/src/app/account/notifications/page.tsx:32), 'Done / Invitation sent' (settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users/page.tsx:41), the 'Check your email' box of about 120 words (apps/web/src/app/screening/page.tsx:43) and 'PaidWhole staff' on the terms page (apps/web/src/app/terms/page.tsx:45). After the server action's redirect React inserts each as a new assertive alert, so a screen reader interrupts whatever it was saying to read the whole box, success the same as failure, while the sign-in notices already use role="status" (apps/web/src/app/sign-in/notice.tsx:26) and one location notice does too (apps/web/src/app/console/practices/[practiceId]/page.tsx:72).
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 4.1.3 Status Messages (AA): success and advisory messages are status messages (role=status), keeping role=alert for errors; ARIA 1.2 alert role (important, time-sensitive information)
- **Best fix:** Make Alert's default role depend on its variant: role="alert" for destructive, role="status" for default, still overridable; keep a static explanatory box (the staff note, the terms note) as role="note". Then move focus to the page's h1 (tabIndex=-1) after a redirect that carries a notice, so the reading starts at the top where the notice is, not wherever the browser left it.
- **Notes:** The image checkers noted that these boxes look neutral; that is the brand reviewer's. This finding is only about how they are announced and where focus lands: on screening-result.converted.1280 the 'Audit request received' box sits at the foot of a 3,930 px page while the page reopens at its top.

### `a11y-other-17`: The specialist queue's counts are headings and their labels are not: the heading list reads '2, 0, 0, 0'

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `console.specialist-queue.default.1280`, `console.specialist-queue.all.1280`, `console.specialist-queue.default.1280-dark`
- **Routes:** `/console/specialist-queue`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/console/specialist-queue/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Each stat card puts its label in CardDescription (a <p>) and the number in CardTitle, an <h2> (apps/web/src/app/console/specialist-queue/page.tsx:38-65). A screen-reader user moving by heading hears four headings '2', '0', '0', '0' and no word of what each counts, and the label precedes its number only by position. The screening result does this right with a <dl> of <dt> label and <dd> figure (apps/web/src/components/screening/result-summary.tsx:106-117).
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 2.4.6 Headings and Labels (AA); 1.3.1 Info and Relationships (A)
- **Best fix:** Render the four counts as one <dl> (each card a <div> holding <dt> label and <dd> number in the mono face), the way result-summary.tsx's Stat does, with an <h2 className="sr-only">Work waiting</h2> above the group if a heading is wanted. Keep the look.
- **Notes:** Only this page has number-as-heading cards (grep CardTitle with text-3xl). axe has no rule for meaningless heading text.

### `a11y-other-18`: The console ends a membership in one click, while the customer's own page asks for a tick first

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `console.organization.default.1280`, `console.organization.firm.1280`, `settings.users.end-membership-ticked.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** console.organization.default.1280: every member row has an 'End membership' button that posts at once (apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx:161-165); its card says 'Ending a membership revokes the user's sessions at once'. Undoing it takes a new invitation, a new temporary password and MFA enrolment. The customer's Users and roles page guards the same action with a required 'They lose access now' checkbox (apps/web/src/app/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users/page.tsx:83-85). A keyboard or switch user who presses Enter on the wrong row, among identically named buttons (a11y-other-10), has no chance to stop.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 3.3.4 Error Prevention (Legal, Financial, Data) (AA): an action that deletes or changes stored data is reversible, checked or confirmed
- **Best fix:** Give the console's End membership the same confirmation the users page has (a required 'They lose access now' checkbox in the form, named with the member's email), and keep one shared EndMembershipForm component so the two pages cannot drift.
- **Notes:** The flows reviewer may raise the other one-click switches (maintenance, kill switch, suspend, approve split); those are reversible from the same screen, so 3.3.4 is met for them and this finding does not cover them. touches42 because the shared component would touch the users page, which PR #42 changes.

### `a11y-other-19`: Dark (tokens only, advisory): the sign-in steps put light text on the light Paper ground, and native controls keep light-scheme arrows and boxes

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `sign-in.default.1280-dark`, `sign-in-mfa.default.1280-dark`, `sign-in-enroll.default.1280-dark`, `sign-in-hardware-key.default.1280-dark`, `sign-in-new-password.default.1280-dark`, `sign-in-register-key.default.1280-dark`, `console.prospects.default.1280-dark`, `console.status.default.1280-dark`, `settings.users.default.1280-dark`, `account.notifications.default.1280-dark`, `console.organization.default.1280-dark`
- **Routes:** `/sign-in`, `/sign-in/mfa`, `/sign-in/enroll`, `/sign-in/hardware-key`, `/sign-in/new-password`, `/sign-in/register-key`, `/console/prospects`, `/console/status`, `/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users`, `/account/notifications`, `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`
- **Files:** `packages/brand/src/tokens.css`, `apps/web/src/app/sign-in/layout.tsx`, `apps/web/e2e/brand.spec.ts`
- **Evidence:** With data-theme="dark" the sign-in layout keeps bg-paper (apps/web/src/app/sign-in/layout.tsx:11) and --pw-paper is defined only in :root (packages/brand/src/tokens.css:12), so the h1 (#F4F6FA) sits on #F6F4EE at 1.02:1, the links (#8FB3F0) at 1.93:1 and the muted footer (#A7B1C2) at 1.97:1, and the wordmark turns white on cream (sign-in.default.1280-dark, sign-in-mfa.default.1280-dark). Across the app no rule sets color-scheme: dark (none in packages/brand/src, packages/ui/src or apps/web/src), so native controls stay in the light scheme: select chevrons are dark on the navy field (console.prospects.default.1280-dark, 'Choose a reason'), checkboxes are white squares (console.status.default.1280-dark, settings.users.default.1280-dark, account.notifications.default.1280-dark), and date-picker icons nearly vanish (console.organization.default.1280-dark).
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 1.4.3 Contrast (Minimum) (AA) and 1.4.11 Non-text Contrast (AA), advisory: no user reaches dark today (brief: dark captured as tokens only)
- **Best fix:** Whatever the theme ruling, land these with it: add --pw-paper (and a dark Paper value) to the dark block in tokens.css, or have the sign-in layout use bg-background in dark; add color-scheme: light to :root and color-scheme: dark to :root[data-theme="dark"] in tokens.css so native selects, checkboxes, date inputs and scrollbars follow; and run the e2e axe pass once with data-theme="dark" on the sign-in steps and one console page, so a dark contrast failure turns CI red.
- **Notes:** Advisory severity: it becomes blocking the day dark ships (for example by following prefers-color-scheme), so it must land in the same pull request that turns dark on. The theme choice itself belongs to the brand reviewer. axe would catch the sign-in contrast if it ever ran in dark; it never does.

### `a11y-other-20`: Format and no-patient-information instructions live in card text, not on the fields that need them

- **Flags:** polish; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `console.support.default.1280`, `console.compliance.default.1280`, `console.organization.default.1280`, `support.default.1280`, `support.error.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/support`, `/console/compliance`, `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`, `/support`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/console/support/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/compliance/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/support/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/field.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Fields that refuse by pattern have no hint saying the format, so the browser shows its generic 'Please match the requested format.': Ticket (pattern [A-Za-z0-9-]{1,64}, apps/web/src/app/console/support/page.tsx:56), the compliance SHA-256 (apps/web/src/app/console/compliance/page.tsx:191; the terms and agreement SHA-256 fields do carry the hint), NPI and Tax ID last four (apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx:108-109). On /support the rule that most often refuses a ticket, no patient names, dates of birth, member IDs or claim numbers, is in the card description (apps/web/src/app/support/page.tsx:42-46), not tied to Subject or the message, so a screen-reader user tabbing into Subject does not hear it before support.error.1280's refusal.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 3.3.2 Labels or Instructions (A); 3.3.3 Error Suggestion (AA); 1.3.1 Info and Relationships (A)
- **Best fix:** Give every pattern-checked field a hint that states the format ('Letters, digits and hyphens, as in SYN-1234'; '64 hexadecimal characters'; 'Ten digits'; 'Four digits'), through Field's hint prop so aria-describedby carries it, and add a unit test that fails when a Field has pattern without hint. On /support put the no-patient-information rule in the Subject and message hints ('No patient names, dates of birth, member IDs or claim numbers; name the screen and the record's ID').
- **Notes:** The six-digit code fields are a11y-other-05. console/support/page.tsx is in PR #42.

### `a11y-other-21`: Three data tables have no header cells, two agreement forms share their labels with no heading, and the notification checkboxes have no group

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `status.default.1280`, `status.maintenance.1280`, `console.status.default.1280`, `console.compliance.default.1280`, `account.notifications.default.1280`, `console.organization.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/status`, `/console/status`, `/console/compliance`, `/account/notifications`, `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/status/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/status/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/compliance/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/account/notifications/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The public Components table (apps/web/src/app/status/page.tsx:34-35), the console Components table (apps/web/src/app/console/status/page.tsx:60-61) and the compliance Policies table (apps/web/src/app/console/compliance/page.tsx:159-160) have a <tbody> and no <th> at all, so a screen reader cannot announce 'Status' or 'Version' with a cell, and axe treats them as layout tables and skips them. On the console organization page the 'Record agreement' form has no heading of its own and the upload form below it repeats the labels Kind, Practice, Version, Signer and Signed on (apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx:265-293), so a screen-reader user tabbing hears each label twice with nothing to tell the two forms apart (console.organization.default.1280). On /account/notifications the two checkboxes sit under the card title 'What reaches you' with no <fieldset>/<legend> (apps/web/src/app/account/notifications/page.tsx:42-50), so the question they answer is not read with them.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 1.3.1 Info and Relationships (A)
- **Best fix:** Give each table a header row (visually hidden if the design wants none: <thead className="sr-only">) and make the first cell of each row <th scope="row"> (component name, policy title); give the agreement forms headings ('Record a signed agreement', and the existing 'Or upload the signed PDF' as a real heading) and name each <form> with aria-labelledby to its heading; wrap the notification checkboxes in <fieldset> with <legend>What reaches you</legend>, as the status page's 'Components affected' already does (apps/web/src/app/console/status/page.tsx:115-122).
- **Notes:** Folds with a11y-other-10 when the same pull request adds row headers to the action tables.

### `a11y-other-22`: The current rail item is marked by a brass underline at 2.4:1 and a small weight step

- **Flags:** polish; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `console.billing.default.1280`, `account.devices.default.1280`, `account.notifications.saved.1280`, `console.organizations.default.1280`, `settings.billing-org.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `every page in the AppShell`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`
- **Evidence:** RailLink marks the current page with font-medium and a 2 px brass underline (apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx:117-120). Brass #C99A3B on the rail's #F7F8FA is 2.42:1 (2.57:1 on white), under the 3:1 a state indicator needs, and the 400 to 500 weight step is hard to see at 14 px (console.billing.default.1280, 'Billing status'). aria-current="page" is set, so screen readers are fine; the gap is for low-vision sighted users. In dark the same underline is 6.46:1 on Ink.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 1.4.11 Non-text Contrast (AA), for the visual indicator of the current state; .claude/rules/brand.md: Brass is 'the single active-nav underline' (kept)
- **Best fix:** Keep the brass underline the brand requires and make the second cue strong enough to carry the state on its own: font-semibold (600) or bold for the current item, so the state does not depend on brass's contrast. No new colour is needed.
- **Notes:** Taste is not the rule here: the 3:1 figure is 1.4.11's; the brand rule is respected. The brand reviewer may prefer a different second cue; any cue that is not brass-dependent meets the criterion.

### `a11y-other-23`: The code and enrolment steps expire in three to five minutes with no warning and no way to ask for more time

- **Flags:** blocking; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `sign-in.reason-step-expired.1280`, `sign-in-enroll.default.1280`, `sign-in-mfa.default.1280`, `sign-in-new-password.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/sign-in/enroll`, `/sign-in/mfa`, `/sign-in/new-password`, `/sign-in`
- **Files:** `infra/lib/constructs/identity.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/auth/pending.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/sign-in.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/sign-in/enroll/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/sign-in/mfa/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/sign-in/new-password/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Each sign-in step after the password lives in a sealed cookie that lasts five minutes (fifteen for key registration) (apps/web/src/lib/auth/pending.ts:15-17), and Cognito's own sign-in session is set to its three-minute minimum (infra/lib/constructs/identity.ts:14, 108). Nothing on the enrolment, code or new-password pages says there is a limit, shows when it ends, or warns before it does; when it passes, the person is sent to /sign-in?reason=step_expired (apps/web/src/lib/routes/sign-in.ts:116) and must type the email and password again (sign-in.reason-step-expired.1280: 'That sign-in step expired. Start again from your email and password.'). Enrolment is the step most likely to run over: with no QR code (a11y-other-06) the person types a 32-character key into a phone, then waits for a code. docs/product/sessions.md sets the 30-minute idle and 12-hour limits but says nothing of this step's length.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 2.2.1 Timing Adjustable (A): a time limit the content sets is warned of with a way to extend it (at least ten times), unless it is essential; WCAG 2.2 3.3.7 Redundant Entry for the retyped email
- **Best fix:** Raise Cognito's authSessionValidity to its 15-minute maximum and the pending cookie to match for the enrol, code and new-password steps; say the limit on each step ('This step stays open for 15 minutes'); and at two minutes left show a polite live-region warning with a 'Start this step again' button that restarts from the current step without asking for the email again (keep the email in the sealed pending state and prefill it). After an expiry, prefill the email on /sign-in.
- **Notes:** infra/lib/constructs/identity.ts is in PR #42. The three-minute value is a deliberate tightening ('Cognito's minimum', identity.ts:8), so the security reviewer should confirm 15 minutes for these unauthenticated-session steps; the challenge stays single-use either way. Audit #48 covers the 30-minute idle limit on PHI screens, not these steps. Blocking because enrolment is mandatory before anything else and each restart issues a new key: a person who cannot add the key and enter a code inside three minutes (Cognito's window in production) can never get in.

### `a11y-other-24`: What the axe journeys miss on these screens: 19 of 34 routes never checked, no refused, forbidden, open-menu or dark state, no WCAG 2.2 rules, and a focus check that passes an invisible ring

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `sign-in.default.1280`, `sign-in-mfa.error.1280`, `console.support.error.1280`, `console.organizations.account-menu.1280`, `console.organizations.error-forbidden.1280`, `sign-in.default.1280-dark`
- **Routes:** `/`, `/account/devices`, `/account/notifications`, `/console/billing`, `/console/organizations`, `/console/practices/[practiceId]`, `/console/rule-releases`, `/console/specialist-queue`, `/console/status`, `/console/support`, `/console/terms`, `/console/tickets`, `/maintenance`, `/sign-in`, `/sign-in/enroll`, `/sign-in/hardware-key`, `/sign-in/mfa`, `/sign-in/new-password`, `/sign-in/register-key`
- **Files:** `apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/onboarding.spec.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/security.spec.ts`
- **Evidence:** expectAccessible runs axe with the tags wcag2a, wcag2aa, wcag21a and wcag21aa only (apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts:12). Across the e2e specs it is called on 15 of the 34 routes in this set (billing, models, monitoring, onboarding, screening, security specs) and never on the 19 listed here, the six sign-in steps among them. It runs on pages as loaded: never after a refusal (a11y-other-01, -02, -08, -09), never on a permission state (a11y-other-13), never with a header menu open (a11y-other-03) and never in dark (a11y-other-19). The WCAG 2.2 rules axe has (target-size, tagged wcag22aa) and the best-practice rules that would catch a11y-other-10, -13 and -21 (empty-table-header, page-has-heading-one) are outside those tags, and label-content-name-mismatch (a11y-other-04) is experimental. tabTo accepts any focused element whose outlineStyle is not 'none' or whose boxShadow is not 'none' (a11y.ts:38-41), so a transparent or zero-width outline, or a decorative shadow, passes as a visible focus ring, and nothing checks that the focused element is on screen (2.4.11).
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.2 conformance as audit #103 defines it (combined-report.md item 103: every route, WCAG 2.2 rules, 'focus not hidden under the sticky header', and the criteria no tool checks recorded as manual checks); PRD.md:256 (accessibility on every screen)
- **Best fix:** Inside audit #103's every-route check (Lane A, PR 7): add wcag22aa and best-practice to the tags; visit each route also in its refused state (submit the first ActionForm empty), its permission state and with data-theme="dark"; make tabTo assert a non-transparent outline at least 2 px wide (or a ring) with 3:1 against the page, and that document.elementFromPoint at the focused element's centre returns it (2.4.11). Record as manual checks, per route, what no tool sees here: 3.3.7 (values kept after a refusal), 3.3.8 (paste into the code field), 2.5.3 (visible words in the name), 2.2.1 (step time limits) and focus return after menus.
- **Notes:** The every-route gate is audit #103, assigned to Lane A's PR 7; this finding gives that PR the list for these screens, so the synthesizer may hand it to Lane A rather than queue it in Lane B. Sign-in steps need the synthetic personas (apps/web/scripts/synthetic-personas.ts) to reach /sign-in/enroll and /sign-in/mfa.

### `a11y-other-25`: No skip link: a keyboard user tabs through up to 17 header and rail stops before each console page's content

- **Flags:** polish; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `console.organizations.default.1280`, `console.support.default.1280`, `console.compliance.default.1280`, `account.devices.internal-shell.1280`
- **Routes:** `every page in the AppShell`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/public-header.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/sign-in/layout.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The shell renders the lockup link, the switcher or 'No practice access', the account menu, then the rail's navs (nine console links, Support tickets and four account links for a platform administrator, apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx:230-294), then 'Help with this screen' (shell.tsx:335) before the page's h1. There is no 'Skip to content' link anywhere in apps/web/src (no match for skip in components or app). The <header>, <aside> with three named <nav>s and <main> landmarks (shell.tsx:315, 331, 334) do let a screen-reader user jump, so 2.4.1 is met through landmarks; a sighted keyboard user has no such jump.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 2.4.1 Bypass Blocks (A): met by the landmarks (technique ARIA11); the skip link is the technique that serves sighted keyboard users (G1)
- **Best fix:** Add a first focusable 'Skip to the page's content' link in AppShell, visually hidden until focused (sr-only focus:not-sr-only, with the brand focus ring), targeting <main id="main" tabIndex={-1}>; give the public and sign-in layouts the same link.
- **Notes:** shell.tsx is in PR #42.

### `a11y-other-26`: Loading states are never announced: the live region marks itself busy and arrives already filled

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** none
- **Routes:** `/account/notifications`, `/settings/billing`, `/settings/billing/[organizationId]`, `/console/billing`, `/console/rule-releases`, `/console/specialist-queue`, `/console/practices/[practiceId]`
- **Files:** `packages/ui/src/states/loading-state.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/states/states.test.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/layout.tsx`
- **Evidence:** LoadingState is <div role="status" aria-live="polite" aria-busy="true"> holding a visually hidden label such as 'Loading billing' (packages/ui/src/states/loading-state.tsx:20-27; its test asserts aria-busy on the status element, states.test.tsx). aria-busy="true" tells assistive technology to hold back announcements from that region until it turns false, and it never does: Next replaces the whole loading.tsx when the page arrives. The region is also inserted with its text already inside, which screen readers often do not announce at all. So the seven loading.tsx files on these routes (e.g. apps/web/src/app/settings/billing/loading.tsx:6-8, apps/web/src/app/console/billing/loading.tsx) say nothing to a screen-reader user, who hears silence until the page lands. No loading shot is in this set (the capture did not delay these routes), so this rests on the code.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 4.1.3 Status Messages (AA); ARIA 1.2 aria-busy (a busy region's changes are not announced until it is no longer busy)
- **Best fix:** Drop aria-busy from the status element; keep role="status" on an element that is in the page before the text changes (render the empty live region in the root layout, or render LoadingState's label one tick after mount), and put aria-busy="true" on the <main> that is loading instead. Change the test to assert the label is announced (status without aria-busy) and that main carries aria-busy.
- **Notes:** axe checks aria-busy only for validity, not for its effect, so the journeys cannot catch this. The same LoadingState serves the practice screens' 33 other loading.tsx files.

## a11y-practice-1

### `a11y-practice-1-01`: A refused form names the field by its schema key, not by the label the person sees

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.cases.error.1280`, `p.abstraction.error-tag.1280`, `p.abstraction-ledger.error-adjust-zero.1280`, `p.abstraction-version.error-amend.1280`, `p.case.error.1280`, `p.submissions.error.1280`, `p.claims.error.1280`, `p.contracts.error.1280`, `p.contract.error.1280`, `p.line.error.1280`, `p.plan.error.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions`, `/p/[practiceId]/claims`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Every refusal in the set lists the raw key: 'lineMateriality: dollars and cents, such as 5.00' under a field labelled 'Per line (dollars)'; 'title:', 'amount:', 'effectiveFrom:', 'text:', 'payerClaimControlNumber:', 'note: required', 'contractVersionId:'. ActionForm prints `{labels[issue.field] ?? issue.field}` (apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx:76) and only 2 of the 112 <ActionForm> call sites pass `labels` (app/p/[practiceId]/settings/pricing/page.tsx:97,112). The same list item is wired as the control's aria-describedby (action-form.tsx:50,75), so when focus lands on the field a screen reader says 'Per line (dollars), invalid entry, lineMateriality colon dollars and cents'.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 3.3.1 Error Identification (the item in error is identified in text) and 3.3.3 Error Suggestion; docs/product/PRD.md:256 (every error shown with a next step).
- **Best fix:** Make the visible label the default instead of an opt-in map: when a refusal arrives, ActionForm resolves each issue's field to its control (it already does, action-form.tsx:43) and takes the name from the control's own <label> (control.labels[0].textContent, available for input, select and textarea), keeping `labels` only for a field with no control (a group or hidden input). In development, log an error when an issue resolves to no label. Add a component test that renders each form's refusal and fails if a camelCase key appears in the visible text.
- **Notes:** The words reviewer will likely raise the same wording; this entry carries the screen-reader effect (the key is read as the field's description).

### `a11y-practice-1-02`: The refused field is marked by a red outline alone, not at all on selects, text areas and raw inputs, and the message is not beside it

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.plan.error.1280`, `p.line.error.1280`, `p.abstraction-ledger.error-adjust-zero.1280`, `p.abstraction.error-tag.1280`, `p.cases.error.1280`, `p.contracts.error.1280`, `p.contract.error.1280`, `p.submissions.error.1280`, `p.claims.error.1280`, `p.case.error.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions`, `/p/[practiceId]/claims`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/field.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/styles.css`, `apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Only the Input component styles the invalid state (`aria-invalid:border-destructive`, packages/ui/src/components/input.tsx:11): the Title, Per line, Amount accepted, Payer claim control number and Effective from fields turn from grey #808996 to red #B5452E, a change of hue only (about 1.6:1 between the two outline colours), with no icon or text at the field. Selects and text areas get no invalid style at all (apps/web/src/components/field.tsx:75 and :106; raw controls at app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/page.tsx:208,271, lines/[lineId]/page.tsx:299, abstraction/ledger/page.tsx:31,35): in p.plan.error the 'Contract version (for a contract mapping)' select, in p.line.error the Note text area and in p.abstraction-ledger.error-adjust-zero the amount input show only the focus ring. The message itself sits in the summary after the fields, at the foot of the form or in the grid cell beside the field (p.abstraction.error-tag, p.contracts.error), and says 'Check the fields marked below.' (apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts:162,180) although the marked field is above it. field.tsx has a FieldError for a message under the field (field.tsx FieldError, rendered when `error` is passed) but ActionForm never feeds it.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 1.4.1 Use of Color, 3.3.1 Error Identification, 1.3.3 Sensory Characteristics ('below' points to a place, and the wrong one).
- **Best fix:** ActionForm publishes its refusal through a React context; Field, SelectField and TextAreaField read it and render the field's own message directly under the control, prefixed with the word 'Error:' in the danger colour, set aria-invalid and aria-describedby; the raw <select>, <textarea> and <input> call sites move onto those components. One base rule styles every invalid control the same way, not by hue alone: `[aria-invalid='true'] { border-color: var(--pw-danger); border-width: 2px }` in packages/ui/src/styles.css. The summary moves to the top of the form as 'N fields need a change', each item a link that focuses its field, and the words lose the direction ('Check the fields marked in the form').
- **Notes:** Pairs with -01 (the label) and -03 (the values kept); one ActionForm PR can carry all three.

### `a11y-practice-1-03`: A refusal wipes what the person entered, and on the write-off form silently resets the reason to 'Window passed'

- **Flags:** blocking; size M
- **Screens:** `p.case.error.1280`, `p.submissions.error.1280`, `p.contracts.error.1280`, `p.contract.error.1280`, `p.abstraction-version.error-amend.1280`, `p.abstraction-ledger.error-adjust-zero.1280`, `p.claims.error.1280`, `p.cases.error.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]/claims`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx`, `apps/web/e2e/triage.spec.ts`
- **Evidence:** After each refusal the form comes back emptied or at its defaults: the write-off Reason select reads 'Window passed' although 'Other' was refused, and its consequence box is unticked (p.case.error); Response reads 'Accepted', not the 'Partial' chosen (p.submissions.error); dates back to mm/dd/yyyy and Contract back to 'A new contract with this payer' (p.contracts.error, p.contract.error, p.abstraction-version.error-amend); amount, reason and tick gone (p.abstraction-ledger.error-adjust-zero); the searched claim number gone (p.claims.error); the Per line field shows the saved 5.00, not the refused entry (p.cases.error). Cause: ActionForm hands useActionState's action to <form action> (apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx:32,65); React 19 resets uncontrolled fields after a form action, and the controls use defaultValue. On the write-off path a screen-reader user is moved to the 'Why' field (action-form.tsx:52), never to the Reason select that changed under them; clearing 'Why' and submitting again writes the line off as 'Window passed', a false reason on the line's record.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.2 SC 3.3.7 Redundant Entry (A; audit #103 adds 2.2), WCAG 2.1 SC 3.3.1; docs/product/PRD.md:256 (no screen depends on state a refresh loses).
- **Best fix:** Stop the reset at its source in ActionForm: keep `action={formAction}` for the no-script post, and add an onSubmit that calls preventDefault and dispatches `formAction(new FormData(form))` inside startTransition, which React does not follow with a reset, so every value, choice and tick the person made stays as it was. Add an e2e that refuses each form in this set and asserts the entered values are still there, the write-off reason first.
- **Notes:** Blocking because the write-off path can record a false reason without the person seeing the change (screen readers in particular). The states/390 reviewer may raise the reset itself; keep one entry.

### `a11y-practice-1-04`: Submitting a form drops keyboard focus to the page body and says nothing while it works or after it saves

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.cases.notice.1280`, `p.plan.notice.1280`, `p.contract-version.notice.1280`, `p.submissions.notice.1280`, `p.packet.notice-exported.1280`, `p.packet.notice-requested.1280`, `p.packet-review.notice.1280`, `p.abstraction-ledger.notice.1280`, `p.abstraction-regulations.notice.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/regulations`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/regulations/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/contracts/versions/[versionId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** ActionForm disables the submit button while the action runs (`disabled={pending}`, apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx:86); the focused button leaves the focus order and the browser moves focus to <body>. Its 'Working…' text (action-form.tsx:87) is not in a live region. On success the action redirects to the same path with ?notice= and the notice renders (e.g. 'The materiality thresholds are saved…', 'Mapped', 'Signed off', 'The submission is logged…'), but it takes no focus, and because the page title does not change, Next's route announcer (next/dist/client/components/app-router-announcer.js, announces only a changed document.title) says nothing; the next Tab starts again at the lockup, 21 stops before the content.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 2.4.3 Focus Order, 4.1.3 Status Messages.
- **Best fix:** Keep the submit button focusable while pending (aria-disabled='true' and an early return in onSubmit, not the disabled attribute) and put 'Working…' in a polite role='status' element inside the form. Render every ?notice= confirmation through one Notice component: role='status', tabIndex=-1, focused on mount, so the person lands on the confirmation and continues from it.
- **Notes:** The brand reviewer may raise the notices' neutral look; this entry is about focus and announcement only.

### `a11y-practice-1-05`: Almost every Alert is role='alert', so static page states interrupt as urgent announcements

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.contract-version.default.1280`, `p.abstraction-version.default.1280`, `p.contract-version.error-unresolved.1280`, `p.packet.approver-ready.1280`, `p.packet.drafter.1280`, `p.home.error-last-run-failed.1280`, `p.by-reference.error.1280`, `p.coverage.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/by-reference`, `/p/[practiceId]/coverage`
- **Files:** `packages/ui/src/components/alert.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/states/error-state.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Alert defaults its role to 'alert' (packages/ui/src/components/alert.tsx:18); of 203 <Alert> uses under app/p and components, 7 set a role. So standing explanations are announced assertively on every navigation, ahead of the page: 'Pricing uses this version' (apps/web/src/components/contracts/review.tsx:52), 'Publication is blocked' (review.tsx:212), 'Not exported yet' (app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx:119), 'You drafted or last edited this packet' (packet/page.tsx:386), 'Pricing can run' on Coverage; while a refusal ('Not done') and a success notice carry the same role, so nothing distinguishes urgency to a screen-reader user.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 4.1.3 Status Messages (the role matches the message), 1.3.1; WAI-ARIA 1.2 alert role (important, time-sensitive information).
- **Best fix:** Change Alert's default to no role. Set role='alert' explicitly only where something just failed: ActionForm's refusal, ErrorState, the run-failed and suspended banners. Confirmations after an action use role='status' (with -04's focus). Standing explanations get role='note' or none. Add a test in packages/ui that Alert renders no role unless given one.

### `a11y-practice-1-06`: The practice switcher and account menu are named 'Practice switcher' and 'Account menu', not by the practice and email they show

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.home.default.1280`, `p.cases.default.1280`, `p.contracts.default.1280`, `p.claims.default.1280`, `p.packet.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`, `/p/[practiceId]/claims`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Each summary's aria-label replaces its visible text (apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx:56 and :84). Every shot shows the current practice ('SYNTHETIC OTHER PRACTICE L…') and the signed-in email in the header; a screen reader hears only 'Practice switcher' and 'Account menu', so it never learns which practice it is in, and a speech-input user who says 'click SYNTHETIC OTHER PRACTICE' gets no match. The practice name is cut with an ellipsis and the full name is nowhere in the control (shell.tsx:57).
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 2.5.3 Label in Name (A), 4.1.2 Name, Role, Value.
- **Best fix:** Remove both aria-labels. Let the visible practice name be the start of the name and add the purpose as visually hidden text after it ('…, switch practice'); keep the full practice name in the DOM and truncate only with CSS. For the account, the visible email (or its initial below md) followed by visually hidden ', account and sign-out'.
- **Notes:** axe's label-content-name-mismatch rule is experimental and off by default, so the e2e gate cannot see this.

### `a11y-practice-1-07`: The header menus stay open over the page: Escape does not close them, leaving them does not close them, and focus can move underneath

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.home.default.1280`, `p.packet.default.1280`, `p.contracts.help.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The practice switcher and account menu are <details> panels positioned absolute at z-40 under the header (apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx:46,55,83). A native <details> does not close on Escape, on a click elsewhere or when focus leaves it. The account panel is 288 px wide, right-aligned, from about 60 to 200 px down; with it open, tabbing on past 'Sign out' runs down the rail and lands on 'Help with this screen' (top right of <main>, about 98 px down in every shot), which sits wholly under the open panel.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.2 SC 2.4.11 Focus Not Obscured (Minimum) (AA; named by audit #103); task checklist for menus: Escape and focus return.
