- **Best fix:** Replace both with one small client disclosure: a <button aria-expanded aria-controls> that opens its panel; Escape closes it and returns focus to the button; a pointer press outside or focus leaving the button and panel closes it. Use the same component for the Menu below md.

### `a11y-practice-1-08`: The sticky header can cover the focused control when tabbing backwards up a page

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.packet.default.1280`, `p.line.default.1280`, `p.claims.default.1280`, `p.case.default.1280`, `p.packet-review.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/claims`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/brand.css`, `apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts`
- **Evidence:** The header is sticky, 56 px tall, opaque, z-30 (apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx:315). No scroll-padding-top or scroll-margin exists in apps/web/src/app/globals.css, apps/web/src/app/brand.css or packages/ui/src/styles.css. On the long pages here (packet 4,510 px, line trace 4,952 px, claims 3,828 px), Shift+Tab scrolls each newly focused control to the top edge of the viewport, behind the header: a 36 px field or a 20 px link there is fully hidden. The e2e helper tabTo (apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts:37-41) checks only that an outline style is set, not that the element is visible.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.2 SC 2.4.11 Focus Not Obscured (Minimum), which audit #103 names ('focus not hidden under the sticky header').
- **Best fix:** Add `html { scroll-padding-top: 4rem }` (the header's 3.5rem plus a margin) to apps/web/src/app/brand.css, with a comment tying it to the header's h-14, and extend tabTo to Tab and Shift+Tab and assert, by document.elementFromPoint at the element's centre, that the focused element is not under the header.

### `a11y-practice-1-09`: No skip link: 21 tab stops of header and rail before each page's content

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.home.default.1280`, `p.cases.default.1280`, `p.claims.default.1280`, `p.coverage.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases`, `/p/[practiceId]/claims`, `/p/[practiceId]/coverage`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Tab order on every practice page: the lockup link, the practice switcher, the account menu, then 14 practice links and 4 account links in the rail (apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx:185-294), then 'Help with this screen', and only then the page. No skip link exists anywhere in apps/web/src. The landmarks (header, nav, main) let a screen-reader user jump, but a sighted keyboard user has no way past the rail on any page.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 2.4.1 Bypass Blocks (landmarks meet its letter for assistive technology; a visible skip link is what serves keyboard users who do not use one); docs/product/PRD.md:256 keyboard navigation on every screen.
- **Best fix:** Make the first element of AppShell a link 'Skip to the page', visually hidden until it takes focus, then shown at the top left with the focus ring, targeting <main id='main' tabIndex={-1}>.

### `a11y-practice-1-10`: The rail is a complementary landmark ('aside' named 'Navigation') holding three navigation landmarks

- **Flags:** polish; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.home.default.1280`, `p.abstraction.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`
- **Evidence:** <aside aria-label='Navigation'> wraps the Practice, Internal console and Account <nav>s (apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx:331). A screen reader's landmark list reads 'Navigation, complementary' and then the three navigations; the site's primary navigation is not complementary content.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 1.3.1 Info and Relationships.
- **Best fix:** Make the rail a plain <div> (or one <nav aria-label='Main'>) holding the three named nav groups.

### `a11y-practice-1-11`: The current rail item is told apart by a 2.4:1 brass underline and one step of weight

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.home.tiles.1280`, `p.cases.default.1280`, `p.claims.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases`, `/p/[practiceId]/claims`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The active RailLink is font-medium with a 2 px brass underline (apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx:119); the others are the same colour at normal weight (crop of p.home.tiles: 'Practice home' against 'Uploads'). Brass #C99A3B on the rail's #F7F8FA is 2.42:1, under 3:1, and 400 against 500 weight at 14 px is hard to see. aria-current='page' is set (shell.tsx:116), so screen readers are told; a low-vision sighted user is not. Dark tokens pass (brass on #0D1E3A is 6.5:1).
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 1.4.11 Non-text Contrast (the indicator of a state). .claude/rules/brand.md keeps brass as the single active-nav underline and is silent on a second cue.
- **Best fix:** Keep the brass underline and add a cue that does not depend on it: the active item at weight 600 with a 2 px Ink rule on its left edge (14.7:1), so the state reads without changing brass's one job. The brand reviewer should confirm the rule's colour.
- **Notes:** Recommendation where the brand rules are silent; not taste over a written rule.

### `a11y-practice-1-12`: Placeholder text is 3.3:1, and placeholders carry the expected format

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.claims.default.1280`, `p.coverage.default.1280`, `p.coverage.viewer.1280`, `p.cases.default.1280`, `p.by-reference.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/claims`, `/p/[practiceId]/coverage`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/by-reference`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/brand.css`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/claims/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/coverage/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/packets/reference-search.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Tailwind v4's preflight colours ::placeholder at 50% of the text colour (node_modules tailwindcss@4.3.3 preflight.css:296-300); measured RGB(134,142,156) on white, 3.3:1, on '97110' (claims Code), '97110, 97530' (coverage Procedure codes) and 'SK6CS-K6CSK' (packet reference). apps/web/src/app/brand.css sets no placeholder colour. These placeholders are the only place the format is shown (claims/page.tsx:76, coverage/page.tsx:379, components/packets/reference-search.tsx:9), and they vanish on typing.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 1.4.3 Contrast (Minimum); .claude/rules/brand.md 'text at least 4.5:1'.
- **Best fix:** In brand.css (base layer) set `::placeholder { color: var(--pw-fg-muted) }` (6.4:1 light, 8.4:1 dark), and move each format example into the field's hint under it, tied by aria-describedby.
- **Notes:** axe's colour-contrast rule does not test placeholder text, so the gate passes it.

### `a11y-practice-1-13`: Cards are region landmarks named differently from their visible titles, and empty states add more regions

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.home.default.1280`, `p.packet.default.1280`, `p.packet-review.default.1280`, `p.abstraction-version.default.1280`, `p.contracts.default.1280`, `p.contract.default.1280`, `p.coverage.default.1280`, `p.contract-version.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/coverage`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/versions/[versionId]`
- **Files:** `packages/ui/src/components/card.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/states/empty-state.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/versions/[versionId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/contracts/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/coverage/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/contracts/review.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/contracts/display.tsx`
- **Evidence:** 101 <Card role='region' aria-label> sit under app/p and components; 44 of the labels differ from the card's visible title. On these routes: home 'Dashboard' titled 'Where things stand' (app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx:279), 'Headline' titled 'The headline' (:195); packet 'Line table' / 'Line schedule' (cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx:264), 'Citation' / 'Contract citation' (:248), 'Calculation' / 'Calculation per line' (:292), 'Evidence' / 'Evidence list' (:311), 'Export history' / 'Versions and export history' (:537); review 'Export' / 'Exported files' (packet/[versionId]/review/page.tsx:101); abstraction version 'Approve the version' / 'Approve version 1', 'Amend the version' / 'Amend version 1' (abstraction/versions/[versionId]/page.tsx:377,409); 'Upload a contract or exhibit' / '…exhibit or payer letter' (contracts/page.tsx:192); 'Add a document' (contracts/[contractId]/page.tsx:142); 'Request-to-payer letter' / 'Request a missing fee schedule from a payer' (coverage/page.tsx:338). The rate record review nests a section 'Cited page' inside a region 'Cited page' titled 'The page' (components/contracts/review.tsx:188, components/contracts/display.tsx:65). Every EmptyState is one more named region (packages/ui/src/states/empty-state.tsx:27-29). The packet builder exposes about 13 region landmarks.
- **Rule:** docs/adr/0056 line 64 ('A region's accessible name carries the same words as its visible label'); WCAG 2.1 SC 1.3.1, 2.4.6 Headings and Labels.
- **Best fix:** Name each region by its own heading: Card with role='region' takes aria-labelledby pointing at its CardTitle's id (CardTitle accepts an id; a region card without a title is a type error), so the two can never drift. Drop role='region' from cards nobody navigates to by landmark and from EmptyState (its heading stays), and remove the nested 'Cited page' section.
- **Notes:** The 44 mismatches span routes outside this set too (reports, releases, reconciliation); one sweep fixes all.

### `a11y-practice-1-14`: Heading levels do not follow the page: card titles and empty states are always h2, and visual sub-headings are plain text

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.line.default.1280`, `p.submissions.attestation-waiting.1280`, `p.coverage.default.1280`, `p.abstraction-ledger.default.1280`, `p.contract-version.default.1280`, `p.plan.default.1280`, `p.packet-review.default.1280`, `p.packet.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions`, `/p/[practiceId]/coverage`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`
- **Files:** `packages/ui/src/components/card.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/states/empty-state.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/contracts/review.tsx`, `apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts`
- **Evidence:** CardTitle is always <h2> (packages/ui/src/components/card.tsx:21) and EmptyState's title always <h2> (packages/ui/src/states/empty-state.tsx:33), so a state inside a titled card or section becomes its sibling: 'Notes' h2 then 'No notes yet' h2 (line trace); 'Submission log' then 'Not submitted yet'; 'What to obtain' then 'The contract specialist is not waiting on any document'; 'Shortfalls' then the empty state (abstraction/ledger/page.tsx:127-129); 'Rate records' then 'Record 1: 97110, 97140, 97530' (components/contracts/review.tsx:210,226); 'Waiting on mapping' then 'How the plan's lines resolve now', also h2 (mapping/[planId]/page.tsx:97). Text that looks like a heading is not one: each line-trace step title ('Step 1: the agreement' …) is a <p class='font-medium'> (lines/[lineId]/page.tsx:143) on a 4,952 px page; 'Evidence, with its SHA-256 and gaps' and 'Interest, line by line' (cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review/page.tsx:233,237); 'Numbered sources every sentence of the narrative cites' (cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx:319); the review's second table (the calculation) has no heading at all.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 1.3.1 Info and Relationships, 2.4.6 Headings and Labels.
- **Best fix:** Give CardTitle and EmptyState a level (as='h2'|'h3'|'h4', default h2) and pass h3 wherever they sit under a section heading; mark the trace step titles, the review's sub-sections, the packet's sources line and a 'Calculation per line' title over the review's second table as h3. Add a check to apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts that heading levels never skip and never repeat a parent's level inside it (axe's heading-order is a best-practice rule, outside the wcag tags the gate runs, a11y.ts:12).
- **Notes:** #38 will rework the line-trace and packet explanations; mark its new step titles as headings there rather than twice.

### `a11y-practice-1-15`: A permission page has no heading and no way back to where the person came from

- **Flags:** polish; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.packet.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.packet-review.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.home.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.contracts.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.digest.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.abstraction.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.abstraction-ledger.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.abstraction-regulations.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.abstraction-version.error-forbidden.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review`, `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`, `/p/[practiceId]/digests/[digestId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/regulations`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/versions/[versionId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Forbidden renders only the PermissionState card inside the shell (apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx:358-363): no h1, so heading navigation finds nothing and the browser title ('Packet builder | PaidWhole') is the only sign of where the person is; the next step names a place ('open the case to see its state') with no link to it.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 AA is silent (2.4.10 Section Headings is AAA; 2.4.6 governs headings that exist); docs/product/PRD.md section 7 (a permission state that explains what the role cannot do). Recommendation.
- **Best fix:** Forbidden renders an h1 naming the screen ('Packet builder') above the PermissionState and takes an optional link the page passes (the case, the packet, the abstraction queue).
- **Notes:** The flows and words reviewers may raise the next-step wording; this entry is the heading and the link.

### `a11y-practice-1-16`: Wide tables and the cited page's text scroll in unnamed containers that a keyboard may not reach

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.claims.default.1280`, `p.cases.default.1280`, `p.anomalies.cohort.1280`, `p.abstraction-ledger.default.1280`, `p.home.default.1280`, `p.packet.default.1280`, `p.contract-version.default.1280`, `p.abstraction-version.default.1280`, `p.line.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/claims`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases`, `/p/[practiceId]/anomalies`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/anomalies/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/claims/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/coverage/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/contracts/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/mapping/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/contracts/review.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/contracts/display.tsx`
- **Evidence:** ScrollTable (apps/web/src/components/scroll-table.tsx:9-14) gives a named, focusable scroller and the packet's line schedule and calculations use it (correctly: in p.packet.error-export-blocked the schedule is cut at the card edge and scrolls inside it). Everywhere else a plain div.overflow-x-auto wraps the table with no name and no tabIndex: home agreements (app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx:438), cases list (cases/page.tsx:246), anomaly cohorts and flagged lines (anomalies/page.tsx:58,162), ledger entries (abstraction/ledger/page.tsx:48), claims (claims/page.tsx:134), packet versions (cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx:546), rate record fields (components/contracts/review.tsx:240), trace blocks (lines/[lineId]/page.tsx:157); Coverage's Unpriced reasons, Contracts and Mapping tables have no scroller at all. The rate record review's page text is a max-h-[32rem] overflow-auto box (components/contracts/display.tsx:91) with no tabIndex or name: a real contract page longer than 32 rem cannot be scrolled from the keyboard in Safari and is not announced as scrollable. The synthetic pages do not overflow at 1280, so axe's scrollable-region-focusable never fires, and atPhoneWidth (apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts:53) is not run on these routes.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 2.1.1 Keyboard, 4.1.2 Name, Role, Value (a scroll region with a name).
- **Best fix:** Wrap every wide table in ScrollTable (named after the table) and give CitedPageView's text box role='region', aria-label naming the document and page, tabIndex=0 and the focus ring; run atPhoneWidth on each of these routes in their specs.
- **Notes:** The 390 px reviewer owns the overflow itself (the earlier tour's agreements table); this entry is the keyboard reach and the name.

### `a11y-practice-1-17`: Loading states show no words, and their label is unlikely to be announced

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** none
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/claims`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`, `/p/[practiceId]/coverage`, `/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping`, `/p/[practiceId]/anomalies`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction`
- **Files:** `packages/ui/src/states/loading-state.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/loading.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/loading.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/loading.tsx`
- **Evidence:** No loading shot is in this set; from the code: LoadingState puts its label in an sr-only span (packages/ui/src/states/loading-state.tsx:27) inside a role='status' element that is inserted with its text already in it and with aria-busy='true' (loading-state.tsx:22-24). Screen readers commonly say nothing for a live region created together with its content, and aria-busy asks them to wait. Sighted people see only the still mark, a moving bar and grey rows. Each route's loading.tsx (e.g. app/p/[practiceId]/cases/loading.tsx, cases/[caseId]/packet/loading.tsx) renders a bare <main> without the shell, so the header and rail vanish while a page loads.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 4.1.3 Status Messages; the brief (a loading label shown on screen, not only read to screen readers); docs/product/PRD.md section 7 (every screen has a loading state).
- **Best fix:** Show the label as text beside the bar ('Loading the case…', text-sm, muted); remove aria-busy from the status element; have each loading.tsx render a still frame of the shell (the lockup header and an empty rail, no data) with the status inside its <main>, so the region exists and the frame does not flash away.
- **Notes:** Every loading.tsx under app/p follows the same pattern; the states reviewer captured the loading shots.

### `a11y-practice-1-18`: 'Download the letter' with a code it refuses leaves the app for a raw JSON error

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.coverage.error-letter-codes.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/coverage`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/coverage/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/coverage/letter/route.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/http/handler.ts`
- **Evidence:** The browser shows only {"status":"invalid","issues":[{"field":"codes","message":"procedure codes of five letters or digits, separated by commas"}]}: no shell, no form, no link back, and the entries are gone. The letter form is a plain GET to /coverage/letter (app/p/[practiceId]/coverage/page.tsx:347) served by downloadHandler (coverage/letter/route.ts:7), which answers a refusal with JSON; the codes input has no client constraint (coverage/page.tsx:379).
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 3.3.1 Error Identification, 3.3.3 Error Suggestion; docs/product/PRD.md:256 (every error with a next step).
- **Best fix:** On refusal the letter route redirects back to /coverage with the payer, contract and codes in the query (no PHI in them) and an error key; the form renders the message under the codes field with aria-invalid and aria-describedby, keeping the entries. Give the input a pattern and a hint stating the format so most refusals never reach the server.
- **Notes:** Other lenses will see the same dead end; keep one entry.

### `a11y-practice-1-19`: On 'Find a packet by its reference', the refused field is not marked or tied to its message

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.by-reference.error.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/by-reference`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/packets/reference-search.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/by-reference/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** 'Not a reference' shows in an alert under the form while the field still holding 'hello' has no aria-invalid and is described only by its hint (apps/web/src/components/packets/reference-search.tsx:9; app/p/[practiceId]/cases/by-reference/page.tsx:35-40).
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 3.3.1 Error Identification.
- **Best fix:** Pass the refusal into ReferenceSearch: set aria-invalid, render the message under the field with an id, and add that id to aria-describedby ahead of the hint.

### `a11y-practice-1-20`: The hidden file input is a tab stop with no visible focus, beside the 'Choose files' button

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.contracts.default.1280`, `p.contracts.layout.1280`, `p.contracts.default.1280-dark`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/uploads/uploader.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Uploader's <input type='file' className='sr-only'> (apps/web/src/components/uploads/uploader.tsx:376-386) has no tabIndex=-1, so on the Contracts upload card a keyboard user tabs onto a clipped 1 px control whose focus outline cannot be seen, then onto the visible 'Choose files' button that opens the same picker; a screen reader announces two controls for one action.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 2.4.7 Focus Visible, 2.4.3 Focus Order.
- **Best fix:** Give the file input tabIndex={-1} and aria-hidden='true'; the 'Choose files' button stays the one control and opens the picker as it does now.
- **Notes:** The same Uploader serves the Uploads screen (outside this set).

### `a11y-practice-1-21`: Repeated link text ('Line trace', 'Adjust', 'PDF', 'CSV') with no row context, because no table has row headers

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.claims.default.1280`, `p.packet.default.1280`, `p.packet-review.default.1280`, `p.abstraction-ledger.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/claims`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/claims/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/packets/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Claims and lines has 35 'Line trace' links (app/p/[practiceId]/claims/page.tsx:221) in a cell headed only 'Status'; the packet and review line schedules repeat 'Line trace' under 'Trace' (apps/web/src/components/packets/display.tsx:197); the ledger repeats 'Adjust' under 'Correct' (abstraction/ledger/page.tsx:24); the packet's versions table repeats 'PDF' and 'CSV' (cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx:599). None of these tables has a row header (th scope='row'), so a links list, or a cell read on its own, does not say which line, entry or version the link is for.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 2.4.4 Link Purpose (In Context), 1.3.1 Info and Relationships (row headers).
- **Best fix:** Make each row's identifying cell a <th scope='row'> (claims: the patient and claim cell, or 'Line n of N' for a viewer; schedules: Row; ledger: #; versions: Version) and give each repeated control a name carrying its row through visually hidden text ('Line trace, line 3 of 3', 'Adjust entry 7', 'PDF of version 3').

### `a11y-practice-1-22`: The rate record's fields table has no column headers

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.contract-version.default.1280`, `p.abstraction-version.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/versions/[versionId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/contracts/review.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The 'Fields' table has a row header per field but no <thead> (apps/web/src/components/contracts/review.tsx:241-265), so the value, the approval and (for the specialist) the Approve button columns are read with no column name.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 1.3.1 Info and Relationships.
- **Best fix:** Add a header row, visible or visually hidden: 'Field', 'As abstracted', 'Approval', and 'Approve' when the specialist's controls are present.

### `a11y-practice-1-23`: Dark tokens set no color-scheme, so native date pickers and checkboxes keep light-scheme glyphs (advisory)

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.contracts.default.1280-dark`, `p.submissions.default.1280-dark`, `p.plan.default.1280-dark`, `p.abstraction.default.1280-dark`, `p.claims.default.1280-dark`, `p.packet.default.1280-dark`, `p.contract.default.1280-dark`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction`, `/p/[practiceId]/claims`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]`
- **Files:** `packages/brand/src/tokens.css`
- **Evidence:** Dark (tokens only): the calendar buttons in the date and month inputs are dark glyphs on the navy field and nearly invisible; the evidence checkboxes render as light squares. The dark block of packages/brand/src/tokens.css (:73-100) sets no color-scheme. No user reaches dark today (docs/adr/0056, Dark mode).
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 1.4.11 Non-text Contrast (advisory; dark is tokens only).
- **Best fix:** Set color-scheme: light in :root and color-scheme: dark in the dark block of tokens.css, so native controls follow the theme; do it with the dark-mode decision.
- **Notes:** The brand reviewer owns the dark-mode decision; this is the contrast consequence.

### `a11y-practice-1-24`: The quoted words on the cited page are marked only visually

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.contract-version.default.1280`, `p.contract-version.notice.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/versions/[versionId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/contracts/display.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The quote is a <mark> with a faint inset tint and thin underline inside the page text (apps/web/src/components/contracts/display.tsx:94). Most screen readers do not announce <mark>, so a screen-reader user reading the page cannot tell where the cited words start and end, which is the check this screen exists for; the record card repeats the quote, but not its place on the page.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 1.3.1 Info and Relationships.
- **Best fix:** Put visually hidden 'Start of the cited words' and 'End of the cited words' around each marked segment and add a 'Go to the cited words' link above the text that moves focus to the first mark (tabIndex -1).
- **Notes:** The brand reviewer may raise the faint mark; this is the non-visual side.

### `a11y-practice-1-25`: How the observed allowed was derived is shown only in a hover tooltip

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.claims.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/claims`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/claims/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The Observed allowed cell carries its basis ('AMT B6 (the payer's allowed amount)', 'Paid plus patient responsibility…') only as a title attribute (app/p/[practiceId]/claims/page.tsx:197): a mouse can hover it, a keyboard or touch user cannot reach it.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 1.3.1 and 2.1.1 (information available only on pointer hover).
- **Best fix:** Show the basis as small text under the amount, as 'allocated' already is, or leave it to the line trace and drop the tooltip.

### `a11y-practice-1-26`: The narrative's live check re-announces its counts while the person types

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.packet.error-live-check.1280`, `p.packet.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/packets/narrative-editor.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The whole check block, badge, counts and mismatch list, is role='status' aria-live='polite' and also the textarea's description (apps/web/src/components/packets/narrative-editor.tsx:34-36). Its counts ('4 sentences, 15 figures, placeholders and citations checked…') change whenever a sentence or a figure is added, so a screen reader speaks over the typing.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 4.1.3 Status Messages (announce the status, not every recount).
- **Best fix:** Keep the visible block as it is but not live; add a visually hidden polite region that announces only a change of verdict ('Live check: 1 mismatch', 'Live check passes'), a second after typing stops.

### `a11y-practice-1-27`: Page titles do not name the record, so moving between two cases, packets or versions announces nothing

- **Flags:** polish; size M
- **Screens:** `p.case.default.1280`, `p.packet.default.1280`, `p.contract-version.default.1280`, `p.plan.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/contracts/versions/[versionId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Titles are fixed per route ('Case', 'Packet builder', 'Rate record review', 'Plan mapping', 'Line trace', 'Contract': e.g. app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/page.tsx metadata), so two browser tabs on two cases read the same, and Next's route announcer, which speaks only a changed document.title, is silent when the person opens another version (?version=) or another plan.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 2.4.2 Page Titled (titles that describe the page's topic).
- **Best fix:** generateMetadata per record from non-PHI facts the page already loads: 'SYNTHETIC PAYER Q, CE:SYNPLAN-FIX-ONE: case', 'Packet version 3: …', 'Version 1 of SYNTHETIC PAYER C AGREEMENT'; never a patient, claim number or service date (the line trace keeps 'Line trace: 97110').
- **Notes:** Titles must stay free of PHI (CLAUDE.md 'Never'); payer, plan and contract names are not PHI.

### `a11y-practice-1-28`: The e2e accessibility gate never visits half of these routes, checks no refusal state, and cannot see most of the failures above

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; in flight: #103 (Lane A, PR 7)
- **Screens:** none
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/regulations`, `/p/[practiceId]/anomalies`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/contracts.spec.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/mapping.spec.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/triage.spec.ts`
- **Evidence:** expectAccessible (apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts:11-15) runs on 11 of these 21 routes; it never runs on /abstraction, /abstraction/ledger, /abstraction/regulations, /anomalies, /contracts, /contracts/[contractId], /contracts/versions/[versionId], /lines/[lineId], /mapping or /mapping/[planId] (contracts.spec.ts:167 calls atPhoneWidth with axe: false; mapping.spec.ts, pricing.spec.ts, reconciliation.spec.ts and viewer-phi.spec.ts call none). It runs the WCAG 2.1 tags only, on default states; no refusal state of these routes is checked. tabTo (a11y.ts:22-46) proves an outline style is set, not that the focused element is visible. What axe cannot see here: -01 to -04 (error wording, marking, reset, focus), -05 (alert misuse), -06 (label-content-name-mismatch is experimental and off), -07 and -08 (2.4.11), -09, -11 (non-text contrast), -12 (placeholder text is not tested), -13, -14 (heading-order is best-practice), -16 (fires only when a region overflows at test time), -17, -20, -21.
- **Rule:** Combined audit #103 (a generated check that visits every route with a role that can open it, WCAG 2.2 rules, and manual checks for the 2.2 criteria no tool tests).
- **Best fix:** Fold into #103's generated per-route check: each route above, with its refusal state, at both widths, under the WCAG 2.2 tags; add the tab-and-shift-tab visibility check from -08 to tabTo, and list 2.5.3, 2.4.11, 3.3.7 and 4.1.3 in the manual checks with these screens as their cases.
- **Notes:** Recorded so #103's PR gets this route list and these failure kinds; not a separate Lane B PR.

## a11y-practice-2

### `a11y-practice-2-01`: A refused form comes back emptied: what the person typed is gone, and a valid default sits beside the error

- **Flags:** blocking; size M
- **Screens:** `p.pricing-runs.error.1280`, `p.roi.error.1280`, `p.correspondence.error.1280`, `p.defaults.error.1280`, `p.pricing-settings.error.1280`, `p.reconciliation.error-reverse.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/correspondence`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/pricing`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx`, `apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts`
- **Evidence:** After a server refusal every uncontrolled field is reset to its default, because ActionForm posts through <form action={formAction}> with useActionState and React 19 resets the form when the action returns (apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx:32, :65; nothing restores the submitted values). Seen in six shots: pricing runs' First and Last month come back blank (the months entered are lost) with Last month outlined red; ROI's First/Last day show the suggested 07/01/2026 to 09/30/2026, a valid period, beside 'periodTo: the period ends on or after its first day' (reports/roi/page.tsx:53-54 defaults); correspondence returns all nine fields blank (settings/correspondence/page.tsx:73-86 default only to the stored contact); the staff rate is blank; Pricing settings shows the stored 27601, a valid ZIP, outlined red beside 'Billing ZIP: five digits...'; Reverse this match comes back with the reason empty and the consequence checkbox unticked (reconciliation/page.tsx:42-46). The same component serves every mutation in this set (pricing-runs/[runId], releases, reports, review checklist, monthly, agreements, proof-level, template). axe cannot see it: it scans a static page and never submits a refused value.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.2 3.3.7 Redundant Entry (A; audit #103 adopts WCAG 2.2); WCAG 3.3.1 Error Identification (A): the marked field shows a value that is not the one refused; docs/product/PRD.md:256 (every error with a next step; no state a refresh loses)
- **Best fix:** In ActionForm, capture the submitted FormData on submit (an onSubmit handler that does not prevent the action, kept in a ref) and, when the action returns a refusal, write each named value back into its control (text, textarea, select, checkbox, radio; never the hidden request key, the CSRF token or a password) before marking and focusing the first invalid control. Keep the form's action prop so it still submits without script. Add an e2e helper that types values, submits a refused one and asserts every typed value survives, and run it on each error state the route map names.
- **Notes:** Rated blocking because the person's entry is lost (the rubric's 'loses data') and it is a Level A failure on every form; the ROI and ZIP cases also mislead by showing a valid value next to the error. The words lens will likely flag the same shots for wording; this finding is the mechanism.

### `a11y-practice-2-02`: Refusals name the field by its code, sit below the fields while saying 'marked below', and mark the field by colour alone

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.pricing-runs.error.1280`, `p.roi.error.1280`, `p.correspondence.error.1280`, `p.defaults.error.1280`, `p.reconciliation.error-reverse.1280`, `p.pricing-settings.error.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/correspondence`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/pricing`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/field.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/components/input.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts`
- **Evidence:** The summary prints labels[issue.field] ?? issue.field (action-form.tsx:76) and only Pricing settings passes labels (settings/pricing/page.tsx:97, :112), so the others show 'toMonth:', 'periodTo:', 'name:', 'staffRatePerHour:', 'reason:'; p.pricing-settings.error shows the right form ('Billing ZIP:'). The summary renders after the fields (action-form.tsx:67-84) yet reads 'Check the fields marked below.' (lib/http/next.ts:162, :180). The field's only visible mark is its border turning red (packages/ui/src/components/input.tsx:11 aria-invalid:border-destructive): no text or icon beside it, so a person who cannot tell the red from the grey outline, and who reads a code in the summary, cannot find the field. The reverse-match text area gets no visible mark at all (reconciliation/page.tsx:42 is a bare textarea; field.tsx TextAreaField has no error either), nor does a select (field.tsx:83-91). Field's inline error slot (components/field.tsx:26-32) is never fed by ActionForm. The ARIA wiring itself is right (aria-invalid, aria-describedby to the summary item, focus to the first invalid control), so axe passes, but a screen reader hears 'toMonth: the same month as the first, or later'.
- **Rule:** WCAG 3.3.1 Error Identification (A); 1.4.1 Use of Color (A); 3.3.3 Error Suggestion (AA); PRD.md:256 (every error with a next step)
- **Best fix:** ActionForm names each issue by the control's own label (control.labels[0].textContent; the labels prop only as an override), with a unit test that fails when a summary item equals a field key. Render the summary at the top of the form ('Not done: 1 field to fix', each item a link that focuses its field) and an inline message under each field through a small context that Field, SelectField and TextAreaField read (text with an icon, not colour alone); give textarea and select the same aria-invalid style as Input; ReverseForm uses TextAreaField. Reword the summary to match its place.
- **Notes:** Overlaps the words lens on the raw keys and 'marked below'; the accessibility part is the colour-only mark and the missing inline message.

### `a11y-practice-2-03`: Upload and template-sample errors are not tied to their control, and the template preview is never announced

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.uploads.error-era-system.1280`, `p.reconciliation.error-empty-file.1280`, `p.template.error.1280`, `p.template.preview.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/uploads`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/template`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/uploads/uploader.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/reconciliation/template-mapper.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Uploads: choosing files while 'Which system's export' is unset discards the files and shows 'Nothing was uploaded / Choose which system's ERA export...' in an alert under the drop zone (components/uploads/uploader.tsx:182-183, :393-404); the select itself gets no aria-invalid, no message and no focus (uploader.tsx:345-361). 'File 1 is empty.' names the file by its position in the batch, not its name (uploader.tsx:189-191). Template: 'That file is not a CSV with a header row.' is a plain <p> (components/reconciliation/template-mapper.tsx:100-104), not a live region, not in the file input's aria-describedby, and the input is not marked invalid; the preview ('1 row reads; 0 rows are left out...', or 'The sample cannot be read as a CSV.') appears with no status role (template-mapper.tsx:156-207). A screen-reader user who chooses a sample hears nothing. axe never chooses a file, so it cannot reach either state.
- **Rule:** WCAG 3.3.1 Error Identification (A); 4.1.3 Status Messages (AA); 3.3.3 Error Suggestion (AA)
- **Best fix:** Uploads: when the kind needs a system and none is chosen, do not open the file picker; mark the select aria-invalid, show the message under it (aria-describedby) and move focus to it; name files in every problem by file name ('posting-export.csv is empty'). Template: tie the sample error to the file input (aria-describedby, aria-invalid, role="alert") and put the preview's one-line result in a role="status" region.

### `a11y-practice-2-04`: Repeated and bare link names ('Line trace' x32, 'case', '0', 'the ledger's', 'PDF', 'Report') with no row header to give them context

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.pricing-run.april.1280`, `p.pricing-run.default.1280`, `p.compare.default.1280`, `p.reconciliation.default.1280`, `p.reconciliation.case-ledger.1280`, `p.access-log.default.1280`, `p.access-log.record-filter.1280`, `p.monthly.default.1280`, `p.report-document.default.1280`, `p.reports.audit-reports.1280`, `p.uploads.default.1280`, `p.roi-statement.default.1280`, `p.remittance.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/compare`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/access-log`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/monthly`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/documents/[documentId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports`, `/p/[practiceId]/uploads`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/remittances/[remittanceId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/pricing/diff.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/access-log/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/reports/documents.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/uploads/receipts.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/reports/roi.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/remittances/[remittanceId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The April run lists 32 links all named 'Line trace' over rows that are themselves identical (pricing-runs/[runId]/page.tsx:267-270); Compare lists 32 'Second trace' and 3 'First trace' (components/pricing/diff.tsx:164-175); every ledger row links 'Case ledger' (reconciliation/page.tsx:121-123, :165-170); the access log's 50 Record links are named by the raw object type 'case', 'service_line', 'submission' (settings/access-log/page.tsx:69-75), which a screen reader reads as 'service underscore line'; the monthly By stage table's links are named '0', '3', '$0.00' and 'the ledger's' (components/reports/documents.tsx:146-158, :185-187); the generated-reports table has 'PDF' and 'CSV' per row (reports/page.tsx:264-271); receipts have 'Report' per row and document links named by an 8-character hex prefix (components/uploads/receipts.tsx:165-167, :176-178); the ROI statement has 'In its case's ledger' per entry (components/reports/roi.tsx:70-72). No table in this set marks its identifying cell as a row header (<th scope="row">), so the programmatic context 2.4.4 allows (the cell or its headers) never names the row; a links list or a table walk gives 32 indistinguishable 'Line trace'. axe has no rule for link purpose.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.4.4 Link Purpose (In Context) (A); 1.3.1 Info and Relationships (A)
- **Best fix:** In each table make the identifying cell a <th scope="row"> (code and service date, stage, period, document, entry number) and give each repeated link a name that carries its row in visually hidden text, e.g. 'Line trace<span class="sr-only"> for 97110 on 2026-04-03, line 4</span>'; name the access log's links in words ('Reads of this case'); name the monthly table's figures ('3 lines at actionable', 'Actionable dollars, $90.00', 'Payer approved dollars on Reconciliation'). One shared RowLink helper keeps the pattern.
- **Notes:** Audit #107 (reasons in words on the lines table) is the claims screen, not these; the words lens will raise the raw keys themselves.

### `a11y-practice-2-05`: Stacked small links sit 20 px apart or touching: below the 24 px target size

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.remittance.viewer.1280`, `p.compare.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/remittances/[remittanceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/compare`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/remittances/[remittanceId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/pricing/diff.tsx`, `apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts`
- **Evidence:** On a remittance's Claims table the Links cell stacks 'Reversed in a later remittance', 'Superseded by a corrected claim' and '1 line' as 12 px text links about 16 px tall at a 20 px pitch (p.remittance.viewer.1280, x 926-1102, y 745-799), from remittances/[remittanceId]/page.tsx:181-201 (text-xs, flex-col gap-1). The 24 px circles centred on neighbours overlap, so neither the size nor the spacing exception of 2.5.8 holds; they are not inline in a sentence. The diff table stacks 'First trace' and 'Second trace' with no gap at all (components/pricing/diff.tsx:164-175), which renders both for every line priced in both runs (the 'Priced differently' rows of Compare and of a re-run's page). axe's target-size rule exists but is tagged wcag22aa, which e2e/a11y.ts:12 does not run.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.2 2.5.8 Target Size (Minimum) (AA; audit #103)
- **Best fix:** Give every stacked or adjacent small link a 24 px box: render them as inline-flex items-center min-h-6 (or py-1) with gap-2, in both cells; better still, one 'Open the line' link per row with the relationships as text beside it. Add 'wcag22aa' to the axe tags so target-size runs on every visit.

### `a11y-practice-2-06`: The 56 px sticky header covers what keyboard focus and in-page links bring into view

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.roi-statement.default.1280`, `p.report.default.1280`, `p.report-review.default.1280`, `p.reconciliation.case-ledger.1280`, `p.access-log.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/review`, `every practice route`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/brand.css`, `apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts`
- **Evidence:** The app header is sticky at the top, 56 px tall, z-30 (components/shell.tsx:315), and nothing sets scroll-padding-top or scroll-margin-top anywhere (apps/web/src/app/brand.css, packages/brand/src/tokens.css, globals.css). A fragment link scrolls its target to the viewport's top edge, under the header: the ROI statement's 'In its case's ledger' goes to reconciliation?case=...#entry-<id> (components/reports/roi.tsx:70; target row id at reconciliation/page.tsx:110), and that ledger row, about 29 px tall, lands entirely behind the header; the audit report's and checklist's stage tiles and 'Flagged'/'Validated' links jump to #stage-* sections (components/reports/display.tsx:69-82, 93-104) whose titles land under it. Shift+Tab up a long page (access log, reconciliation) scrolls each focused control to the top edge, where the header hides it. Neither axe nor tabTo checks this: tabTo (e2e/a11y.ts:36-41) only asserts an outline style exists, never that the focused element is in view.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.2 2.4.11 Focus Not Obscured (Minimum) (AA), named in audit #103; 2.4.3 Focus Order (A)
- **Best fix:** One rule in apps/web/src/app/brand.css: html { scroll-padding-top: calc(3.5rem + 0.75rem); } (the header's height plus a gap), so both focus scrolling and fragment jumps stop below the header. Add the keyboard test #103 asks for: Shift+Tab through a long page and assert each focused element's rectangle lies below the header's bottom edge, and assert the #entry-<id> row is visible after following an ROI entry link.

### `a11y-practice-2-07`: Header practice switcher and account menu: their labels hide the visible name, the practice name is cut off, and the menus never close on Escape or when focus leaves

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.pricing-runs.default.1280`, `p.pricing-runs.viewer.1280`, `p.reconciliation.default.1280`, `p.packet-reviews.default.1280`, `p.notifications.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `every practice route`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The switcher's summary has aria-label="Practice switcher" (components/shell.tsx:56), which replaces its visible text, so a screen reader announces 'Practice switcher' and never which practice is open; the account summary's aria-label="Account menu" (shell.tsx:84) likewise replaces the visible email. The visible name is truncated at 14rem with no other way to read it (shell.tsx:57): LAYOUT and LEDGER both read 'SYNTHETIC OTHER PRACTICE L...' (p.pricing-runs.default vs p.pricing-runs.viewer). Both menus and the narrow-screen Menu are <details> with no script (shell.tsx:55, 83, 322): Escape does nothing, and they stay open when focus moves on. The open panel is absolute, z-40, under the header's right side (shell.tsx:46, 60, 91), which is where 'Help with this screen' sits (shell.tsx:335-336, top right of main); after the rail, Tab reaches that control while the panel can still cover it. axe: label-content-name-mismatch is an 'experimental' rule that e2e/a11y.ts does not enable; menus are never opened in a scan.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.5.3 Label in Name (A); 2.4.11 Focus Not Obscured (Minimum) (AA, WCAG 2.2); 4.1.2 Name, Role, Value (A)
- **Best fix:** Drop both aria-labels and let the visible text be the name, with a visually hidden prefix ('Practice: SYNTHETIC OTHER PRACTICE LEDGER', 'Account: ledger-manager@synthetic.test'). Show the whole practice name (let it wrap to two lines, or widen the control; a title attribute only as a fallback). Wrap the disclosures in a small client component that closes a menu on Escape (focus back to its summary), when focus leaves it and on an outside click.
- **Notes:** The truncation is also a flows/brand observation in the checker notes; the accessibility part is that the name is unreachable by any means.

### `a11y-practice-2-08`: No skip link: about 22 tab stops before the page content on every practice screen

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.notifications.default.1280`, `p.pricing-runs.default.1280`, `p.reconciliation.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `every practice route`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The shell's first focusable elements are the lockup link, the switcher, the account menu, then the rail's 14 practice links and 4 account links, then 'Help with this screen' (components/shell.tsx:313-336); there is no skip link in the shell or the root layout (apps/web/src/app/layout.tsx:33-35). A keyboard user who does not use a screen reader's landmark navigation tabs about 22 times on every page before reaching the first control of the screen.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.4.1 Bypass Blocks (A) is technically met through the landmarks (header, nav, main: technique ARIA11), so this is not a conformance failure; the recommendation rests on PRD.md:256 (keyboard navigation on every screen) and G1, the usual sufficient technique.
- **Best fix:** Add 'Skip to main content' as the first focusable element of AppShell, hidden until focused and styled with --pw-focus, pointing to <main id="main" tabIndex={-1}> (shell.tsx:334).

### `a11y-practice-2-09`: The current page, tab and filter are marked below 3:1 or by weight alone

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.settings.default.1280`, `p.access-log.default.1280`, `p.compare.diff-filter.1280`, `p.pricing-run.default.1280`, `p.releases.release.1280`, `p.releases.default.1280`, `p.pricing-runs.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `every practice route`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/*`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/compare`, `/p/[practiceId]/releases`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/settings/nav.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/compare/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/releases/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Rail: the active item's only marks are a brass underline, Brass #C99A3B on the rail's #F7F8FA = 2.42:1, and font weight 500 against 400 (components/shell.tsx:119). Settings tabs: the current tab is a #EFF1F4 fill on white, 1.13:1, plus weight 500 (components/settings/nav.tsx:41; p.settings.default 'Overview', p.access-log.default 'Access log'). Line and diff filters: the current filter differs from the others only by weight 500 vs 400, both underlined links (pricing-runs/[runId]/page.tsx:227; pricing-runs/compare/page.tsx:91-92; p.compare.diff-filter). Releases: the chosen release carries aria-current with no visual mark at all, so p.releases.release is pixel-identical to p.releases.default (releases/page.tsx:106). axe does not test state indicators for contrast.
- **Rule:** WCAG 1.4.11 Non-text Contrast (AA) for the visual information that identifies a state; .claude/rules/brand.md:32 keeps Brass as the single active-nav underline, so the fix adds a second cue instead of recolouring Brass. For the releases list WCAG is silent (the state is programmatic); recommend a visible mark so sighted users get it too.
- **Best fix:** Keep the brass underline and add a cue that reaches 3:1: the active rail item in font-semibold Ink with a 2 px Ink inline-start bar; the current settings tab with a 1.5 px Ink border and font-semibold; the current filter rendered as bold text that is not a link, with a 2 px Ink underline, and a count beside each filter; the chosen release row in bold with 'shown above'. Dark mode uses --pw-fg for the same cues.
- **Notes:** Brand lens owns the look of the second cue; the contrast numbers are computed from tokens.css.

### `a11y-practice-2-10`: Loading states: the label is read to screen readers only, aria-busy can silence it, the shell vanishes while loading, and nine routes have no loading state

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** none
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports`, `/p/[practiceId]/uploads`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/*`, `/p/[practiceId]/support-access/[supportGrantId]`
- **Files:** `packages/ui/src/states/loading-state.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/layout.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/loading.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/uploads/loading.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/support-access/[supportGrantId]/loading.tsx`
- **Evidence:** No loading shot is in this set; code only. LoadingState puts role="status", aria-live="polite" and aria-busy="true" on the same node and its label in an sr-only span (packages/ui/src/states/loading-state.tsx:22-27): sighted people see a moving bar and grey rows with no words; aria-busy="true" on the live region asks assistive technology to hold announcements until it clears, and it never clears because the node is replaced; a live region inserted together with its text is often not announced at all. Each route's loading.tsx renders it inside its own <main> (e.g. app/p/[practiceId]/reports/loading.tsx:4-9) and no layout exists under /p, so the header and rail disappear while a page loads. settings, settings/access-log, agreements, correspondence, data, defaults, pricing, proof-level, uploads and support-access have no loading.tsx. The receipts list's in-page LoadingState (components/uploads/receipts.tsx:131) has the same silent label.
- **Rule:** WCAG 4.1.3 Status Messages (AA); PRD.md section 7 (every screen has a loading state). WCAG does not itself require visible loading text: showing the label on screen is this review's rule and the PRD's.
- **Best fix:** LoadingState shows its label as visible text beside the bar ('Loading pricing runs...') and drops aria-busy from the status node; announcements go through one polite live region that exists in the root layout from first paint (apps/web/src/app/layout.tsx), written by a client hook when a loading boundary mounts and cleared when the page arrives; add loading.tsx to the settings routes, uploads and support-access.

### `a11y-practice-2-11`: Long-running jobs say 'reload in a moment': nothing refreshes and nothing announces when the work is done

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.report.generating.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/documents/[documentId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/compare`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/packets/export-status.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/documents/[documentId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/compare/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** A queued audit report shows a static 'Generating / ...reload in a moment' alert (reports/[reportId]/page.tsx:51-56); the same manual reload stands for a printing document (reports/documents/[documentId]/page.tsx:51), a printing ROI statement (reports/roi/[statementId]/page.tsx:79), a run being priced (pricing-runs/[runId]/page.tsx:173-177) and Compare waiting on a run (pricing-runs/compare/page.tsx:75). The page never refreshes, so a screen-reader user, or anyone, learns of completion only by reloading. The product already has the right pattern for packet exports: a polite status region that refreshes itself and offers 'Check again' (components/packets/export-status.tsx:20-44).
- **Rule:** WCAG 4.1.3 Status Messages (AA); PRD.md:256 (no state a refresh loses; every state with a next step)
- **Best fix:** Generalise ExportStatus into a JobStatus component and use it for report generation, document and ROI printing, a run being priced and Compare waiting: role="status" aria-live="polite", router.refresh every 5 s while queued, a 'Check again' link, and a status message when the job ends ('The report is ready: its PDF and CSV are below').
- **Notes:** The states lens may raise the same 'reload' wording; this finding is the missing announcement and refresh.

### `a11y-practice-2-12`: Headings do not nest: every card and empty state is an h2, forbidden states have no h1, and region names differ from their visible titles

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.reports.default.1280`, `p.reports.audit-reports.1280`, `p.pricing-run.default.1280`, `p.reconciliation.default.1280`, `p.roi.default.1280`, `p.packet-reviews.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.access-log.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.report-review.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.support-access.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.reconciliation.case-ledger.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/reports`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi`, `/p/[practiceId]/packet-reviews`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/access-log`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/review`, `/p/[practiceId]/support-access/[supportGrantId]`
- **Files:** `packages/ui/src/components/card.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/states/empty-state.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/uploads/uploader.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** CardTitle is always an h2 (packages/ui/src/components/card.tsx:21) and EmptyState always an h2 (packages/ui/src/states/empty-state.tsx:33), so Reports reads h2 'Audit reports' > h2 'Generate an audit report' > h2 'No audit report yet'; a run's h2 'Coverage' holds h2 'No line of this run is unpriced'; Reconciliation's h2 'Upload posting and EFT exports' holds the uploader's h2 'Drop posting or EFT exports here' (components/uploads/uploader.tsx:313). Forbidden renders no heading at all (components/shell.tsx:358-363): the four forbidden shots have no h1. The ROI statements table has no heading (reports/roi/page.tsx:59-63). The case ledger's h1 is 'Case ledger' with no case named (reconciliation/page.tsx:223). Reconciliation's column titles ('ERA: the remittance that reprocessed it', 'Posting', 'EFT or bank deposit', 'Remittances of its number or its amount') are styled <p> (reconciliation/page.tsx:92, 338, 470). Card regions are named by aria-label strings that differ from their visible titles ('Exports waiting' vs 'Exports waiting for an attestation', packet-reviews/page.tsx:91; 'Upload exports', reconciliation/page.tsx:257; 'Runs it would change' vs 'Your runs', releases/page.tsx:38). axe: heading-order, page-has-heading-one and empty-heading are best-practice rules e2e/a11y.ts:12 does not run, and h2 inside h2 passes even heading-order.
- **Rule:** WCAG 1.3.1 Info and Relationships (A); 2.4.6 Headings and Labels (AA)
- **Best fix:** Give @tp/ui a heading-level context (a Section that raises the level by one; CardTitle, EmptyState and the uploader's heading render the level they are given), so a card inside an h2 section titles at h3 and an empty state inside a card at h4. Forbidden takes the page's title and renders it as the h1 above the permission note. Give the ROI list an h2 'Statements', name the case line in the case ledger's h1, make the reconciliation column titles headings one level below the case line, and name each Card region by aria-labelledby its own title instead of a second string.

### `a11y-practice-2-13`: Wide tables scroll in unnamed wrappers a keyboard cannot reach, or in none; some tables are unnamed or carry an empty header

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.packet-reviews.default.1280`, `p.reconciliation.default.1280`, `p.reconciliation.viewer.1280`, `p.pricing-run.april.1280`, `p.compare.default.1280`, `p.access-log.default.1280`, `p.support-access.default.1280`, `p.uploads.default.1280`, `p.remittance.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/packet-reviews`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/template`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/pricing`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/compare`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/access-log`, `/p/[practiceId]/support-access/[supportGrantId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/uploads`, `/p/[practiceId]/remittances/[remittanceId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/scroll-table.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/packet-reviews/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/pricing/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/reconciliation/template-mapper.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/reports/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/support-access/[supportGrantId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/uploads/receipts.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The keyboard-reachable, named scroller exists (components/scroll-table.tsx:9-15) but only Pricing settings' months table and the packet pages use it. Five tables sit in a bare overflow-x-auto div with no tabindex and no name (packet-reviews/page.tsx:48, reconciliation/page.tsx:136, settings/pricing/page.tsx:137, components/reconciliation/template-mapper.tsx:173, components/reports/display.tsx:120): once it scrolls, a keyboard user cannot scroll it and a screen-reader user is not told it scrolls. Most others have no wrapper at all (pricing-runs/[runId]/page.tsx:235, components/pricing/diff.tsx:115, remittances/page.tsx:71, remittances/[remittanceId]/page.tsx:107, :145, settings/access-log/page.tsx:46, components/uploads/receipts.tsx:145, components/reports/documents.tsx:171, :234, reports/page.tsx:239, :287, reports/roi/page.tsx:63, support-access page.tsx:45), so at 320 CSS px (1280 at 400% zoom) the page itself scrolls sideways. The support-access and receipts tables have no accessible name (support-access/[supportGrantId]/page.tsx:45, receipts.tsx:145). For a viewer the ledger tables keep an empty last header cell over an empty column (reconciliation/page.tsx:147; p.reconciliation.viewer). axe's scrollable-region-focusable only fires when the region overflows at scan time; a11y.ts scans at 1280 except where a spec calls atPhoneWidth, which none of these routes do; empty-table-header is a best-practice rule not run.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1.1 Keyboard (A); 1.4.10 Reflow (AA; data tables may scroll in two dimensions but must stay operable); 4.1.2 Name, Role, Value (A) for the scroll region; 1.3.1 (A) for the empty header
- **Best fix:** Wrap every data table in ScrollTable, named from the table's own name, and make ScrollTable a small client component that sets tabIndex=0 and role=region only while the table actually overflows (so 1280 gets no idle tab stop); give the two unnamed tables an aria-label; drop the Reason column, header and cells, when the role cannot read PHI instead of leaving it empty. The 390 px lens owns the layout side; this is the keyboard and naming side.
- **Notes:** touches42 only through the support-access page's table name; the rest can land before #42.

### `a11y-practice-2-14`: Month fields never say the format, and in Firefox and desktop Safari they are plain text boxes

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.pricing-runs.default.1280`, `p.remittances.default.1280`, `p.monthly.default.1280`, `p.reports.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs`, `/p/[practiceId]/remittances`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/monthly`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/remittances/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/monthly/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Four forms use <input type="month"> with labels 'First month of service', 'Last month (blank for one month)', 'Paid from (month)', 'Paid to (month)' and 'Month of service' and no hint (pricing-runs/page.tsx:86-92, remittances/page.tsx:46-51, reports/page.tsx:198, reports/monthly/page.tsx:53). Chromium shows '--------- ----' when empty; Firefox and desktop Safari have no month picker and render a plain text box, where the person must guess that '2026-03' is wanted. A wrong guess then meets the refusal of finding 01. axe sees a labelled input and passes it.
- **Rule:** WCAG 3.3.2 Labels or Instructions (A)
- **Best fix:** Use Field with a hint tied by aria-describedby, 'Year and month, such as 2026-03', on every month field (the route already parses YYYY-MM); the same hint on the remittance filters.

### `a11y-practice-2-15`: Dark tokens set no color-scheme, so native control glyphs stay black on navy (advisory: no user reaches dark today)

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.pricing-runs.default.1280-dark`, `p.remittances.default.1280-dark`, `p.reports.default.1280-dark`, `p.monthly.default.1280-dark`, `p.roi.default.1280-dark`, `p.template.default.1280-dark`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs`, `/p/[practiceId]/remittances`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/monthly`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/template`
- **Files:** `packages/brand/src/tokens.css`
- **Evidence:** The dark block of packages/brand/src/tokens.css:73-102 changes the semantic colours but not color-scheme, so native controls keep their light-scheme parts: the month and date inputs' calendar button is black on the #0B1626 field, 1.26:1 (crop of p.pricing-runs.default.1280-dark), and native radios stay light-filled (p.template.default.1280-dark). Every other text and control pair in the dark shots of this set meets its ratio (muted #A7B1C2 on #0B1626 8.4:1, link #8FB3F0 8.5:1, input outline #5A6E8E 3.5:1).
- **Rule:** WCAG 1.4.11 Non-text Contrast (AA); judged advisory because dark is tokens-only (brief, Method step 1)
- **Best fix:** Add color-scheme: light to :root and color-scheme: dark to the :root[data-theme="dark"], .dark block in tokens.css, so native controls draw their dark parts; land it before any theme switch ships.
- **Notes:** On the dark-mode choice: in this set the dark tokens pass text contrast everywhere; this is the only failure found.

### `a11y-practice-2-16`: Related controls are not grouped: the six attestation checkboxes and the three pursuit-cost fields share a rule only the eye can see

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.report-review.default.1280`, `p.defaults.default.1280`, `p.defaults.error.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/review`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/review/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The unsigned checklist renders six required checkboxes and the attestation sentence with no fieldset or legend (reports/[reportId]/review/page.tsx:108-114; the shot shows the signed state, the form is code-read). The pursuit cost's rule 'All three together, or all blank to clear.' is the first field's hint only (settings/defaults/page.tsx:70-72), so a screen reader hears it on the staff rate and never on the two minute fields, and the refusal names only staffRatePerHour.
- **Rule:** WCAG 1.3.1 Info and Relationships (A); 3.3.2 Labels or Instructions (A)
- **Best fix:** Wrap each set in a fieldset whose legend carries the rule: 'Confirm every item before you sign' with the attestation sentence as its description; 'Pursuit cost: all three together, or all blank to clear'.

### `a11y-practice-2-17`: Support access marks 'Practice home' as the current page

- **Flags:** polish; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.support-access.default.1280`, `p.support-access.ended.1280`, `p.support-access.error-forbidden.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/support-access/[supportGrantId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/support-access/[supportGrantId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The page renders AppShell and Forbidden with section="practice" (support-access/[supportGrantId]/page.tsx:18, :22), so the rail gives 'Practice home' aria-current="page" and the brass underline (components/shell.tsx:116-119) on a page that is not the practice home; screen readers announce 'current page' on the wrong link.
- **Rule:** WCAG 4.1.2 Name, Role, Value (A) (a state the element does not have); 1.3.1 (A)
- **Best fix:** Use section="settings": the page is the access log's detail for administrators, reached from the access log and the support-grant notices.

### `a11y-practice-2-18`: Every PDF prints untagged: the payer's copy, reports and statements have no structure for a screen reader

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.packet-pdf.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/pdf`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/documents/[documentId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]`
- **Files:** `apps/worker/src/report/pdf.ts`
- **Evidence:** The worker prints with page.pdf({ format: 'Letter', printBackground: true, preferCSSPageSize: true }) (apps/worker/src/report/pdf.ts:29): no tagged and no outline option, so the PDF has no structure tree, no headings, no table headers and no bookmarks, though the HTML it prints already has lang="en" and real headings (apps/worker/src/report/brand.ts:149). playwright-core 1.56.1 (apps/worker/package.json:53) supports both options. The payer's copy has no HTML equivalent for its reader.
- **Rule:** WCAG 1.3.1 Info and Relationships (A) applied to PDF (techniques PDF6 table headers, PDF9 headings); audit #103 ('Report and packet PDFs print untagged')
- **Best fix:** page.pdf({ ..., tagged: true, outline: true }) and the test #103 asks for: each printed PDF has a StructTreeRoot, a /Lang and TH cells in its tables.
- **Notes:** In audit #103's scope, which lands in Lane A's PR 7; recorded here as confirmed on the packet PDF shot.

### `a11y-practice-2-19`: What axe misses here: 18 of the 28 routes are never scanned, no error state but one is, and the rules that would catch several findings are not enabled

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** none
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/packet-reviews`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/compare`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/template`, `/p/[practiceId]/releases`, `/p/[practiceId]/remittances`, `/p/[practiceId]/remittances/[remittanceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/access-log`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/data`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/proof-level`, `/p/[practiceId]/support-access/[supportGrantId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/uploads`, `/p/[practiceId]/uploads/[documentId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/reports.spec.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/onboarding.spec.ts`
- **Evidence:** expectAccessible runs on 10 of this set's 28 routes (notifications monitoring.spec.ts:97; reports, report, checklist, monthly, document reports.spec.ts:105-223; settings and agreements onboarding.spec.ts:203, :243; pricing settings :419, :539; correspondence packets.spec.ts:194); packets.spec.ts:328 opens packet-reviews but scans only the review page it clicks into. The 18 listed routes are never scanned, and of the error states only the refused ZIP is. e2e/a11y.ts:12 runs tags wcag2a, wcag2aa, wcag21a and wcag21aa, so target-size (wcag22aa), heading-order, page-has-heading-one, region, landmark-unique, empty-table-header (best-practice), label-content-name-mismatch and td-has-header (experimental) never run. tabTo (a11y.ts:36-41) accepts any outline style other than none and never checks the focused element is visible. Beyond any tool: findings 01 to 04, 06 to 11 and 14 need a person or a scripted interaction (refusal with typed input, link purpose, focus behind the sticky header, menus, announcements, format instructions).
- **Rule:** PRD.md:256 (accessibility on every screen); audit #103 (generated per-route check, WCAG 2.2 rules, focus-not-hidden test, manual checks recorded)
- **Best fix:** Land #103's generated visit (every page route with a role that opens it, failing when one has none) and add each error and interaction state in the route map; add 'wcag22aa' to the tags and enable heading-order, page-has-heading-one, landmark-unique, empty-table-header and label-content-name-mismatch by rule id; make tabTo assert the focused rectangle is inside the viewport and below the header; record 3.3.7, 2.4.4 in context, 2.4.11 and 4.1.3 as manual checks with this review's findings as their first cases.
- **Notes:** Audit #103 lands in Lane A's PR 7; this lists the gap for this half of the practice routes.

### `a11y-practice-2-20`: The brand's .pw-status-unpriced class sets 12 px text at 3.34:1

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** none
- **Routes:** none
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/brand.css`
- **Evidence:** apps/web/src/app/brand.css:42 colours .pw-status-unpriced text #7A8594 on #F0F2F5, 3.34:1, for 12 px text (brand.css:47). No component in apps/ or packages/ uses the class today, but it is the brand's documented status class and the next person to reach for it ships a failure; StatusChip already solved it (components/status.tsx:128: Slate text, the unpriced hue on the border).
- **Rule:** WCAG 1.4.3 Contrast (Minimum) (AA); .claude/rules/brand.md:40 (text at least 4.5:1)
- **Best fix:** Make .pw-status-unpriced match StatusChip: color var(--pw-fg-muted) with a 1 px var(--pw-unpriced) border, or delete the unused class.

### `a11y-practice-2-21`: Success notices use role="alert" and look like any card

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.template.notice.1280`, `p.agreements.notice.1280`, `p.correspondence.notice.1280`, `p.defaults.notice.1280`, `p.proof-level.notice.1280`, `p.pricing-settings.notice.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/template`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/agreements`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/correspondence`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/proof-level`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`
- **Files:** `packages/ui/src/components/alert.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/proof-level/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/agreements/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/correspondence/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/template/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Alert defaults to role="alert" (packages/ui/src/components/alert.tsx:18), so 'Saved', 'Matched' and 'Agreement chain confirmed' (settings/defaults/page.tsx:31, settings/proof-level/page.tsx:37, settings/agreements/page.tsx:31, settings/correspondence/page.tsx:38, reconciliation/template/page.tsx:44, reconciliation/page.tsx:229) interrupt as assertive alerts, while Pricing settings already passes role="status" for its own notice (settings/pricing/page.tsx:61). Visually the six notices are neutral cards with no word or mark that says the change was made beyond the title.
- **Rule:** WCAG 4.1.3 Status Messages (AA): not a failure (both roles are announced); a success is a status, not an alert
- **Best fix:** One Notice component with role="status" for successes (keep Alert for refusals), its title first ('Saved: ...'), used by all six; the brand lens decides its visual cue (status colours are for data only, brand.md:26).

### `a11y-practice-2-22`: The settings tabs' 'admin' badge explains itself only in a hover title
