- **Best fix:** Give every console action that stops customers, people or money a confirmation that states the consequence with the real numbers, in the page and without a modal: a required tick or a typed name beside the button, for example 'Suspend SYNTHETIC BILLING FIRM: 1 practice stops new runs, its data stays readable', 'Charge $2,500.00 to SYNTHETIC THERAPY GROUP's ACH account ending 6789', 'Close every customer screen now', 'Approve: 3 facility and 2 non-facility places; 9 practices' next runs use it; differs from the split in force in: none'. The audit invoice's organization select starts empty ('Choose an organization'). Put Suspension at the foot of the organization page, after the records it acts on. Keep the reversible ones (Reinstate, maintenance off, kill switch off) one click.
- **Notes:** The confirmation is checked in the route (a required 'confirm' field), not only by the browser's required attribute, so a replayed form cannot skip it. console/support/page.tsx, routes/console.ts and lib/screening/routes.ts are in #42.

### `flows-other-10`: 'Set a flag' starts on the kill switch for Everyone and takes its value as typed text: 'on', 'off' or a budget in cents

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `console.models.default.1280`, `console.models.notice.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/models`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/console/models/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/console-models.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/console/models/actions.ts`
- **Evidence:** The Flag select's first option is 'Kill switch (every model feature off)' and Applies to's first is 'Everyone' (apps/web/src/lib/routes/console-models.ts:24-28,127-131), so the form opens one typed word away from switching every model feature off for every customer. 'Value' is a free text field, 'on or off for a switch; whole cents for a budget (500 is $5.00)' (apps/web/src/app/console/models/page.tsx:81): an operator who means $5 and types 5 sets a five-cent budget, while the practice settings page takes dollars ('Per-line materiality (dollars)', console.practice.default.1280). The notice after a set says 'The flag is set' without naming the flag, the scope or the value (models/page.tsx:32-36; console.models.notice.1280), and the global kill switch already has its own button two cards above.
- **Rule:** CLAUDE.md rule 2 (money is integer cents in storage; nothing requires a person to type cents); docs/product/PRD.md:302 (destructive actions confirm with the consequence stated)
- **Best fix:** Split the form by flag: the kill switch for an organization or a practice as an On/Off choice (the global one stays the card above), the daily budget as a dollar amount converted to cents in the route (as the practice settings do), and the letter reader as On/Off; the Applies to select starts empty. The notice names what was set: 'Daily budget for SYNTHETIC OTHER PRACTICE set to $8.00'.

### `flows-other-11`: A suspended organization's billing page says 'In good standing' above 'New runs have stopped', and gives no reason or next step

- **Flags:** blocking; size M; needs a migration; touches #42; in flight: #135 the bank account shown first (billing pages; PR #47): same page, different defect; land with or after it
- **Screens:** `settings.billing-org.suspended.1280`, `console.organization.suspended.1280`
- **Routes:** `/settings/billing/[organizationId]`, `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/settings/billing/[organizationId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/billing.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/console.ts`
- **Evidence:** The Standing badge reads billing.standing alone (apps/web/src/app/settings/billing/[organizationId]/page.tsx:41-44), which stays 'good' when a platform administrator suspends the organization, while the suspension alert reads organization.status (page.tsx:45-50; apps/web/src/lib/routes/billing.ts:156-158). So the firm principal sees 'Standing: In good standing' and directly under it, in red, 'New runs have stopped: A platform administrator has suspended the organization ... new runs start again once it is reinstated' (settings.billing-org.suspended.1280). Neither says why or what to do: the console's Suspend posts only a status (apps/web/src/lib/routes/console.ts:96), although its card promises runs are refused 'with the reason' (console.organization.suspended.1280), and the customer's alert links to no ticket or address. The same page still invites 'Choose plan' and test payment methods as if payment would lift it.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (every error shown with a next step); brief: blocking when a person is misled about status
- **Best fix:** One standing, worked out in one place: when the organization is suspended or closed, the badge reads 'Suspended by PaidWhole since <date>' (or 'Closed'), never 'In good standing'. Ask the operator for the reason when suspending, from a closed list (unpaid invoice, at the customer's request, security hold, agreement ended) with an optional note, recorded with the audit event; show the customer that reason and its next step ('Pay invoice SIM-0002', or 'Open a ticket' linking to /support?from=billing). Test this pairing (suspended plus good billing) in the billing page's test.
- **Notes:** The standing fix alone needs no migration; storing the suspension reason does (a column on organization or the status event), so that part goes to Lane A. routes/console.ts is in #42.

### `flows-other-12`: 'Created' and 'Location saved' report success beside a billing ZIP that cannot price anything, in plain text that does not say the months will keep waiting

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `console.practice.location-saved.1280`, `console.practice.saved.1280`, `console.practice.default.1280`, `console.organization.created.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/practices/[practiceId]`, `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/console/practices/[practiceId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/pricing/location.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/console.ts`
- **Evidence:** After a save the card shows the 'Location saved' alert, then 'Billing ZIP: 27601', then the status sentence 'Medicare's ZIP code list in force does not include 27601. Check the ZIP code.' as an ordinary paragraph (apps/web/src/app/console/practices/[practiceId]/page.tsx:59-67; console.practice.location-saved.1280). The card's own description says saving 'queues the practice's monitoring, so months waiting on the location are priced once it is ready' (page.tsx:56), but nothing says this location is not ready, so the operator reads 'saved' and moves on while the practice's months stay unpriced. On the organization page a new practice gets 'Created. Medicare's ZIP code list in force does not include 27601. Check the ZIP code.' as one unstyled line (apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx:95-99; console.organization.created.1280). The settings 'Saved' notice also sits above that same unsettled status (console.practice.saved.1280). The statuses are known and named (zip_missing, zip_unknown, zip_plus_four_needed, zip_plus_four_unknown: apps/web/src/lib/pricing/location.ts:145-150).
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (every error shown with a next step); docs/adr/0056 (a refused check or gap is danger text on the danger tint)
- **Best fix:** Let the save's result follow the location's status. When the status is settled, 'Location saved: lines price at carrier X, locality Y'. When it is not, no success notice: an alert in the danger style titled by the status's step ('Check the billing ZIP'), saying what it means for pricing ('Months waiting on the location stay unpriced until a ZIP in Medicare's list for release 900001 is saved') and keeping the field focused with the value entered. Show the same alert on the organization page under the new practice instead of 'Created.', and on the practice's Getting started step for its administrator, who confirms the ZIP.
- **Notes:** Accepting an unlisted ZIP stays as designed (the field's hint says so); only the reporting changes. routes/console.ts is in #42; the page-only change does not touch it.

### `flows-other-13`: The terms page asks a customer to read the terms and accept them, but shows no terms: only a version, a reference string and a hash prefix

- **Flags:** blocking; size M
- **Screens:** `terms.default.1280`, `terms.account-menu.1280`, `terms.help.1280`, `terms.internal.1280`, `console.terms.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/terms`, `/console/terms`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/terms/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/onboarding.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/console/terms/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/console-onboarding.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts`
- **Evidence:** Every customer screen sends a person who owes the terms in force to /terms (route-map.json /terms; apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts:65-66). There the card shows 'Published ... Document terms/synthetic-2026-10, SHA-256 ca88437093319f39…' (apps/web/src/app/terms/page.tsx:34-41), and for someone who has not accepted, 'Before you use the workspace, read the terms of use and accept them.' with the button 'I accept these terms' (page.tsx:59-67): the document reference is plain mono text, not a link, and no text of the terms is on the page. The console's publish form records only a version, a reference to 'where the signed-off document is kept' and a SHA-256 (console.terms.default.1280; apps/web/src/lib/routes/console-onboarding.ts:312-327), so the product holds nothing it could show. After accepting, 'Continue to your workspace' goes to '/' rather than the screen the gate interrupted (page.tsx:52-55).
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (every error shown with a next step: 'read the terms' has nothing to read); combined audit #51 ('Show the terms before I accept'); brief: blocking when a person cannot complete a task
- **Best fix:** Publish the terms as a document the product serves: the console's publish form takes the signed-off file (PDF or HTML), hashes it on the server as the agreement upload does and refuses a hash that differs from the one typed, and keeps it under an opaque key. /terms shows the text (or an inline viewer of the PDF with a download link) above the button, names what changed since the version the person last accepted, and records acceptance of that exact hash. After accepting, return the person to the screen they were sent from.
- **Notes:** Combined audit #51 already plans 'Show the terms before I accept' in the legal-surfaces pull request before PR 16; this finding is the screen evidence for it and should be folded into that PR, not a separate one. Presenting the text changes no legal wording; which file is the signed-off version is the founder's (through the co-founder). If the reference cannot hold the stored object's key, a column is needed (then Lane A).

### `flows-other-14`: The screening result buries its answer and its next step: no summary at the top, the audit request at the foot of a 4,500 px page, and the confirmation where the person no longer is

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `screening-result.default.1280`, `screening-result.converted.1280`
- **Routes:** `/screening/results/[resultId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/screening/result-view.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/screening/result-summary.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/screening/convert-form.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/screening/routes.ts`
- **Evidence:** The page runs: heading, a ten-line retention paragraph (apps/web/src/components/screening/result-summary.tsx:138-152), then file totals, left-out rows, quarters not screened, the Medicare-priced figures, the contract-only dollars, could-not-price and not-screened, anomalies, coverage, top rows, and only then 'Ask for a PaidWhole audit' (apps/web/src/components/screening/result-view.tsx:22-29). The two facts that answer 'why would I want an audit' (here $7,321.09 of $11,716.59 cannot be priced without a contract, and 1 group of 4 is an anomaly) sit in the fifth card and in a paragraph halfway down; nothing near the top links to the request form, which starts about 4,000 px down (screening-result.default.1280). Submitting it redirects to ?requested=1 with no anchor (apps/web/src/lib/screening/routes.ts:1219), the page reloads at its top, and the 'Audit request received' alert replaces the form at the foot (apps/web/src/components/screening/convert-form.tsx:37-44), so the person sees the unchanged top of the page and no sign the request went through (screening-result.converted.1280). Confirmed from the earlier tour: at 1280 the anomalies Status badges and the top rows' Difference columns are still cut at the card edge.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (every action shows its result and a next step); CO-11 (screening never says owed, underpaid or recovered: the summary must keep to 'below its Medicare figure' and 'cannot be priced without a contract'); silent on page order: recommendation
- **Best fix:** Lead with what the screening found and what to do next. Under the heading, a summary in three lines from the figures the page already has: the share of allowed dollars priced under Medicare, the share that cannot be priced without a contract (with the dollars), and how many groups are anomalies (investigatory, below their lowest Medicare figure by more than $5 a claim line); then one primary button 'Ask for an audit of the rows a contract prices' that jumps to the form. Move the retention paragraph into a 'What we keep' disclosure beside the link's closing date (legal surface screening-kept-result: placement, not wording). After a request, redirect to #screening-convert and show the confirmation at the top as well as in place of the form.
- **Notes:** The summary's wording passes the screening copy lint (no owed, underpaid or recovered). Moving the retention text is a placement change of a legal surface (docs/product/legal-surfaces.md screening-kept-result): counsel's review covers wording, but tell the co-founder. lib/screening/routes.ts is in #42 (only the redirect anchor touches it).

### `flows-other-15`: The screening asks for the practice's details before it says what file to make, and its 'sent' state repeats the file guide instead of the next step

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `screening.default.1280`, `screening.file-ready.1280`, `screening.sent-sent.1280`, `screening.sent-soon.1280`, `screening.sent-minutes.1280`, `screening.sent-waiting.1280`
- **Routes:** `/screening`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/screening/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/screening/upload-form.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/screening/sent.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/screening/retention.ts`
- **Evidence:** A prospect's real work is producing a file of totals from their billing system, but the page opens with 'About your practice' (name, state, ZIP, a work email whose 12 px hint runs to nine lines of retention terms: apps/web/src/components/screening/upload-form.tsx:181-182), then 'Your file' and the 'Screen the file' button, and only below the button 'What the file holds' (the columns, the 11-claim-line rule, the denied-lines rule) and 'What each payer product gets' (apps/web/src/app/screening/page.tsx:68-118; screening.default.1280). A visitor who reads top-down fills four fields before learning whether they can make the file at all. After sending, the form becomes one dense 'Check your email' paragraph whose first sentence is the only instruction and whose remaining eight lines repeat the retention terms (page.tsx:42-55), while the two reference cards stay below; the only actions are 'start again', which replaces the screening just sent, and the footer links (screening.sent-waiting.1280).
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (every step shows its next step); silent on the order of the public screening: recommendation
- **Best fix:** Order the page as the task runs: (1) a three-step outline at the top: 'Make a file of totals (download the template), check it here (nothing leaves your browser until you send), get the result by email'; (2) 'What the file holds' and the template button before the form, collapsed to its first lines with 'Show every column'; (3) the file chooser and its checks; (4) the practice and email last, beside 'Screen the file', with the retention terms one line plus 'What we keep' to open. The sent state names the address it went to only if that can be shown without putting it in the URL (else 'the work email you gave'), states the wait, and offers the next steps: 'While you wait: request an audit' and 'Did not arrive? Check spam, then send again'; the reference cards drop away.
- **Notes:** The retention texts are legal surfaces awaiting counsel (screening-kept-upload, screening-kept-sent): this moves them behind a disclosure without changing a word; tell the co-founder. screening/page.tsx, upload-form.tsx and lib/screening/retention.ts are in #42: land after it.

### `flows-other-16`: Maintenance throws a customer out of the page and any form they were sending, never brings them back, and the status page shows every part 'Operational' meanwhile

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; needs a migration
- **Screens:** `maintenance.on.1280`, `maintenance.redirected.1280`, `maintenance.default.1280`, `status.maintenance.1280`, `console.status.default.1280`, `console.status.maintenance-on.1280`
- **Routes:** `/maintenance`, `/status`, `/console/status`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/maintenance/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/support.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/console/status/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/status/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/public-header.tsx`
- **Evidence:** While maintenance is on, every customer page and every form post is redirected to /maintenance (apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts:68-69, used by runAction at :150-153), so a form being sent is dropped with its text, and the page does not record where the person was. /maintenance is static: it neither checks again nor returns the person when the switch goes off, and its off state offers 'Back to your workspace' to '/' (apps/web/src/app/maintenance/page.tsx:17-36; maintenance.default.1280). It says 'scheduled' but gives no expected end: the console's switch is one button with no field for a message or an end time (apps/web/src/app/console/status/page.tsx:49-52). The public status page then says 'Scheduled maintenance is in progress.' in small plain text above seven components all 'Operational' and 'No incidents in the last 14 days' (apps/web/src/app/status/page.tsx:24-26,36-41; status.maintenance.1280), which contradicts the workspace being closed. Both pages carry the public header, whose lockup links a signed-in customer to /sign-in (apps/web/src/components/public-header.tsx:9).
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (no screen depends on client-side state a refresh loses; every error with a next step); brief: notices that contradict the page
- **Best fix:** Make maintenance a state the person can wait through. The switch takes an expected end (and an optional message) and opens a maintenance entry on the status page automatically, marking the components it closes 'Under maintenance' until it is turned off. /maintenance shows that end time, checks the status every 30 seconds (a plain fetch of a public JSON route, honouring reduced motion) and returns the person to the page they were on, which the redirect carries as a same-origin path. A form post refused for maintenance answers the form with 'Maintenance started; nothing was saved. Your text is still here; send it again after <time>' instead of redirecting, so the typed text stays (with flows-other-01).
- **Notes:** An expected end and message need a place to live (the maintenance flag's value or a status row): migration, Lane A, for that part only. The return path is an internal path (opaque IDs only), validated as same-origin, never a full URL. #48 covers the sign-out case of the same loss on packet pages; this is the maintenance case everywhere. On /console/status the Incidents card promises open and recently resolved incidents but renders nothing when there are none (apps/web/src/app/console/status/page.tsx:86): add "No open incidents" so the operator knows the list is empty, not broken.

### `flows-other-17`: Signing in again after a timeout, a gate or a step that expired lands on the home page, not the screen the person was on

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; in flight: #48 a narrative draft survives sign-out (packet pages)
- **Screens:** `sign-in.reason-idle.1280`, `sign-in.reason-step-expired.1280`, `terms.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/sign-in`, `/sign-in/mfa`, `/terms`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/sign-in.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/auth/pending.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/terms/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/onboarding.ts`
- **Evidence:** A refused session redirects to '/sign-in' or '/sign-in?reason=<code>' with no return path (apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts:53-71), and the last sign-in step sends everyone to '/' (apps/web/src/lib/routes/sign-in.ts:80-82), which forwards to the first practice's home (apps/web/src/app/page.tsx:19-22). So a billing manager signed out after 30 idle minutes on a case, a claims filter or a packet signs in again and starts from the practice home; the 'Session expired' notice (sign-in.reason-idle.1280) promises 'Sign in again to continue', but nothing continues. The terms gate's 'Continue to your workspace' also goes to '/' (apps/web/src/app/terms/page.tsx:52-55).
- **Rule:** combined audit #48 (best option: after signing back in the person returns to where they were); docs/product/PRD.md:256
- **Best fix:** Carry the interrupted page through sign-in: the redirect adds next=<same-origin path> (path and query only, opaque IDs, validated against the route registry so it is never an open redirect), the pending sign-in cookie keeps it across the password, code and key steps, and the final step, the terms gate and the maintenance page return there.
- **Notes:** Recorded as part of #48's best option so it is folded into that pull request; it applies to every page, not only packets. A path can carry an opaque ID but never a name or claim number (CLAUDE.md 'Never').

### `flows-other-18`: Setting up two-step verification on a computer means typing a 32-character key into a phone: there is no QR code

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `sign-in-enroll.default.1280`, `sign-in-new-password.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/sign-in/enroll`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/sign-in/enroll/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/sign-in.ts`, `apps/web/package.json`
- **Evidence:** The enrolment card shows the secret grouped by four (X3UZ 3MDW TUKI GIF2 YOQM 2CFM HECL BU7K) and a link 'Open in an authenticator app on this device' (apps/web/src/app/sign-in/enroll/page.tsx:24-35). Most invited people sign in on a work computer and keep the authenticator on a phone, where that link does nothing; their only path is to type 32 characters correctly, then the six-digit code. There is no step indicator either: from 'Choose a password' to 'Set up two-step verification' to the workspace the person is never told how many steps remain (sign-in-new-password.default.1280, sign-in-enroll.default.1280).
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.2 SC 3.3.8 Accessible Authentication (Minimum) (combined audit #103 adds WCAG 2.2): transcribing a 32-character key is a cognitive function test unless an alternative is offered; docs/product/PRD.md:266 (MFA enrolment is part of Sign-in and MFA)
- **Best fix:** Show a QR code of the otpauth URI beside the key, rendered on the server as inline SVG (no third-party request, no script; the page is under the CSP), with the grouped key and a copy button as the fallback and the same-device link kept. Add a one-line step marker to the invited person's path: 'Step 2 of 3: two-step verification'.
- **Notes:** Needs a small QR encoder (a pinned dependency or a vendored encoder); the secret never leaves the response it is already in. apps/web/package.json is in #42.

### `flows-other-19`: Staff sign-in takes two key ceremonies and a retyped email after registering a key, and every customer reads a staff-only note above their sign-in

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `sign-in-register-key.default.1280`, `sign-in-hardware-key.reason-key-registered.1280`, `sign-in-hardware-key.reason-hardware-key-required.1280`, `sign-in-hardware-key.default.1280`, `sign-in.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/sign-in/register-key`, `/sign-in/hardware-key`, `/sign-in`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/sign-in/hardware-key/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/sign-in/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/sign-in/notice.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/hardware-key.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/sign-in.ts`
- **Evidence:** After 'Register hardware key' the person is sent to /sign-in/hardware-key?reason=key_registered (apps/web/src/lib/routes/sign-in.ts:86-88, which also clears the pending sign-in that held the email), where the Email field is empty and they must type it and touch the key again (apps/web/src/app/sign-in/hardware-key/page.tsx:12-37); the same page still ends with 'No key registered yet? Sign in with your password and code once to register it.' (page.tsx:28-34) just under 'Your hardware key is registered'. On that page the two notices ('Hardware key required' and the static staff note) stack above the form (page.tsx:17-18), pushing the task down. On /sign-in, the bordered 'PaidWhole staff sign in with a hardware key' card sits between the customer's sign-in card and the links (apps/web/src/app/sign-in/page.tsx:32; notice.tsx:34-44), as heavy as the form though it concerns four internal roles.
- **Rule:** docs/brand/BRAND.md:122 (Sign-in: stacked lockup, Paper ground, nothing else decorative); PRD.md:266 (internal roles see the hardware-key requirement: a line meets it); silent on step count: recommendation
- **Best fix:** After registration keep a short-lived sealed cookie naming the account (instead of clearing the pending sign-in outright) so /sign-in/hardware-key opens with the Email filled and a 'Sign in now' button that starts the key prompt, and hide 'No key registered yet?' when reason=key_registered. On /sign-in, replace the staff card with one line under the form: 'PaidWhole staff: sign in with a hardware key'. On /sign-in/hardware-key, show the staff note only when no reason notice is shown.
- **Notes:** The prefill carries the email only from the server-side pending cookie, never from the URL.

### `flows-other-20`: The SOC 2 page says 12 controls need action but never lists the actions; each one is done through one generic form 9,000 px down

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `console.compliance.default.1280`, `console.compliance.sent.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/compliance`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/console/compliance/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/compliance/actions.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/compliance.ts`
- **Evidence:** The summary reads '4 controls ready, 12 needing action, 1 partly built' (apps/web/src/app/console/compliance/page.tsx:99), then 17 control cards, each with an 'Action needed' badge and its evidence list (page.tsx:122-138). Reading the cards shows that the actions are few and repeated: approve six draft policies (the same 'Awaiting approval docs/policies/access-control.md' appears under five controls), record 17 workforce attestations ('17 of 17 have not attested within 365 days'), record the risk analysis and name the security official. None of these rows links to the place to do it; the only way is the 'Record evidence' form at the foot (page.tsx:175-200; at about y=8,800 in the 9,515 px shot), where the person picks 'What', a policy or a workforce member and a date, one record at a time, then scrolls back up to see whether the badge changed.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (every state with a next step); silent on the evidence checklist's layout: recommendation
- **Best fix:** Put a 'To do' list under the summary: one line per distinct action with its count and the controls it unblocks ('Approve 6 policies: unblocks 9 controls'; 'Record 17 workforce attestations: Workforce'; 'Record the risk analysis'; 'Name the security official'), each opening the record form with that action preselected. Each 'Not recorded' or 'Awaiting approval' row in a control card links to the same prefilled form. For attestations, allow several members on one record of the same training date and document. Keep the control cards below as the evidence.
- **Notes:** Recording several attestations in one action still writes one evidence record per member (an audit event each). The three unexpanded '%s' test names the checker noted are a words or code item, left to that lens.

### `flows-other-21`: Rule releases says 'Prepare and check releases here' and then, on any server without a content checkout, sends the operator to the command line and 'the load-release skill'

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; in flight: the release content checks (PR #49): check that its copy of this page agrees before changing the words
- **Screens:** `console.rule-releases.default.1280`, `console.rule-releases.prepared.1280`, `console.rule-releases.notes-open.1280`, `console.rule-releases.release.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/rule-releases`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/console/rule-releases/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/rule-releases.ts`
- **Evidence:** The lede promises 'Prepare and check releases here; CI publishes a prepared release when its pull request merges, and pnpm release:load and pnpm release:rollback load and withdraw it' (apps/web/src/app/console/rule-releases/page.tsx:38-41). The Drafts card on this server says 'This server has no content checkout (CONTENT_ROOT). Prepare and check releases with pnpm release:assemble and pnpm verify-content in one, as the load-release skill says.' (page.tsx:149-152; console.rule-releases.default.1280): the page cannot do what its first line offers, and its next step names an environment variable, two commands and a Claude Code skill rather than a person's step or a runbook. The prepared notice's next steps are likewise a CLI dry run and a pull request title ('Do not merge it yourself', page.tsx:43-48). The selected release (?release=) gets aria-current but no visible mark, so with several releases nobody can tell which one the card below describes (console.rule-releases.release.1280 is identical to the default).
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:297 (Rule releases: drafts, checks, published releases; prepare, check, publish); PRD.md:256 (every state with a next step); CO-11 (product copy: no internal tooling terms)
- **Best fix:** Say what this server does and what the operator does next. Where there is no content checkout, the lede reads 'Releases are prepared and checked in the content repository and published by CI when their pull request merges; this page shows what is published and which runs it affects', and the Drafts card links the release runbook (docs/runbooks) instead of naming commands, CONTENT_ROOT or a skill; where there is one, keep the prepare and check controls. Mark the selected release in the Published table (bold row and 'Shown below').
- **Notes:** No change to the content pipeline or its hooks (CO-001 freeze): words and layout only. The raw Markdown of RELEASE.md in the notes box is a brand or words item for those lenses.

### `flows-other-22`: An audit request from the screening arrives in the console without the screening: the operator prepares the scoping call blind

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; needs a migration
- **Screens:** `console.prospects.default.1280`, `console.prospects.all.1280`, `screening-result.converted.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/prospects`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/console/prospects/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/console-onboarding.ts`, `packages/db/migrations`
- **Evidence:** The prospect list reads prospect_request alone (apps/web/src/lib/routes/console-onboarding.ts:91-93) and shows organization, contact, locations, payers, billing, received and status (apps/web/src/app/console/prospects/page.tsx:53-60). SYNTHETIC SCREENING CLINIC came from a screening whose kept summary is linked to the request (screening_lead.prospect_request_id, packages/db/migrations/20261104090000_free_screening.sql:773) and which the prospect was told is kept 'with your request' for 12 months (screening-result.converted.1280), yet the row looks exactly like the form-only SYNTHETIC TOUR CLINIC: no 'from a screening' mark, no state or ZIP, no screened totals by payer product, no share that needs a contract, no anomaly count, no 'how did you hear', no referring firm. The only actions are 'Create practice group', 'Mark contacted' and Decline (page.tsx:91-107).
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:260 (each screen shows what its role needs to act); the screening's retention notice (docs/product/legal-surfaces.md screening-kept-convert: the summary is kept with the request for this purpose); silent on the console view: recommendation
- **Best fix:** Show each request's source and context: a 'From a screening' or 'From the form' label; for a screening, a row detail with the kept summary (screened date and release, totals by payer product, the share needing a contract, anomaly rows) and the request's heard-from and referring firm; for both, state and ZIP when given. Sort and filter by source. Read it through a console function that returns only what the request keeps (counts and totals, never rows), with an audit event on open.
- **Notes:** The app role does not read screening_lead today (no reference in apps/web/src outside labels.ts), so a SECURITY DEFINER console read function and its grant are a migration: Lane A. No PHI: the screening holds totals only.

### `flows-other-23`: Success notices come from the address bar alone, name nothing, and come back on every refresh or Back

- **Flags:** polish; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `console.models.notice.1280`, `console.compliance.sent.1280`, `console.rule-releases.prepared.1280`, `account.notifications.saved.1280`, `console.practice.saved.1280`, `console.practice.location-saved.1280`, `settings.users.notice-invited.1280`, `console.screening.approved.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/models`, `/console/compliance`, `/console/rule-releases`, `/account/notifications`, `/console/practices/[practiceId]`, `/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users`, `/console/screening`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/console-models.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/compliance.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/rule-releases.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/monitoring.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/console.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/settings.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/screening/routes.ts`
- **Evidence:** Each mutation redirects to a query flag that the page turns into a notice without checking that anything happened: '/console/models?notice=set' (apps/web/src/lib/routes/console-models.ts:93,168), '/console/compliance?sent=<reference>' whose text is echoed as 'The compliance platform received <reference>' for any 1-120 characters of [A-Za-z0-9:._-] (apps/web/src/lib/routes/compliance.ts:110,219), '?prepared=<n>', '?saved=1', '?location=saved', '?notice=invited', '?approved=1'. Opened by URL with no write, the screens assert 'The flag is set' beside 'No model flag is set' (console.models.notice.1280), 'Release 900002 prepared' beside 'This server has no content checkout' (console.rule-releases.prepared.1280) and 'Invitation sent' beside 'No invitations yet' (settings.users.notice-invited.1280). In real use the same notice reappears on refresh, Back and a shared link, and most say only 'Saved' or 'The flag is set' without naming what was saved.
- **Rule:** brief: notices that contradict the page; docs/product/PRD.md:256 (no screen depends on state a refresh loses: the inverse, a notice a refresh keeps, misleads too)
- **Best fix:** Tie each notice to the write that earned it: the redirect carries the mutation's audit-event ID (opaque), and the page shows the notice only if that event is the actor's own, from the last few minutes, and of the page's kind, reading what it says from the event: 'Daily budget for SYNTHETIC OTHER PRACTICE set to $8.00', 'Invitation sent to a…@synthetic.test, lasts 7 days', 'Export tp-compliance-evidence-… received'. A refresh after a few minutes, or a crafted link, shows no notice.
- **Notes:** The audit event already exists for every mutation (CLAUDE.md conventions), so no table changes. The neutral notice style itself is on the rules (ADR 0056: system states are neutral badges), not a defect. routes/console.ts, routes/settings.ts and lib/screening/routes.ts are in #42.

### `flows-other-24`: Public pages have no way between them: the lockup always goes to sign-in, even for a prospect reading a result or a signed-in customer on the status page

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `screening.default.1280`, `screening-result.default.1280`, `request-audit.default.1280`, `request-audit.sent.1280`, `status.default.1280`, `maintenance.on.1280`, `security.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/screening`, `/screening/results/[resultId]`, `/request-audit`, `/status`, `/maintenance`, `/security`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/public-header.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/request-audit/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/screening/result-view.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Every public page uses PublicHeader, whose only content is the lockup linking to /sign-in (apps/web/src/components/public-header.tsx:5-13). A prospect on their result who clicks the logo lands on a sign-in form they have no account for; a customer on /status or /maintenance is sent to sign in again. The links between the free screening, the audit request, the status page and sign-in exist only as sentences in each page's footer (apps/web/src/app/request-audit/page.tsx:66-80; apps/web/src/components/screening/result-view.tsx:30-40), different on each page. After 'Request received' the request page still invites 'Not ready for an audit? Try a free screening' (request-audit.sent.1280), and gives no time frame for the email it promises.
- **Rule:** silent in the brand rules and PRD on public navigation: recommendation (docs/brand/BRAND.md:120-121 covers the app header only)
- **Best fix:** Give PublicHeader a small, consistent set of links on the right: 'Free screening', 'Request an audit', 'Sign in' (or 'Your workspace' when a session exists), with the current page marked; the lockup goes to the screening, the public entry, for a visitor without a session and to '/' for one with a session. On the request page's sent state, replace 'Not ready for an audit?' with what happens next and when, and offer the screening as 'While you wait'.
- **Notes:** Reading whether a session exists in the public header must not make public pages depend on the session store's availability; a cookie presence check is enough for the link's words.

### `flows-other-25`: A prospect's coverage table shows the engine code ENGINE_ERROR for a place of service outside the split, which reads as a fault in the product

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `screening-result.default.1280`, `screening-result.converted.1280`
- **Routes:** `/screening/results/[resultId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/screening/coverage.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/screening/labels.ts`
- **Evidence:** The Reason cell prints the raw code in mono above its words (apps/web/src/components/screening/coverage.tsx:89-91): 'ENGINE_ERROR' over 'The place of service is not in the approved place-of-service split' (screening-result.default.1280, last coverage row). ENGINE_ERROR's meaning in the closed list is 'Unexpected input' with the remedy 'Operator alert' (docs/product/unpriced-reasons.md:22), so a prospect deciding whether to trust PaidWhole sees an error code for a row the screening simply does not price.
- **Rule:** docs/product/unpriced-reasons.md (reasons are shown with their meaning and remedy); CO-11 (product copy); silent on showing codes to a prospect: recommendation
- **Best fix:** On the public result show the reason in words only (keep the code in a data attribute and in the CSV), and give it the prospect's next step: 'Place of service 99 is not in the screening's split; an audit prices it under your contract.' Customers' screens keep code plus words, where support uses the code.
- **Notes:** Whether a POS outside the split should carry ENGINE_ERROR at all is an engine question outside this lens and under the freeze; this changes only what the page shows.

### `flows-other-26`: Notification preferences are one switch for every practice, and staff with no practice access are offered practice digests

- **Flags:** polish; size M; needs a migration; touches #42
- **Screens:** `account.notifications.default.1280`, `account.notifications.internal-shell.1280`, `account.notifications.saved.1280`
- **Routes:** `/account/notifications`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/account/notifications/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/monitoring.ts`, `apps/worker/src/digest/job.ts`
- **Evidence:** The card offers two checkboxes 'For every practice you reach' (apps/web/src/app/account/notifications/page.tsx:36-53): a firm analyst or a group's billing manager cannot keep the digest for the practices they work and drop the others. The platform administrator, whose header reads 'No practice access', sees the same two practice digests (account.notifications.internal-shell.1280), which can never reach them. The two checkboxes are native inputs inside labels, not the design system's Checkbox (route-map.json /account/notifications notes).
- **Rule:** silent in the PRD on per-practice preferences (PRD.md:282 lists notification preferences under Settings): recommendation
- **Best fix:** List the practices the person reaches, each with its digest-by-email and in-app choices, plus 'All practices' to set them together; for a person with no practice access, say 'You reach no practice, so no digest is sent' and offer only what applies to their role. Use the shared Checkbox component.
- **Notes:** Preferences live in user.settings today (one object); per-practice choices may fit there as JSON without a column, in which case no migration: needsMigration is set for the worst case. apps/worker/src/digest/job.ts is in #42.

### `flows-other-27`: The console's organization page is a 4,000 px stack of forms in which the records are hard to find, the Actions column is empty, and a firm's agreement hides the practice the grant names

- **Flags:** polish; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `console.organization.default.1280`, `console.organization.created.1280`, `console.organization.firm.1280`, `console.organization.suspended.1280`, `console.organization.help.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/console.ts`
- **Evidence:** The page runs Suspension, Practices with its create form, Members with its invite form, Invitations, Agreements with two five-field forms (record by reference, or upload the PDF), then Grants (apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx; 4,068 px at 1280, console.organization.default.1280), with no in-page links, so reaching a member to unlock or the grants means scrolling past four forms. In the Members table the Account and Actions cells are flex boxes (page.tsx:147,153), which takes them out of table layout: the End membership buttons render under 'Account' and the 'Actions' header stands over nothing (console.organization.default.1280). A firm's engagement letter lists its practice as 'Another organization's practice' (page.tsx:252) while the Grants card on the same page names SYNTHETIC PHYSICAL THERAPY NORTH (console.organization.firm.1280). On a suspended firm the grant still reads 'in force' with no word that its runs are refused (console.organization.suspended.1280).
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:295 (Organizations and practices: records, agreements, grants, billing status, legal holds); WCAG 2.1 SC 1.3.1 Info and Relationships (the action cells lose their column header); brief: notices that contradict the page
- **Best fix:** Lead with the records and put each create form behind its own button: a short summary (kind, status, billing standing with a link to Billing status, practices, members, open invitations, grants), in-page links to each card, then the cards with their tables first and 'Add a practice', 'Invite a member', 'Record an agreement' (one form with 'by reference' or 'upload the PDF' as a choice) opening below each table; Suspension last (see flows-other-09). Wrap the cells' contents in a div so the td stays a table cell. Name the practice on a firm's agreement from the grant it rests on, and mark grants of a suspended organization 'in force; runs refused while suspended'.
- **Notes:** Naming another organization's practice to the platform administrator is within organization.manage (the console already lists every practice). routes/console.ts is in #42.

### `flows-other-28`: The platform administrator's rail has three 'support' entries and a 'Terms of use' that is not the terms, so the names do not say which page does what

- **Flags:** polish; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `account.devices.internal-shell.1280`, `console.support.default.1280`, `console.tickets.default.1280`, `console.terms.default.1280`, `terms.internal.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/support`, `/console/tickets`, `/support`, `/console/terms`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/support/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/terms/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/help.ts`
- **Evidence:** The rail lists 'Support' (support grants), 'Support tickets' (the desk) and, under Account, 'Help and support' (the customer's ticket form) (apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx:238-240,264-268,291-293; account.devices.internal-shell.1280). The page behind 'Support' is headed 'Support' (apps/web/src/app/console/support/page.tsx:13,32) though it holds only grants. 'Terms of use' in the console opens the versions and the publish form, headed 'Terms of use' (apps/web/src/app/console/terms/page.tsx:27), the same words as the customers' page 'PaidWhole terms of use' that the platform administrator also reaches (terms.internal.1280).
- **Rule:** silent in the PRD and brand rules on rail labels: recommendation (PRD.md:298 names the screen 'Support: open support grants')
- **Best fix:** Name rail items by what they hold: 'Support grants' (page heading the same), 'Support tickets', and 'Terms versions' (heading 'Terms of use: versions'); hide the customer 'Help and support' form for internal roles, who work the desk, or label it 'Ask a colleague' if staff need it.
- **Notes:** shell.tsx, help.ts and console/support/page.tsx are in #42; e2e specs that find the rail link by name ('Support') change with it.

### `flows-other-29`: 'Revoke device' ends another browser's sessions on one click, between two cards both called 'Chrome on Windows'

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `account.devices.default.1280`, `account.devices.internal-shell.1280`
- **Routes:** `/account/devices`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/account/devices/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/account.ts`
- **Evidence:** Each device card has a one-click outline button, 'Revoke device' or 'Revoke this device and sign out' (apps/web/src/app/account/devices/page.tsx:44-50), with no confirmation. The two cards the practice administrator sees are both titled 'Chrome on Windows' and differ only in their first-seen and last-seen times (account.devices.default.1280), so the person picks the one to revoke by comparing timestamps.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:302 (every destructive action, revoke named, confirms with the consequence stated)
- **Best fix:** Confirm in place, stating the consequence: 'Revoke Chrome on Windows, last used 2026-10-26 01:25 UTC: its 1 open session ends now and it must sign in again with a code'. Order devices by last use with 'This device' first, and let the person name a device ('Office PC') so two of the same browser can be told apart.
- **Notes:** Naming a device writes user_device.label, which exists; if the browser-given label must stay, a separate name column needs a migration.

### `flows-other-30`: Billing status lists organizations as dead text: no way to open one, its invoices, or what to do about a past-due standing

- **Flags:** polish; size S; in flight: #135 the bank account shown first (billing pages; PR #47): check it does not already change this table
- **Screens:** `console.billing.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/billing`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/console/billing/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/billing.ts`
- **Evidence:** Each row prints the organization's name and kind, a standing badge, the plan, the amount owed and 'Practices (granted, active)' (apps/web/src/app/console/billing/page.tsx:51-67); the name is not a link to /console/organizations/<id>, there is no link to the organization's invoices, and a past-due or suspended row offers no next step (reminder, suspend, reinstate) although those live on the organization page. 'Practices (granted, active)' reads '—' on every row (console.billing.default.1280) with no word that it fills only after the nightly snapshot (page.tsx:66).
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:295 (Organizations and practices shows billing status; allows suspend); PRD.md:256 (every state with a next step)
- **Best fix:** Link each organization's name to its console page and its open amount to its invoices; for past due, show 'since <date>, new runs stop <date>' and a link to the organization's Suspension card; replace '—' with 'After tonight's count' until the first snapshot exists. Sort past due and suspended first.

## flows-practice

### `flows-practice-01`: Materiality and pursuit settings show raw cents as dollars, and saving the form unchanged sets thresholds 100 times too high

- **Flags:** blocking; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.defaults.default.1280`, `p.defaults.admin-view.1280`, `p.defaults.notice.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/routes/settings.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** On NORTH, whose thresholds are the defaults ($5.00 per line, $250.00 per case, as the Cases screen's Materiality card states for its practice), the billing manager reads 'Now $500 per line and $25000 per case' and the fields 'Per line (dollars)' and 'Per case (dollars)' arrive prefilled 500 and 25000. The handler turns cents into a bare cents string (apps/web/src/lib/routes/settings.ts:292, const text = (cents) => centsToString(cents)); the page prints it after a literal '$' (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults/page.tsx:40) and uses it as the field default (:48-49), and the pursuit rate likewise (:62, :70). The action parses the field as dollars (settings.ts:270-274 'dollars and cents, such as 5.00'), so a person who opens the page and presses 'Save materiality' without changing anything sets $500.00 and $25,000.00, and triage then drops every line short by less than $500 and every case under $25,000. The Cases screen's own Materiality form formats the same values correctly with dollars() (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/page.tsx:177, :181), so the two screens that set one value disagree by a factor of 100.
- **Rule:** CLAUDE.md rule 2 and docs/product/PRD.md:256 ('every money value rendered from cents with the practice's locale'); .claude/rules/brand.md:36 (money in the mono font)
- **Best fix:** Format the thresholds and the staff rate once in the handler with the shared money formatter (formatCents for the prose, the same dollars() helper the Cases form uses for the field defaults), render the prose figures through <Money>, and add a route test that the defaults page round-trips: loading the page and posting its defaults unchanged leaves practice.settings exactly as it was. Then keep one place to set materiality (see flows-practice-12).
- **Notes:** The image checkers flagged it on all three defaults shots. It is misleading about money and silently loses cases on a save, so it is blocking.

### `flows-practice-02`: Logging the payer's answer sends the person to Uploads, which cannot take a payer letter; the only working path files the letter as a contract document in the specialist's abstraction queue

- **Flags:** blocking; size M
- **Screens:** `p.submissions.default.1280`, `p.submissions.notice.1280`, `p.uploads.default.1280`, `p.uploads.era-format.1280`, `p.abstraction.default.1280`, `p.contracts.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions`, `/p/[practiceId]/uploads`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/packets/submissions.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/contracts/contract-queries.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/uploads/uploader.tsx`, `apps/web/e2e/packets.spec.ts`
- **Evidence:** 'Log a payer response' needs a response document and, with none, renders no form at all, only 'Upload the payer's letter (as a payer letter or other document) on the Uploads screen' (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions/page.tsx:290 and :293-297). The Uploads screen offers only '835 remittance' and 'ERA export from a practice-management system' in 'Kind of document' (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/uploads/page.tsx:40-48; shots p.uploads.default, p.uploads.era-format). A payer letter can be uploaded only from the Contracts screen, whose panel takes contract, exhibit and payer letter (apps/web/src/lib/routes/contracts.ts:47, :123), and the e2e journey does exactly that (apps/web/e2e/packets.spec.ts:421-423, 'goto(/contracts)'), not what the screen tells a person. 'Other document', which the same sentence offers, cannot be uploaded anywhere. Worse, a letter uploaded there joins the contract documents: on PACKETS the payer's partial-acceptance letter (3876b778) sits under the contract specialist's 'Documents awaiting abstraction' with 'A new contract with this payer' and 'Tag to the payer' (p.abstraction.default; the documents query takes kind IN ('contract','exhibit','payer_letter'), apps/web/src/lib/contracts/contract-queries.ts:16), and in the practice's 'Documents to tag' on Contracts.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 ('every error shown with a next step'; the step given must work); PRD.md:279 (Submissions and responses: 'Log a submission or response' with documents); PRD.md:260 (every screen's empty state says what to do next)
- **Best fix:** Upload the response document where it is used: give 'Log a payer response' its own file field (kind payer_letter, the same presigned upload, scan and receipt as Uploads) so the letter is chosen or uploaded inside the form, and keep the typed fields while it scans. Keep payer letters that arrive this way out of the contract-tagging and abstraction lists (filter those lists to contract and exhibit, or tag a response letter to its submission at upload). Change the empty state's words to the new path, and add an e2e step that logs a response from the submissions page alone.
- **Notes:** Verified in code and in the e2e: the working path is undocumented on screen. If tagging a payer letter to its submission at upload needs a column on source_document, that part goes to Lane A; the in-form upload alone needs none.

### `flows-practice-03`: Nothing tells the second person a packet waits for their approval: no notification, no queue, no case-list column

- **Flags:** blocking; size M; needs a migration; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.packet.approver-ready.1280`, `p.packet.drafter.1280`, `p.home.tiles.1280`, `p.home.default.1280`, `p.cases.default.1280`, `p.notifications.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/notifications`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/dashboard/queries.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/cases/workbench.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/monitoring/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/monitoring/queries.ts`
- **Evidence:** 'Request approval' records only a case_activity row (apps/web/src/lib/cases/workbench.ts:111-130): no notification (the notification kinds are monitoring_run, digest, recoupment and follow_up only, packages/db/migrations/20261027090000_packet_and_ledger_review.sql:418; apps/web/src/components/monitoring/display.tsx:28-60) and no mail. The practice home's Queues list files held, plans waiting, questions, anomalies, cases with no owner, follow-ups and unmatched payments, but no 'packets waiting for approval', 'exports to make' or 'attested exports to log' (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx:120-130; shots p.home.default, p.home.tiles). The Cases table shows state, lines, actionable, deadline, pursuit and owner, never the packet's step (p.cases.default). The approver learns of the request only by opening each case's packet page and scrolling about 3,000 px to 'Approval requested ... by pricing-manager' (p.packet.approver-ready). The same gap follows each hand-off: the drafter is not told of an approval, nor the practice that the reviewer attested an export ('The practice may now log its submission', p.packet-review.notice).
- **Rule:** PRD.md:267 ('jump to any queue' from the dashboard) and PRD.md:278 (Packet builder: request approval, approve by a second person); CLAUDE.md 'Never: mark a packet approved by the person who drafted it' makes the second person's step mandatory, so a silent request stalls the dispute against its filing deadline
- **Best fix:** Make each packet hand-off a queue and a notification: add queues on the practice home and filters on Cases for 'Packets waiting for approval (not yours)', 'Approved, not exported', 'Exported, waiting for attestation' and 'Attested, submission not logged', each with its count and earliest filing deadline; add a packet column to the Cases table (draft, approval requested, approved, exported, attested, submitted, answered); and send an in-app notification (counts only, no PHI) to the practice's packet approvers when approval is requested and to the drafter when it is approved, linking to the packet's Approval region.
- **Notes:** The queues and the Cases column need no migration and can land first in Lane B; the new notification kinds widen notification_kind_check, which is a migration for Lane A. Rated blocking because the two-person rule makes the approver's step mandatory and nothing in the product leads to it.

### `flows-practice-04`: The packet page never says where the packet stands or what to do next; each person's one action is buried in the ninth region, about 3,000 px down

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.packet.approver-ready.1280`, `p.packet.drafter.1280`, `p.packet.default.1280`, `p.packet.notice-requested.1280`, `p.packet.notice-exported.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/packets/queries.ts`
- **Evidence:** The page opens with Version, Contract citation, Line schedule, Deadline, Calculation, Interest, Evidence and Narrative regions (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx:224-358) before Approval (:360) and Export (:446). On p.packet.approver-ready (4,178 px) the approver's 'Approve this version' and 'Approval requested ... by pricing-manager' sit at about y 3,100; on p.packet.default the next step after attestation, 'Log its submission', is a small inline link at about y 3,900 inside the Export prose; the drafter on p.packet.drafter learns that approval is pending only near the bottom. The header shows only the case state chip ('Open' or 'Resolved') and a subtitle about the actionable figure, never the packet's own step (draft, approval requested, approved, exported, attested, submitted, answered).
- **Rule:** PRD.md:278 (Packet builder: approval panel, export history, log submission); PRD.md:260 (each screen says what to do next); the lens's 'next step obvious' test
- **Best fix:** Add a status strip under the title, server-rendered from the same facts the regions use: the packet's step as a short stepper (Written, Approval requested, Approved, Exported, Attested, Submitted, Answered) with who and when, and one primary action for this viewer in this state (Request approval; Approve this version, with a jump to the line schedule and narrative to review; Export PDF and CSV; Download for the payer; Log the submission; Log the payer's answer), or the reason none is theirs ('Waiting for a second person's approval, requested 01:41 by ...'). Keep the regions below as the evidence.
- **Notes:** The subtitle's wording ('Actionable: $0.00 on 0 of the 3 lines not written off ...') is #38's (figures in words, packet pages); this finding is the missing status and action, not the figure's words.

### `flows-practice-05`: The full narrative editor stays open under an approved, exported and submitted version, right above Approve

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; in flight: #48
- **Screens:** `p.packet.default.1280`, `p.packet.notice-exported.1280`, `p.packet.notice-requested.1280`, `p.packet.approver-ready.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/packets/version-form.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The VersionForm (prompt-pay state, evidence checkboxes, the narrative textarea, 'Save as a new version', 'Write a new version from the code-built draft', 'Draft the narrative with the model') renders whenever the version is current (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx:334), whatever its approval, export, attestation or submission. On p.packet.default it sits open under version 3, which is approved, exported, attested, submitted and answered. On p.packet.approver-ready it sits directly above 'Approve this version': an approver who fixes a typo there becomes the last editor and, by the rule the Approval card states, can no longer approve, and nothing at the editor says so. After submission nothing says what a new version would be for (a second-level dispute, a correction after a reopening).
- **Rule:** PRD.md:278 ('versioned edits'; 'the drafter cannot approve'); PRD.md:302 (a consequential action states its consequence); CLAUDE.md 'Mark a packet approved by the person who drafted it' (never)
- **Best fix:** Collapse the editor behind a 'Write a new version' disclosure once a version has an approval request, and state its consequence there for the viewer ('A new version replaces version 3 for approval; you would then be its writer and could not approve it'; after export: 'for a second-level dispute or a correction; the exported version and its submission stay as they are'). Keep it open by default only while the current version is a draft without a request.
- **Notes:** Touches the same form as #48 (narrative survives sign-out); fold into that PR or land right after it.

### `flows-practice-06`: After the payer answers, the case says 'Resolved' and drops the amount asked and the amount refused, so nobody decides what to do with the rest

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.case.default.1280`, `p.case.write-off-open.1280`, `p.submissions.default.1280`, `p.cases.default.1280`, `p.home.tiles.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases`, `/p/[practiceId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/cases/workbench.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/packets/submissions.ts`
- **Evidence:** PACKETS' case asked $90.00 on 3 lines (packet line schedule), and the payer partially accepted $60.00 (submission log on p.submissions.default). The case page reads 'Resolved' with Actionable $0.00, Payer approved $60.00, Recovered (posted) $0.00 and Written off $0.00 (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/page.tsx:73-106); no figure states the $90.00 asked or the $30.00 not accepted, and 'Packet and dispute' says only 'the payer has answered' (page.tsx:140-142), not what it answered. Every line shows 'Payer approved' with 'Write this line off'. The Cases list and the home say '1 resolved', $0.00 actionable (p.cases.default, p.home.tiles), so the case leaves the working queue while no cash has posted and $30.00 is unexplained.
- **Rule:** Combined audit #89 (one case status, a closed state only for a case that was paid; 'the loop to a second-level dispute'); PRD.md:279 (submissions and responses per case); the lens's 'numbers a person needs to decide are on the screen'
- **Best fix:** On the case summary show the dispute's own figures from code: asked (the exported version's variance), accepted by the payer (the latest response), not accepted (asked less accepted), payer approved and recovered (ledger), each with its stage label; under 'Packet and dispute' print the latest answer in words ('Partially accepted on 2026-10-26: $60.00 of $90.00') with the next step for the remainder (write a level-2 version, or write the remainder off with 'payer correct on review'). Show the state as #89's step-plus-outcome wording ('Answered: partly accepted, cash not posted') instead of 'Resolved' until the ledger reaches posted or deposited.
- **Notes:** The state vocabulary itself is #89 (Lane A, PR 8/10/13); the figures and the answer in words need no migration and can land first. Audit item #11 (a partial answer spread over every line) is the ledger side and is not touched here.

### `flows-practice-07`: Choices that record a fact arrive pre-answered: a write-off reason of 'Window passed' and a payer response of 'Accepted'

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.case.write-off-open.1280`, `p.submissions.default.1280`, `p.submissions.notice.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/triage.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/submissions.ts`
- **Evidence:** The write-off form's Reason select has no empty option, so it arrives on the first reason, 'Window passed' (packages/triage/src/cases.ts:93; apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/page.tsx:207-213), and its 'required' never fires; on p.case.write-off-open it offers 'Window passed' for a line whose window is open until 2036-04-05. The 'Log a payer response' Response select likewise arrives on 'Accepted' (submissions/page.tsx:307-311) with 'Received on' prefilled with today (:317), so a hurried 'Record the response' logs an acceptance the payer never gave. Both records are audited facts about money (a write-off reason; the payer's answer) that feed the ledger and reports.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:302 (a destructive action confirms with its consequence stated); WCAG 2.1 SC 3.3.2 Labels or Instructions and SC 3.3.4 Error Prevention (Legal, Financial, Data)
- **Best fix:** Give both selects a first, empty, disabled option ('Choose the reason', 'Choose the payer's answer') so 'required' forces a choice; for a write-off, list only the reasons that can be true for the line (omit 'Window passed' while its window is open) and require the 'Why' text for 'Other' server-side; for a response, require 'Amount accepted' for accepted and partial and show the asked amount beside it.
- **Notes:** Bulk write-off (#125) should reuse the same empty-first reason select.

### `flows-practice-08`: Success notices come from a '?notice=' URL parameter, so a refresh, the back button or a shared link replays them over a page that has moved on

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.packet.notice-requested.1280`, `p.packet.notice-exported.1280`, `p.packet-review.notice.1280`, `p.agreements.notice.1280`, `p.contract-version.notice.1280`, `p.abstraction-version.model-notice.1280`, `p.coverage.model-notice.1280`, `p.defaults.notice.1280`, `p.cases.notice.1280`, `p.plan.notice.1280`, `p.abstraction-ledger.notice.1280`, `p.submissions.notice.1280`, `p.template.notice.1280`, `p.correspondence.notice.1280`, `p.proof-level.notice.1280`, `p.pricing-settings.notice.1280`, `p.abstraction-regulations.notice.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/agreements`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/coverage`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/http/route.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/agreements/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/models/notices.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/settings.ts`
- **Evidence:** Every mutation redirects to its page with '?notice=<code>' (for example apps/web/src/lib/routes/settings.ts:318; mapping.ts:154), and 21 practice pages render the notice from the URL alone, with no check that the action happened or still holds (10 route schemas take 'notice: optionalText(...)'). In normal use this contradicts the page: the drafter's tab, refreshed after the approver acts, still reads 'Approval is requested. A second person who approves packets approves it here.' above 'Approved ... by packet-approver' and an exported, attested version (p.packet.notice-requested; packet/page.tsx:35); 'once the reimbursement reviewer attests it' sits above 'Attested' (p.packet.notice-exported); 'Agreement chain confirmed' sits above two 'Missing' agreements (p.agreements.notice; settings/agreements/page.tsx:30-32); 'Its calendar dates are recorded on the contract' sits beside a calendar that says 'No dates ahead' (p.contract-version.notice); and Coverage accepts the abstraction notice 'nothing prices until you approve every field' (p.coverage.model-notice; apps/web/src/lib/models/notices.ts:27). A link copied from the address bar to a colleague carries the notice too. The notices are also the plain neutral Alert, so a confirmation looks like any info box.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:256 ('no screen depends on client-side state that a refresh loses', read with PRD.md:302: the URL carries filters so a view can be shared, not one-time outcomes); the lens's 'notice saying one thing while the page shows another'
- **Best fix:** Make a notice a one-time flash bound to the request that wrote it: the action sets a short-lived, signed, HttpOnly flash cookie (or a flash row keyed to the audit event id) that the next render of that page reads and clears, and the redirect URL stays clean. Where a page still takes a notice from the URL, render it only when the state it reports still holds (approval requested and not yet approved; chain confirmed; contact saved) and only on the page that issued it. Give confirmations the success tone with a title and live-region role='status'.
- **Notes:** The checkers opened these by URL without the write; the contradiction is still reachable by a person refreshing or sharing the post-action URL. touches42 because lib/routes/settings.ts is in #42.

### `flows-practice-09`: An attested export reads to any other reviewer as their own first-person attestation, without naming who attested

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; in flight: #38
- **Screens:** `p.packet-review.default.1280`, `p.packet-review.notice.1280`, `p.packet.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/packets/review.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** On the review page the Attestation region of an attested export says 'Attested 2026-10-26 01:26 UTC over these files' and names the attester only when it is the viewer ('by you'; apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review/page.tsx:248), then prints the checklist and the first-person 'I attest that I downloaded and reviewed this packet's exported PDF and CSV ...' (:266; apps/web/src/lib/packets/review.ts:105), while the same viewer's Exported files say 'You have not downloaded it yet' for both files (:120, :129). reviewer@synthetic.test, who did not attest (packet-reviewer did), reads an attestation in the first person over files it never downloaded. On the practice's packet page the attester is 'the reimbursement reviewer' (p.packet.default). The notice variant adds 'The practice may now log its submission' with nothing for the reviewer to do next and no link back to 'Packets to attest' (p.packet-review.notice).
- **Rule:** PRD.md:278 and docs/adr/0061 (the attestation is the reviewer's own signed act over the two SHA-256); the lens's 'states that contradict each other'
- **Best fix:** Name the attester on both pages (the reviewer's sign-in, as every other actor on these pages is named), print the attested checklist as a record in the third person ('packet-reviewer@... attested on ... that they downloaded and reviewed ...'), keep the first-person sentence only above an unsigned checklist for the viewer who can sign it, and after attesting return the reviewer to 'Packets to attest' with the next export in deadline order.
- **Notes:** Packet review is in #38's set of pages (figures in words); this finding is the attester's identity and voice, which #38 can carry.

### `flows-practice-10`: A case reopened by a recoupment reads 'Awaiting response' although nothing was ever sent

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.case.viewer.1280`, `p.home.default.1280`, `p.reconciliation.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/triage/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/dashboard/queries.ts`
- **Evidence:** LEDGER's case shows the chip 'Awaiting response' beside 'No packet yet. Not submitted yet.' (p.case.viewer), and the home counts it as '1 awaiting a response' (p.home.default; apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx:110-117). The state is derived as awaiting_response whenever a line was reopened from payer approved or recovered (packages/db/migrations/20261023120000_recovery_ledger.sql:662-663), here by the payer's $18.00 PLB take-back (timeline: 'reopened from Recovered (posted or deposited) to Actionable'). What the practice must do (re-dispute the taken-back amount, or accept it) is the opposite of waiting.
- **Rule:** Combined audit #89 ('one shared wording for status everywhere (step plus outcome, such as "Reopened by a recoupment")'); the lens's 'states that contradict each other'
- **Best fix:** Render the case state everywhere through one function that words the derived state by its cause: when the derivation came from reopened lines with no submission, show 'Reopened: the payer took back $18.00 on 2026-08-03' with the next step (build a packet for the reopened line, or write it off), and count it on the home under a 'reopened by a payer' state rather than 'awaiting a response'. The database state and its transitions stay #89's work.
- **Notes:** A display-layer fix ahead of #89; #89's migration (Lane A) then replaces the derivation.

### `flows-practice-11`: A run a person creates shows its underpaid total but is never triaged, and its page neither says so nor leads to Cases

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.pricing-run.default.1280`, `p.pricing-runs.default.1280`, `p.cases.default.1280`, `p.cases.deadline-14.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases`
- **Files:** `apps/worker/src/price/job.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/pricing.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The price job queues triage only for runs the monitoring chain created (apps/worker/src/price/job.ts:13-15, :47-49). A run a billing manager creates on Pricing runs (p.pricing-runs.default) completes with 'Underpaid (validated): $90.00 across 1 payer pattern', and its page lists 'Variance by payer pattern' (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]/page.tsx:216-217) with no triage state, no 'Triage this run' and no link to the case or patterns it feeds (no cases or triage reference in that page apart from the triage date, :129). Triage lives on Cases as 'Triage a run' with a run select (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/page.tsx:61-88), so the person must know to go there; until then the $90.00 is in no case and no packet.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:275 (Pricing runs), PRD.md:277 (Cases: create from a pattern); the lens's 'next step obvious'
- **Best fix:** Triage every completed run the same way, whoever created it (enqueue triage on completion for person-created runs too, keyed as today so it runs once), and on the run page show its triage outcome: 'Triaged 2026-10-26: 1 case opened (SYNTHETIC PAYER Q, $90.00 actionable), 0 patterns waiting below the case materiality' with links to each case, or 'Triage queued' while it runs. Keep 'Triage a run' on Cases only for a re-triage on today's date.
- **Notes:** If auto-triage of person-created runs was deliberate (a re-run awaiting acceptance under #10), keep it off for re-runs only and still show the triage state and the button on the run page.

### `flows-practice-12`: Cases puts the case list under four setup panels, even when the person arrives from 'Deadlines in 14 days', and materiality is set in two places

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M

On p.cases.deadline-14, reached from the home's deadline link with the filter already applied, the one case due within 14 days ('2026-11-02, within 14 days') sits at about y 1,340 of 1,406, below the fold at 1280x800. The Materiality card here duplicates Settings, Materiality and pursuit, which sets the same thresholds with other words ('apply from the next triage' against 'Triage uses them from its next run') and, today, other figures (flows-practice-01).
