### `flows-practice-13`: An actionable line names no case: Claims and lines and the line trace have no path to the case, packet or reversal that explains it

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; in flight: #38
- **Screens:** `p.claims.default.1280`, `p.line.default.1280`, `p.line.viewer.1280`, `p.pricing-run.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/claims`, `/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/claims/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/remittances.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The three Actionable 97110 lines on p.claims.default carry the chip and a 'Line trace' link only. The line trace page links to Claims and lines, the contract version, the run, other runs and the remittance (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]/page.tsx:40, :100, :112, :124, :212) but never to the case that holds the line or its packet; p.line.default is 'Line 1: 97110, Actionable' with no case named. On p.line.viewer the badges 'Actionable', 'Reversed' and 'Superseded' stand together (page.tsx:49-51) with no link to the reversal or the correcting claim and no words on which figure counts. The run's 'Variance by payer pattern' row and its three lines also lead nowhere near the case (p.pricing-run.default).
- **Rule:** PRD.md:270 (Claims and lines: open the line trace; status chips for the five stages); PRD.md:277 (Cases: detail with lines, trace); combined audit #107 (filters that reach the remedy)
- **Best fix:** Where a line belongs to a case, show 'In case: SYNTHETIC PAYER Q, CE:SYNPLAN-FIX-ONE (packet version 3, submitted)' with links on the line trace, make the stage chip on Claims and lines a link to that case, and on a reversed or superseded line name and link the reversal and the correcting claim with one sentence on which line now carries the stage. Link each 'Variance by payer pattern' row to its case or to 'Patterns waiting for a case'.
- **Notes:** The line trace's raw JSON steps are #38's (line-trace pages); this finding is the missing links only, so it can ride in #38's PR or beside #107's.

### `flows-practice-14`: After an upload the receipt says what loaded but never what it found or what happens next

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.uploads.default.1280`, `p.upload.default.1280`, `p.upload.export-receipt.1280`, `p.uploads.viewer.1280`, `p.remittance.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/uploads`, `/p/[practiceId]/uploads/[documentId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/remittances/[remittanceId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/uploads/[documentId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/uploads/uploader.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/uploads/receipts.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/uploads.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/remittances/[remittanceId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** When a batch settles, 'This batch' reports 'N of N sent; N of N taken in' and the page refreshes its lists (apps/web/src/components/uploads/uploader.tsx:252-258, :422-427); there is no next step. The 835 receipt (p.upload.default) shows Loaded, size, SHA-256, document id and a parse report whose only onward link is the remittance by an 8-character id (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/uploads/[documentId]/page.tsx:51-73, :236-298): nothing says which service months the lines fall in, whether monitoring will price them or a person must create a run, how many plans now wait on a mapping, or what pricing found once it ran. The posting-export receipt reads only 'Loaded.' (p.upload.export-receipt) and does not link to Reconciliation or say what matched. The remittance it links to lists charge, paid and patient share per claim, without expected or short (p.remittance.default). The person's first question after uploading, 'what did it find?', is answered only by visiting Pricing runs, Coverage, Mapping and Cases in turn.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:268 (Uploads: receipts with status and reasons; batch progress) and PRD.md:267 (Practice dashboard: jump to any queue); PRD.md:260 (every screen says what to do next); time to value
- **Best fix:** Give each settled 835 receipt (and the batch card) a 'What happens next' block computed from the loaded lines: service months covered and, for each, the run that priced it (linked) or 'monitoring prices it tonight' / 'Create the run'; plans now waiting on a mapping (linked to Mapping, filtered); and, once priced, the validated shortfall and cases opened from it (linked). For a posting or EFT export, show rows read, matched and unmatched with a link to Reconciliation's unmatched section. On the remittance, add expected and short per claim where priced.

### `flows-practice-15`: 'Pricing can run' and 'Mapped' stop short of pricing: no link or button to price the months the change affects

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; in flight: #32
- **Screens:** `p.coverage.no-run.1280`, `p.coverage.default.1280`, `p.plan.notice.1280`, `p.plan.default.1280`, `p.mapping.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/coverage`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/coverage/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/mapping.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/coverage.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/actions.ts`
- **Evidence:** On a practice whose lines are loaded and mapped but never priced, Coverage says 'No pricing run has priced this practice's lines yet' and, in an alert, 'Pricing can run', with no link or button (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/coverage/page.tsx:117-119, :142-147; p.coverage.no-run). Saving a mapping returns 'Mapped / The plan is mapped from the date given. Its lines on those dates no longer wait on mapping' (p.plan.notice; apps/web/src/lib/routes/mapping.ts:154), but those lines stay unpriced until a run prices their months, and nothing on the page says so or offers it.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:271, :274, :275 (Mapping, Coverage 'links each reason to its remedy screen', Pricing runs 'create a run'); PRD.md:260 (each screen says what to do next)
- **Best fix:** Where pricing can now run, say which service months hold unpriced lines and offer 'Price 2026-03 to 2026-04' as a button that posts the same create-run action (or, where monitoring will do it, say when). After a mapping is saved or corrected, list the months it affects with that button, and note that a re-run of a month already priced shows its differences before anyone accepts it.
- **Notes:** #32 (one unmapped plan no longer freezes a month) is in flight on mapping and coverage; land this with it.

### `flows-practice-16`: The practice home is the same page for every role: no role-based landing, the work queues come fourth, and the administrator's setup lives elsewhere

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.home.default.1280`, `p.home.support-access.1280`, `p.home.tiles.1280`, `p.settings.default.1280`, `p.settings.mostly-done.1280`, `p.agreements.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/practice.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/dashboard/queries.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/settings.ts`
- **Evidence:** apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx renders one fixed order for every customer role (:157-495): the headline, Data freshness, Where things stand, Queues, Notifications, Practice record, Agreements, Support access. Only the subtitle changes ('as Billing manager' / 'as Practice administrator'; :160-162). For the billing manager the 'Queues' card, the list of what waits for them, is the fourth block (about y 1,030 on p.home.default) and lists all seven queues at equal weight, zeros included. For the practice administrator (p.home.support-access) the page leads with the billing headline and queues they cannot work (they hold no file.upload, mapping.edit or case.work; packages/db/src/authz/permissions.ts:169-187), while their own open items, the 'Getting started' checklist with 'Confirm the agreement chain', 'Check your billing ZIP' and 'Upload your first remittances' still to do (p.settings.default), are only on Settings; the home's Agreements card lists the two on file and omits the two Missing (p.agreements.default). Before the first file every role, the administrator included, gets 'Upload your first 835 files ... Go to Uploads' (page.tsx:182-192). A viewer (the owner or CFO) gets the same work queues.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:267 (Practice dashboard: 'jump to any queue'); PRD.md:260 (an empty state that says what to do next, a permission state that explains what the role cannot do); combined audit #90 ('The checklist sends the administrator to an upload page they cannot use')
- **Best fix:** Keep one URL and order its sections by the viewer's permissions, computed in the handler: for those who work cases (billing manager, analyst, firm roles) lead with 'Your work', the non-zero queues sorted by the earliest filing deadline (with the packet hand-offs of flows-practice-03), then the headline; for the practice administrator lead with the Getting started checklist until it is complete, then agreements missing, grants and support access, then the headline read-only; for a viewer lead with the headline, this quarter's recovered figure and the latest ROI statement, and show queues as counts without action links. The empty state names who uploads when the viewer cannot.
- **Notes:** touches42 via apps/web/src/lib/routes/practice.ts and settings.ts (the checklist is built in settings.ts and rendered in settings/page.tsx; move it to a shared component). The extra admin-plus-biller role is #90 (Lane A, PR 14); this finding needs no role change.

### `flows-practice-17`: The rail is one flat list of 14 screens for every customer role, in pipeline order, and the contract specialist's rail hides two of its three practice screens

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.home.default.1280`, `p.settings.default.1280`, `p.uploads.viewer.1280`, `p.abstraction.default.1280`, `p.abstraction-ledger.default.1280`, `p.abstraction-regulations.default.1280`, `p.packet-reviews.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/regulations`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/http/route.ts`
- **Evidence:** ShellNav gives every customer role the same 14 links in data-pipeline order: Practice home, Uploads, Remittances, Claims and lines, Payer and plan mapping, Contracts, Coverage, Pricing runs, Anomalies, Cases, Reconciliation, Reports, Notifications, Settings (apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx:185-228). Cases, the billing manager's daily screen, is tenth; the practice administrator and the viewer get Uploads, Mapping and Cases they cannot act on (p.settings.default, p.uploads.viewer), and the account group shows 'Billing' to every role, where a billing manager or viewer learns only that the administrator manages it (apps/web/src/app/settings/billing/page.tsx:30-31). The contract specialist's practice rail has only 'Contract abstraction' (shell.tsx:169-174); 'Regulations to confirm' and 'Ledger corrections', both its practice work, are reachable only from a small link row above the abstraction heading (p.abstraction.default), and on those pages the rail still marks 'Contract abstraction' as current (p.abstraction-ledger.default, p.abstraction-regulations.default).
- **Rule:** PRD.md:260 ('Navigation is a left rail ... a permission state that explains what the role cannot do'); PRD.md:300-302 (information architecture); the lens's 'what it sees first'
- **Best fix:** Group the customer rail by job with short group labels, and order within each by use: Work (Cases, Anomalies, Reconciliation), Results (Practice home, Reports), Data (Uploads, Remittances, Claims and lines), Set-up (Contracts, Payer and plan mapping, Coverage, Pricing runs), Settings. Keep every screen reachable for every role (each has a read view), but mark screens whose actions the role lacks as read-only and list them after the role's own group; show 'Billing' only to roles with organization.billing. Give the specialist's practice rail its three screens (Contract abstraction, Regulations to confirm, Ledger corrections) with the current one marked.
- **Notes:** touches42: shell.tsx is in #42. The grouping is a recommendation where the brand rules are silent (BRAND.md fixes the brass underline for the active item, not the rail's order).

### `flows-practice-18`: Read-only roles are given the doer's card titles and instructions, then told at the bottom that someone else does it

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.pricing-runs.viewer.1280`, `p.correspondence.read-only.1280`, `p.proof-level.read-only.1280`, `p.pricing-settings.viewer.1280`, `p.case.viewer.1280`, `p.plan.viewer.1280`, `p.coverage.viewer.1280`, `p.contracts.viewer.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/correspondence`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/proof-level`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/pricing`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/correspondence/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/proof-level/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/settings.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/pricing/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** A viewer sees a card titled 'Create a run' with the release it would pin, ending in 'Runs are created by the roles that map plans' (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/page.tsx:57-59, :96). A billing manager sees 'Set the contact' and its fill-in rules before 'The correspondence contact is set by the practice administrator' (settings/correspondence/page.tsx:63, :92). The proof level reads 'The next reconcile, queued when you save, makes the change' to a role that cannot save (apps/web/src/lib/routes/settings.ts:353; p.proof-level.read-only). A viewer is told 'Check the ZIP code' (packages/contracts/src/location.ts:65; p.pricing-settings.viewer). On the case page a viewer reads 'write the line off below ... and write, approve and export the packet again' under 'Pricing not current', with no write-off form (cases/[caseId]/page.tsx:175-183; p.case.viewer). The plan page keeps its how-to-map paragraph above 'As Viewer, you cannot map payer plans' (mapping/[planId]/page.tsx:142-150; p.plan.viewer). None of these names the person who can act or offers a way to reach them.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:260 ('a permission state that explains what the role cannot do'); PRD.md:256 ('every error shown with a next step')
- **Best fix:** Give each action card a read-only variant chosen in the handler from the permission: title it by what the reader can see ('Runs', 'The correspondence contact', 'Proof level: deposited'), drop the imperative steps, and put one PermissionState line in their place that names who acts, by name where the practice has one ('Set by the practice administrator, Dana Admin') with a 'Ask them' mailto or in-app request where available. Word status lines without a command for roles that cannot act ('27601 is not on Medicare's ZIP list in force; the practice administrator or billing manager corrects it').
- **Notes:** touches42 via lib/routes/settings.ts. Naming the administrator needs only a read of the practice's memberships, which the settings handlers already scope.

### `flows-practice-19`: A missing correspondence contact blocks every export, but it surfaces only at the last step, to people who cannot set it

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.packet.approver-ready.1280`, `p.packet.drafter.1280`, `p.correspondence.read-only.1280`, `p.home.support-access.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/correspondence`, `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/dashboard/queries.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/packets/contact.ts`
- **Evidence:** On LAYOUT, which has no contact ('No correspondence contact is set, so no packet is exported yet', apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/correspondence/page.tsx:54), a billing manager can draft, request approval and approve; only the Export region, near the foot of the packet page, says '... the practice has no correspondence contact ... The practice's administrator sets the correspondence contact under Settings, Correspondence' (apps/web/src/lib/packets/export-attempts.ts:46; p.packet.approver-ready, p.packet.drafter). The billing manager cannot set it (the link is shown only to those who can, packet/page.tsx:82-90) and is not told who the administrator is. The administrator's home for the same practice (p.home.support-access, other-admin on LAYOUT) says nothing about it; the case page's 'Packet and dispute' card says nothing either.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:256 ('every error shown with a next step'); PRD.md:260 (a permission state that says who can); the lens's dead-end test
- **Best fix:** Treat the contact as a practice-level blocker shown where the work starts: an alert on the case page and at the top of the packet page while none is set ('Exports need the practice's correspondence contact. Dana Admin, the practice administrator, sets it' with a request action), a 'Packets waiting on the correspondence contact' item at the top of the administrator's home with the count of approved versions held, and an in-app notice to the practice's administrators the first time an export is refused for it.
- **Notes:** A notification for it would widen notification_kind_check (Lane A migration); the alerts and the home item need none.

### `flows-practice-20`: The headline's Recovered figure has no period, while a row below with the same label and a period shows a different amount

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.home.default.1280`, `p.home.tiles.1280`, `p.home.support-access.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/dashboard/queries.ts`
- **Evidence:** On LEDGER the tile reads 'Recovered (deposited) $12.00 ... stage five: cash deposited, proved by the bank export, net of recoupments and reversals' with no period (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx:215-221), and 'Where things stand' shows 'Recovered (deposited) this quarter $0.00 (2026-Q4, 2026-10-01 to 2026-12-31 ...)' (:356-363). The tile is all-time net and the row is this quarter, but nothing on the tile says so; an owner reading '$12.00' and then '$0.00' under the same label cannot tell which is right. Payer approved and Actionable on the same row of tiles are also undated (they are current balances).
- **Rule:** .claude/rules/brand.md:37 ('Recovered' with posted or deposited, satisfied) read with PRD.md:267 ('recovered this quarter') and CO-11 (no figure the product cannot prove); the lens's 'numbers a person needs to decide'
- **Best fix:** State each tile's time basis in its label or note: 'Actionable now', 'Payer approved, not yet cash', 'Recovered (deposited) to date, since 2026-06-09' (the first ledger entry), and put this quarter's figure as a second line inside the Recovered tile ('this quarter: $0.00') instead of a separately labelled row, so the two amounts sit together with their periods.
- **Notes:** Checker lead on p.home.default. The earlier tour's 'Actionable $0.00 against $30.00 in open cases' is still present on LEDGER (home tile $0.00 with '1 line in cases still being worked'; ROI statement 'Actionable in open cases: $30.00') and is not duplicated here.

With ?case= the heading becomes 'Case ledger' (:223) with no payer, plan or case reference beside it, and every entry's 'Case ledger' link and the line's own link point back to the same page (:122, :169; p.reconciliation.case-ledger). The home's 'Payments, postings and deposits matching nothing: 1' queue lands at the top of this page, not at the unmatched section.

On the case ledger, title it with the case ('Case ledger: SYNTHETIC PAYER L, CE:SYNPLAN-FIX-LED') and link back to the case, and drop the self-links.

### `flows-practice-22`: A contract version the specialist approved waits for the practice's sign-off with no queue or notice, so pricing under it waits unseen

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.home.default.1280`, `p.home.support-access.1280`, `p.contracts.default.1280`, `p.contract-version.default.1280`, `p.abstraction.default.1280`

- **Evidence:** A version prices only 'once the contract specialist has verified every term ... and the practice has signed it off' (Contracts lead, p.contracts.default). The specialist's approval is the hand-off, but the home's queues count the specialist's open questions and nothing for versions awaiting the practice's sign-off (apps/web/src/lib/dashboard/queries.ts:115-130; apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx:120-130), and no notification kind exists for it (notification kinds: monitoring_run, digest, recoupment, follow_up). The Contracts table can be filtered by 'Latest version' status, but nothing directs the administrator or billing manager to it; lines under the version stay unpriced (DATE_OUTSIDE_VERSIONS or NO_MAPPING on Coverage) until someone happens to open the version.

- **Best fix:** Add 'Contract versions waiting for your sign-off' to the home's queues for roles holding contract.sign_off (count, payer and the billed dollars of lines it would price), a 'Waiting for sign-off' default filter link on Contracts, and a status strip on the version page for those roles ('Approved by the contract specialist on ...; sign it off to price N lines, $X billed').
- **Notes:** Same family as flows-practice-03 (hand-offs without a queue); can land in the same PR as its queues.

### `flows-practice-23`: Missing agreements are a dead end for the administrator, and the checklist's 'Terms of use: Done' sits against the chain's 'Terms of service: Missing'

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.agreements.default.1280`, `p.agreements.non-admin.1280`, `p.settings.default.1280`, `p.home.support-access.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/settings/agreements`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings`, `/p/[practiceId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/agreements/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/settings.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** On NORTH the chain lists 'Missing: Terms of service' and 'Missing: Data-rights addendum' and ends 'The chain can be confirmed once every agreement is on file; the platform records each as it is signed' (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/agreements/page.tsx:47, :66). The administrator is offered no way to get them signed (no signing link, no request, no contact), and the home's Agreements card lists only the two on file (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx:428-466). Settings' Getting started shows 'Terms of use: Done, accepted with their version and time' (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/page.tsx:15) beside 'Confirm the agreement chain: To do', while the chain names a 'Terms of service' as Missing (apps/web/src/lib/routes/settings.ts:72): two documents with near-identical names, one done and one missing, and nothing says they differ.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:256 ('every error shown with a next step'); PRD.md:282 (Settings: grants and agreements); the lens's dead-end and contradiction tests
- **Best fix:** For each missing agreement give the administrator its next step: 'Request it for signature' (which opens the e-signature request or a support ticket pre-filled with the agreement kind) and who records it; list missing agreements on the administrator's home with the same action. Name the two terms so they cannot be confused: the person's 'Terms of use (accepted at sign-in)' in the checklist and the practice's signed 'Terms of service (practice agreement)' in the chain, each with one line on what it is.
- **Notes:** touches42 via lib/routes/settings.ts. The documents' names are counsel's: if 'Terms of service' and 'Terms of use' are fixed legal titles, keep them and add the explanatory line; any rename of a legal document goes to the founder through the co-founder (brief, 'The founder's').

### `flows-practice-24`: The administrator cannot ask the access log the questions it exists for: no filter by person, route, record or date, and no page links into it

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.access-log.default.1280`, `p.access-log.older.1280`, `p.access-log.record-filter.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/settings/access-log`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/access-log/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/settings.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The access log is a newest-first table of 50 rows with 'Older entries' (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/access-log/page.tsx:23-85). Its only filter is one record, taken from the URL (object_type and object_id; apps/web/src/lib/routes/settings.ts:797-815), which the page's own comment says is reached 'from that record's own page' (page.tsx:16), but no page links to it: the only link into the access log is the Settings tab (apps/web/src/components/settings/nav.tsx:30). The first page on PACKETS is 50 reads by one person within two minutes (p.access-log.default); support reads under 'ticket SYN-TOUR-1' appear only on the second page (p.access-log.older), which has no 'Newer' link and still says 'newest first'. The practice administrator, who holds no phi.read, cannot open a claim to start from it either.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:251 (Audit log: 'the access report answers "who read claim X" in one query'); PRD.md:282 (Settings: access log, read-only); PRD.md:302 (filters in the URL)
- **Best fix:** Give the access log server-side filters in the URL: person, route (ownership, grant, support ticket), action, record kind, and a date range, plus a 'Find a claim' field that takes the payer claim control number and resolves it server-side to its record ids without showing PHI to a role that lacks phi.read; add 'Newest' and 'Newer' links and say which page is shown; and link 'Who read this' from the case, claim, line and packet pages for roles that read them.
- **Notes:** practice_access_log() takes object filters and a cursor today; person, route and date filters may need its signature widened, which is a migration for Lane A if done in SQL.

### `flows-practice-25`: A support grant's 'Records read' is about 80 raw rows of UUIDs and code names, unlinked and ungrouped, with no way back

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.support-access.default.1280`, `p.support-access.ended.1280`, `p.home.support-access.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/support-access/[supportGrantId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/support-access/[supportGrantId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/practice.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/copy/words.ts`
- **Evidence:** The page the home's Support access card opens ('Records read') prints one row per read with the record type as a snake_case key (contract_version, source_document, rate_record), the record as a full UUID in mono, and the purpose as a key (rate_record_review, contract_evidence) (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/support-access/[supportGrantId]/page.tsx:38-41); on SYN-CON-E2E about 80 rows repeat the same four records (p.support-access.default, 2,828 px). Nothing is linked, nothing is grouped by record, and there is no back link to the home or the access log; the rail marks 'Practice home' as current. The administrator, who is notified when a grant opens, cannot tell in one look what support read or why.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:251 (practice-visible access report); PRD.md:298 (Support: open support grants with expiry and reads); words.ts labelOf for codes (apps/web/src/lib/copy/words.ts:28)
- **Best fix:** Group the reads by record: one row per record with its kind in words (labelOf), a human name where the role may see it (the contract's title, 'version 1', the document's short id and kind), the purposes in words, the count and first and last read, linked to the record; keep the raw list behind 'Every read' with paging. Add a back link to the home and to the access log filtered to this grant.
- **Notes:** touches42: the support-access page and lib/routes/practice.ts are in #42.

### `flows-practice-26`: Compare two runs defaults to runs of different months, so it reports every line as in one run only; a run 'Not in effect' never says what replaced it

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; in flight: #10
- **Screens:** `p.pricing-runs.default.1280`, `p.pricing-runs.viewer.1280`, `p.compare.default.1280`, `p.compare.diff-filter.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/compare`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/pricing/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/pricing.ts`
- **Evidence:** The compare form preselects the two newest runs whatever their periods (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/page.tsx:110, :120: options[1] and options[0]): March against April on LAYOUT, March against July on LEDGER. The result on p.compare.default is '0 of 0 lines both runs priced are priced differently; 3 only in the first run; 32 only in the second', 35 rows of 'Only one run priced the line' (apps/web/src/components/pricing/diff.tsx:152), which answers no question a billing manager has. The question compare exists for, 'what does this re-run change against the run it re-prices', needs two runs over the same lines, which the person must find by reading option labels clipped after 'release 900001,'. On LEDGER's list two March runs differ ($0.00 underpaid in effect, $30.00 not in effect) and the older one carries only 'Not in effect' (apps/web/src/components/pricing/display.tsx:66-69), with no link to the run that superseded it or a 'Compare with the run in effect' action.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:275 (Pricing runs: 'compare two runs; re-run under a new release with a diff'); the lens's 'next step obvious'
- **Best fix:** Offer comparison where it has meaning: on each run that is not in effect or awaits acceptance, a 'Compare with the run in effect for this period' link, and on a run in effect, 'Compare with the run it replaced'; in the free form, default the second select to a run over the same period as the first and mark runs of other periods as such. Name the replacing run on a 'Not in effect' badge ('Replaced by the run of 2026-10-26 01:26, which is in effect').
- **Notes:** PR #50 (#10, a superseded re-run cannot be accepted) works on the same run states; land with or after it. The cause wording ('Only one run priced the line' for lines neither run priced) is the words lens's.

### `flows-practice-27`: Three upload screens take different kinds, while the Uploads screen lists every kind's receipts but takes only 835s and ERA exports

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.uploads.default.1280`, `p.uploads.era-format.1280`, `p.uploads.viewer.1280`, `p.contracts.default.1280`, `p.reconciliation.default.1280`, `p.submissions.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/uploads`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/uploads/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/uploads/uploader.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/uploads.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/uploads/limits.ts`
- **Evidence:** The rail's 'Uploads' takes '835 remittance' and 'ERA export from a practice-management system' only (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/uploads/page.tsx:40-48). Contracts takes contract, exhibit and payer letter (apps/web/src/lib/routes/contracts.ts:47), and Reconciliation takes posting and EFT exports (p.reconciliation.default, 'Upload posting and EFT exports'). Yet the Uploads receipts list shows every kind: a contract 'Stored', a posting export and an EFT export 'Loaded' (p.uploads.viewer, p.uploads.era-format). A billing manager who opens Uploads to send this month's posting export, or the payer's letter (flows-practice-02), sees those kinds in the list but cannot upload them there, and nothing on the drop zone says where they go.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:268 (Uploads: drop zone; file receipts with status and reasons); the lens's dead-end test
- **Best fix:** Make Uploads take every kind a practice sends, with 'Kind of document' listing 835, ERA export, posting export, EFT or bank export, contract or exhibit, and payer letter, each routed to its own intake as today, with one line under the chooser on what happens next for that kind (tagging for contracts; matching for exports; the submission it answers for a letter). Keep the contextual drop zones on Contracts, Reconciliation and the submissions page as shortcuts that preselect the kind.
- **Notes:** Pairs with flows-practice-02; land together.

### `flows-practice-28`: Data freshness's 'Last run' shows whatever job ran last (an ROI statement), not when the practice's lines were last priced

- **Flags:** polish; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.home.default.1280`, `p.home.tiles.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/routes/practice.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** freshness() takes the newest job_run of any type (apps/web/src/lib/routes/practice.ts:108, 'ORDER BY started_at DESC ... LIMIT 1'). On LEDGER the card reads 'Last run: ROI statement, 2026-10-26 01:27 UTC: Succeeded' (p.home.default); on PACKETS 'Monitoring of new files' (p.home.tiles). A billing manager reading 'Data freshness' wants to know which service months are priced and when pricing last ran; a report print or a statement says nothing about that, and a later export would push the last pricing run out of sight.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:267 (Practice dashboard: 'Data freshness (last file, last run), ... last run status')
- **Best fix:** Show the last pricing run explicitly ('Last priced: service 2026-07-01 to 2026-07-31, complete 2026-10-26 01:27 UTC', linked to the run), the last monitoring pass, and the latest service month with loaded lines; keep 'Last job' with its status only as a secondary line, and keep the failed-run banner as it is.
- **Notes:** touches42 via apps/web/src/lib/routes/practice.ts.

### `flows-practice-29`: The ledger's '#' is the platform-wide entry sequence, so a practice's append-only ledger reads 1, 2, 3, 7, 8, as if entries were removed

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; in flight: #12
- **Screens:** `p.reconciliation.default.1280`, `p.reconciliation.case-ledger.1280`, `p.reconciliation.viewer.1280`, `p.abstraction-ledger.default.1280`, `p.abstraction-ledger.adjust-open.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/reconciliation/queries.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The '#' column prints ledger_entry.seq (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/page.tsx:111; apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger/page.tsx:64; selected at apps/web/src/lib/reconciliation/queries.ts:263), an identity shared by every practice (packages/db/migrations/20261023120000_recovery_ledger.sql:177, 'seq bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY'). LEDGER's five entries read 8, 7, 3, 2, 1. The page says 'An entry never changes; a correction is an entry of its own', so a billing manager or an auditor reading a gap of three in an append-only ledger has every reason to ask what was deleted, and nothing answers. The gap also tells one practice how many entries other practices received in between.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:280 (Reconciliation: 'every match shows its evidence') and CLAUDE.md rule 4 (nothing of another tenant reaches a practice, here a count); the lens's 'states that contradict each other'
- **Best fix:** Number entries per practice in the query (row_number() over the practice's entries ordered by seq, computed server-side and stable because entries are append-only), label the column 'Entry', and keep the global seq out of the page (it can stay in the CSV under its own name for support).
- **Notes:** PR #51 (#12) edits these ledger tables; fold in. The checkers flagged the gap on three shots.

### `flows-practice-30`: The monthly summary opens on last calendar month, which is usually empty because service months lag remittances; nothing lists the months that have lines

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.monthly.default.1280`, `p.monthly.month.1280`, `p.reports.default.1280`, `p.reports.audit-reports.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/reports/monthly`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/routes/monitoring.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/monthly/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/reports.ts`
- **Evidence:** The summary and the Reports card's month field default to the previous calendar month (apps/web/src/lib/routes/monitoring.ts:131, :169, 'params.month ?? lastMonth()'; apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/page.tsx:198). On MONITORING that is September 2026, and every By stage figure is 0 with 'No line has a service date in 2026-09' (p.monthly.default), although March 2026 holds $90.00 actionable on 3 lines (p.monthly.month). Remittances arrive weeks after service, so last month is routinely near empty; the person must guess which month to type, and 'Generate the month's PDF' would print an empty month.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:281 (Reports: monthly summary); PRD.md:260 (an empty state that says what to do next)
- **Best fix:** Default to the latest service month that has priced lines, and show the months that have lines as a short list or a select with their actionable totals (newest first), so the month is chosen, not typed; when a chosen month is empty, name the nearest months that have lines.

(apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/pricing/page.tsx:160) with no link, while the card above offers 'Contact support' for a move. A billing manager whose clinic bills place 22 at the non-facility rate has no path from the screen that tells them it is set by request.

### `flows-practice-32`: Choosing a release on Rule release notes gives no sign of which release is shown

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.releases.release.1280`, `p.releases.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/releases`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/releases/page.tsx`
