The chosen release's link carries aria-current but no visual style (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/releases/page.tsx:130), and the card title does not say it is the chosen one versus the one in force. With more than one release, a person comparing notes cannot tell which they are reading without reading the number.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 1.3.1 Info and Relationships (state conveyed only programmatically) and the lens's 'what it sees first'
- **Best fix:** Mark the current row with weight and a 'Showing' badge (not brass, which .claude/rules/brand.md:32-33 keeps for the single active-nav underline), and title the card 'Rule release 900001 (in force)' or 'Rule release 900001 (shown; 900002 is in force)'.
- **Notes:** With one published release the state is identical by design; the fix matters once a second release is published.

### `flows-practice-33`: A refused form clears what the person typed: every ActionForm resets its fields to their defaults when the server returns 'Not done'

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; in flight: #48
- **Screens:** `p.correspondence.default.1280`, `p.correspondence.set.1280`, `p.abstraction-ledger.adjust-open.1280`, `p.reconciliation.reverse-open.1280`, `p.case.write-off-open.1280`, `p.submissions.default.1280`, `p.plan.default.1280`, `p.contracts.default.1280`, `p.abstraction-version.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/settings/correspondence`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/field.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/packets/version-form.tsx`
- **Evidence:** ActionForm passes useActionState's dispatcher to <form action> (apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx:32, :65), and the refusal comes back as state, not a redirect (:9-11). React 19 requests a form reset before every function action it runs, whatever the action returns (node_modules react-dom 19.3.1, react-dom-client.development.js:9326-9343, 'requestFormReset$1(formFiber); return action(formData)'), so on 'Not done' every uncontrolled field returns to its defaultValue; ActionState carries no field values to put back. 62 ActionForms on 33 practice pages are affected, among them the nine-field correspondence contact (a wrong phone format wipes the edit back to the saved contact), the ledger 'Change in dollars' and 'Why the amount is corrected', 'Why it is reversed', the write-off reason and 'Why', the submission and response forms (dates, channel, payer's reference, amount accepted, notes), mapping dates, the contract tagging form, and the abstraction's 'Ask the practice' question. The narrative editor alone is controlled (apps/web/src/components/packets/narrative-editor.tsx:19, :27), so it survives; the version form's other fields (prompt-pay state, evidence) do not.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.2 SC 3.3.7 Redundant Entry (A), added by combined audit #103; PRD.md:256 ('every error shown with a next step': the next step is to correct one field, not retype the form)
- **Best fix:** Return the submitted values with the refusal (ActionState.values, the FormData's non-secret entries, never a password or one-time code) and render each field's defaultValue from them when present, keyed so React remounts the fields with the person's text; or make ActionForm's fields controlled through a small form-state context. Add one e2e check per form family that a refused submit keeps the typed values and focuses the first invalid field.
- **Notes:** Verified in React's source, not by a capture (no refused submit is in the set). #48 keeps a narrative through sign-out; this keeps every other form's input through a refusal, and can share its PR.

### `flows-practice-34`: A report being generated tells the person to 'reload in a moment', while the packet export already refreshes itself

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.report.generating.1280`, `p.reports.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/documents/[documentId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/documents/[documentId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/packets/export-status.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The queued audit report shows 'Generating' and 'The report job triages the run and prints the report; reload in a moment' with nothing else on the page (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/page.tsx:54; p.report.generating); the ROI statement and printed documents say the same (reports/roi/[statementId]/page.tsx:79; reports/documents/[documentId]/page.tsx:51). The packet export's status already polls with router.refresh about every five seconds and offers 'Check again' (apps/web/src/components/packets/export-status.tsx:15-27), so one product has two behaviours for the same wait, and the reports leave the person refreshing by hand without knowing how long.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:260 (every screen has a loading state); the lens's 'next step obvious'
- **Best fix:** Reuse ExportStatus's pattern on the three report pages: a live region that refreshes every few seconds while the job is queued or running, says how long it has been waiting, links 'Check again', and, past a time limit, says the job is late and names the next step (it is retried; the failed-run banner appears on the home if it fails three times).

### `flows-practice-35`: The payer's copy opens on a cover that states no amount, no claim count, no service dates, no filing deadline and no return contact

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; in flight: #38
- **Screens:** `p.packet-pdf.default.1280`, `p.packet.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/pdf`
- **Files:** `apps/worker/src/packets/html.ts`, `apps/worker/src/report/brand.ts`, `apps/worker/src/report/brand.test.ts`
- **Evidence:** Page 1 of the exported packet is a Paper cover with the practice name, 'PAYMENT DISPUTE', 'Request to reprocess underpaid claims', the addressee, 'Dispute level 1; prepared 2026-10-26' and the reference, and is otherwise empty (p.packet-pdf.default; apps/worker/src/packets/html.ts:234-241, meta holds only addressee, level and date, reference). The amount asked ($90.00 on 3 lines, on PACKETS), the claim count, the service-date range, the filing deadline the practice is relying on and whom to call ('Approval and correspondence') come pages later. A payer's examiner triaging mail reads the cover first; the practice's own reviewer attesting the PDF also sees nothing there to check against the case.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:278 (Packet builder: citation, line table ...); .claude/rules/brand.md:42-43 (covers use the Paper token; the seal only when earned): the brand rules govern the cover's look and are silent on its content, so this is a recommendation
- **Best fix:** Add a summary block to the cover's meta, computed by code from the version: 'Amount requested: $90.00 on 3 claim lines (service 2026-03-05 to 2026-03-07)', 'Filing deadline relied on: 2036-04-05 (contract, verified)', and the correspondence contact; keep the Paper cover and the white-label rule, and regenerate the cover baseline under ADR 0058.
- **Notes:** Every figure must come from the version's stored content (rule 1); #38 (figures in words on packet pages) is the nearest in-flight item.

### `flows-practice-36`: 'Convert to a validation request' drops the person on Coverage with no trace of the cohort, payer or request, and nothing tracks it afterwards

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.anomalies.cohort.1280`, `p.anomalies.default.1280`, `p.coverage.default.1280`, `p.coverage.viewer.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/anomalies`, `/p/[practiceId]/coverage`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/routes/triage.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/coverage.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/coverage/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/dashboard/queries.ts`
- **Evidence:** On an open cohort (SYNTHETIC PAYER N, 97110, 4 lines short of the mode; p.anomalies.cohort) the billing manager's two actions are 'Mark reviewed' and 'Convert to a validation request' (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/anomalies/page.tsx:103-117). The conversion records the actor and redirects to plain /coverage (apps/web/src/lib/routes/triage.ts:150-168, redirect `/p/${practiceId}/coverage`), whose route takes no payer, cohort or notice (apps/web/src/lib/routes/coverage.ts:92): the page shows no confirmation, does not say which payer to ask, and its 'Request a missing fee schedule from a payer' letter is preselected to whichever payer sorts first, not the cohort's (p.coverage.viewer shows SYNTHETIC PAYER N only because it sorts first). No screen lists open validation requests or their state; the cohort's own line 'Converted to a validation request ... by ...' is the only record, and the home's anomaly queue keeps counting the cohort.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:276 (Anomalies: 'convert to a validation request'); PRD.md:274 (Coverage: 'create a request-to-payer letter for a missing schedule'); the lens's dead-end test
- **Best fix:** Redirect to Coverage with the cohort's payer and codes in the URL, a one-time confirmation ('Validation requested for SYNTHETIC PAYER N, CE:SYNPLAN-OON-CAS, 97110'), and the letter form preselected to that payer, plan and codes; list open validation requests on Coverage's 'What to obtain' with their age and a link back to each cohort; and drop a cohort from the home's 'to review' queue once it is converted.
- **Notes:** Follow flows-practice-08 for the confirmation (a one-time flash, not a ?notice).

## narrow-other

### `narrow-other-01`: A refused submit wipes or replaces what the person typed, on every ActionForm

- **Flags:** blocking; size S
- **Screens:** `console.models.error.390`, `console.status.error.390`, `console.organization.error.390`, `console.practice.error.390`, `console.support.error.390`, `console.terms.error.390`, `request-audit.error.390`, `support.error.390`, `settings.billing-org.error.390`, `screening-result.error-name.390`, `sign-in.error-wrong-password.390`, `sign-in.error-locked.390`
- **Routes:** `/console/models`, `/console/status`, `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`, `/console/practices/[practiceId]`, `/console/support`, `/console/terms`, `/request-audit`, `/support`, `/settings/billing/[organizationId]`, `/screening/results/[resultId]`, `/sign-in`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/action-form.test.tsx`
- **Evidence:** What the tour typed (screens/capture/tour.spec.ts) against what each refusal shows: console.models.error.390 typed Flag 'Daily budget (cents)', Everyone, '5.00' and shows Flag 'Kill switch (every model feature off)', Everyone, an empty Value beside 'value: a budget in whole cents'; console.status.error.390 typed Title 'SYNTHETIC STATUS CHECK' and First update 'SYNTHETIC CHECK', both empty; console.practice.error.390 typed ZIP '0001' and shows the stored 27601 (page.tsx:82 defaultValue) under 'zip: five digits'; settings.billing-org.error.390 typed routing '12345' and shows the valid test value 110000000 ([organizationId]/page.tsx:113 defaultValue={testRouting}) under 'routingNumber: a nine-digit routing number'; support.error.390 loses Subject and the whole message; console.terms.error.390 loses Version, Document reference and SHA-256; console.support.error.390 loses Ticket 'SYN-REFUSED-1'; request-audit.error.390 loses all five typed fields; screening-result.error-name.390 loses Locations and Payers; console.organization.error.390 loses the invited email; sign-in error shots lose the email. Cause: apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx:65 passes the action as <form action={formAction}>, and React 19 resets a form's uncontrolled fields after every form action, refused or not, so each field goes back to its defaultValue. The two forms that submit through onSubmit keep everything: the screening upload (components/screening/upload-form.tsx:117-147, preventDefault + startTransition) in screening.error-zip.390 and screening.error-no-phi.390, and the hardware-key form (components/hardware-key.tsx:70-72) in sign-in-hardware-key.error-locked.390. 112 ActionForm uses share the cause.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.2 3.3.7 Redundant Entry (A; #103 adopts 2.2) and 3.3.3 Error Suggestion; docs/product/PRD.md:256 'every error shown with a next step' (the next step, fix the marked field, is impossible when the field is emptied or shows a different, valid value).
- **Best fix:** In ActionForm, submit through onSubmit: preventDefault, then startTransition(() => formAction(new FormData(form))), as upload-form.tsx does, keeping action={formAction} for a submit without JavaScript. Nothing is reset on a refusal, so every typed value, choice and textarea stays where the person put it; nothing is echoed from the server, so no refused text (which may be what the no-PHI check caught) travels back in the action state. Clear only password and one-time-code inputs on a refusal (3.3.7's security exception). On success the action still redirects to a fresh page. Add action-form.test.tsx: a refused state keeps an input's, a select's and a textarea's values and clears a password.
- **Notes:** The brief's two named cases are both this cause: the models form 'resets to Kill switch for everyone' and the status form 'clears its title'. See narrow-other-02 for the models form's dangerous default. A refresh losing unsent input is normal; a refusal losing it is not.

### `narrow-other-02`: The model flag form defaults to 'Kill switch' for 'Everyone', so any reset or untouched submit targets every model feature for every customer

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `console.models.default.390`, `console.models.error.390`
- **Routes:** `/console/models`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/console/models/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/console-models.ts`
- **Evidence:** Both shots show Flag 'Kill switch (every model feature off)' and Applies to 'Everyone' preselected in 'Set a flag'. The options come in that order from apps/web/src/lib/routes/console-models.ts:24-28 (MODEL_FLAGS, kill switch first) and :127-129 (targets, 'global' first), rendered without a placeholder by app/console/models/page.tsx:79-80. After a refused budget (narrow-other-01) the form falls back to exactly this pair, with the budget error still shown beside it.
- **Rule:** The brand and PRD rules are silent on a destructive default; recommendation. The global kill switch already has its own explicit button in the Kill switch card (page.tsx:60).
- **Best fix:** Give Flag and Applies to an empty first option ('Choose a flag', 'Choose who it applies to') and make both required, so the form never submits a kill switch or a global scope nobody chose; keep the kill switch for everyone on the Kill switch card's own button.
- **Notes:** Independent of 01: even with 01 fixed, a fresh page preselects the most far-reaching option.

### `narrow-other-03`: Thirteen console and settings screens scroll sideways at 390 px because their tables have no scroller

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `console.compliance.default.390`, `console.compliance.sent.390`, `console.organization.default.390`, `console.organization.created.390`, `console.organization.empty.390`, `console.organization.error.390`, `console.organization.firm.390`, `console.organization.help.390`, `console.organization.menu.390`, `console.organization.suspended.390`, `console.prospects.default.390`, `console.prospects.all.390`, `console.rule-releases.default.390`, `console.rule-releases.notes-open.390`, `console.rule-releases.prepared.390`, `console.rule-releases.release.390`, `console.specialist-queue.default.390`, `console.specialist-queue.all.390`, `console.support.default.390`, `console.support.ended.390`, `console.support.error.390`, `console.support.help.390`, `console.support.menu.390`, `console.terms.default.390`, `console.terms.error.390`, `console.tickets.default.390`, `console.tickets.all.390`, `settings.billing-org.default.390`, `settings.users.default.390`, `settings.users.end-membership-ticked.390`, `settings.users.firm.390`, `settings.users.notice-invited.390`
- **Routes:** `/console/compliance`, `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`, `/console/prospects`, `/console/rule-releases`, `/console/specialist-queue`, `/console/support`, `/console/terms`, `/console/tickets`, `/settings/billing/[organizationId]`, `/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/console/compliance/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/prospects/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/rule-releases/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/specialist-queue/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/support/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/terms/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/tickets/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/settings/billing/[organizationId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/scroll-table.tsx`, `apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts`
- **Evidence:** Measured capture widths at a 390 px viewport: prospects 959, specialist queue 768, support 739-745, users 667 (firm 590), organization 641-654 (internal org 515), tickets 575, rule releases 502, compliance 492, terms 482, billing organization 422. The header stays 390 wide, so panning right also slides the Menu and the account out of view. Each overflowing table sits bare in its card, not in ScrollTable (components/scroll-table.tsx, used by only 4 files): console/compliance/page.tsx:159; console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx:133, 236 and 305; console/prospects/page.tsx:50; console/rule-releases/page.tsx:56 and 112; console/specialist-queue/page.tsx:81; console/support/page.tsx:65; console/terms/page.tsx:31; console/tickets/page.tsx:46; settings/billing/[organizationId]/page.tsx:140; settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users/page.tsx:51. Cut-off content includes the Status buttons (tickets), Received, Status and Next (prospects), the Access links (specialist queue), the Paid column (invoices) and Lines re-runs changed (rule releases). The 390 px guard exists (apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts:53, atPhoneWidth) but only 5 specs call it, none on these routes.
- **Rule:** docs/adr/0056:51-53 'At a phone's width no page scrolls sideways: a wide table scrolls inside its own card'; WCAG 1.4.10 Reflow (AA).
- **Best fix:** Put every table of figures in ScrollTable (rule releases, compliance policies and records, agreements, grants, invoices, specialist queue, terms versions), and stack the record tables whose rows carry forms (narrow-other-04). Then make the guard total: run atPhoneWidth on every page route with a role that can open it, generated from the route registry so a new screen cannot skip it (the same per-route visit #103 asks for), and add an ESLint rule or render test that a <table> in apps/web/src/app sits inside ScrollTable or a stacked layout.
- **Notes:** touches42 via console/support/page.tsx and settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users/page.tsx (in #42's file list); the other pages can land first. The rule releases page also overflows from its unbroken manifest hash (narrow-other-13). Billing organization: not inside #135's scope (bank account shown first), but PR #47 edits the same page.

### `narrow-other-04`: At 390 px each row's action sits off-screen, apart from the row it acts on (End membership, Change role, End now, Mark answered, Decline)

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `settings.users.default.390`, `settings.users.end-membership-ticked.390`, `settings.users.firm.390`, `settings.users.notice-invited.390`, `console.organization.default.390`, `console.organization.empty.390`, `console.organization.firm.390`, `console.support.default.390`, `console.support.ended.390`, `console.tickets.default.390`, `console.tickets.all.390`, `console.prospects.default.390`, `console.prospects.all.390`
- **Routes:** `/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users`, `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`, `/console/support`, `/console/tickets`, `/console/prospects`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/support/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/tickets/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/prospects/page.tsx`, `docs/adr/0056-wear-the-paidwhole-brand-from-one-token-source-self-hosted-fonts-and-status-as-text-on-tint.md`
- **Evidence:** settings.users.default.390: the Role select, Change role, 'They lose access now' and End membership run from x 370 to 667, right of the 390 viewport (users/page.tsx:51-89, one ActionForm pair per row in an unlabelled fourth column, :57). console.support.default.390: 'End now' at x 650-740 while Ticket and Practice are at x 75-230 (console/support/page.tsx:65). console.tickets.default.390: Mark answered / Mark closed at x 441-575 (tickets/page.tsx:46). console.prospects.default.390: Create practice group, Mark contacted, Why declined and Decline at x 598-959 (prospects/page.tsx:50). Even inside a sideways scroller the person scrolls the email, ticket or practice out of view to reach the button, so ending the wrong membership or grant is easy.
- **Rule:** docs/adr/0056:51-53 (no page scrolls sideways at a phone's width; 'a definition list stacks its terms over their values'); WCAG 1.4.10 Reflow; PRD.md:256 keyboard and labels (the users table's action column has no header, users/page.tsx:57).
- **Best fix:** Below the sm breakpoint, render each row of these record lists as a stacked item: the identity (email, ticket and practice, organization) as its heading, the facts as a definition list, and the row's forms full width underneath, so each action reads with the row it changes. Keep the table from sm up, and give the users table's last column a visible 'Actions' header. Record the pattern in ADR 0056's chrome paragraph beside the definition-list rule: a table of figures scrolls in its card, a list of records with per-row actions stacks.
- **Notes:** touches42 via users/page.tsx and console/support/page.tsx. Lands with or after narrow-other-03. The ADR line is an addition, not a reversal: the page still never scrolls sideways.

### `narrow-other-05`: On a phone the screening result's tables hide Allowed, Difference and Status with nothing to say they scroll, so all four 'anomaly' rows read alike

- **Flags:** blocking; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `screening-result.default.390`, `screening-result.converted.390`, `screening-result.error-name.390`, `screening-result.loading.390`
- **Routes:** `/screening/results/[resultId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/screening/anomalies.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/screening/coverage.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/screening/result-view.tsx`, `apps/web/e2e/screening.spec.ts`
- **Evidence:** screening-result.default.390: 'Anomalies by code and payer product' shows Code, Payer product, Rows, Claim lines and the first Medicare figure; Allowed in your file, both differences and Status are past the card edge. The text above says '1 group of 4 is an anomaly', but the four rows show no status, so on a phone they all read as anomalies. Coverage shows only Payer product and Outcome (Reason, Rows, Claim lines, Allowed hidden); Rows with the largest differences stops at a clipped Payer product, with a blank header band about 140 px tall from the hidden headers. Causes: components/screening/anomalies.tsx:286-287 and :359-360 (overflow-x-auto regions with no visible cue) and components/screening/coverage.tsx:61-62 (a bare overflow-x-auto div with no role, name or tabIndex, so a keyboard cannot scroll it). The anomalies table has ten columns, so a scroll cue alone would still leave the comparison off-screen.
- **Rule:** .claude/rules/brand.md 'expected, paid and short appear in that order' and docs/brand/BRAND.md section 7 Tables (the three numbers cannot be seen together); docs/adr/0056:51-53; WCAG 2.1.1 Keyboard for coverage.tsx:61; PRD.md:256.
- **Best fix:** Below sm, render each row of the three tables as a stacked block: the code and payer product (or the row number) as its heading; the Medicare figure(s), Allowed in your file and the Difference in mono, in the brand's order, as a definition list; the Status chip on its own line. Keep the tables from sm up, inside ScrollTable (coverage.tsx:61 included). Add a 390 px check to screening.spec.ts that the anomaly rows' Status and Difference are visible without scrolling.
- **Notes:** Updates the earlier tour's '1280 screening result tables run past the edge' for 390: at phone width they are clipped, not overflowing. touches42 only through e2e/screening.spec.ts (in #42's list); the components are not. This is the prospect's first and only view of the product.

### `narrow-other-06`: Sideways scrollers give no sign that they scroll (the screening's column table and the place-of-service split history)

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `screening.default.390`, `screening.file-ready.390`, `screening.error-dos.390`, `screening.error-member-id.390`, `screening.error-no-phi.390`, `screening.error-zip.390`, `screening.loading.390`, `screening.sent-minutes.390`, `screening.sent-sent.390`, `screening.sent-soon.390`, `screening.sent-waiting.390`, `console.screening.default.390`, `console.screening.approved.390`, `console.screening.error.390`
- **Routes:** `/screening`, `/console/screening`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/scroll-table.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/screening/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/screening/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** screening.default.390: in 'What the file holds' the Example column is cut to 'Exa', '971', 'ma_', '202', '123' at the card edge, with no shadow, fade or words saying more lies to the right; phone scrollbars are hidden at rest. Cause: components/scroll-table.tsx:9-14 is a bare overflow-x-auto region. console.screening.default.390: the Approved splits table shows Approved and a cut 'contract-specialist@synthetic.tes'; Facility, Non-facility and Status ('In force') are hidden; its wrapper app/console/screening/page.tsx:82 is a plain overflow-x-auto div without role, name or tabIndex, so a keyboard cannot scroll it either.
- **Rule:** docs/adr/0056:51-53 (scroll inside the card; silent on a cue: recommendation); WCAG 2.1.1 Keyboard for console/screening/page.tsx:82; PRD.md:256.
- **Best fix:** Give ScrollTable a visible cue: CSS-only edge shadows that appear only while columns lie past an edge (the background-attachment: local gradient technique, written as classes, so no style attribute meets the CSP), plus a short visible caption below sm, 'Scroll sideways for more columns'. Route console/screening/page.tsx:82 through ScrollTable. The screening's three-column reference table reads better stacked below sm: each column as a definition list (name in mono, what it holds, example), with no scroller at all.
- **Notes:** Confirms and updates the earlier tour's 'screening template table cut off at 390': it is not cut off. It scrolls inside a keyboard-reachable region, but nothing on screen says so. touches42 via app/screening/page.tsx.

### `narrow-other-07`: Loading screens drop the header and Menu and say nothing visible about what is loading

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `account.notifications.loading.390`, `console.billing.loading.390`, `console.practice.loading.390`, `console.rule-releases.loading.390`, `console.specialist-queue.loading.390`, `settings.billing.loading.390`, `settings.billing-org.loading.390`
- **Routes:** `/account/notifications`, `/console/billing`, `/console/practices/[practiceId]`, `/console/rule-releases`, `/console/specialist-queue`, `/settings/billing`, `/settings/billing/[organizationId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/states/loading-state.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/**/loading.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Each loading shot is the still mark, a progress bar and 4 or 6 grey rows on a blank page: no header, no lockup, no Menu, no Sign out, and the label ('Loading your notification preferences') is sr-only (packages/ui/src/states/loading-state.tsx:27). The shell is drawn by each page from its route result (components/shell.tsx:304-355; no app layout holds it), so all 40 loading.tsx files render a bare <main className="mx-auto flex max-w-5xl flex-col gap-4 p-8"> (e.g. app/account/notifications/loading.tsx:6). At 390 the content starts at x 32 rather than the shell's 16 (shell.tsx:334 px-4), so the page jumps sideways and the header pops back in when the data arrives. The rule releases and specialist queue shots are byte-identical: nothing tells them apart.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:265 (every screen has a loading state) and docs/adr/0056 Chrome (the header and Menu are the frame); the brand's loading rule (mark still, plain progress bar) is already met; PRD.md:256 keyboard (no way to navigate away while waiting).
- **Best fix:** Add a LoadingFrame that draws the same header and rail as AppShell with no per-request data (the lockup link, a Menu button and neutral placeholders where the switcher and the avatar go) and the shell's own main padding; every loading.tsx renders LoadingState inside it. LoadingState shows its label as visible text beside the progress bar ('Loading your notification preferences…'), keeping role=status. Share the header markup with shell.tsx so the two cannot drift.
- **Notes:** touches42 via components/shell.tsx. The same 40 loading files serve the practice routes outside this set; one fix covers them all.

### `narrow-other-08`: Nothing keeps a focused control out from under the sticky header

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `console.practice.error.390`, `console.models.error.390`, `console.status.error.390`, `console.support.error.390`, `console.terms.error.390`, `console.organization.error.390`, `settings.billing-org.error.390`, `support.error.390`
- **Routes:** `/console/practices/[practiceId]`, `/console/models`, `/console/status`, `/console/support`, `/console/terms`, `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`, `/settings/billing/[organizationId]`, `/support`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/globals.css`, `apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts`
- **Evidence:** The app header is sticky and 56 px tall (components/shell.tsx:315, 'sticky top-0 z-30 h-14'), and no scroll-padding-top or scroll-margin is set anywhere (none in apps/web/src/app/brand.css, globals.css, packages/ui or packages/brand). After a refusal ActionForm moves focus to the first refused control (components/action-form.tsx:52). On a long phone form (practice settings is 1,896-2,022 px tall at 390; the agreement forms run past 4,000 px on the organization page), that control is often above the viewport, and a browser that scrolls it to the nearest edge puts it under the header. The same happens when Shift+Tab walks back up a form. Full-page captures cannot show the viewport, so this rests on the code and the page heights.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.2 2.4.11 Focus Not Obscured (Minimum) (AA), adopted through combined audit #103, whose best option adds 'focus not hidden under the sticky header' to the keyboard test.
- **Best fix:** Set scroll-padding-top on the root to the header's height plus a gap (3.5rem + 8px) whenever the app header is present (in globals.css, for example :root:has([data-slot=app-header])), so focus(), anchor jumps and Tab all stop below it. Add the check #103 names to the e2e keyboard helper (apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts tabTo): the focused element's top is at or below the header's bottom.
- **Notes:** #103 lands the per-route accessibility gate in Lane A's PR 7; this one-line CSS fix need not wait for it.

### `narrow-other-09`: Form fields are 14 px, so iPhone Safari zooms the page in on every field a person taps

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `request-audit.default.390`, `screening.default.390`, `screening-result.default.390`, `sign-in.default.390`, `sign-in-mfa.default.390`, `sign-in-enroll.default.390`, `sign-in-new-password.default.390`, `sign-in-hardware-key.default.390`, `support.default.390`, `settings.users.default.390`, `settings.billing-org.default.390`, `console.organizations.default.390`, `console.organization.default.390`, `console.practice.default.390`, `console.models.default.390`, `console.status.default.390`, `console.support.default.390`, `console.terms.default.390`, `console.screening.default.390`, `console.compliance.default.390`, `console.billing.default.390`
- **Routes:** `/request-audit`, `/screening`, `/screening/results/[resultId]`, `/sign-in`, `/sign-in/mfa`, `/sign-in/enroll`, `/sign-in/new-password`, `/sign-in/hardware-key`, `/support`, `/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users`, `/settings/billing/[organizationId]`, `/console/organizations`, `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`, `/console/practices/[practiceId]`, `/console/models`, `/console/status`, `/console/support`, `/console/terms`, `/console/screening`, `/console/compliance`, `/console/billing`
- **Files:** `packages/ui/src/components/input.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/field.tsx`, `docs/adr/0056-wear-the-paidwhole-brand-from-one-token-source-self-hosted-fonts-and-status-as-text-on-tint.md`
- **Evidence:** The input is 'text-sm' (14 px) at every width (packages/ui/src/components/input.tsx:11), as are the select (apps/web/src/components/field.tsx:75) and the textarea (field.tsx:106). iOS Safari zooms into any focused field under 16 px and leaves the page zoomed, so after typing an email on /sign-in or /request-audit the person has to pinch back out. The viewport export sets no maximum scale (app/layout.tsx:29), which is correct and must stay. The captures are Chromium, so they cannot show the zoom; the cause is in the code.
- **Rule:** docs/adr/0056:49-50 sets 'tables and dense controls 14', written for the desktop, and is silent on phones. Recommendation; never meet it with maximum-scale=1, which would fail WCAG 1.4.4 Resize Text.
- **Best fix:** Make form controls 16 px below the sm breakpoint and 14 px from sm up ('text-base sm:text-sm' on Input, the SelectField select and TextAreaField), and note the phone exception in ADR 0056's type-scale sentence.
- **Notes:** It hits the public prospect forms (screening, request-audit) and every sign-in step, the screens most likely to be opened on a phone.

### `narrow-other-10`: At 390 px the practice switcher shrinks to 'Choose a ...', so on a phone nobody can see which practice is open

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `account.devices.default.390`, `account.notifications.default.390`, `account.notifications.saved.390`, `console.organizations.error-forbidden.390`, `console.screening.approved.390`, `console.screening.default.390`, `console.screening.error.390`, `console.specialist-queue.all.390`, `console.specialist-queue.default.390`, `settings.billing.default.390`, `settings.billing.empty.390`, `settings.billing.firm.390`, `settings.billing-org.default.390`, `settings.billing-org.empty.390`, `settings.billing-org.error-forbidden.390`, `settings.billing-org.error.390`, `settings.billing-org.suspended.390`, `settings.users.default.390`, `settings.users.end-membership-ticked.390`, `settings.users.error-forbidden.390`, `settings.users.firm.390`, `settings.users.notice-invited.390`, `support.account-menu.390`, `support.default.390`, `support.error.390`, `support.help.390`, `support.menu.390`, `support.ticket-opened.390`, `terms.account-menu.390`, `terms.default.390`, `terms.help.390`, `terms.menu.390`
- **Routes:** `/account/devices`, `/account/notifications`, `/console/organizations`, `/console/screening`, `/console/specialist-queue`, `/settings/billing`, `/settings/billing/[organizationId]`, `/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users`, `/support`, `/terms`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`
- **Evidence:** components/shell.tsx:57 caps the switcher's label at 'max-w-[5rem] truncate' below sm, so 'Choose a practice' renders as 'Choose a ...' in every shot listed. The same cap cuts a current practice's name to about 9 characters on the practice routes, so 'SYNTHETIC OTHER PRACTICE LEDGER' and 'SYNTHETIC OTHER PRACTICE PACKETS' would both read 'SYNTHETIC...'. The practice in view is the scope of every figure on a practice screen.
- **Rule:** docs/brand/BRAND.md section 7 and docs/adr/0056 Chrome: the header carries the practice switcher. The rules are silent on how it folds at phone width: recommendation. PRD section 7 makes the switcher the way a group or firm moves between practices.
- **Best fix:** Below md, take the switcher out of the header row: open the Menu panel with a 'Practice' section listing the reachable practices, the current one marked (aria-current) and its name in full; on a practice route, show the current practice's full name as a one-line band under the header (or as an eyebrow above the page title). The header keeps the lockup, the account and the Menu.
- **Notes:** touches42 via components/shell.tsx.

### `narrow-other-11`: On the console organization page, 'End membership' sits in the Account column and the Actions column is empty: the cells are flex boxes, not table cells

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `console.organization.default.390`, `console.organization.created.390`, `console.organization.error.390`, `console.organization.firm.390`, `console.organization.help.390`, `console.organization.menu.390`, `console.organization.suspended.390`, `console.organization.empty.390`
- **Routes:** `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** console.organization.default.390: the 'active' badges and the 'End membership' buttons stack at x 303-447 under 'Account', while the 'Actions' header at x 447-495 has nothing under it; the empty column widens the table, which adds to the overflow. console.organization.default.1280 (outside this set) shows the same at 1280. Cause: app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx:147 and :153 put 'flex flex-wrap' on the <td>s themselves. A <td> with display:flex is no longer a table cell, and two adjacent ones are wrapped into a single anonymous cell, so the Account and Actions cells merge.
- **Rule:** WCAG 1.3.1 Info and Relationships (the visible header no longer labels its cells, and a screen reader's column for the buttons can be wrong); PRD.md:256 labels.
- **Best fix:** Keep both <td>s as plain table cells and put a <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1"> (and gap-2 for the actions) inside each; at phone width this table then stacks with the other record lists (narrow-other-04).
- **Notes:** The only two flex <td>s in apps/web (grep '<td className="[^"]*flex').

### `narrow-other-12`: Refusals name fields by their code keys ('routingNumber', 'contactName', 'organizationName'), not by the labels on screen

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `request-audit.error.390`, `screening-result.error-name.390`, `settings.billing-org.error.390`, `console.models.error.390`, `console.practice.error.390`, `console.support.error.390`, `console.terms.error.390`, `console.status.error.390`, `console.organization.error.390`, `support.error.390`, `sign-in-new-password.error-mismatch.390`
- **Routes:** `/request-audit`, `/screening/results/[resultId]`, `/settings/billing/[organizationId]`, `/console/models`, `/console/practices/[practiceId]`, `/console/support`, `/console/terms`, `/console/status`, `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`, `/support`, `/sign-in/new-password`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/field.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The alert items read 'organizationName: …', 'contactName: …', 'routingNumber: …', 'value: …', 'zip: …', 'reason: required', 'version: …', 'components: …', 'email: …', 'subject: …' and 'confirm: the two passwords differ'. Cause: components/action-form.tsx:76 prints labels[issue.field] ?? issue.field, and only 2 of the 112 ActionForm uses pass 'labels'. At 390 the alert sits below the last field, just above the button, so with the refused field scrolled off-screen above, the key is the person's only pointer to it.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 'every error shown with a next step'; WCAG 3.3.1 Error Identification and 3.3.3 Error Suggestion (the item must identify the field as the person knows it).
- **Best fix:** Let each Field, SelectField and TextAreaField register its name and visible label with the enclosing ActionForm through a small context, and have the alert name a refused field by that label (an explicit 'labels' prop still wins). Each alert item becomes a link that focuses its field, so on a phone the person can jump back up to it.
- **Notes:** Overlaps the words lens (the alert's wording, and 'marked below' pointing the wrong way, since the fields sit above it); this finding is the mechanism, which one component fixes for all forms.

### `narrow-other-13`: Hashes run off the screen, and dates and IDs split at their hyphens at 390 px ('2026-10-' / '26', 'SIM-' / '0001', 'SHA-' / '256')

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `console.rule-releases.default.390`, `console.rule-releases.release.390`, `console.rule-releases.notes-open.390`, `console.rule-releases.prepared.390`, `console.compliance.default.390`, `console.compliance.sent.390`, `console.organizations.default.390`, `console.organization.default.390`, `console.organization.firm.390`, `console.organization.suspended.390`, `console.terms.default.390`, `console.terms.error.390`, `console.support.default.390`, `console.prospects.default.390`, `console.tickets.default.390`, `settings.billing-org.default.390`, `terms.default.390`, `terms.internal.390`, `console.practice.error.390`, `console.prospects.empty.390`
- **Routes:** `/console/rule-releases`, `/console/compliance`, `/console/organizations`, `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`, `/console/terms`, `/console/support`, `/console/prospects`, `/console/tickets`, `/settings/billing/[organizationId]`, `/terms`, `/console/practices/[practiceId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/format.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/console/rule-releases/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/compliance/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/terms/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/terms/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/settings/billing/[organizationId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Rule releases: the 64-character manifest hash runs out of the Release 900001 card and past the viewport (app/console/rule-releases/page.tsx:101, a mono span with no break rule), one cause of that page's 502 px width. Compliance Policies: the mono paths sit in cells with no horizontal padding (app/console/compliance/page.tsx:163-165, 'py-1' only), so 'management' runs into 'management.md'. Dates split at hyphens in table cells: '2026-10-' / '26' (console/organizations/page.tsx:72), '2026-' / '10-26' / '01:31' / 'UTC' (support grants, prospects, tickets, agreements, grants); 'synthetic-' / '2026-10' (console terms); 'SIM-' / '0001' (invoices); 'SHA-' / '256' (terms.default.390); '27601-' / '1234' (practice ZIP alert); '/request-' / 'audit' (prospects empty state). Truncated hashes keep the full value only in a title attribute (console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx:257, console/compliance/page.tsx:215, console/terms/page.tsx:49, terms/page.tsx:37), which a touch screen never shows.
- **Rule:** .claude/rules/brand.md (every code in the mono face, read as one token); docs/adr/0056:51-53 (no page scrolls sideways); WCAG 1.4.10 Reflow.
- **Best fix:** Render dates and date-times through one <When> element (a <time> with whitespace-nowrap, from components/format.ts's utc/utcDate) and short IDs and codes ('SHA-256', invoice numbers, versions, ZIP+4, paths in prose) with whitespace-nowrap. Shorten every hash to its first 12 characters plus an ellipsis, with a 'Copy' button that copies the whole value (the manifest included), and use break-all wherever a full hash must show. Give the compliance policy cells pr-3.
- **Notes:** The earlier tour's 'ROI statement's SHA-256 does not wrap at 390' is the same cause on a screen outside this set; fold both into one change.

### `narrow-other-14`: An incident opened with no component ticked is refused with Zod's raw 'Invalid input: expected array, received undefined'

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `console.status.error.390`
- **Routes:** `/console/status`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/routes/support.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/onboarding-routes.db.test.ts`
- **Evidence:** console.status.error.390 shows 'components: Invalid input: expected array, received undefined'. apps/web/src/lib/routes/support.ts:392-395 preprocesses the components field: an array stays an array and a string is split, but with no box ticked the field is missing, so undefined passes through and z.array fails with Zod's default text before the written '.min(1, "choose at least one component")' can run.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 'every error shown with a next step'.
- **Best fix:** In the preprocess, map a missing value to [] (value === undefined ? [] : …) so the route's own 'choose at least one component' message shows, and add the empty case to the route's test (lib/routes/onboarding-routes.db.test.ts:504-507 already covers the other inputs).
- **Notes:** On this screen the title and first update are also wiped (narrow-other-01), so a single missed tick costs the operator the whole incident text.

### `narrow-other-15`: The console practice settings title wraps to four lines at 390 px

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `console.practice.default.390`, `console.practice.empty.390`, `console.practice.error.390`, `console.practice.location-saved.390`, `console.practice.saved.390`
- **Routes:** `/console/practices/[practiceId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/console/practices/[practiceId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** 'SYNTHETIC OTHER PRACTICE LAYOUT: pricing and triage settings' and 'SYNTHETIC PHYSICAL THERAPY NORTH: pricing and triage settings' each take four lines of the 32 px title, about 150 px of an 844 px screen, before any content. Cause: app/console/practices/[practiceId]/page.tsx:48 puts the practice's name and the page's name in one h1; the back link above it says only 'Organization' (:45). The other long titles in the set stop at two or three lines (organization names, 'Notification preferences', 'We are doing scheduled maintenance').
- **Rule:** docs/adr/0056:48 fixes the page title at 32 px/700 and is silent on wrapping at phone width: recommendation.
- **Best fix:** Make the h1 'Pricing and triage settings' and put the practice's name on the eyebrow line above it, with the back link naming the organization instead of the bare word 'Organization' (a breadcrumb: the organization's name › the practice's name). The document title keeps both names. Keep the 32 px scale.

### `narrow-other-16`: Success notices live in the URL, so a refresh, Back, or a saved link shows them again ('Invitation sent' over 'No invitations yet')

- **Flags:** polish; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `settings.users.notice-invited.390`, `account.notifications.saved.390`, `console.practice.saved.390`, `console.practice.location-saved.390`, `console.models.notice.390`, `console.organization.created.390`, `console.rule-releases.prepared.390`, `console.screening.approved.390`, `console.compliance.sent.390`, `support.ticket-opened.390`
- **Routes:** `/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users`, `/account/notifications`, `/console/practices/[practiceId]`, `/console/models`, `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`, `/console/rule-releases`, `/console/screening`, `/console/compliance`, `/support`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/account/notifications/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/practices/[practiceId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/models/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/rule-releases/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/screening/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/compliance/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/support/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Each notice is drawn from a query parameter: ?notice=invited (users/page.tsx:40-43), ?saved=1 (account/notifications/page.tsx:31-33; console/practices/[practiceId]/page.tsx:51), ?location=saved (:64), ?notice=set (console/models/page.tsx:32), ?created=<practice> (console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx:95-97), ?prepared=, ?approved=1, ?sent=. settings.users.notice-invited.390, opened from the URL alone, says 'Done: Invitation sent. It lasts seven days.' above 'No invitations yet.' A refresh, Back or Forward through history, or a bookmark replays a confirmation for an action that did not just happen.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 'no screen depends on client-side state that a refresh loses' (here the URL keeps a state that should not survive the refresh); otherwise silent: recommendation.
- **Best fix:** Carry each confirmation in a one-time, HttpOnly flash cookie (an opaque notice code, never PHI) that the action sets with its redirect and the next render reads and clears, so it shows once, works without JavaScript, and leaves clean URLs. Keep URL state for what a person should be able to refresh or share: filters, and the public screening's and audit request's 'sent' pages.
- **Notes:** touches42 via users/page.tsx. What a refresh loses is otherwise sound in this set: filters are in the URL (?status=, ?all=1), and the screening's chosen file is lost by design (it never leaves the browser).

### `narrow-other-17`: The phone Menu looks the same open as closed, has no backdrop, stays open on Escape or a tap outside, and never marks 'Help and support' as current

- **Flags:** polish; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `home.menu.390`, `console.organization.menu.390`, `console.support.menu.390`, `support.menu.390`, `terms.menu.390`, `home.account-menu.390`, `support.account-menu.390`, `terms.account-menu.390`
- **Routes:** `/`, `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`, `/console/support`, `/support`, `/terms`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`
- **Evidence:** In the menu shots the 'Menu' button is unchanged while the panel is open, and the panel lies over the page with no backdrop, the form and cards showing straight beneath its bottom edge (support.menu.390, console.organization.menu.390). The Menu and the account menu are separate <details> (components/shell.tsx:83 and :322-327), so both can be open at once, and neither closes on Escape or on a tap outside. On /support no item is marked current, because the rail link is hard-coded active={false} (shell.tsx:291). The panel's height is capped at 100vh-3.5rem (:324), which on iOS Safari runs under the bottom toolbar.
- **Rule:** docs/adr/0056:46 ('below the md breakpoint the rail folds into the header's Menu') and BRAND.md section 7 (one brass underline marks the active item); silent on open state and dismissal: recommendation.
- **Best fix:** Style the open summary as pressed and label it 'Close menu' while open; add a backdrop below the panel that closes it on tap; add a small client enhancement that closes either popover on Escape, on an outside tap, or when the other opens; mark 'Help and support' current when the page is /support; cap the panel with 100dvh.
- **Notes:** touches42 via components/shell.tsx. Menu items themselves are 32 px tall rows, which is fine.

### `narrow-other-18`: Touch targets meet WCAG 2.2's 24 px minimum but not a comfortable phone size: text controls are about 20 px tall and buttons and fields 32-36 px

- **Flags:** polish; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `home.default.390`, `console.prospects.default.390`, `console.prospects.empty.390`, `console.tickets.default.390`, `console.tickets.empty.390`, `console.specialist-queue.default.390`, `console.organizations.default.390`, `sign-in.default.390`, `request-audit.default.390`, `account.devices.default.390`
- **Routes:** `/`, `/console/prospects`, `/console/tickets`, `/console/specialist-queue`, `/console/organizations`, `/sign-in`, `/request-audit`, `/account/devices`
- **Files:** `packages/ui/src/components/button.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/components/input.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/field.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/prospects/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/tickets/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Measured at 390: 'Help with this screen' is a bare text-sm summary about 20 px tall (components/shell.tsx:336); the New / Contacted / Converted / Declined / All filters are 20 px text links 12 px apart (console/prospects/page.tsx:40-42, console/tickets/page.tsx:36-38); table links ('Open the practice's abstraction', 'Regulations', 'Ledger corrections') are 20 px; buttons are 36 px (packages/ui/src/components/button.tsx:22), 'sm' buttons such as Sign out 32 px (:23), and inputs and selects 36 px (input.tsx:11, field.tsx:75). Each passes 2.5.8 by size or by its spacing.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.2 2.5.8 Target Size (Minimum) is met. The brand and PRD say nothing beyond it; 2.5.5 (AAA, 44 px) and the platforms' 44-48 px guidance are recommendations.
- **Best fix:** Under (pointer: coarse), raise buttons, inputs and selects to 44 px, render the status filters as a segmented control of 44 px tabs (keeping aria-current), and pad the help summary and the stacked table links to 44 px rows. The desktop's dense sizes stay unchanged.
- **Notes:** touches42 via components/shell.tsx (the help summary) only; the rest can land first.

### `narrow-other-19`: Filtered empty lists don't name the filter or offer a way back, and the home empty state gives a support engineer nothing to tap

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `console.tickets.empty.390`, `console.prospects.empty.390`, `home.default.390`
- **Routes:** `/console/tickets`, `/console/prospects`, `/`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/console/tickets/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/prospects/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** console.tickets.empty.390 ('answered' filter) says 'No tickets here. Tickets opened from any screen's help appear here.' (console/tickets/page.tsx:44); console.prospects.empty.390 ('contacted') says 'No requests here. Requests from the public form at /request-audit appear here.' (console/prospects/page.tsx:48). Neither says the list is empty only under this filter, nor offers 'All'. home.default.390, the support engineer's only page, ends with 'Ask your administrator' (app/page.tsx:29-32) and no link, though this person's next step is the Support tickets queue in their own Menu.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:265 'every screen has an empty state that says what to do next'; packages/ui/src/states/empty-state.tsx:9 ('What to do next').
- **Best fix:** Name the filter and give the way out: 'No answered tickets' with 'Show all tickets (N open)' as the EmptyState action, and the same for audit requests. On home, give each internal role its action: for support, 'Open the support tickets' linking /console/tickets.
- **Notes:** The words lens may also take the copy; the missing action is the state's gap.

Status components: names wrap beside the select, the 'Operational' badge is squeezed under them, each row repeats a 'Status' label, and the 'Set' buttons end at x 370 against the border at 373 (console/status/page.tsx:60-72; the name cell has no right padding, :64). Billing status: organization names wrap to three lines (console/billing/page.tsx:40).

### `narrow-other-21`: On a phone the specialist queue's four count cards fill the first screen and push the practice list, the actual work, below it

- **Rule:** Silent: recommendation (PRD section 7: the specialist queue is the contract specialist's work list).

### `narrow-other-22`: Empty states inside cards keep 32 px of padding at 390 px and wrap into a narrow column

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `console.organization.empty-new-group.390`, `console.organization.empty.390`, `settings.billing-org.empty.390`, `settings.billing-org.error.390`

The dashed box has p-8 (packages/ui/src/states/empty-state.tsx:30) inside the card's own padding.

### `narrow-other-23`: Password fields have no 'Show password', so the phone's commonest refusal (the two passwords differ) is hard to avoid or fix

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `sign-in-new-password.default.390`, `sign-in-new-password.error-mismatch.390`, `sign-in-new-password.error-policy.390`, `sign-in.default.390`, `sign-in.error-wrong-password.390`
- **Routes:** `/sign-in/new-password`, `/sign-in`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/field.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/sign-in/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/sign-in/new-password/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The new password must be at least 12 characters with a lowercase and an uppercase letter, a digit and a symbol, typed twice, masked, on a phone keyboard (app/sign-in/new-password/page.tsx:28-29); sign-in's password is masked too (app/sign-in/page.tsx:28). error-mismatch and error-policy are the refusals a phone produces most, and both fields are cleared after each one.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.2 3.3.8 Accessible Authentication (Minimum) (AA) is met, since a password manager can fill the field; showing the password is the usual way to support it. Otherwise silent: recommendation.
- **Best fix:** Add a PasswordField with a 'Show password' toggle (a button with aria-pressed that switches the input's type, the text staying in the field), on sign-in and on both new-password fields; keep autocomplete as it is.

## narrow-practice-1

### `narrow-practice-1-01`: At 390 px the Contracts list, a contract's Versions and Payer and plan mapping scroll the whole page sideways: their tables have no scroller

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.contracts.default.390`, `p.contracts.help.390`, `p.contracts.layout.390`, `p.contracts.viewer.390`, `p.contract.default.390`, `p.contract.layout.390`, `p.contract.viewer.390`, `p.mapping.all-plans.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/contracts/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/mapping/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/coverage/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/models/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/scroll-table.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Measured image widths at a 390 px viewport: Contracts 664 px (default, help, viewer) and 666 px (layout); contract detail 439 px (default, viewer) and 541 px (layout, where the unbroken signer e-mail 'pricing-manager@synthetic.test,' widens the Signed off column); mapping All plans 589 px. In every one the sticky header stops at 390 px while the content runs on. Effective, Method, Status and Coverage share sit outside the Contracts card, the card's right border runs through the Billed figure '$2,400.00' on mapping, and Coverage share wraps one word per line. Cause: the tables are put straight into CardContent or a section, with no scroller: contracts/page.tsx:89-91 (Contracts) and :148 (Calendar), contracts/[contractId]/page.tsx:53 (Versions), mapping/page.tsx:107-109 (Payers and plans). The same bare pattern holds tables that fit today only because the synthetic rows are short: mapping/[planId]/page.tsx:101 and :202, coverage/page.tsx:195 and :236, lines/[lineId]/page.tsx:242, abstraction/page.tsx:100, components/models/display.tsx:86.
- **Rule:** docs/adr/0056-wear-the-paidwhole-brand-from-one-token-source-self-hosted-fonts-and-status-as-text-on-tint.md:52-53 ('At a phone's width no page scrolls sideways: a wide table scrolls inside its own card'); WCAG 2.1 SC 1.4.10 Reflow (the page, not only the table, scrolls in two dimensions).
- **Best fix:** Give the app one table wrapper and use it for every table: the existing ScrollTable (components/scroll-table.tsx:9-15), extended as in narrow-practice-1-03 and -04, with the table inside it. Long unbroken values in cells (e-mail addresses, plan keys) get overflow-wrap:anywhere so they wrap inside their column. Add a Playwright check, beside the 1280 px check the founder's tour fix added, that every route captured by the tour has document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= 390 at a 390 px viewport, so a new bare table fails CI.
- **Notes:** The founder's tour fix (L1 to L3) added the no-sideways-scroll check at 1280 px only; these pages fit at 1280 and break at 390, so this is not a re-raise. Same class as the earlier tour's '390 px home agreements table cut off', but a different failure: there the table scrolls inside its card; here the page scrolls.

### `narrow-practice-1-02`: The line trace's rule release manifest SHA-256 does not wrap, so every priced line's page scrolls sideways at 390 px

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.line.default.390`, `p.line.viewer.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/packets/display.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Both shots are 509 px wide at a 390 px viewport. In the Pricing card the Rule release value '900001 (manifest 6d346c8f...cdbce45a)' runs 119 px past the card and the viewport, and the sticky header stops at 390 px. The viewer, who sees no trace, gets the same overflow, so it is on every priced line for every role. The not-priced line (p.line.empty-not-priced.390) has no manifest and fits at 390 px. Cause: lines/[lineId]/page.tsx:109 prints the 64-character hash in a plain span ('font-mono text-xs'), with nothing that lets it break. The app already has a breaking hash, Hash in components/packets/display.tsx:73-82, and the home page's shortened form at page.tsx:456-458.
- **Rule:** docs/adr/0056-wear-the-paidwhole-brand-from-one-token-source-self-hosted-fonts-and-status-as-text-on-tint.md:52 (no page scrolls sideways at a phone's width); WCAG 2.1 SC 1.4.10 Reflow.
- **Best fix:** Move Hash out of components/packets into a shared component (components/hash.tsx) with two forms. In running text and definition lists it wraps (overflow-wrap:anywhere). In a table cell it shows the first 12 characters and an ellipsis, with a control that copies the full value and the full value as its accessible name (see narrow-practice-1-05). Use it on lines/[lineId]/page.tsx:109 and for every other SHA-256, mapping ID and rate-record ID on the page (:94, :100-102, :106).
- **Notes:** The same class as the earlier tour's 'ROI statement's SHA-256 does not wrap at 390 px'. This is a new instance on another screen, not a duplicate. One shared hash component fixes both. The line-trace page is in #38's scope only for the wording of its figures, not for this layout.

### `narrow-practice-1-03`: Eight wide tables scroll inside a bare overflow div that a keyboard cannot reach or scroll, and that no screen reader hears named

- **Screens:** `p.home.default.390`, `p.home.empty.390`, `p.home.error-last-run-failed.390`, `p.home.support-access.390`, `p.home.tiles.390`, `p.abstraction-ledger.default.390`, `p.abstraction-ledger.notice.390`, `p.abstraction-ledger.error-adjust-zero.390`, `p.abstraction-version.default.390`, `p.abstraction-version.error-amend.390`, `p.abstraction-version.model-notice.390`, `p.contract-version.default.390`, `p.contract-version.cited-page-2.390`, `p.contract-version.error-page-missing.390`, `p.contract-version.error-unresolved.390`, `p.contract-version.notice.390`, `p.anomalies.default.390`, `p.anomalies.cohort.390`, `p.anomalies.cohort-viewer.390`, `p.cases.default.390`, `p.cases.deadline-14.390`, `p.cases.error.390`, `p.cases.notice.390`, `p.packet.default.390`, `p.packet.approver-ready.390`, `p.packet.drafter.390`, `p.packet.error-export-blocked.390`, `p.packet.error-live-check.390`, `p.packet.notice-exported.390`, `p.packet.notice-requested.390`, `p.claims.default.390`, `p.claims.error.390`, `p.claims.patient.390`, `p.claims.viewer.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/anomalies`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/claims`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/scroll-table.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/contracts/review.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/anomalies/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/claims/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** At 390 px each of these tables is wider than its scroller, so its right-hand columns are hidden. The hidden columns: Agreements SHA-256 on home; Correct, the Adjust action, on Ledger entries; the approval times cut at '01:25 UT(' in a version's Fields; Observed allowed, Mode and Short of the mode in Flagged lines, and Mode, Range and Reviewed in Cohorts; Deadline, Pursuit cost and Owner in Cases; Files, the PDF and CSV downloads, in a packet's Versions and export history; Short, Paid, Patient share and Status in Claims and lines. The scrollers are bare divs with no tabindex, no role and no name: page.tsx:438, abstraction/ledger/page.tsx:48, components/contracts/review.tsx:240, anomalies/page.tsx:58 and :162, cases/page.tsx:246, cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx:546, claims/page.tsx:134. A keyboard user can scroll a column into view only by tabbing to a link inside it. Columns with no link (Deadline, Owner, Short, Status, Mode, SHA-256) can never be reached without a mouse or touch. The app's own ScrollTable (components/scroll-table.tsx:9-15: role=region, a name ending 'scrolls sideways', tabIndex 0, a focus ring) was written for exactly this case. Only the packet's Line schedule and Calculations, the pricing location table and the screening use it.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 2.1.1 Keyboard (axe rule scrollable-region-focusable, which apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts runs); SC 4.1.2 / 1.3.1 (the region has no name); docs/product/PRD.md:256 ('Accessibility (keyboard navigation ...) on every screen'); combined audit #103 (every route checked under WCAG 2.2's rules).
- **Best fix:** Replace every bare overflow-x-auto table wrapper with ScrollTable, keeping 'relative' on it as ADR 0056:52-53 asks, so the visually hidden header stays inside. Then add the 390 px viewport to #103's generated per-route axe check: scrollable-region-focusable only fires when the region actually overflows, and at 1280 these tables fit.
- **Notes:** Confirms and widens the earlier tour's '390 px practice home agreements table is cut off' item: home is one of eight tables with the same cause. The visible cue and the columns a person needs are a separate problem (narrow-practice-1-04). The line trace's JSON blocks have the same defect, inside #38's scope (narrow-practice-1-06). Blocking because a keyboard-only user at phone width, or at 400% zoom on a desktop, cannot read a case's deadline or owner, or a line's status, at all.

### `narrow-practice-1-04`: At 390 px a table's hidden columns give no visible sign they exist, and on the working lists they are what the person came for: a case's deadline and owner, a line's shortfall and status, the ledger's Adjust

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.cases.default.390`, `p.cases.deadline-14.390`, `p.cases.error.390`, `p.cases.notice.390`, `p.claims.default.390`, `p.claims.error.390`, `p.claims.patient.390`, `p.claims.viewer.390`, `p.abstraction-ledger.default.390`, `p.abstraction-ledger.notice.390`, `p.abstraction-ledger.error-adjust-zero.390`, `p.anomalies.default.390`, `p.anomalies.cohort.390`, `p.anomalies.cohort-viewer.390`, `p.packet.default.390`, `p.packet.approver-ready.390`, `p.packet.drafter.390`, `p.packet.error-export-blocked.390`, `p.packet.error-live-check.390`, `p.packet.notice-exported.390`, `p.packet.notice-requested.390`, `p.packet-review.default.390`, `p.packet-review.error-needs-downloads.390`, `p.packet-review.notice.390`, `p.home.default.390`, `p.home.empty.390`, `p.abstraction-version.default.390`, `p.contract-version.default.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases`, `/p/[practiceId]/claims`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]/anomalies`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review`, `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/versions/[versionId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/scroll-table.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/claims/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/anomalies/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/packets/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/globals.css`
- **Evidence:** Every cut-off table ends flush at the card edge, mid-word or mid-figure, with no fade, arrow or words to say more lies to the right: 'Actiona... total' on Cases, units '1.0(' on Claims, 'Written by' cut to 'membe' and 'reconcil' on Ledger entries, 'Coh...' on Cohorts, Code '97:' on the packet's Line schedule, patient share '$14.0(' on Calculation per line, '01:25 UT(' on a version's Fields. The ScrollTable tables (Line schedule and Calculations) are no better; only their keyboard access differs. Three of these are a person's working lists, and at 390 px they show what the person did not come for. (1) Cases (cases/page.tsx:246-287) shows Payer and plan, Pattern, State, Lines and Actionable total; Deadline, Pursuit cost and Owner are off screen. On p.cases.deadline-14.390 the list is filtered to 'Within 14 days (or passed)', the dashboard's deadline deep link, and the deadline column itself cannot be seen. (2) Claims and lines (claims/page.tsx:136-154) shows patient, service date and code, so the brand's comparison stops after Expected allowed. On the viewer's shot Observed allowed is the last column; Short, Paid, Patient share and Status are hidden. (3) Ledger entries (abstraction/ledger/page.tsx:50-58) puts its only action, Correct > Adjust, in the last column, entirely past the edge. The case page's lines, by contrast, already reflow into stacked cards (cases/[caseId]/page.tsx:160-172) and read well at 390 px (p.case.default.390).
- **Rule:** docs/brand/BRAND.md:123-124 and .claude/rules/brand.md (expected, paid and short appear together, in that order); docs/product/PRD.md:270 and :277 (Claims and lines shows status; the case list shows deadline and owner, 'Deadline colors'); ADR 0056:52 sets the scroller but is silent on which columns must stay in view and on a scroll cue, so this recommends one.
- **Best fix:** Three parts, in one table PR with narrow-practice-1-01 and -03. (a) ScrollTable shows when it overflows: CSS-only edge shadows (the background-attachment:local technique, written as classes so the CSP holds; shadows on content, not on the mark, so brand.md allows them), plus its visible caption 'Scroll sideways for more columns', shown only while scrollWidth exceeds clientWidth. (b) The row's naming cell (payer and plan, claim, entry number) is position:sticky at the left, so a person scrolling knows which row the far columns belong to. (c) Below the sm breakpoint the four working lists (Cases, Claims and lines, Ledger entries, the anomaly's Flagged lines) render each row as a stacked card, as the case page's lines already do. The naming link comes first. Expected, observed and short follow in the brand's order, with the status chip, deadline chip and owner. The row's action (Adjust) comes last, full width. The table stays the layout from sm up. Reference tables (Agreements, Fields, Versions) keep (a) and (b).
- **Notes:** Confirms the earlier tour's 'practice home's agreements table cut off at 390 px' and generalises it; do not raise that item twice. The packet and review pages are #38's screens, but #38 is about explaining figures in words, not table layout, so this is not in flight. Coordinate with #38, which will rewrite the Calculation per line cells. Combined audit #107 (payer column, reasons in words) adds columns to Claims and lines, so the narrow layout should land with or before it.

### `narrow-practice-1-05`: A full SHA-256 set to break anywhere inside a table cell squeezes its column to a character or two, leaving rows hundreds of pixels tall and mostly empty on the packet pages

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.packet.default.390`, `p.packet.approver-ready.390`, `p.packet.drafter.390`, `p.packet.error-export-blocked.390`, `p.packet.error-live-check.390`, `p.packet.notice-exported.390`, `p.packet.notice-requested.390`, `p.packet-review.default.390`, `p.packet-review.error-needs-downloads.390`, `p.packet-review.notice.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/packets/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** On p.packet.default.390, in Versions and export history, the Version 3 row is about 500 px tall, and on screen it shows only 'Version 3 (current)', the written line and 'Passed'. The rest is white space made by the off-screen 'Exported, SHA-256' column. In Calculation per line, each row is 250 to 300 px tall with half of it empty, for the same reason (the Trace SHA-256 column). Cause: Hash (components/packets/display.tsx:73-82) renders the whole 64-character value as <code class='break-all'>. In an auto-layout table, break-all lets the column's minimum width drop to one character, so the hash stacks into a tall thin column: packet/page.tsx:584 and :587 (PDF and CSV in the Versions table), display.tsx:324 (trace). Hash's own comment says 'shortened on screen with the whole value as its title', but it is never shortened. The home page's Agreements cell does shorten it (page.tsx:456-458: the first 12 characters and an ellipsis, with the value as title).
- **Rule:** docs/adr/0056-wear-the-paidwhole-brand-from-one-token-source-self-hosted-fonts-and-status-as-text-on-tint.md:52 (a wide table scrolls inside its card and stays readable); the component's own contract (display.tsx:73); the brand rules are silent on hash display, so this recommends one.
- **Best fix:** The shared hash component of narrow-practice-1-02, in its table-cell form: the first 12 hex characters and an ellipsis in mono, whitespace-nowrap. Beside it, a 'Copy' button (at least 24 by 24 px) that copies the whole value, with the whole value in the button's accessible name, since title alone is unreachable by touch and keyboard. The full value stays in data-sha256 for tests. Running-text uses (the review's Exported files, the Attestation sentence, the submissions log) keep the wrapping form.
- **Notes:** #38 (every figure explained in words) will rewrite the Calculation per line cells; land this with it or before it and tell that PR. Fixing this does not bring back the Files column (PDF and CSV links) at 390 px. That is narrow-practice-1-04.

### `narrow-practice-1-06`: The line trace's recorded steps are raw JSON in scroll boxes that clip at 390 px and that a keyboard cannot scroll

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; in flight: #38
- **Screens:** `p.line.default.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** In the Trace section (rule release and engine, practice settings, claim facts, line facts, steps 1 to 8), every step is a grey <pre> of JSON cut at the box's edge: '"manifest_sha256": "6d346c8fa596c0f720ff317', '"payer_plan_key": "6bf0abf9-c434-439e-aba2-', '"service_line_id": "5ddd75e0-5d0d-4d5a-b850'. Nothing shows that the lines go on. Cause: lines/[lineId]/page.tsx:157, <pre className='mt-1 overflow-x-auto ...'>{item.recorded}</pre>, which has no tabindex, no role and no name. So a keyboard user cannot scroll it (there is nothing focusable inside), and a screen-reader user hears raw JSON punctuation.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 2.1.1 Keyboard (axe scrollable-region-focusable); docs/product/PRD.md:256 (accessibility on every screen); combined audit #38 (every figure explained in words on the line-trace pages).
- **Best fix:** Fold into #38. Where the trace is shown as words, as #38 plans, each step's recorded inputs become a definition list (term over value at 390 px, as ADR 0056:53 asks), with hashes in the shared wrapping hash component. Wherever the raw record stays as an 'as recorded' disclosure for auditors, the <pre> wraps (white-space:pre-wrap; overflow-wrap:anywhere), so it never needs a sideways scroll.
- **Notes:** Kept apart from narrow-practice-1-03 so that it goes into #38's PR, which is already rewriting this page's trace.

### `narrow-practice-1-07`: A refused submit wipes the form: React 19 resets every uncontrolled field after the action, so typed values vanish and selects jump back to their first option

- **Flags:** blocking; size S
- **Screens:** `p.claims.error.390`, `p.case.error.390`, `p.submissions.error.390`, `p.abstraction-ledger.error-adjust-zero.390`, `p.abstraction-version.error-amend.390`, `p.plan.error.390`, `p.cases.error.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/claims`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx`, `apps/web/e2e/action-form.spec.ts`
- **Evidence:** What the capture typed (tour.spec.ts), and what each 390 px shot shows after the refusal. Claims: 'SYNNOMATCH0001' in Payer claim control number; the field is empty and marked red. Case write-off: reason 'Other', box ticked; the Reason select reads 'Window passed' and the box is unticked, on a line whose window is open until 2036-04-05. Submissions: response 'Partially accepted'; it reads 'Accepted', so fixing the amount and submitting again records the wrong payer response. Ledger adjust: 0.00 plus 'SYNTHETIC CHECK' plus ticked; all three are cleared. Amendment: effective from 2026-01-01; the field reads mm/dd/yyyy under an error about that date. Plan mapping: effective from 2026-01-01; it is empty. Materiality: 'abc' per line; the field shows the saved '5.00', next to an error saying 'dollars and cents, such as 5.00'. Cause: ActionForm (components/action-form.tsx:32 and :65) passes useActionState's formAction as <form action>. React 19.3.1 (pnpm-workspace.yaml:20) resets a form's uncontrolled fields when a function action's transition ends, whatever the action returned. The pages' fields are uncontrolled, some with a defaultValue: cases/[caseId]/page.tsx:208-221, submissions/page.tsx:307, cases/page.tsx:177, abstraction/versions/[versionId]/page.tsx:421, mapping/[planId]/page.tsx:178, claims/page.tsx:110, abstraction/ledger/page.tsx:31-38.
