ActionForm's comment, 'On success the action redirects, so a fresh page replaces the form', assumes the form survives a refusal. It does not.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 ('every error shown with a next step'; a next step that needs the person to retype everything, and that silently changes their choices, is not one); WCAG 2.1 SC 3.3.1 and 3.3.3 (an error identified and corrected against the person's own input); the brief's error-state judgment ('is the person's input kept after a refused submit?').
- **Best fix:** Fix it once, in ActionForm. Keep action={formAction} so the form still posts without JavaScript, and add onSubmit={(event) => { event.preventDefault(); const data = new FormData(event.currentTarget); startTransition(() => formAction(data)); }}. A form action dispatched by hand is not followed by React's automatic reset, so every field keeps what the person typed or chose, on every form, with no per-page change. Add an e2e assertion to one refusal per form family (claims lookup, write-off, payer response, ledger adjust, amendment, mapping, materiality) that the refused value is still in the field and the select still holds the refused choice. On success the action still redirects, so nothing stale survives.
- **Notes:** Not width-specific: the 1280 shots show the same thing (the image checkers noted it on each). One fix covers every ActionForm in the app, including the console and settings. The write-off form opens on 'Window passed' for a line whose window is open, a flows and words matter in its own right; the reset makes it worse, because a refusal silently puts that wrong reason back. File apps/web/e2e/action-form.spec.ts is proposed (new).

### `narrow-practice-1-08`: The refusal summary says 'Check the fields marked below' from beneath the fields, names each field by its code key, and puts no message under the field itself

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.claims.error.390`, `p.case.error.390`, `p.submissions.error.390`, `p.abstraction-ledger.error-adjust-zero.390`, `p.abstraction-version.error-amend.390`, `p.plan.error.390`, `p.cases.error.390`, `p.abstraction.error-tag.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/claims`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts`
- **Evidence:** Every refusal shot shows the 'Not done' box after the last field and just above the button, with the text 'Check the fields marked below.', yet the marked field is always above it. The issue lines name raw keys: 'payerClaimControlNumber:', 'text:', 'amount:', 'effectiveFrom:', 'contractVersionId:', 'lineMateriality:', 'title:'. At 390 px the gap matters. On p.submissions.error.390 the marked field (Amount accepted) is about 330 px above the summary. In a ledger row the summary is squeezed into a 150 px column. Cause: the sentence is fixed in lib/http/next.ts:162 and :180. ActionForm renders the summary after {children} (components/action-form.tsx:66-84) and labels issues with labels[issue.field] ?? issue.field (:76), and only one page in the app passes labels (settings/pricing/page.tsx). Only Field (components/field.tsx) can print an error under its control, and no ActionForm feeds it.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 ('every error shown with a next step'); WCAG 2.1 SC 3.3.1 Error Identification and 3.3.3 Error Suggestion (the field is named in words the person can see); .claude/rules/brand.md copy rules and CO-11's plain product copy.
- **Best fix:** In ActionForm: place the summary at the top of the form, before the first field, so 'below' is true and a screen reader meets it first after focus. Name each issue by the label text of the control it marks, read from the DOM (the <label for> of form.elements.namedItem(issue.field)), so no page has to pass labels and a key can never reach the screen. Also render each issue's message directly under its control (a <p id> tied by aria-describedby, as Field's FieldError does), so on a phone the reason sits where the person is looking. Change next.ts's sentence to 'Check the fields marked in red.'
- **Notes:** The words reviewer may raise the raw keys too. Keep one finding when merging. Land with narrow-practice-1-07 (same component).

### `narrow-practice-1-09`: A refused letter download replaces the Coverage screen with raw JSON, laid out 980 px wide on a phone, with no frame, no words and no way back

- **Flags:** blocking; size M
- **Screens:** `p.coverage.error-letter-codes.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/coverage`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/coverage/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/http/handler.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/coverage.ts`
- **Evidence:** Typing 'ABC' into Procedure codes and pressing 'Download the letter' navigates to /coverage/letter?payer=...&codes=ABC. The page that comes back is Chrome's JSON viewer with {"status":"invalid","issues":[{"field":"codes","message":"procedure codes of five letters or digits, separated by commas"}]}. The capture is 980 x 2121 at a 390 px viewport, because a JSON response has no viewport meta, so the phone lays it out at 980 px with tiny unwrapped text. There is no header, no Menu and no link back to the form, and the person's codes are gone unless they use the browser's Back. Cause: the form is a GET straight to a download route (coverage/page.tsx:347-385), and downloadHandler answers any refusal with the JSON outcome (lib/http/handler.ts:272-273). The input has no pattern to catch the mistake first (coverage/page.tsx:379).
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 ('every error shown with a next step') and :260 ('an error state with a next step'); WCAG 2.1 SC 3.3.1 Error Identification and 1.4.10 Reflow; .claude/rules/brand.md (a screen in the product's frame and words).
- **Best fix:** Keep the GET form, and make its refusal come back to the form. (1) downloadHandler (lib/http/handler.ts:272-273): when the request's Accept prefers text/html (a navigation, not a fetch), it answers an 'invalid' outcome with 303 to the route's page, /p/<id>/coverage?letter=refused&payer=..&contract=..&codes=ABC#letter, carrying the submitted values and the refused field names, never PHI, which these fields cannot hold. (2) The Coverage page reads those values back into the letter form as defaultValue and shows each issue under its field, inside the app frame. So the person's input is kept, a refresh keeps it, and the next step is on screen. (3) The codes input also gets the route's rule as its pattern, with a hint naming the format, so most mistakes never leave the page. Applying (1) in downloadHandler protects every download link in the app (packet PDF and CSV, reports, exports), not only this one.
- **Notes:** At 1280 the same raw JSON appears (the image checkers flagged it there). The phone makes it worse. Blocking because the person sees no human words and no way forward on the screen itself.

### `narrow-practice-1-10`: Loading drops the whole app frame and names nothing: the header and Menu vanish, and 14 different screens load as the same picture of six grey bars

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.abstraction.loading.390`, `p.abstraction-ledger.loading.390`, `p.abstraction-regulations.loading.390`, `p.anomalies.loading.390`, `p.cases.loading.390`, `p.case.loading.390`, `p.packet.loading.390`, `p.packet-review.loading.390`, `p.submissions.loading.390`, `p.claims.loading.390`, `p.contracts.loading.390`, `p.contract.loading.390`, `p.contract-version.loading.390`, `p.coverage.loading.390`, `p.digest.loading.390`, `p.line.loading.390`, `p.mapping.loading.390`, `p.plan.loading.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/regulations`, `/p/[practiceId]/anomalies`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions`, `/p/[practiceId]/claims`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/coverage`, `/p/[practiceId]/digests/[digestId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]`, `/p/[practiceId]`
- **Files:** `packages/ui/src/states/loading-state.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/loading.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/loading.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/claims/loading.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/contracts/loading.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/coverage/loading.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/mapping/loading.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Each loading shot is a white page holding the mark, a progress bar and grey rows, with no header, no practice switcher, no account and no Menu. Of the 18 loading shots in this set, 14 are byte-identical (md5 2fa1de7c... for abstraction, anomalies, case, cases, claims, contract, contract-version, contracts, coverage, line, mapping, packet-review, plan, submissions) and 3 more are identical to each other (abstraction-ledger, abstraction-regulations, digest). Only the packet differs, by having eight bars. Nothing on screen says what is loading: LoadingState puts its label in a sr-only span (packages/ui/src/states/loading-state.tsx:27). The 33 practice loading.tsx files (e.g. cases/loading.tsx:4-9) each render <main className='mx-auto flex max-w-5xl flex-col gap-4 p-8'>, a 32 px gutter against the page's 16 px (shell.tsx:334), so the content jumps sideways when the page arrives. The shell vanishes because AppShell is drawn by each page from its own route result (shell.tsx:13-19, 304), and there is no p/[practiceId]/layout.tsx. The practice home has no loading.tsx at all (route map: 'page.route delay just leaves a blank navigation'), so a slow dashboard shows the previous screen with no sign anything is happening.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:260 (every screen has a loading state); docs/brand/BRAND.md:127-128 and ADR 0056:42-43 (the mark alone and a plain bar: kept); WCAG 2.1 SC 4.1.3 Status Messages and 3.2.3 Consistent Navigation (the navigation disappears and returns on every move); the brief's 'is there an app frame, and does the screen say what is loading?'. The brand is silent on a visible loading label; this recommends one.
- **Best fix:** One PracticeLoading component, used by every practice loading.tsx and added as p/[practiceId]/loading.tsx for the home. (1) It draws the same header band as AppShell: the lockup, plus inert placeholders the size of the switcher, the account and the Menu, so the frame never blinks. Extract the header markup from shell.tsx into a shared ShellHeader so the two cannot drift. The rail from md up gets the same treatment. It keeps the per-request rule of shell.tsx:13-19, because no practice list or role is rendered while loading. (2) It shows a visible heading in the page-title style, 'Loading cases…' (the label each file already passes), beside the still mark and bar, and keeps the sr-only status. (3) It uses the page's own gutters (px-4 py-6 md:p-8) and a skeleton shaped like the page (cards for a dashboard or detail, a table outline for a list) through a 'shape' prop, not only a row count.
- **Notes:** touches42: the shared header comes out of components/shell.tsx, which PR #42 changes, so this lands after #42 merges. All 33 practice loading.tsx files change (the files list names a sample). The console and settings loading files have the same pattern and belong in the same PR. A p/[practiceId]/layout.tsx that renders the real shell was considered and rejected: shell.tsx:13-19 records that the shell comes from each request's route result, never a cached layout, so the practice switcher lists only practices the actor reaches now.

### `narrow-practice-1-11`: The ledger correction form opens inside the table's last cell: at 390 px it is about 180 px wide, and filling it scrolls away which entry and amount it corrects

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.abstraction-ledger.error-adjust-zero.390`, `p.abstraction-ledger.default.390`, `p.abstraction-ledger.notice.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** On p.abstraction-ledger.error-adjust-zero.390 the Adjust form opened in the 'Correct' column. Its labels wrap ('Change in dollars (a minus sign lowers it)' over two lines), its 120-word consequence runs to about 30 lines in a column about 180 px wide, and the 'Not done' summary breaks every two or three words. Focusing the amount field scrolled the table sideways, so '#', 'Money moved', 'Stage' and the Amount ('00') are off the left edge. The specialist corrects a ledger amount without seeing the entry's number, date, stage or current amount. On the default and notice shots the 'Adjust' summary itself is past the right edge, as narrow-practice-1-04 says. Cause: AdjustForm, a <details> holding an ActionForm (abstraction/ledger/page.tsx:20-43), is rendered inside the row's last <td> (:75). Its summary is 12 px text (:23).
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (accessibility on every screen) and the brief's 'inputs wider than their card' and 'clipped columns that hide what a person needs'; WCAG 2.1 SC 1.3.2 Meaningful Sequence and 3.3.2 Labels or Instructions (the form must show what it acts on). The brand rules are silent on in-table forms; this recommends one.
- **Best fix:** Take the form out of the cell. The row keeps a full-size 'Adjust entry N' button (at least 24 px tall; text-sm with padding), and opening it shows the form in a full-width row directly under the entry (<tr><td colSpan={7}>), or in the entry's stacked card below sm (narrow-practice-1-04). The form opens with one line naming its target: 'Entry 7, Recouped, 2026-08-03, −$18.00, the reconcile job'. Its fields are max-w-md and full width on a phone. The same applies to the Shortfalls list's entries (:137).
- **Notes:** This is the specialist's ledger-correction screen, which changes money. Land it with narrow-practice-1-04's stacked rows and -07's input keeping.

### `narrow-practice-1-12`: At 390 px the header cuts the current practice's name to about nine characters, and on every screen but home nothing else names the practice a person is working in

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.claims.default.390`, `p.cases.default.390`, `p.case.default.390`, `p.packet.default.390`, `p.contracts.layout.390`, `p.coverage.default.390`, `p.mapping.all-plans.390`, `p.abstraction-ledger.default.390`, `p.home.default.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/claims`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`, `/p/[practiceId]/coverage`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Every 390 px shot's header reads 'PaidWhole | SYNTHETI... v | P | Menu'. The header has no room left (switcher about 115 px, avatar, Menu). Claims, Cases, a case, a packet, Contracts, Coverage and Mapping carry no practice name in their headings either; only the home page's H1 does. A billing firm or group user who works several practices (PRD:260, 'a practice switcher for groups and firms') cannot tell on a phone which practice's claims, cases or packet approval is in front of them. Real names that share a first word ('Riverside Physical...', 'Riverside Sports...') truncate to the same text. The switcher's accessible name is the fixed aria-label 'Practice switcher', which replaces its visible text, so a screen reader never hears the current practice either. Cause: shell.tsx:56-57 (aria-label on the summary; <span className='max-w-[5rem] truncate sm:max-w-[14rem]'>).
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:260 (one practice at a time, with a practice switcher for groups and firms); WCAG 2.1 SC 2.5.3 Label in Name (the visible text is not in the accessible name) and 2.4.6 Headings and Labels; docs/brand/BRAND.md:120-121 (practice switcher right of the lockup: kept). The brand is silent on the narrow header; this recommends one.
- **Best fix:** Below the sm breakpoint, give the sticky header a second, 32 px row under the lockup row that shows the current practice's full name, truncating only at the full width with the whole name as title. That row is the switcher's <summary>, so tapping the name opens the list; the first row keeps the lockup, the account and Menu. From sm up the layout is unchanged. Name the summary 'Practice: <name>, switch practice' (aria-label containing the visible text), or drop the aria-label and add a visually hidden 'Practice:' prefix. Add scroll-padding for the taller header (narrow-practice-1-13).
- **Notes:** touches42: components/shell.tsx is in PR #42, so this lands after #42 merges. The synthetic names all begin 'SYNTHETIC', which makes the problem obvious in the shots. Real names hit it too at nine characters.

### `narrow-practice-1-13`: The sticky 56 px header can cover the focused control: no scroll padding is set, so Shift+Tab, a refused form's focus and fragment links can land under it

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.claims.error.390`, `p.case.error.390`, `p.abstraction-ledger.error-adjust-zero.390`, `p.packet.default.390`, `p.case.default.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/claims`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/brand.css`, `apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts`
- **Evidence:** The header is sticky top-0 h-14 z-30 with an opaque background (components/shell.tsx:315). No rule anywhere reserves its height when the browser scrolls something into view. There is no scroll-padding-top on html (apps/web/src/app/brand.css:6-13) and no scroll-margin on targets (searched apps/web/src for scroll-padding, scroll-mt and scroll-margin: none). So when focus moves upward (Shift+Tab through the long 390 px forms, 7,000 to 7,900 px on the packet pages), the browser scrolls the control only to the viewport's top edge, under the header. The same happens when ActionForm moves focus to the first refused control (components/action-form.tsx:52) above the viewport, and when an in-page link targets an id (the case page's #notes card, cases/[caseId]/page.tsx:245). The full-page captures cannot show the sticky overlap. This finding rests on the code, and the fix needs the keyboard test #103 asks for.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.2 SC 2.4.11 Focus Not Obscured (Minimum), which combined audit #103 adds explicitly: 'add focus not hidden under the sticky header to the keyboard test'; .claude/rules/brand.md ('focus visible with --pw-focus').
- **Best fix:** In brand.css: html { scroll-padding-top: calc(3.5rem + 0.5rem); } (header height plus a gap), raised to the two-row height below sm if narrow-practice-1-12 lands. Add #103's keyboard check at 390 px and 1280 px: shift-tab back through a long page (the packet builder) and assert that each focused element's top lies below the header's bottom.
- **Notes:** Kept in brand.css so it stays out of shell.tsx and PR #42.

### `narrow-practice-1-14`: On a phone the case list starts about 1,700 px down, two full screens below three setup panels and the reference search, even when the dashboard's deadline link sent the person there

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.cases.default.390`, `p.cases.deadline-14.390`, `p.cases.error.390`, `p.cases.notice.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** On p.cases.deadline-14.390, reached through the dashboard's 'deadlines in 14 days' deep link (?deadline=14), the person first scrolls past Triage a run (about 330 px), Patterns waiting for a case (about 270 px), Materiality with its form (about 470 px) and Find a packet by its reference. Only then do 'By deadline' (about y=1,730 px) and its four filters come, with the table at about y=1,980 px, more than two 844 px phone screens down. On p.cases.default.390 the order is the same. Cause: cases/page.tsx renders Triage a run (:61), Patterns waiting (:91), Materiality (:162) and ReferenceSearch (:189) before the Case list section (:190-191).
- **Rule:** Combined audit #125, 'on the Cases screen the case list sits below three setup panels ... Put the case list first' (lands in PR 13); docs/product/PRD.md:277 (Cases: list by stage, payer, owner, deadline, actionable total). The 390 px width magnifies #125's problem.
- **Best fix:** As #125 says: the case list (heading, filters, table or stacked rows) comes first, under the page's lede. Find a packet by its reference sits beside the list's filters. Triage a run, Patterns waiting for a case and Materiality follow, below the list, as a 'Set up and triage' section. A deep link with filters (?deadline=14) lands with the list in view.
- **Notes:** Combined audit #125 places this in PR 13. It is not Lane B in-flight. If PR 13 is far off, this reorder is a small no-migration change Lane B can take, leaving #125's bulk work to PR 13.

### `narrow-practice-1-15`: Packet review: after downloading both files the reviewer must reload the page by hand, and on a phone the instruction to do so sits about 4,000 px below the download links

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.packet-review.error-needs-downloads.390`, `p.packet-review.default.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/packets/countdown.tsx`
- **Evidence:** On p.packet-review.error-needs-downloads.390 (5,765 px tall), the two download links and their 'You have not downloaded it yet.' lines are at about y=620 to 760. 'Attest the export' is a disabled grey button at about y=5,600, with the hint 'Download the PDF and the CSV schedule under Exported files first ... Then reload this page.' Nothing on the page changes after a download: the per-file status (review/page.tsx:114-130) and the button (:302-310) come from the server render only. So a reviewer who downloads both files, reads them and scrolls back down still meets a disabled button until they reload, a step the person finds only by reading the grey text beneath it. The packet builder's single-user countdown ends the same way, 'now (reload the page)' (components/packets/countdown.tsx:25).
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 ('no screen depends on client-side state that a refresh loses'; here the screen depends on a refresh the person must know to make) and 'every error shown with a next step'; WCAG 2.1 SC 3.3.2 Labels or Instructions (the disabled control's reason is far from where the person acts); The brand is silent; this recommends.
- **Best fix:** Make the state follow the downloads. A small client component wraps the Exported files card: when a download link is clicked, and again when the tab regains visibility or focus, it calls router.refresh(), so the per-file lines change to 'You downloaded it ... checked against this SHA-256' and the Attestation becomes the live form with no reload. Put the remaining requirement beside the links as well as beside the button: 'Download both files to attest. 1 of 2 downloaded.' Make the countdown call router.refresh() when it reaches zero, so the approval control appears instead of the words 'reload the page'.
- **Notes:** The page is one of #38's screens, but #38 covers explaining figures in words, not this state, so it is not in flight. The server stays the judge: router.refresh() re-reads viewerDownloaded from the database, and nothing is decided in the browser.

### `narrow-practice-1-16`: The permission state has no page heading and no link to the place its next step names, so on a phone it is a dead end behind the Menu

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.packet.error-forbidden.390`, `p.packet-review.error-forbidden.390`, `p.home.error-forbidden.390`, `p.abstraction.error-forbidden.390`, `p.abstraction-ledger.error-forbidden.390`, `p.abstraction-regulations.error-forbidden.390`, `p.abstraction-version.error-forbidden.390`, `p.contracts.error-forbidden.390`, `p.digest.error-forbidden.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review`, `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/regulations`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`, `/p/[practiceId]/digests/[digestId]`
- **Files:** `packages/ui/src/states/permission-state.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Each forbidden shot at 390 px is the header plus one small box, 'Not available to your role', with no H1, so the page has no heading and the tab title alone says where the person is. Most of the 844 px screen below the box is blank. The next steps point somewhere but link nowhere: 'open the case to see its state' (packet, as practice administrator), 'Ask the practice's administrator for access', and for the reviewer the packet. Reaching the case on a phone means Menu, Cases, then finding the case in the list. Cause: Forbidden (components/shell.tsx:358-363) renders PermissionState (packages/ui/src/states/permission-state.tsx:13-24), which takes role, cannot and nextStep text only: no heading, no action. The pages pass only text (e.g. claims/page.tsx:36).
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:260 ('a permission state that explains what the role cannot do') and :256 ('every error shown with a next step'); WCAG 2.1 SC 2.4.6 Headings and Labels and 1.3.1 (a page without a heading); combined audit #90 (the practice administrator who also bills is sent to pages they cannot use).
- **Best fix:** Give PermissionState an optional action (a link, like EmptyState's) and give Forbidden a title (the screen's name as the H1, e.g. 'Packet') and an action prop. Each page passes its nearest useful place: the packet passes 'Open the case' (casePath), the review passes 'Back to Packets to attest' or 'Open the packet', and a practice-level page passes 'Go to Practice home'. Where the next step is a person ('Ask the practice's administrator'), name who it is from the practice's users if the role may see them, else link to Help and support with the screen named (/support?from=<section>), as the help panel already does.
- **Notes:** touches42: Forbidden is in components/shell.tsx (PR #42). The per-page actions can land after #42. The words reviewer may also flag the permission wording ('validate audit findings against contracts', 'Ask the practice's administrator' for internal staff). This finding is the missing heading and control.

### `narrow-practice-1-17`: A signed-in person who reaches a missing record gets the public page: no Menu, no account or sign-out, and one link to the first practice rather than back to the list they came from

- **Flags:** polish; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `not-found.signed-in.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/not-found.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`
- **Evidence:** not-found.signed-in.390 (packet-approver, /p/<unknown>/cases) shows the public header (the lockup alone), then 'Not found ... Go to your workspace'. The image checker found it byte-identical to the signed-out not-found. There is no Menu, practice switcher or account. 'Go to your workspace' goes to / (not-found.tsx:16), which redirects to the person's first practice, not the one they were in. The same page answers an unknown case, packet, line or contract inside a practice the person can open, so a stale link from a notification or an e-mail leaves them outside their practice. Cause: app/not-found.tsx:6-23 renders PublicHeader. The route wrapper turns not_found into Next's notFound() (lib/http/next.ts:108), so the page never draws its AppShell.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 ('every error shown with a next step') and :260 (the shell's navigation on every screen); WCAG 2.1 SC 3.2.3 Consistent Navigation. The brief asks for not-found states to be judged. Rules are silent on the signed-in not-found.
- **Best fix:** Inside a practice the person can open, an unknown child record renders in the frame. The route result carries a not_found kind the page draws (as it already draws 'forbidden') inside AppShell: an EmptyState, 'This case is not in SYNTHETIC ... any more, or the link is wrong', with a link back to the parent list (Cases, Claims and lines, Contracts). For an unknown or unreachable practice, keep the root not-found's single answer, so whether a practice exists is never disclosed. When a session exists it shows the signed-in header with account and sign-out, and its link reads 'Go to your practices'.
- **Notes:** touches42 only if the signed-in header is reused from shell.tsx (PR #42). Must keep the rule that an unknown practice and an unreachable one look the same (not-found.tsx:5).

### `narrow-practice-1-18`: In narrow table cells dates break at their hyphens ('2026-' over '06-10') and plan keys split mid-identifier

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.abstraction-ledger.default.390`, `p.anomalies.cohort.390`, `p.anomalies.cohort-viewer.390`, `p.contracts.layout.390`, `p.contract.layout.390`, `p.contract.default.390`, `p.mapping.all-plans.390`, `p.cases.deadline-14.390`, `p.packet.empty.390`, `p.case.default.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]/anomalies`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/format.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/anomalies/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/contracts/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Ledger entries' Money moved column reads '2026-' over '06-10' on every row. Flagged lines' Service date reads '2026-' over '04-04'. Effective reads 'from 2026-' over '01-01, open-' over 'ended' on Contracts and a contract's Versions. Cohorts' Quarter reads '2026-' over 'Q2'. Plan keys break as 'CE:SYNPLAN-' over 'FIX-ONE' in the Cases list, Mapping, and in the packet and case headings. Approval times wrap as '2026-10-' over '26 01:25' (contract Versions). Causes: date cells have no whitespace-nowrap and are not in the mono face (abstraction/ledger/page.tsx:65; anomalies/page.tsx Flagged lines; contracts/page.tsx Effective; contracts/[contractId]/page.tsx:76 windowText), so the browser breaks at the hyphen.
- **Rule:** .claude/rules/brand.md and docs/brand/BRAND.md:94 (codes and figures in the mono face with tabular numerals; a date is read as one figure); WCAG 2.1 SC 1.3.2 (a value split across lines reads as two). The rules are silent on dates in mono; this recommends nowrap.
- **Best fix:** One small date component (or a 'whitespace-nowrap font-mono tabular-nums' class) for every calendar date and UTC timestamp in tables. Timestamps get a non-breaking space between date and time, so they break only between the two. Plan keys and other identifiers use overflow-wrap:anywhere only when they cannot fit, not at every hyphen. With narrow-practice-1-04's stacked rows below sm, most of these cells gain the width they need.
- **Notes:** Brand reviewer may own the 'dates in mono' question; this finding is the line breaking.

### `narrow-practice-1-19`: Two empty states send the person to another screen in words only, with no link, which on a phone means Menu and a search through 14 rail items

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.claims.empty.390`, `p.anomalies.empty.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/claims`, `/p/[practiceId]/anomalies`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/claims/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/anomalies/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** p.claims.empty.390: 'No claims loaded yet. Upload 835 files on the Uploads screen; each claim that loads appears here with its lines.' No link. p.anomalies.empty.390: 'No anomaly. Triage a complete pricing run from the Cases screen; ...' No link. The same next step elsewhere carries its link: the practice home's 'Upload your first 835 files' has 'Go to Uploads' (page.tsx:183-192), and Mapping's 'No plans here yet' has 'Go to Uploads' (mapping/page.tsx:86-104). EmptyState accepts an action (packages/ui/src/states/empty-state.tsx:50) that these two do not pass: claims/page.tsx:126-130 and anomalies/page.tsx:158. On the claims empty state the filters and Find the claim also sit above it, live, with nothing to filter.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:260 ('every screen has an empty state that says what to do next'); empty-state.tsx:47-50 (the next step, and an optional control that takes it); consistency with the home and mapping empty states.
- **Best fix:** Pass action links: Claims, 'Go to Uploads' (/p/<id>/uploads), shown only to roles that may upload, with the who-can-upload sentence otherwise. Anomalies, 'Go to Cases' (/p/<id>/cases), where Triage a run is, or deep-link to the triage panel once narrow-practice-1-14 moves it. Hide the claims filters and Find the claim while no claim is loaded.

### `narrow-practice-1-20`: The case summary card opens with a 48 px empty band and, at 390 px, stacks eleven facts plus a second Owner control into an 865 px card, so the case's lines start near y=1,560

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.case.default.390`, `p.case.empty.390`, `p.case.error.390`, `p.case.write-off-open.390`, `p.case.viewer.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** On p.case.error.390 the summary card runs from about y=300 to y=1,165. Its first 48 px are empty. Then come 11 label-over-value facts (Actionable through Closed), then 'Owner' again as a select with Assign under 'Owner Unassigned'. The Packet and dispute card follows, and 'Lines (3)' begins at about y=1,560, nearly two phone screens down. Causes: Card's own py-6 plus CardContent's pt-6 with no header (cases/[caseId]/page.tsx:70-71); the dl is one column below sm (:72); the Owner fact (:101-103) and the Assign form's Owner select (:109-124) repeat each other.
- **Rule:** ADR 0056:53 (a definition list stacks its terms over their values: kept); the brand's spacing tokens (packages/brand/src/tokens.css:64-69). Rules are silent on density at phone width; this recommends one.
- **Best fix:** Drop pt-6 from the summary's CardContent (or give the card a CardHeader with the case's state), so the band goes. Below sm, set the short money and date facts in a two-column grid of label-over-value pairs (Actionable | Written off; Payer approved | Recovered; Deadline | Materiality; Opened | Closed), and keep the long-text facts full width. Where the person can assign, the Owner fact is the select itself, labelled 'Owner', with Assign beside it; viewers see the plain fact.
- **Notes:** Combined audit #89 (one case status) and #125 (case lines and layout in PR 13) will touch this page. Fold in there if PR 13 is near.

### `narrow-practice-1-21`: The ledger's six stage tiles stack one per row at 390 px, about 780 px of tiles before the shortfalls and the entries the screen exists to correct

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.abstraction-ledger.default.390`, `p.abstraction-ledger.empty.390`, `p.abstraction-ledger.notice.390`, `p.abstraction-ledger.error-adjust-zero.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/reconciliation/display.tsx`
- **Evidence:** On p.abstraction-ledger.default.390 the practice's ledger card runs from about y=330 to y=1,125. It holds six full-width tiles (Payer approved, Posted, Deposited, Recouped, Interest, Recovered (deposited)), each about 85 to 100 px tall, with figures of six or seven characters that use a third of the tile's width. Shortfalls begin at about y=1,150 and Ledger entries at about y=1,350. Cause: LedgerTotals' <dl className='grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-6'> (components/reconciliation/display.tsx:88) has one column below sm.
- **Rule:** docs/brand/BRAND.md:125-126 and ADR 0056:47-48 (tiles: label in Slate 12 px, value in Plex Mono 500, note in Slate: kept). Rules are silent on tile columns at phone width; this recommends two.
- **Best fix:** grid-cols-2 below sm (sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-6 unchanged), so the six tiles form three rows of two at 390 px. Each tile is then about 160 px wide, enough for '-$18.00' in Plex Mono at text-lg and a wrapped note. The Recovered tile, which carries the posted or deposited label, spans both columns (col-span-2) so its stage-five wording is never squeezed.
- **Notes:** LedgerTotals is shared, so the reconciliation screens (in the other narrow-practice half) gain the same. PR #51 (#12, ledger amounts in payment terms) is in flight on ledger wording. Check that it does not also change this grid.

### `narrow-practice-1-22`: Several primary actions are bare underlined words about 16 to 20 px tall (Adjust, Write this line off, the review's PDF download), small to hit with a thumb

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.abstraction-ledger.error-adjust-zero.390`, `p.case.default.390`, `p.case.write-off-open.390`, `p.packet-review.error-needs-downloads.390`, `p.packet-review.default.390`, `p.home.default.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review`, `/p/[practiceId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Measured in the 390 px shots: the ledger's '▸ Adjust' summary is about 46 x 16 px (12 px text: abstraction/ledger/page.tsx:23). It is the screen's only action. '▸ Write this line off' is about 125 x 20 px (cases/[caseId]/page.tsx:201). The review's 'PDF' download, a step the attestation requires, is about 26 x 20 px (review/page.tsx:115-117). 'Help with this screen' is about 135 x 20 px (components/shell.tsx:336). The home page's queue links are 20 px text lines 8 px apart (page.tsx:373). Each passes WCAG 2.2's 24 px minimum (SC 2.5.8) only through its spacing exception. None comes near the 44 px the enhanced criterion and the platforms' touch guidance use, while the app's own buttons are 36 px (packages/ui/src/components/button.tsx:160).
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.2 SC 2.5.8 Target Size (Minimum): met by spacing; SC 2.5.5 Target Size (Enhanced, AAA): not met. Combined audit #103 brings WCAG 2.2 in. The brand and the PRD are silent on target size; this recommends.
- **Best fix:** Render actions as actions. The ledger's Adjust and the case's 'Write this line off' and 'Reopen' summaries get the outline button's look (Button variant='outline' size='sm' classes on the <summary>: 32 px tall, full text-sm). The review's downloads become two outline buttons, 'Download the PDF' and 'Download the CSV schedule', each followed by its SHA-256 and status line. The home queue list gets py-1.5 per item, giving a 32 px row. Inline links inside sentences stay as they are.
- **Notes:** The help summary sits in shell.tsx (PR #42), so it is left out of the files list and can follow #42.

### `narrow-practice-1-23`: Detail-page titles join the screen's name to the record's long name in one 32 px heading: at 390 px they run three or four lines and break plan keys mid-identifier

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.submissions.default.390`, `p.submissions.empty.390`, `p.submissions.error.390`, `p.packet.empty.390`, `p.packet.default.390`, `p.packet-review.error-needs-downloads.390`, `p.abstraction-version.error-amend.390`, `p.contract.layout.390`, `p.case.error.390`, `p.plan.error.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/versions/[versionId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** At 390 px: 'Submissions and responses: SYNTHETIC PAYER Q, CE:SYNPLAN-FIX-ONE' takes four lines (about 150 px). 'Packet: SYNTHETIC PAYER Q, CE:SYNPLAN-FIX-ONE', 'Packet review: SYNTHETIC PAYER Q, version 1', 'SYNTHETIC FIXED SCHEDULE AGREEMENT, version 1' and the contract's title take three. The plan key breaks at its hyphen as 'CE:SYNPLAN-' over 'FIX-ONE' in the packet, submissions and plan headings. With the badge and lede under them, the first card starts 330 to 490 px down. H1s at cases/[caseId]/submissions/page.tsx:136, packet/page.tsx:178, review/page.tsx:78, abstraction/versions/[versionId]/page.tsx:99, contracts/[contractId]/page.tsx:42, cases/[caseId]/page.tsx:54, mapping/[planId]/page.tsx:73, all at text-[2rem].
- **Rule:** ADR 0056:48 (a page's title 32 px/700: kept); WCAG 2.1 SC 2.4.6 Headings and Labels (a heading that names the screen) and 1.4.10 Reflow. The rules are silent on combining screen and record names; this recommends separating them.
- **Best fix:** Split each title into the record and the screen. The record's name becomes the breadcrumb's last link, above the H1 ('Cases > SYNTHETIC PAYER Q, CE:SYNPLAN-FIX-ONE'), with plan keys kept whole (whitespace-nowrap on the key; the breadcrumb wraps between words). The H1 is the screen's own short name ('Submissions and responses', 'Packet', 'Packet review, version 1'), so it fits in one or two lines at 32 px. Record pages whose title is the record (the contract, the plan, the case) keep it as the H1 but stop breaking keys mid-identifier.
- **Notes:** The brand reviewer owns the type scale. This is the narrow-width consequence only.

### `narrow-practice-1-24`: At 390 px the packet builder is 6,900 to 7,900 px tall with nothing at the top that says where it stands or jumps to Approval and Export; 'Approve this version' sits about 5,900 px down

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.packet.approver-ready.390`, `p.packet.default.390`, `p.packet.drafter.390`, `p.packet.error-export-blocked.390`, `p.packet.error-live-check.390`, `p.packet.notice-exported.390`, `p.packet.notice-requested.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Page heights at 390 px: 6,930 (approver-ready), 7,064 (drafter), 7,108 (live-check), 7,694 (default), 7,784 and 7,804 (notices), 7,898 (export-blocked). On approver-ready, 'Ready for a second person's approval' and 'Approve this version' are at about y=5,880, below the version facts, citation, line schedule, deadline, calculation table, interest, evidence and the narrative editor. On export-blocked the red 'Export blocked' alert with its fix is at about y=6,500. The top of the page shows the case state chip and the version, but not the approval or export state. The ?notice= strip at the top (p.packet.notice-requested.390) is the only top-of-page status, and it is static (see narrow-practice-1-25). The regions are already named (role='region' with aria-labels) but have no ids to link to.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:278 (Packet builder: approval panel, export history; 'Verifier failures block approval'); WCAG 2.1 SC 2.4.1 Bypass Blocks and 2.4.5 Multiple Ways (within a long page). Rules are silent on in-page navigation; this recommends.
- **Best fix:** Under the packet's title, a short status line computed by the server from the same state the panels use: 'Version 3: verifier passed · approval requested by ... · not exported (2 reasons)'. Each part links to its region: #approval, #export, #narrative, which get ids, with scroll-padding from narrow-practice-1-13. Below md, a compact 'On this page' list of the regions follows it. Nothing moves, so a reviewer still reads top to bottom before approving.
- **Notes:** #38 and #48 also change this page (figures in words; the narrative surviving sign-out). Coordinate the order. The flows reviewer may judge the approver's path; this is the narrow-width length.

### `narrow-practice-1-25`: Success notices live in the URL (?notice=...), so a refresh, a bookmark or a shared link shows 'just done' again, even after the state has moved on

- **Flags:** polish; size M
- **Screens:** `p.packet.notice-requested.390`, `p.packet.notice-exported.390`, `p.packet-review.notice.390`, `p.submissions.notice.390`, `p.cases.notice.390`, `p.abstraction-ledger.notice.390`, `p.abstraction-regulations.notice.390`, `p.contract-version.notice.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/regulations`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/versions/[versionId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/packets.ts`
- **Evidence:** p.packet.notice-requested.390 shows 'Approval is requested. A second person who approves packets approves it here.' at the top, while the same page's Approval panel says Approved and Export says exported and attested. The URL alone (?notice=requested) drew the notice, not the packet's state. Every mutation redirects with ?notice=<key> (e.g. lib/routes/settings.ts:266, :318, :415; the packet's NOTICES, packet/page.tsx:189-191), and the page renders it whenever the parameter is present. On a phone, where a refresh is a pull-down gesture and links are shared from the share sheet, the notice outlives the moment: refreshing an hour later still says 'Adjusted' or 'The export is attested', and a colleague opening a shared link sees it as news.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 ('no screen depends on client-side state that a refresh loses'; here a refresh keeps a state it should drop); WCAG 2.1 SC 4.1.3 Status Messages (a status message should report a change that happened). Rules are silent on how notices persist; this recommends.
- **Best fix:** Make notices one-time and server-side. A mutation's success sets a 'flash' cookie and redirects to the plain URL, with no ?notice=. The cookie is HttpOnly and path-scoped to the record's page, names the notice and the record's id (no PHI), and has a Max-Age of a few seconds, because a server component cannot clear a cookie while it renders. So only the redirect's own page load sees it. The page shows the notice when the cookie names this record. The first view still works without JavaScript, while a later refresh, a bookmark or a shared link shows none. Where a record has a current state, the notice's words come from it ('Approval requested by ... at ...').
- **Notes:** Applies app-wide (settings, console). The flows and words reviewers may also note that the notices are neutral grey boxes without a title. This finding is about their persistence.

### `narrow-practice-1-26`: Long option labels are cut inside their selects at 390 px, hiding the part that tells the options apart (the run's release, the document's time)

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.cases.default.390`, `p.cases.deadline-14.390`, `p.submissions.error.390`, `p.submissions.default.390`, `p.plan.default.390`, `p.plan.error.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Triage a run's Pricing run select reads '2026-03-01 to 2026-03-31 (release 9000(', so a run re-priced under a newer release looks identical to the old one (cases/page.tsx:77-82, label '{periodFrom} to {periodTo} (release {releaseNo})'). The payer response's Document select reads 'Payer letter, uploaded 2026-10-26 01:26 U(', so two letters uploaded the same day cannot be told apart without opening the list (submissions/page.tsx:326). The plan's Corrects select reads 'Nothing: a new mapping for dates no mapp(' (mapping/[planId]/page.tsx). The selects themselves fit their cards. Only their chosen value is clipped.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 3.3.2 Labels or Instructions and 1.4.10 Reflow (the chosen value must stay readable at 320 to 390 CSS px); docs/product/PRD.md:256 ('labels'). The rules are silent on option wording; this recommends.
- **Best fix:** Lead each option with what tells it apart and keep it short: 'Release 900001 · Mar 2026', 'Payer letter · 01:26 UTC · a80b2fd7'. Under each such select, a server-rendered hint line repeats the selected option in full (the default selection's full text, updated by a few lines of client code on change), so the whole value is always readable at any width.

## narrow-practice-2

### `narrow-practice-2-01`: A refused form wipes or swaps what the person typed

- **Flags:** blocking; size M
- **Screens:** `p.roi.error.390`, `p.pricing-settings.error.390`, `p.correspondence.error.390`, `p.pricing-runs.error.390`, `p.defaults.error.390`, `p.reconciliation.error-reverse.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/pricing`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/correspondence`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx`, `apps/web/e2e/forms-keep-input.spec.ts (new)`
- **Evidence:** Every ActionForm comes back from a refusal with its controls reset to their defaultValue: React 19 resets an uncontrolled <form action> once the action settles (apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx:32,65), and the refusal carries no values (apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts:124-129,161-162). p.roi.error.390: the refused period is replaced by the suggested 07/01/2026 to 09/30/2026, a valid range shown beside 'the period ends on or after its first day' (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/page.tsx:53-54); pressing Generate again prints a statement for a period nobody asked for. p.pricing-settings.error.390: the refused entry is replaced by the stored 27601, outlined red as if 27601 were the mistake (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/pricing/page.tsx:107). p.correspondence.error.390: all ten contact fields come back blank after one refused name (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/correspondence/page.tsx:73-86). p.pricing-runs.error.390: both months back to '--------- ----' (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/page.tsx:87,91). p.defaults.error.390: the typed staff rate cleared (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults/page.tsx:70). p.reconciliation.error-reverse.390: the reason cleared and the consequence box unticked (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/page.tsx:42,45).
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (every error shown with a next step; no screen depends on client-side state that a refresh loses); WCAG 2.1 SC 3.3.1 and 3.3.3 (the person corrects the entry they made, which must still be there).
- **Best fix:** In ActionForm, wrap the server action so the client keeps the FormData it submitted; on a refusal, write each named control's submitted value back (text, select and textarea values, checkbox and radio checked state), skipping hidden, file and password inputs and the request key, then mark the refused controls invalid as today. Nothing is echoed by the server, so no value travels back in a response. Add an e2e check per form shape (month pair, date pair, ZIP, contact form, reverse reason with its box) that the typed value survives a refusal.
- **Notes:** One shared cause behind every refused form in the app, not only these six; the same change also serves finding narrow-practice-2-06.

### `narrow-practice-2-02`: Materiality shows cents as dollars, and saving it unchanged raises it a hundredfold

- **Flags:** blocking; size S
- **Screens:** `p.defaults.default.390`, `p.defaults.admin-view.390`, `p.defaults.notice.390`, `p.defaults.error.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/format.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/settings-routes.db.test.ts`
- **Evidence:** The card reads 'Now $500 per line and $25000 per case' and the fields under 'Per line (dollars)' and 'Per case (dollars)' are prefilled 500 and 25000. The page route hands the page cents as text (apps/web/src/lib/routes/settings.ts:292,295,299: text = centsToString), the page prints them after a '$' (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults/page.tsx:40,62) and uses them as the fields' defaultValue (:48-49,70), while the action parses the same fields as dollars (settings.ts:271-275,314). The stored default is 500 cents, $5.00 (packages/triage/src/settings.ts:41). Saving the form untouched stores $500.00 per line and $25,000.00 per case, so triage stops pursuing every shortfall under $500. The pursuit rate has the same defect once set (page.tsx:62,70).
- **Rule:** CLAUDE.md rule 2 (money is integer cents) and docs/product/PRD.md:256 (every money value rendered from cents in the practice's locale); .claude/rules/brand.md:36 (money in the mono face).
- **Best fix:** Render both thresholds and the pursuit rate with <Money/> from cents ('Now $5.00 per line and $250.00 per case') and prefill each money field with the dollar text of its cents ('5.00', '250.00') through one shared cents-to-dollar-input helper beside utc() in components/format.ts. Add a test that saving the form unchanged leaves the stored cents unchanged, for materiality and for the pursuit rate.
- **Notes:** Not a 390-only defect: every defaults shot at both widths shows it. The page alone can fix it; settings.ts (in #42) need not change. The test file named is an existing db test; a new page-level test is equally fine.

### `narrow-practice-2-03`: Wide tables push the page sideways or hide columns with no cue at 390 px

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.pricing-run.default.390`, `p.pricing-run.april.390`, `p.pricing-run.empty-filtered.390`, `p.compare.default.390`, `p.reconciliation.default.390`, `p.reconciliation.error-empty-file.390`, `p.reconciliation.error-reverse.390`, `p.reconciliation.reverse-open.390`, `p.reconciliation.viewer.390`, `p.reconciliation.case-ledger.390`, `p.remittances.default.390`, `p.remittance.default.390`, `p.remittance.plb.390`, `p.remittance.viewer.390`, `p.monthly.month.390`, `p.report-document.default.390`, `p.roi.default.390`, `p.roi.error.390`, `p.roi.viewer.390`, `p.access-log.default.390`, `p.support-access.default.390`, `p.support-access.ended.390`, `p.uploads.default.390`, `p.uploads.era-format.390`, `p.uploads.error-era-system.390`, `p.uploads.viewer.390`, `p.packet-reviews.default.390`, `p.report.default.390`, `p.report.viewer.390`, `p.report-review.default.390`, `p.template.preview.390`, `p.releases.default.390`, `p.releases.empty.390`, `p.releases.release.390`, `p.compare.diff-filter.390`, `p.upload.default.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/compare`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`, `/p/[practiceId]/remittances`, `/p/[practiceId]/remittances/[remittanceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/monthly`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/documents/[documentId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/access-log`, `/p/[practiceId]/support-access/[supportGrantId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/uploads`, `/p/[practiceId]/packet-reviews`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/review`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/template`, `/p/[practiceId]/releases`, `/p/[practiceId]/uploads/[documentId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/scroll-table.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/pricing/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/pricing/diff.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/remittances/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/remittances/[remittanceId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/reports/documents.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/reports/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/reports/roi.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/access-log/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/agreements/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/support-access/[supportGrantId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/uploads/receipts.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/packet-reviews/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/reconciliation/template-mapper.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/releases/note.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/uploads/[documentId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/e2e/narrow-overflow.spec.ts (new)`
- **Evidence:** Three forms of one cause. (a) No scroller, so the whole page scrolls sideways and the sticky header and Menu slide off to the left (capture widths): pricing run 473-489 px (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]/page.tsx:235 and apps/web/src/components/pricing/display.tsx:145), compare 434 px with every cell one word per line and no trace link visible (apps/web/src/components/pricing/diff.tsx:115), reconciliation 405 px (Exports, apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/page.tsx:285), remittances 613 px (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/remittances/page.tsx:71), remittance 733-745 px (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/remittances/[remittanceId]/page.tsx:107,145), monthly and report documents 539 px (By payer, apps/web/src/components/reports/documents.tsx:234), ROI statements 514-599 px (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/page.tsx:63), access log 515 px (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/access-log/page.tsx:46), support access 437 px (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/support-access/[supportGrantId]/page.tsx:45), upload receipts 534-540 px with Received held on one line (apps/web/src/components/uploads/receipts.tsx:145,163). (b) A bare overflow-x-auto clips the table at the card edge with no cue and no keyboard stop: the audit report and reviewer checklist hide Variance, Actionable, Deadline, Recovered and Remedy (apps/web/src/components/reports/display.tsx:120); the reconciliation ledger tables hide 'Written by' and 'Reason', so who reversed a match and why cannot be seen (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/page.tsx:136); the template preview hides Payer, Claim reference and Batch (apps/web/src/components/reconciliation/template-mapper.tsx:173); packets to attest cut the PDF SHA-256 column (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/packet-reviews/page.tsx:48). (c) Tables run into the card's right padding: release notes Changed column (apps/web/src/components/releases/note.tsx:31), compare 'First trace' crossing the card border, the upload's parse table. The app already has the right pattern in two places: ScrollTable (apps/web/src/components/scroll-table.tsx:9-15) with a table min-width (apps/web/src/components/pricing/location.tsx:13-14), used by four tables only.
- **Rule:** docs/adr/0056:51-53 (at a phone's width no page scrolls sideways; a wide table scrolls inside its own card, its scroller relative); WCAG 2.1 SC 2.1.1 (a scroller the keyboard can reach, axe scrollable-region-focusable).
- **Best fix:** Put every data table in the app through ScrollTable, with a min-width per table so columns stop collapsing to one word per line (the pricing/location.tsx pattern), and lint for a bare <table> outside it. Give ScrollTable a visible cue the rules do not yet define: CSS-only edge shading that follows the scroll position (background-attachment: local, token colours, no JS, CSP-safe) and a sticky first column so the row's identity stays in view. Extend the founder tour's no-sideways-scroll e2e check (scrollWidth <= 1280 at 1280 px) to scrollWidth <= 390 at 390 px on every route in this set, with the data shapes above.
- **Notes:** touches42 only through support-access/[supportGrantId]/page.tsx; the rest can land before #42 merges. ADR 0056 chose scroll-inside-card over reflow, so this keeps scrolling. The earlier tour's practice-home agreements table and screening template table (other sets) take the same fix. The support-access table also lacks an aria-label.

### `narrow-practice-2-04`: Unbroken SHA-256 hashes widen five report screens; the attestation hash wraps one character per line

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.roi-statement.default.390`, `p.report.default.390`, `p.report.viewer.390`, `p.report-review.default.390`, `p.report-document.default.390`, `p.report-document.layout-coverage.390`, `p.packet-reviews.default.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/review`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/documents/[documentId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/packet-reviews`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/packets/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/sha256.tsx (new)`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/review/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/documents/[documentId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/packet-reviews/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The hash renders as one unbreakable mono word and widens the page to 502-539 px: ROI statement (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]/page.tsx:74), audit report PDF and CSV (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/page.tsx:82,92), the reviewer checklist's signed-over hashes (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/review/page.tsx:72), report documents (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/documents/[documentId]/page.tsx:74). On packets to attest the opposite: Hash (apps/web/src/components/packets/display.tsx:74-82, break-all) sits in an auto-width table column, shrinks to one character a line and is cut at the card edge (header 'P S 2'), so the value a reviewer attests over cannot be read (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/packet-reviews/page.tsx:40,56). The upload receipt shows the right pattern: the hash in its own block, break-all (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/uploads/[documentId]/page.tsx:261).
- **Rule:** docs/adr/0056:51-53 (no page scrolls sideways at a phone's width); WCAG 2.1 SC 1.4.10 Reflow (a hash is not a two-dimensional table).
- **Best fix:** One shared <Sha256 value label/> (moved out of components/packets/display.tsx) used for every hash: the full value in its own block under its label, break-all, mono, with a Copy button; in a table, a cell min-width of 16ch so it wraps in four even lines instead of one character a line. Replace the five raw spans and the packet-reviews cell with it.
- **Notes:** Confirms the 2026-10-25 tour's 'ROI statement SHA-256 does not wrap at 390 px' and widens it: the same cause is on four more report screens. The Verified seal work (#46) on ROI pages does not cover it.

### `narrow-practice-2-05`: Loading states drop the app frame, hide what is loading and look the same everywhere; settings, support access and uploads have none

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.notifications.loading.390`, `p.packet-reviews.loading.390`, `p.pricing-runs.loading.390`, `p.pricing-run.loading.390`, `p.reconciliation.loading.390`, `p.template.loading.390`, `p.releases.loading.390`, `p.remittances.loading.390`, `p.remittance.loading.390`, `p.reports.loading.390`, `p.report.loading.390`, `p.report-document.loading.390`, `p.monthly.loading.390`, `p.roi.loading.390`, `p.roi-statement.loading.390`, `p.uploads.loading.390`, `p.upload.loading.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/notifications`, `/p/[practiceId]/packet-reviews`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/template`, `/p/[practiceId]/releases`, `/p/[practiceId]/remittances`, `/p/[practiceId]/remittances/[remittanceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/documents/[documentId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/monthly`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/uploads/[documentId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/uploads`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/access-log`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/agreements`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/correspondence`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/data`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/pricing`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/proof-level`, `/p/[practiceId]/support-access/[supportGrantId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/loading-frame.tsx (new)`, `packages/ui/src/states/loading-state.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/*/loading.tsx (29 files)`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/loading.tsx (new)`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/support-access/[supportGrantId]/loading.tsx (new)`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/uploads/loading.tsx (new)`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/error.tsx (new)`
- **Evidence:** Every practice loading.tsx is a bare <main className='mx-auto flex max-w-5xl flex-col gap-4 p-8'> around LoadingState (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/notifications/loading.tsx:6-7 and the 28 like it): no header, no Menu, no practice name, so the screen blanks and jumps from the shell's 16 px gutter to 32 px; there is no practice layout because the shell renders per page (apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx:13-19). The label is screen-reader only (packages/ui/src/states/loading-state.tsx:27), so nothing on screen says what is loading. At 390 px, 28 loading shots across the capture are one byte-identical PNG (md5 2fa1de7c..., six rows), nine more share 0e732b53... (four rows) and three share c0573646... (eight rows). The settings routes, the support-access grant and /uploads have no loading.tsx at all, so a slow load leaves the previous page on screen with no feedback (route map). The error boundary has the same frameless layout (apps/web/src/app/error.tsx:13).
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:260 (every screen has a loading state); docs/brand/BRAND.md:127-128 (mark alone, still, plus a plain progress bar: kept); rules silent on the frame and visible label, so this recommends.
- **Best fix:** A LoadingFrame component that draws the header chrome without data (the lockup, neutral placeholders where the switcher and account sit, the Menu button inert; the rail placeholder at md and up) and the shell's own main padding, with the label visible beside the progress bar ('Loading the pricing run'), and three or four skeleton shapes that match the page kind (list, detail with cards, form, report) instead of N identical bars. Use it in every loading.tsx, add settings/loading.tsx, support-access/[supportGrantId]/loading.tsx and uploads/loading.tsx, and a practice-level error.tsx in the same frame. Extract the header markup from shell.tsx so the frame and the shell cannot drift.
- **Notes:** touches42 through shell.tsx (header extraction); the loading files alone could land first with a duplicated header. The same frameless loading screen is on the console and account routes outside this set.

### `narrow-practice-2-06`: A field's error shows only in a summary at the foot of the form, off-screen on a phone

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.correspondence.error.390`, `p.defaults.error.390`, `p.pricing-runs.error.390`, `p.roi.error.390`, `p.reconciliation.error-reverse.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/settings/correspondence`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/field.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** ActionForm renders the 'Not done' summary after its children, just above the button (apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx:66-84), and moves focus to the first refused control (:52). On p.correspondence.error.390 focus lands on Name at y=1075 while its message sits at y=1795, about 720 px further down, so the person sees a red outline and no reason. The Field component already has an inline error slot tied by aria-describedby (apps/web/src/components/field.tsx:11-17,36), but ActionForm never feeds it. The summary also says 'Check the fields marked below' though it sits below them, and names a field by its key when the page passes no labels map (action-form.tsx:76: 'name:', 'toMonth:', 'periodTo:', 'staffRatePerHour:').
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (every error with a next step); WCAG 2.1 SC 3.3.1 Error Identification (the error described in text where it can be perceived); the brand rules are silent on placement.
- **Best fix:** ActionForm provides its refusal through a context; Field, SelectField and a new TextareaField read their own issue and render it in the existing inline slot under the control. The summary moves to the top of the form, links each item to its control, and names it by the control's own label text (read from the label element when the page passes no map), never by its key; its lead line becomes 'Check the fields marked in red'.
- **Notes:** Shares action-form.tsx with finding 01; land them together. The raw keys are a words issue too; the same change removes them.

### `narrow-practice-2-07`: Empty states name a next step on another screen without linking to it, or bury it below zeros

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.remittances.empty.390`, `p.pricing-runs.empty.390`, `p.reconciliation.empty.390`, `p.settings.empty.390`, `p.pricing-run.empty-filtered.390`, `p.report-review.empty.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/remittances`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/review`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/remittances/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/review/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** EmptyState has an action slot (packages/ui/src/states/empty-state.tsx:11,35) and none of the 19 empty states on these routes uses it. Remittances: 'Upload your first 835 files on the Uploads screen' with no link, under a payer and month filter form that has nothing to filter (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/remittances/page.tsx:62-67). Pricing runs: 'Map every plan with lines, then create a run' with no link to mapping (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/page.tsx:143). Reconciliation, empty: six stacked $0.00 tiles fill the first 1,100 px of the phone screen before 'Map the posting template', and the uploader sits above it although nothing reads an export until a template exists (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/page.tsx:234-236 totals, 242-255 template, 256-275 uploader). Settings for a billing manager: the section pills and nothing else, no line on what this person can change here (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/page.tsx). A filtered run with no match: 'No line of this run matches.' at the foot of a long page with no way back to all lines (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]/page.tsx:232-233). Reviewer checklist before the report exists: PDF and line CSV links to files that do not exist yet (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/review/page.tsx:43-50,59-60).
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:260 (every screen has an empty state that says what to do next); packages/ui/src/states/empty-state.tsx:8-11 (the action that takes the next step).
- **Best fix:** Give each empty state its action: 'Go to Uploads', 'Map plans', 'Map the posting template', 'Show all lines'. Hide the remittance filters until something has loaded. On an empty ledger, collapse the six tiles into one line ('No ledger entry yet') and put the template step first, then the uploader. Give the settings overview a short list of what this role changes here. Hide the review page's file links until the files exist.
