Name the summary 'Practice: <name>' so the accessible name carries the visible text. Regenerate the 390 px header baseline of ADR 0058 as the docs describe.
- **Notes:** Every non-loading shot in the set shows it; loading shots have no header at all (finding 05).

### `narrow-practice-2-09`: Fragment links and keyboard focus land under the 56 px sticky header

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.reports.audit-reports.390`, `p.report.default.390`, `p.report-review.default.390`, `p.roi-statement.default.390`, `p.reconciliation.default.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/reports`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/review`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/brand.css`, `apps/web/e2e/a11y.ts`
- **Evidence:** The header is sticky top-0 at h-14 (apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx:315) and nothing sets scroll-padding-top or scroll-margin-top anywhere (apps/web/src/app/brand.css, packages/ui/src/styles.css). The stage links jump to #stage-actionable and its siblings (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/page.tsx:133-149, apps/web/src/components/reports/display.tsx:93,104) and the ROI statement's entry links to reconciliation#entry-<id> (apps/web/src/components/reports/roi.tsx:70), so the target heading or ledger row arrives under the header; reverse Tab order can hide a focused 20 px link the same way.
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.2 SC 2.4.11 Focus Not Obscured (Minimum), which combined audit #103 adds ('focus not hidden under the sticky header'); docs/adr/0056 silent.
- **Best fix:** Set html { scroll-padding-top: 4rem } in the brand base styles (the header's 3.5 rem plus air), and add the #103 keyboard check that a focused control is never fully under the header.

### `narrow-practice-2-10`: 14 px form controls make iOS Safari zoom the page on every field

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.correspondence.error.390`, `p.correspondence.empty.390`, `p.defaults.default.390`, `p.pricing-settings.default.390`, `p.roi.default.390`, `p.pricing-runs.default.390`, `p.remittances.default.390`, `p.plan.notice.390`, `p.template.preview.390`, `p.reconciliation.reverse-open.390`, `p.uploads.default.390`, `p.proof-level.empty.390`, `p.reports.default.390`, `p.monthly.default.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/settings/correspondence`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/pricing`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs`, `/p/[practiceId]/remittances`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/template`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`, `/p/[practiceId]/uploads`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/proof-level`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/monthly`
- **Files:** `packages/ui/src/components/input.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/field.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/uploads/uploader.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/reconciliation/template-mapper.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Input is text-sm at every width (packages/ui/src/components/input.tsx:11), and so are SelectField (apps/web/src/components/field.tsx:75), the upload selects (components/uploads/uploader.tsx:330,353), the run and report selects (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/page.tsx:110,120; apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/page.tsx:98), the template mapper's controls (components/reconciliation/template-mapper.tsx:111,115,141) and the reverse reason (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/page.tsx:42). iOS Safari zooms the page on focus into any control under 16 px, so each field on these screens zooms in and the person pinches back out; on the screens that already scroll sideways it compounds.
- **Rule:** docs/adr/0056:49-50 sets 14 px for tables and dense controls but is silent on phones; WCAG 2.1 SC 1.4.10 Reflow in spirit. Recommendation.
- **Best fix:** Controls take text-base below md and text-sm from md up (input, select, textarea and the native date and month inputs), in the Input component and one shared select and textarea class; nothing else changes at 1280 px.
- **Notes:** Seen in code, not in a still image; the shots show the 14 px controls.

### `narrow-practice-2-11`: Closed selects cut their option text where the options differ

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.pricing-runs.default.390`, `p.pricing-runs.viewer.390`, `p.pricing-runs.error.390`, `p.reports.audit-reports.390`, `p.reports.default.390`, `p.proof-level.empty.390`, `p.proof-level.notice.390`, `p.plan.notice.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/proof-level`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/proof-level/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Compare two runs: both selects read '2026-03-01 to 2026-03-31, release 9000(' because the label puts period and release first and 're-run', 'in effect' and the completion time last (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/page.tsx:35-36); on p.pricing-runs.viewer.390 two March runs, one in effect and one not, look identical when closed. The audit report's run select is cut the same way (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/page.tsx:100-101). Proof level reads 'Deposited: recovered once the deposit is m' (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/proof-level/page.tsx:15-18,57). Mapping's Corrects reads 'Nothing: a new mapping for dates no mapp' (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]/page.tsx:182).
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.1 SC 1.3.1 and 2.4.6 (a control's current value perceivable); rules silent on option length. Recommendation.
- **Best fix:** Lead each option with what tells it apart and keep it short ('March 2026 · in effect · 01:25', 'March 2026 · replaced'), and show the chosen run's full line as text under the select. Proof level has two options: make it a radio pair, each with its one-line consequence under it.
- **Notes:** Comparing the wrong one of two same-period runs is the real risk here; the rest is legibility.

### `narrow-practice-2-12`: A refresh loses the upload batch, the receipts page and a posting-template mapping in progress, with no warning

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.uploads.default.390`, `p.uploads.era-format.390`, `p.uploads.viewer.390`, `p.template.preview.390`, `p.template.default.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/uploads`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/template`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/uploads/uploader.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/uploads/receipts.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/reconciliation/template-mapper.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Uploads keep the kind, the ERA system, the batch and its progress in component state (components/uploads/uploader.tsx:141-149) and the receipts page number in useState (components/uploads/receipts.tsx:72), so a refresh drops the batch card, resets the kind to 835 and returns the receipts to page 1; files not yet sent are dropped silently because no beforeunload guard exists anywhere in apps/web. The template mapper holds the chosen template, system name, date format and all nine column choices in state (components/reconciliation/template-mapper.tsx:44-49), so a refresh, a phone locking or a tab reload throws away a mapping that takes minutes to build.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (no screen depends on client-side state that a refresh loses).
- **Best fix:** Put the receipts page, the upload kind and the ERA system in the URL (router.replace; none is PHI). Warn before leaving while any file of a batch is still sending; after a reload the receipts already show 'Waiting for the upload' with its retry. Keep the template mapper's choices in the URL too (template id, system, date format and each field's column header, the same config the saved template already stores) and warn before leaving with unsaved choices; the sample file stays in the browser only and is chosen again.
- **Notes:** Column headers are configuration the saved template already holds server-side; a file whose first row is data is refused by the mapper before any header is chosen. If the lane prefers no header text in a URL, keep column indexes instead.

### `narrow-practice-2-13`: An ERA upload refused for its missing system throws away the files already chosen

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.uploads.error-era-system.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/uploads`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/uploads/uploader.tsx`
- **Evidence:** With 'ERA export' chosen and no system, choosing files shows 'Nothing was uploaded / Choose which system's ERA export the files are, then choose the files again.' (components/uploads/uploader.tsx:182-184,393-403): the files are discarded, the system select is not marked invalid or focused, and on a phone the person goes back through the file picker for every file.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (every error with a next step); WCAG 2.1 SC 3.3.1 (the field in error identified).
- **Best fix:** Keep the chosen files: mark the system select invalid, focus it, and say 'Choose the system; your 3 files upload as soon as you do', starting the batch on the choice. Until a system is chosen, the Choose files button says why it waits rather than accepting files it must refuse.

### `narrow-practice-2-14`: Stacked 12 px links in the remittance Links column are below the 24 px target size

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.remittance.viewer.390`, `p.remittance.default.390`, `p.remittance.plb.390`, `p.monthly.month.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/remittances/[remittanceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/monthly`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/remittances/[remittanceId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/reports/documents.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The Links cell stacks text-xs links 4 px apart (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/remittances/[remittanceId]/page.tsx:181-202). On p.remittance.viewer.390 '1 line' is a 16 px tall target 4 px under 'Superseded by a corrected claim'; a 24 px circle on '1 line' overlaps its neighbour, so neither the size nor the spacing exception holds. The monthly summary's single-digit count links ('0', '3', about 8 by 20 px) pass only on spacing (apps/web/src/components/reports/documents.tsx:185,211). 'Reverse this match', the way into a ledger reversal, is a 12 px summary (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/page.tsx:35).
- **Rule:** WCAG 2.2 SC 2.5.8 Target Size (Minimum), added by combined audit #103.
- **Best fix:** Give table links and summaries a 24 px minimum box (inline-flex min-h-6 items-center, gap-2 between stacked links) and text-sm in the Links cell; make the count links cover their cell's padding.
- **Notes:** Measured from the capture: link rows at 16-18 px pitch, last two links 4 px apart.

### `narrow-practice-2-15`: The support-access 'Records read' list is unpaged: 80 rows, 7,660 px at 390

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42

At 390 px the page is 7,660 px tall (and 437 px wide, finding 03); the same few records repeat dozens of times with no grouping.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (every list paginated and filterable server-side).
- **Best fix:** Page the reads with the app's Pager (components/remittances/display.tsx:94) and add a grouped view (record type and record, with count and first and last time) as the default, the raw reads one click away.

### `narrow-practice-2-16`: Dates and timestamps break at their hyphens in tables and titles

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.access-log.default.390`, `p.reports.audit-reports.390`, `p.agreements.default.390`, `p.agreements.notice.390`, `p.roi.default.390`, `p.roi.viewer.390`, `p.report.default.390`, `p.report.generating.390`, `p.roi-statement.default.390`, `p.reconciliation.default.390`, `p.support-access.default.390`, `p.report-document.layout-coverage.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/settings/access-log`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/agreements`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`, `/p/[practiceId]/support-access/[supportGrantId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/documents/[documentId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/format.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/when.tsx (new)`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/access-log/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/agreements/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/documents/[documentId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** utc() and utcDate() return plain strings (apps/web/src/components/format.ts:2-12), so a narrow column breaks them at each hyphen: the access log's When runs four lines ('2026-/10-26/01:42/UTC'), export history and grants From/Until two to four lines, the ROI period link '2026-/04-01/to/2026-/09-30', and the 32 px titles 'Audit report, service 2026-03-01 to 2026-/03-31' and 'Coverage report, 2026-/10-26'.
- **Rule:** docs/brand/BRAND.md:94 and .claude/rules/brand.md:36 (figures in mono, tabular); rules silent on wrapping. Recommendation.
- **Best fix:** A <When> and <Day> component that renders each date or timestamp as one nowrap mono span (the date and the time may break between them), used by every table and title; the scroll fix in finding 03 gives tables the width.

### `narrow-practice-2-17`: Work-in-progress states tell the person to reload instead of updating

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.report.generating.390`, `p.report-review.empty.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/review`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/compare`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/documents/[documentId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/refresh-while-pending.tsx (new)`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/documents/[documentId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/compare/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** 'reload in a moment' or 'reload this page in a moment' on a generating report (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/page.tsx:54), a printing document (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/documents/[documentId]/page.tsx:51), a printing ROI PDF (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]/page.tsx:79), an unfinished run (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]/page.tsx:176) and a compare waiting on a run (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/compare/page.tsx:75). Upload receipts already poll (components/uploads/receipts.tsx:23,84-104), so the product has the pattern.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:260 (a loading state per screen); rules silent on polling. Recommendation.
- **Best fix:** A small client <RefreshWhilePending> that calls router.refresh() every few seconds while the status is pending, stops after a few minutes with 'Still working: reload to check', and announces 'Ready' in a polite live region.
- **Notes:** The review page's file links before the files exist are in finding 07.

### `narrow-practice-2-18`: URL-only success notices survive a refresh and can contradict the page

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.defaults.notice.390`, `p.agreements.notice.390`, `p.plan.notice.390`, `p.template.notice.390`, `p.correspondence.notice.390`, `p.pricing-settings.notice.390`, `p.proof-level.notice.390`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/agreements`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/template`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/correspondence`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/pricing`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/proof-level`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/notice.tsx (new)`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/agreements/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/template/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/correspondence/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/pricing/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/proof-level/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Each 'Saved' alert is driven by ?notice= alone (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults/page.tsx:30-35, apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/agreements/page.tsx:30) and nothing removes the parameter, so a refresh, Back or a bookmark shows it again. p.defaults.notice.390 shows 'Saved: The pursuit cost applies from the next triage' beside 'Not set: cases show no pursuit cost'; p.agreements.notice.390 shows 'Agreement chain confirmed' above two Missing agreements.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (no state a refresh changes the meaning of; read in reverse); rules silent. Recommendation.
- **Best fix:** After a notice renders, a tiny client component drops the notice parameter with history.replaceState (other parameters kept); where a notice states a fact (agreements confirmed), render it only when the page's own data agrees.

### `narrow-practice-2-19`: The upload kind select runs past its drop zone; 32 px paddings eat the phone width

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.uploads.empty.390`, `p.uploads.loading.390`, `p.uploads.error-receipts.390`, `p.uploads.default.390`, `p.uploads.era-format.390`, `p.uploads.error-era-system.390`, `p.reconciliation.default.390`, `p.notifications.empty.390`

- **Evidence:** The drop zone is a flex column with items-start and p-8 (components/uploads/uploader.tsx:311); the kind select has no width limit (:330), so its longest option sets its width and it crosses the dashed border, making the page 393 px wide even with no table (p.uploads.empty.390). Inside the reconciliation card the same p-8 zone nests in the card's px-6, leaving 262 px for content. EmptyState also pads p-8 at every width (packages/ui/src/states/empty-state.tsx:30).
- **Rule:** docs/adr/0056:51-52 (no page scrolls sideways at a phone's width); rules silent on padding.
- **Best fix:** The select and its wrapper take w-full max-w-full min-w-0 (as SelectField already does, components/field.tsx:75); the drop zone and EmptyState pad p-4 below sm and p-8 from sm up.

- **Screens:** `p.packet-reviews.error-forbidden.390`, `p.report-review.error-forbidden.390`, `p.access-log.error-forbidden.390`, `p.support-access.error-forbidden.390`

On a phone the only way on is the Menu.

### `narrow-practice-2-23`: The chosen release is not marked on the release notes

## words-other

### `words-other-01`: Form refusals name the field by its code key (routingNumber, organizationId, contactName, confirm, occurredOn)

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `settings.billing-org.error.1280`, `console.billing.error.1280`, `request-audit.error.1280`, `screening-result.error-name.1280`, `sign-in-new-password.error-mismatch.1280`, `console.compliance.error.1280`, `console.organizations.error.1280`, `console.organization.error.1280`, `console.practice.error.1280`, `console.support.error.1280`, `console.terms.error.1280`, `console.models.error.1280`, `settings.users.error.1280`, `support.error.1280`
- **Routes:** `/settings/billing/[organizationId]`, `/console/billing`, `/request-audit`, `/screening/results/[resultId]`, `/sign-in/new-password`, `/console/compliance`, `/console/organizations`, `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`, `/console/practices/[practiceId]`, `/console/support`, `/console/terms`, `/console/models`, `/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users`, `/support`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/http/invoke.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/schemas.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/billing.ts`, `apps/worker/src/billing/account.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/compliance.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/console.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/console-onboarding.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/sign-in.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/support.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/onboarding.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/screening/routes.ts`
- **Evidence:** Every ActionForm refusal list item starts with the schema key: 'routingNumber: a nine-digit routing number', 'organizationId: the organization has no payment method on file; its administrator adds one in Settings first', 'organizationName: this form takes no patient information: remove anything that looks like an identifier, a date or a phone number', 'contactName: your name, at most 200 characters', 'confirm: the two passwords differ', 'occurredOn: evidence is recorded for what has happened', 'name: required', 'reason: required', 'version: letters, digits and . _ -', 'zip: five digits, or ZIP+4 such as 27601-1234', 'email: an internal user cannot join a practice group or a firm; support access is a support grant', 'subject: a ticket takes no patient information: ...'. Cause: apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx:76 renders {labels[issue.field] ?? issue.field}, and only 2 of the 112 ActionForm uses pass labels (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/pricing/page.tsx:97,112); apps/web/src/lib/http/invoke.ts:83-85 falls back to the key 'input' for a refusal with no path. The screening's own upload form already does it right (apps/web/src/components/screening/upload-form.tsx:66, issueField: 'Practice name: ...' on screening.error-no-phi.1280). Several messages also give no next step: 'evidence is recorded for what has happened' never says the date is in the future; 'required' never says what to enter.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (every error shown with a next step); WCAG 2.1 SC 3.3.1 Error Identification and 3.3.3 Error Suggestion (the item in error is identified in text the person knows); brief: no raw keys in what a person reads
- **Best fix:** ActionForm names each refused field by the visible label of the control it marks (control.labels[0].textContent, read in the effect that already finds the control, held in state), uses the labels map only as an override, and for an issue with no control (hidden field, 'input', a database rule) shows the message alone, never a key. Rewrite the terse messages as instructions. Exact replacements: 'routingNumber: a nine-digit routing number' -> 'Routing number: enter the nine digits of the routing number.'; 'organizationId: the organization has no payment method on file; its administrator adds one in Settings first' -> 'Organization: SYNTHETIC BILLING FIRM has no payment method on file. Ask its administrator to add one under Billing, then issue the invoice.' (the chosen organization's name); 'organizationName: this form takes no patient information: remove anything that looks like an identifier, a date or a phone number' -> 'Organization: this form takes no patient information. Remove anything that looks like an identifier, a date or a phone number, then send again.'; 'contactName: your name, at most 200 characters' -> 'Your name: enter your name, up to 200 characters.'; 'confirm: the two passwords differ' -> 'New password again: the two passwords do not match. Type the same password in both fields.'; 'occurredOn: evidence is recorded for what has happened' -> 'Date it happened: the date is after today. Enter the day it happened, today or earlier.'; 'name: required' -> 'Name: enter the organization’s name.'; 'reason: required' -> 'Reason: say what the ticket needs, with no patient details.'; 'version: letters, digits and . _ -' -> 'Version: use only letters, digits, dots, underscores and hyphens, such as 2026-11.'; 'zip: five digits, or ZIP+4 such as 27601-1234' -> 'Billing ZIP: enter five digits, or a ZIP+4 such as 27601-1234.'; 'email: an internal user cannot join a practice group or a firm; support access is a support grant' -> 'Email: PaidWhole staff cannot join a customer’s organization. To give support access, open a support grant from the ticket under Support.'; 'subject: a ticket takes no patient information: ...' -> 'Subject: a ticket takes no patient information. ...'. Add a component test that an issue whose key differs from its label never renders the key, and a route test that every refusal key of every action route maps to a control of its form or to a key-free message.
- **Notes:** The ActionForm change alone touches no #42 file; the message rewrites in apps/web/src/lib/routes/console.ts and apps/web/src/lib/screening/routes.ts do, so land those after #42. The billing page is also in PR #47 (#135), but this is outside #135's scope (the bank account first). Inline messages under a field (screening upload form) should also start with a capital.

### `words-other-02`: A raw Zod type error reaches the operator: 'components: Invalid input: expected array, received undefined'

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `console.status.error.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/status`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/routes/support.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/schemas.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/console/status/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Opening an incident with no component ticked shows 'Not done / Check the fields marked below. / components: Invalid input: expected array, received undefined', and no field is marked. Cause: the checkboxes are named components[] (apps/web/src/app/console/status/page.tsx:119); with none ticked the key is absent from the form (apps/web/src/lib/http/form.ts:7-21), and the preprocess in apps/web/src/lib/routes/support.ts:391-394 passes undefined on, so z.array's type check fires before the designed .min(1, 'choose at least one component'). Generally, there is no app-wide Zod error map (no z.config/customError under apps/web/src/lib), so any schema without its own message shows Zod's English.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (every error with a next step); brief: no raw validation text
- **Best fix:** Preprocess a missing checkbox group to [] in support.ts:391-394 so the designed message shows, and mark the fieldset. Install one Zod error map at module load in apps/web/src/lib/routes/schemas.ts that turns every default code into plain words ('Enter a value.', 'Choose at least one.', 'Enter a whole number.'), with a test that runs each route's sample input with each field removed and fails on any message that starts with 'Invalid input' or 'Expected'. Exact replacement: 'components: Invalid input: expected array, received undefined' -> 'Components affected: tick at least one component.'

### `words-other-03`: 'Check the fields marked below.' points down, while the marked field sits above the message

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `console.organizations.error.1280`, `console.practice.error.1280`, `console.support.error.1280`, `console.terms.error.1280`, `console.billing.error.1280`, `console.models.error.1280`, `console.compliance.error.1280`, `console.status.error.1280`, `request-audit.error.1280`, `screening.error-no-phi.1280`, `screening.error-zip.1280`, `screening-result.error-name.1280`, `settings.billing-org.error.1280`, `settings.users.error.1280`, `sign-in-new-password.error-mismatch.1280`, `support.error.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/organizations`, `/console/practices/[practiceId]`, `/console/support`, `/console/terms`, `/console/billing`, `/console/models`, `/console/compliance`, `/console/status`, `/request-audit`, `/screening`, `/screening/results/[resultId]`, `/settings/billing/[organizationId]`, `/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users`, `/sign-in/new-password`, `/support`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts`
- **Evidence:** Every refusal summary reads 'Check the fields marked below.' (apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts:162 and :180), but ActionForm renders the summary after its fields, just above the button (apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx:66-84), so the red-marked field is always above the sentence (e.g. Name field above the alert on console.organizations.error.1280). On console.status.error.1280 and on the password-policy refusal (sign-in-new-password.error-policy.1280) no field is marked at all.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (an error with a next step the person can follow); WCAG 2.1 SC 3.3.1
- **Best fix:** Make the sentence true wherever the summary sits: 'Check the fields marked below.' -> 'Fix the fields listed here, then try again.' Each list item then names the field by its label (finding words-other-01). Whether the summary also moves to the top of the form with links to its fields is the accessibility lens's call; the wording holds either way.

### `words-other-04`: The code-entry, enrolment and hardware-key steps tell people to check an email and password the step never asked for

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `sign-in-mfa.error.1280`, `sign-in-enroll.error.1280`, `sign-in.error-wrong-password.1280`
- **Routes:** `/sign-in/mfa`, `/sign-in/enroll`, `/sign-in`, `/sign-in/hardware-key`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/routes/sign-in.ts`
- **Evidence:** Every step shows one sentence: 'That did not match. Check the email, the password or the code, and try again.' On /sign-in/mfa and /sign-in/enroll the person typed only a six-digit code; on /sign-in they typed no code; on /sign-in/hardware-key (an unknown email) only an email. Cause: MESSAGES.credentials and MESSAGES.no_account in apps/web/src/lib/routes/sign-in.ts:44,46, used by settle() for every step (:93-101) and by the hardware-key options handler (:248).
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (every error with a next step); WCAG 2.1 SC 3.3.3 Error Suggestion
- **Best fix:** Give settle() the step and keep one message per step, none of which says whether the account exists. Exact replacements: /sign-in: 'That email and password do not match. Check both and try again. Ten failed attempts lock the account.'; /sign-in/mfa: 'That code did not match. Enter the six-digit code your authenticator app shows now; it changes every 30 seconds.'; /sign-in/enroll: 'That code did not match. Check that your app holds the account for {email} with the setup key above, then enter the code it shows now.'; /sign-in/hardware-key: 'That email cannot sign in with a hardware key yet. Check the address; if you have not registered a key, sign in with your password and code once to register it.'
- **Notes:** Keep the same answer for an unknown account and a wrong password on each step, so the wording does not reveal which accounts exist.

### `words-other-05`: A locked customer is told 'A platform administrator can unlock it', a role they cannot reach

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `sign-in.error-locked.1280`, `sign-in-hardware-key.error-locked.1280`
- **Routes:** `/sign-in`, `/sign-in/hardware-key`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/routes/sign-in.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/sign-in/notice.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/sign-in/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/hardware-key.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Alert 'Account locked / This account is locked after ten failed attempts. A platform administrator can unlock it.' (apps/web/src/lib/routes/sign-in.ts:45; the same text as the ?reason=locked notice, apps/web/src/app/sign-in/notice.tsx:17). A practice's staff do not know who PaidWhole's platform administrators are or how to reach them, and only they unlock (apps/web/src/lib/routes/console.ts:579 unlockUser). The page shows no support address; the only lead is 'Trouble signing in? Check the service status.' The tense also reads as a rule, not as what happened.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (every error with a next step); PRD 7 Sign-in and MFA (lockout after ten failures)
- **Best fix:** Name the way to get it unlocked, with the support address the status page already shows (services.supportEmail, apps/web/src/lib/server/config.ts:269): pass it to the sign-in page and the hardware-key options result. Exact replacement: 'This account is locked after ten failed attempts. A platform administrator can unlock it.' -> 'This account was locked after ten failed attempts. Write to support@... from this address to have it unlocked.'

### `words-other-06`: Sign-in offers no way forward for a forgotten password or a lost authenticator, and 'New here? Request an audit' misdirects invited users

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `sign-in.default.1280`, `sign-in-mfa.default.1280`, `sign-in.error-wrong-password.1280`
- **Routes:** `/sign-in`, `/sign-in/mfa`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/sign-in/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/sign-in/mfa/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/sign-in.ts`
- **Evidence:** The sign-in page's only help is 'New here? Request an audit. Trouble signing in? Check the service status.' (apps/web/src/app/sign-in/page.tsx:38-47); the code page says nothing about a lost phone (apps/web/src/app/sign-in/mfa/page.tsx:20). There is no password or two-step reset anywhere (no reset route in apps/web/src/lib/routes), so a person who forgot either has no next step until ten failures lock the account. A person invited by their practice is 'new here' too, and the link sends them to the prospect form.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (every error with a next step)
- **Best fix:** Exact replacement for sign-in/page.tsx:38-47: 'Invited by your organization? Sign in with the email and temporary password from your invitation. Forgot your password or lost your authenticator app? Write to support@... from your work address. New to PaidWhole? Request an audit. Service status.' Add under the code field on /sign-in/mfa: 'Lost your authenticator app? Write to support@... from this address.' Pair it with a supported reset path for support (a flows item); the words must not promise a self-service reset that does not exist.
- **Notes:** Support has no reset route today; the flows lens should own the reset itself. The wording here only names the channel that answers within the published target.

### `words-other-07`: Internal staff are told to ask a customer's administrator for access

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `home.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/`, `/p/[practiceId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** As support@synthetic.test the home page says 'No practice to open / You have no current access to a practice. A practice administrator approves access for your organization; support access comes from a ticket. Ask your administrator.' (apps/web/src/app/page.tsx:32). Per the route map this page renders only for internal staff without a grant. Once a reviewer holds a support grant, '/' redirects to /p/<practice>, where a role without practice.open gets 'Ask the practice's administrator for access.' (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx:153, and the same string on 32 practice pages; 'Ask the practice administrator.' on 7 settings pages). A practice administrator cannot grant PaidWhole staff anything: support access is a grant a platform administrator opens from a ticket.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (an error with a next step); PRD 7 (a permission state that explains what the role cannot do); brief item: forbidden message telling internal staff to ask the practice's administrator
- **Best fix:** Choose the next step by who is reading: Forbidden (apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx:358-364) and the home page read shell.platformRoles. For internal roles: 'Your role reaches a practice only under a support grant for this work. Ask a platform administrator to open one from the ticket.' with a link to Support tickets. Home, internal: 'No practice to open / Support reaches a practice only under a support grant, which a platform administrator opens from a ticket for four hours. Open Support tickets to see what is waiting.' Home, customer: 'You have no current access to a practice. Ask your organization’s administrator to give you a role in one.' Customers keep 'Ask your practice administrator for access.'
- **Notes:** shell.tsx is in #42's file list; land after #42 merges.

### `words-other-08`: The permission state reads as a template: no article, the role named twice, and a raw permission that does not match the screen

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `console.organizations.error-forbidden.1280`, `console.screening.error-forbidden.1280`, `console.specialist-queue.error-forbidden.1280`, `settings.billing-org.error-forbidden.1280`, `settings.users.error-forbidden.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/organizations`, `/console/screening`, `/console/specialist-queue`, `/settings/billing/[organizationId]`, `/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users`
- **Files:** `packages/ui/src/states/permission-state.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/format.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/console/specialist-queue/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/rule-releases/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/screening/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/settings/billing/[organizationId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Current texts: 'As Contract specialist, you cannot do this here (Contract specialist cannot manage organizations, practices and memberships). The internal console is for platform administrators.'; 'As Platform administrator, you cannot do this here (Platform administrator cannot approve rate records for publication). The place-of-service split is approved by a contract specialist.' (the split page's permission is rate_record.approve, apps/web/src/lib/screening/routes.ts:1268-1290, so the reason names rate records on a split screen); 'As Platform administrator, you cannot do this here (Platform administrator cannot review model-proposed contract abstractions). The specialist queue is the contract specialists''.'; 'As Billing manager, you cannot do this here (Billing manager cannot choose the organization’s plan and payment method and read its invoices). Your organization's administrator manages its billing.'; 'As Billing manager, you cannot do this here (Billing manager cannot invite users to the organization). Users and roles are managed by the organization's administrator.' Causes: packages/ui/src/states/permission-state.tsx:19 ('As {role}, you cannot {cannot}.'), apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx:361 ('do this here (${message})'), packages/db/src/authz/require.ts:21-30 ('${roleNames} cannot ${what}'), capitalised role names in apps/web/src/components/format.ts:14-29; bare possessives in the next steps (apps/web/src/app/console/specialist-queue/page.tsx:26, apps/web/src/app/console/rule-releases/page.tsx:30 'Rule releases are the platform administrators'.').
- **Rule:** PRD 7 (a permission state that explains what the role cannot do); docs/brand/BRAND.md:23 (serious, exact, warm)
- **Best fix:** Each page passes the action its screen does; the permission state reads 'As a {role in lower case with its article}, you cannot {action}.' with no parenthesis, and the next step is a whole sentence with a link onward. Exact replacements: -> 'As a contract specialist, you cannot manage organizations and practices. This part of the console is for platform administrators; your work is in the Specialist queue.'; -> 'As a platform administrator, you cannot approve the place-of-service split. A contract specialist approves it.'; -> 'As a platform administrator, you cannot work the specialist queue. It is for contract specialists.'; -> 'As a billing manager, you cannot see or change what your organization pays PaidWhole. Your organization’s administrator manages it.'; -> 'As a billing manager, you cannot invite people or change roles. Your organization’s administrator manages users and roles.'; 'Rule releases are the platform administrators'.' -> 'Rule releases are for platform administrators.'
- **Notes:** shell.tsx and the users page are in #42's file list. The missing page heading on the permission state is the accessibility lens's.

### `words-other-09`: SOC 2 evidence shows unexpanded '%s' test titles

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `console.compliance.default.1280`, `console.compliance.error.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/compliance`
- **Files:** `packages/compliance/src/controls.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/console/compliance/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/compliance.ts`
- **Evidence:** Three evidence lines read 'gives %s of another practice nothing of this practice, and changes nothing of it', 'answers %s as the persona matrix says' and 'never makes the internal %s a member of the practice’s group'. They are it.each title templates copied as the evidence's words (packages/compliance/src/controls.ts:109, :156, :339; the tests at apps/web/src/lib/routes/route-tests/define.ts:93,115). The same titles go into the JSON export and to the compliance platform, which the auditor reads.
- **Rule:** docs/brand/BRAND.md:23 (exact); brief: no internal codes in what a person reads
- **Best fix:** Give each evidence item two strings: the template the existence check matches (test) and the words a person reads (shown), and render shown on the page and in the export. Exact replacements: 'gives %s of another practice nothing of this practice, and changes nothing of it' -> 'gives every persona of another practice nothing of this practice, and changes nothing of it (one test per persona)'; 'answers %s as the persona matrix says' -> 'answers every persona as the persona matrix says (one test per persona)'; 'never makes the internal %s a member of the practice’s group' -> 'never makes an internal role a member of the practice’s group (one test per role)'.

### `words-other-10`: The screening says 'Upload a file' and 'upload again', while promising the file is 'never uploaded'

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `screening.default.1280`, `screening.error-dos.1280`, `screening.error-member-id.1280`, `screening.error-no-phi.1280`, `screening.error-zip.1280`
- **Routes:** `/screening`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/screening/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/screening/messages.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/screening/routes.ts`
- **Evidence:** Lede: 'Upload a file of totals, with no patient information, and see where the allowed amounts in it sit against the Medicare fee schedule ...' (apps/web/src/app/screening/page.tsx:38). Your file card: 'It is read in this browser and never uploaded: only its rows of totals are sent ...' (apps/web/src/components/screening/upload-form.tsx:191). Refusals: 'Column 4 ("Member ID") holds member IDs; remove it and upload again. A screening takes totals only, never patient information.' and 'Column 6 ("DOS") holds dates of service; remove it and upload again.' (apps/web/src/lib/screening/messages.ts:83, :95; the server's twin at apps/web/src/lib/screening/routes.ts:231). The privacy promise and the verbs around it disagree, on the page that asks a prospect to trust it.
- **Rule:** Brief: honest claims on the screening, no promise the product cannot prove; docs/brand/BRAND.md:23 (exact)
- **Best fix:** Use 'choose' and 'send', never 'upload', for the file. Exact replacements: lede -> 'Choose a file of totals, with no patient information: your browser reads it and sends only its rows of totals. See where the allowed amounts in it sit against the Medicare fee schedule at your locality, by code and payer product, and how much of your volume cannot be priced without a contract.'; 'remove it and upload again' -> 'remove the column, save the file and choose it again' (both refusals and routes.ts:231).
- **Notes:** The kept texts also say '30 days from your upload' and 'from the upload' (apps/worker/src/screening/wording.ts:43, apps/web/src/lib/screening/retention.ts:34); those are legal surfaces (screening-kept-*), so the same change there is the founder's sign-off.

### `words-other-11`: The screening result shows a prospect the raw reason code ENGINE_ERROR and the word 'engine'

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `screening-result.default.1280`, `screening-result.error-name.1280`
- **Routes:** `/screening/results/[resultId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/screening/coverage.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/screening/labels.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/screening/result-summary.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Coverage row: 'Original Medicare / Could not be priced / ENGINE_ERROR / The place of service is not in the approved place-of-service split'. A prospect reads ENGINE_ERROR as 'the product broke'. Causes: apps/web/src/components/screening/coverage.tsx:90 prints entry.unpricedReason in mono above its label; apps/web/src/lib/screening/labels.ts:175 ('approved place-of-service split' is an internal setting); 'Medicare rows the engine left without a price, or whose rates change inside the quarter; the reasons are under coverage below.' (apps/web/src/components/screening/result-summary.tsx:267); 'The engine left the row without a price, for the reason shown.' (labels.ts:164).
- **Rule:** Brief: no internal codes in what a person reads; docs/product/unpriced-reasons.md (a reason is shown in words); docs/brand/BRAND.md:23
- **Best fix:** Show the reason in words only (keep the code in data-reason for tests), and say 'PaidWhole', not 'the engine'. Exact replacements: 'ENGINE_ERROR / The place of service is not in the approved place-of-service split' -> 'This place of service is in neither the facility nor the non-facility list a screening prices by, so the row has no Medicare figure.'; 'Medicare rows the engine left without a price, or whose rates change inside the quarter; the reasons are under coverage below.' -> 'Medicare rows PaidWhole could not price, or whose rate changes inside the quarter. Coverage, below, gives each reason.'; 'The engine left the row without a price, for the reason shown.' -> 'PaidWhole could not price the row, for the reason shown.'

### `words-other-12`: The screening result's 'Difference' columns never say which way the sign runs

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `screening-result.default.1280`, `screening-result.error-name.1280`
- **Routes:** `/screening/results/[resultId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/screening/labels.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/screening/anomalies.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Anomalies and top-rows tables head two columns 'Difference, before the reduction across codes' and 'Difference, fully reduced across codes' (apps/web/src/lib/screening/labels.ts:46,49) holding '$629.00' and '-$266.40'. Only the summary card explains the sign in words ('Allowed in your file is above the figure ... in total', result-summary.tsx:248); in the tables a prospect cannot tell whether a positive figure means paid more or less than Medicare. The top-rows text says it obliquely: 'A row is below a figure where that difference is above zero.' (apps/web/src/components/screening/anomalies.tsx:354).
- **Rule:** Brief: dates and money formats, honest claims; severity rule: misled about money
- **Best fix:** Name the subtraction in the header and say it once in words. Exact replacements: 'Difference, before the reduction across codes' -> 'Expected less allowed, before the reduction across codes'; 'Difference, fully reduced across codes' -> 'Expected less allowed, fully reduced across codes'; 'A row is below a figure where that difference is above zero.' -> 'A positive figure means the allowed amount in your file is below Medicare’s figure; a negative one, above it.'
- **Notes:** Neither 'owed' nor 'underpaid' is needed (CO-11); 'below' and 'above' stay within the screening's words.

### `words-other-13`: The top-rows table heads its claim-line count 'Claims'

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `screening-result.default.1280`, `screening-result.error-name.1280`
- **Routes:** `/screening/results/[resultId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/screening/anomalies.tsx`
- **Evidence:** 'Rows with the largest differences' has a column 'Claims' (apps/web/src/components/screening/anomalies.tsx:370) holding claim_count, which the file's own column table defines as 'How many claim lines the row sums'; every other card on the page says 'Claim lines'. A prospect reads 50 claims where the file says 50 claim lines.
- **Rule:** Brief: plain language and counts; consistency of a count's noun across one page
- **Best fix:** Exact replacement: 'Claims' -> 'Claim lines'.

### `words-other-14`: 'An audit prices every line' promises what the product cannot: some lines stay unpriced with a reason

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `screening-result.default.1280`, `screening-result.error-name.1280`
- **Routes:** `/screening/results/[resultId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/screening/convert-form.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The convert card: 'An audit prices every line under your signed contracts and the contracts' own rules, the rows this screening could not. We already have your practice's name and your email from the screening. No patient information.' (apps/web/src/components/screening/convert-form.tsx:50). An audit leaves lines unpriced under the closed list of reasons (docs/product/unpriced-reasons.md; CLAUDE.md: 'Never a guessed price'), so 'every line' is a promise it cannot prove. The sentence's last clause also dangles, and its two straight apostrophes sit beside the curly ones of the kept text that follows.
- **Rule:** Brief: honest claims on the screening pages; docs/brand/BRAND.md:23 (never hype); CLAUDE.md conventions (unpriced reasons, never a guessed price)
- **Best fix:** Exact replacement: -> 'An audit prices your lines under your signed contracts and their own rules, including the rows this screening could not, and names the reason for any line it cannot price. We already have your practice’s name and your email from the screening. No patient information.'
- **Notes:** The kept span after it (screening-kept-convert) is a legal surface and is not changed here.

### `words-other-15`: The screening's ZIP refusals end in 'Try again later' or nothing, with no way forward

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `screening.error-zip.1280`
- **Routes:** `/screening`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/screening/routes.ts`
- **Evidence:** 'PaidWhole has not loaded Medicare’s ZIP+4 list for this ZIP code yet, so a screening cannot price it. Try again later.' (apps/web/src/lib/screening/routes.ts:701) gives no time and no other route; 'Medicare’s ZIP+4 list in force does not list this ZIP+4. If the four digits after the hyphen are right, a screening cannot price this ZIP+4 yet.' (:700) ends with no step at all. 'this ZIP code is not in the Medicare locality map in force; check it' (:710) names an internal map.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (every error with a next step)
- **Best fix:** Exact replacements: :701 -> 'PaidWhole has not loaded Medicare’s ZIP+4 list for this ZIP code yet, so a screening cannot price it. Write to support@... to ask when it will be, or request an audit.'; :700 -> 'Medicare’s ZIP+4 list does not include this ZIP+4. Check the four digits after the hyphen against your practice’s mailing address. If they are right, a screening cannot price this ZIP+4 yet: write to support@... or request an audit.'; :710 -> 'Medicare’s ZIP code list does not include this ZIP code. Check it and try again.'
- **Notes:** The first two sentences are the founder's ruled wording (Q12 of 2026-10-21, 'for counsel to confirm before the screening opens', routes.ts:691-695): changing them changes a recorded founder ruling, so this goes to the founder through the co-founder.

### `words-other-16`: The closed screening link buries its useful advice after a 13-line retention paragraph

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `screening-result.not-found.1280`
- **Routes:** `/screening/results/[resultId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/screening/results/[resultId]/not-found.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The page leads with the kept paragraph and ends it with the two things a person needs: 'A link may also be one whose screening is still being priced: the email with the link is sent once it is ready. Check that the whole link was copied.' (apps/web/src/app/screening/results/[resultId]/not-found.tsx:20-25). Someone who clicked a link too early or copied half of it reads 13 lines before learning either.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (an error state with a next step); PRD 7 (every screen has an error state with a next step)
- **Best fix:** Put the next steps first, in their own paragraph, then the links, then the kept paragraph unchanged. Exact replacement for the two sentences: 'Check that you copied the whole link from the email. If you sent your file only a few minutes ago, the screening may still be pricing: the email with its link comes once the result is ready.'
- **Notes:** The legal span (data-legal-surface="screening-kept-closed-link") keeps its words; only the non-legal sentences move and change.

### `words-other-17`: The kept notices are single run-on sentences placed before the result and as a 12 px field hint

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `screening-result.default.1280`, `screening.default.1280`, `request-audit.default.1280`, `screening-result.not-found.1280`
- **Routes:** `/screening/results/[resultId]`, `/screening`, `/request-audit`
- **Files:** `apps/worker/src/screening/wording.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/screening/retention.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/screening/result-summary.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/screening/upload-form.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/request-audit/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The result page opens with an 11-line paragraph before any figure (apps/web/src/lib/screening/retention.ts:23, rendered at apps/web/src/components/screening/result-summary.tsx:147); its record-of-counts list is one 110-word sentence (KEPT_COUNTS, apps/worker/src/screening/wording.ts:24-25). The Work email hint on /screening is the same kind of paragraph in 12 px text (upload-form.tsx:181, UPLOAD_EMAIL_HINT, retention.ts:37). The request-audit card's kept sentence says 'the campaign codes and kind of paid click of the link you came from (which a cookie of ours holds for 30 days from your first visit)'.
- **Rule:** docs/brand/BRAND.md:23 (exact, warm); plain language; legal-surfaces list (docs/product/legal-surfaces.md)
- **Best fix:** Keep every clause, change the shape: one lead sentence and two short lists. Proposed text for the result page: 'This link opens your result until 2026-11-25, or until a later screening for the same practice or from the same email address replaces it. Then we delete: the result’s rows and figures; the practice name, ZIP code and email address you gave. We keep a record of counts that names no one: ...' with KEPT_COUNTS as a bulleted list of its own clauses; show a one-line summary at the top ('This link opens until 2026-11-25. What we keep and delete is at the foot of the page.') and the full notice below the result. Make the email hint a 'What we keep' disclosure under the field at body size. 'kind of paid click' -> 'whether you came from a paid ad'.
- **Notes:** legal text: the founder's sign-off

### `words-other-18`: The terms page asks people to read terms it never shows

- **Flags:** blocking; size M
- **Screens:** `terms.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/terms`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/terms/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/onboarding.ts`
- **Evidence:** The card shows 'Version synthetic-2026-10 / Published 2026-10-26 01:25 UTC. Document terms/synthetic-2026-10, SHA-256 ca88437093319f39…' (apps/web/src/app/terms/page.tsx:34-40): a storage reference and a hash, with no link and no text. Before acceptance it says 'Before you use the workspace, read the terms of use and accept them.' (:61) beside 'I accept these terms' (:64). A customer cannot read what they accept.
- **Rule:** docs/product/legal-surfaces.md (the terms shown before 'I accept'); PRD milestone 11 (terms acceptance)
- **Best fix:** Show the terms themselves on the page (or a link that opens the exact signed-off document whose SHA-256 is recorded) above the accept button, and keep the reference and hash as a small 'Version record' line. Exact replacement for the card line: 'Published 2026-10-26. Read the terms of use (version synthetic-2026-10).' followed by the text or the link; 'Document terms/synthetic-2026-10, SHA-256 ca884370…' moves to a muted 'Version record: terms/synthetic-2026-10, SHA-256 ca884370…'.
- **Notes:** legal text: the founder's sign-off. The combined audit places the terms shown before 'I accept' in the legal-surfaces pull request (items #51 and #52).

### `words-other-19`: The invite refusal says 'change their role below' (the list is above) and sends the customer to a platform administrator

- **Screens:** `settings.users.error.1280`

The Members table with its Change role controls is above the Invite someone card, and a customer has no way to reach a platform administrator.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (every error with a next step)
- **Best fix:** Exact replacement: -> 'Email: this address cannot be invited from here. If the person is already a member, change their role under Members above. If not, write to support@... and PaidWhole will add them.'

### `words-other-20`: 'Billing' names two things: what the organization pays PaidWhole, and the billing manager's claims work

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `settings.billing.empty.1280`, `settings.billing.default.1280`, `settings.billing-org.error-forbidden.1280`, `settings.billing-org.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/settings/billing`, `/settings/billing/[organizationId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/settings/billing/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/settings/billing/[organizationId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The account rail's 'Billing' (apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx:288-290) opens PaidWhole's own subscription and invoices. A billing manager, whose role name says billing, opens it and reads 'No organization's billing to manage / Billing is managed by your organization's administrator (the practice administrator of a group, the firm principal of a firm).' (apps/web/src/app/settings/billing/page.tsx:31), and the permission state says a billing manager 'cannot choose the organization’s plan ...'. The H1 'Billing' and 'Billing: SYNTHETIC OTHER GROUP' do not say whose bill it is.
- **Rule:** docs/brand/BRAND.md:23 (exact); plain language
- **Best fix:** Name the page for what it holds. Exact replacements: rail item 'Billing' -> 'Subscription'; H1 'Billing' -> 'Subscription and invoices'; intro -> 'What each organization you administer pays PaidWhole: its plan, payment method and invoices. This page holds the organization’s name and its invoices only, never a patient’s or a claim’s details.'; 'Billing: SYNTHETIC OTHER GROUP' -> 'Subscription: SYNTHETIC OTHER GROUP'; empty state 'No organization's billing to manage / Billing is managed by ...' -> 'No subscription to manage / Your organization’s administrator manages what it pays PaidWhole (a group’s practice administrator, a firm’s principal).'
- **Notes:** shell.tsx is in #42's files; the billing pages are also in PR #47 (#135, the bank account first), so land after both or fold into #47. Whether the rail shows the item to roles that administer no organization is the flows lens's.

### `words-other-21`: The model budget is typed in cents in a free-text 'Value' field

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `console.models.default.1280`, `console.models.error.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/models`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/console/models/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/console-models.ts`
- **Evidence:** Set a flag has one text field 'Value' with the hint 'on or off for a switch; whole cents for a budget (500 is $5.00)' (apps/web/src/app/console/models/page.tsx:81); the refusal reads 'value: a budget in whole cents, such as 500 for $5.00' (apps/web/src/lib/routes/console-models.ts:158). A slip of two digits sets a practice's daily budget 100 times too high or too low, and a switch is typed as the word on or off.
- **Rule:** docs/product/PRD.md:256 (money rendered from cents in the practice's locale; the person never handles cents); CLAUDE.md rule 2 (cents are storage, not entry)
- **Best fix:** Show the control the chosen flag needs: an On/Off choice for a switch, and 'Daily budget (dollars)' for a budget, which code converts to integer cents. Exact replacements: hint -> 'Such as 5.00.'; refusal -> 'Daily budget: enter dollars and cents, such as 5.00.'
- **Notes:** After this refusal the form resets to 'Kill switch' for 'Everyone' (checker note on console.models.error.1280); that is the states lens's finding.

### `words-other-22`: Status values and record types appear as raw lowercase codes

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `console.prospects.default.1280`, `console.tickets.default.1280`, `console.tickets.empty.1280`, `console.organizations.default.1280`, `console.organization.default.1280`, `console.organization.empty.1280`, `console.terms.default.1280`, `console.status.default.1280`, `console.rule-releases.default.1280`, `settings.billing-org.default.1280`, `settings.users.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/prospects`, `/console/tickets`, `/console/organizations`, `/console/organizations/[organizationId]`, `/console/terms`, `/console/status`, `/console/rule-releases`, `/settings/billing/[organizationId]`, `/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/console/prospects/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/tickets/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/terms/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/status/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/console/rule-releases/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/settings/billing/[organizationId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/copy/words.ts`
- **Evidence:** Current: badge 'new' (apps/web/src/app/console/prospects/page.tsx:77); filters 'open answered closed all', badge 'open' and 'screen: remittances' (apps/web/src/app/console/tickets/page.tsx:38-40, 69, 77); badge 'active' (apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/page.tsx:70); header 'group · active · created 2026-10-26' and 'internal · active' (apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx:50); member badges 'active', 'invited' (:148); 'in force' (console terms); Impact select 'minor' (apps/web/src/app/console/status/page.tsx:114) and an incident line built from the raw impact and component keys (:92); customer pages: 'Monitoring, monthly, active' (apps/web/src/app/settings/billing/[organizationId]/page.tsx:83, where a Stripe status such as past_due would show as is) and 'active, no MFA' (apps/web/src/app/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users/page.tsx:69); record types 'assistant_modifier', 'ncci_ptp', 'carc' (rule releases), though labelOf() exists in apps/web/src/lib/copy/words.ts:27.
- **Rule:** Brief: no raw keys or internal codes in what a person reads; apps/web/src/lib/copy/words.ts (a code is shown as a label in sentence case, initialisms in capitals)
- **Best fix:** One label map per enum, in sentence case, used wherever the value is shown; labelOf() for record types with CARC and RARC added to INITIALISMS. Exact replacements: 'new' -> 'New'; 'open answered closed all' -> 'Open  Answered  Closed  All'; 'screen: remittances' -> 'Screen: Remittances'; 'group · active · created 2026-10-26' -> 'Practice group · Active · created 2026-10-26'; 'internal · active' -> 'Internal · Active'; 'active' / 'invited' -> 'Active' / 'Invited'; 'in force' -> 'In force'; 'minor' -> 'Minor'; 'Started …; minor; uploads, web' -> 'Started …; minor impact; Uploads and parsing, Web application'; 'Monitoring, monthly, active' -> 'Monitoring, monthly: Active' (past_due -> 'Past due'); 'active, no MFA' -> 'Active, two-step verification not set up'; 'ncci_ptp' -> 'NCCI PTP', 'carc' -> 'CARC'.
- **Notes:** The users page is in #42's files; the billing page is in PR #47 (#135) but this is outside #135's scope. The customer-facing two (billing status, users state) carry the should_fix; the console ones alone would be polish.

### `words-other-23`: The copy lint does not read words these screens show, and has no rule for raw keys, placeholders or straight apostrophes

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `console.compliance.default.1280`, `console.screening.error-forbidden.1280`, `console.billing.error.1280`, `console.status.error.1280`
- **Routes:** `/console/compliance`, `/console/screening`, `/console/billing`, `/console/status`
- **Files:** `tools/brand-copy/scan.ts`, `tools/brand-copy/scan.test.ts`
- **Evidence:** tools/brand-copy/scan.ts:47-75 (COPY_ROOTS, COPY_FILES) leaves out packages/db/src/authz/permissions.ts (every permission state's reason), packages/compliance/src/controls.ts (the evidence titles with '%s'), apps/worker/src/billing/account.ts (the console billing refusal) and Zod's default messages (no source at all). Its rules (scan.ts:8-38, FORBIDDEN_TERMS :117-122) catch only the brand's forbidden terms, so a key before a colon ('routingNumber: ...'), a '%s', an internal planning note ('named (PRD 16)', apps/web/src/app/console/compliance/page.tsx:143; '(milestone 12)' on practice settings) or a straight apostrophe all pass. CO-11's own required words hold on every screen in this set.
- **Rule:** Change Order 001 CO-11 (a copy lint over the product's words); docs/adr/0058 (words held by checks)
- **Best fix:** Add those three files to COPY_FILES and add rules: 'placeholder' (/%[sdif]/ in copy), 'internal-reference' (/\((PRD|milestone|ADR|audit) ?#?\d+/i in copy), and 'straight-apostrophe' (a ' between letters in copy). Add the ActionForm test of words-other-01 and the Zod test of words-other-02, so raw keys and default messages fail in CI rather than in review.

(apps/web/src/app/settings/organizations/[organizationId]/users/page.tsx:100) and 'active, no MFA' (:69); the console says 'no MFA yet' (apps/web/src/app/console/organizations/[organizationId]/page.tsx:149).

Connect it and try again.' on the registration step: Problem's title is fixed for both flows (apps/web/src/components/hardware-key.tsx:31-32; registration's catch at :116). The catch also covers a cancelled or timed-out prompt, which 'did not answer' misdescribes.

Connect it and try again.' -> 'The key was not registered: it did not answer, or the request was cancelled. Connect it, press Register hardware key and touch the key when it asks.'

### `words-other-31`: Filtered lists show one empty sentence for every filter, and one names a raw path

- **Routes:** `/console/prospects`, `/console/tickets`

Under 'answered', while an open ticket exists: 'No tickets here / Tickets opened from any screen's help appear here.' (apps/web/src/app/console/tickets/page.tsx:44).
- **Rule:** PRD 7 (every screen has an empty state that says what to do next)
- **Best fix:** One empty sentence per filter. Exact replacements: prospects New -> 'No new requests / Requests from the public audit form and from screenings appear here.'; Contacted -> 'No contacted requests / A request moves here when you mark it contacted.'; Converted -> 'No converted requests / A request moves here when you create its practice group.'; Declined -> 'No declined requests / A request moves here when you decline it with a reason.'; tickets open -> 'No open tickets / Tickets people open from a screen’s help, or by mail to support@..., appear here.'; answered -> 'No answered tickets / A ticket moves here when you mark it answered.'; closed -> 'No closed tickets / A ticket moves here when you mark it closed.'

- **Screens:** `screening-result.default.1280`, `settings.billing-org.error-forbidden.1280`, `settings.billing.empty.1280`

### `words-other-44`: The screening never says how soon the result comes until after the file is sent

The 60-second promise is backed: production opens the screening only after the release's binding capacity run passes with time enforced (docs/adr/0062, owner ruling 13; tools/screening-capacity/launch-check.ts), and an upload in the waiting tier is outside it (ruling 14), which 'usually' covers.
- **Rule:** Brief: the screening's 60-second promise and honest claims; time to value
- **Best fix:** Add to the lede, after words-other-10's text: 'The result comes to your email as a link, usually within a minute.' No figure beyond that: '60 seconds' is the measured line for a 50,000-row file, not a promise for every visit.
- **Notes:** The sent page's wording is the founder's (Q12 of 2026-10-21, for counsel); this repeats it before sending, so it goes with that ruling to the founder through the co-founder. apps/web/src/app/screening/page.tsx is in #42's files.
