## words-practice

### `words-practice-01`: Counts print "1 lines" and "1 cases": counted() is used in 2 files, and about 25 other sites put a fixed plural after a number

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.home.default.1280`, `p.home.error-last-run-failed.1280`, `p.notifications.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/notifications`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/regulations/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/mapping/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/releases/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/contracts/review.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/monitoring/display.test.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/monitoring/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/packets/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/pricing/diff.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/pricing/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/reports/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/reports/documents.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/uploads/receipts.tsx`, `tools/brand-copy/scan.ts`
- **Evidence:** p.home.default.1280: "A payer took back -$18.00 on 1 lines of a case: the lines are reopened and recovered (posted or deposited) is reduced by that amount." p.home.error-last-run-failed.1280 and p.notifications.default.1280: "1 cases nearing their deadline" (digest) and "validated $90.00 on 3 lines; 1 cases; 0 lines flagged (investigatory)" (pricing run). Cause: apps/web/src/components/monitoring/display.tsx:34-35 (run), :55 (recoupment) and :67 (digest) interpolate count(...) before a fixed plural noun; the counted() helper the earlier tour added (apps/web/src/lib/copy/words.ts:8) is not used here. The worker's own digest page gets it right with plural() (apps/worker/src/digest/view.ts:146), so the same figure reads correctly on the digest and wrongly in its notification. The same pattern is live wherever a count can be 1, though these shots happen to show other counts: the packet's line schedule "{lines} lines on {claims} claims of {patients} patients" (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx:268; review/page.tsx:82), the case summary "({openLines} of {lines} lines not written off)" and "({n} lines, struck through below)" (cases/[caseId]/page.tsx:77, :80), mapping "{waiting} of {total} lines" (mapping/page.tsx:141), reports "{flagged_lines} lines" (reports/page.tsx:148), regulations "{lines} lines on {claims} claims" (abstraction/regulations/page.tsx:83), release impact (releases/page.tsx:88), interest "{days_late} days late" (components/packets/display.tsx:357), pricing coverage (components/pricing/display.tsx:84; diff.tsx:58), report documents (components/reports/documents.tsx:37, :57; display.tsx:158), receipts "{total} files" (components/uploads/receipts.tsx:197), contract windows "{days} days" (components/contracts/review.tsx:31-40). counted() (apps/web/src/lib/copy/words.ts:8) is imported only by components/reports/roi.tsx and components/screening/result-summary.tsx.
- **Rule:** apps/web/src/lib/copy/words.ts:2-3 (a count with its noun, singular for one); the 2026-10-25 tour's C1 copy defect
- **Best fix:** Build every count in NotificationText with counted(): run: `${counted(priced, 'line')} of ${counted(lines,'line')} priced` is wrong for "3 of 3", so write "3 of 3 lines priced" as `${priced} of ${counted(lines, 'line')} priced`; then "validated $90.00 on ${counted(n,'line')}; ${counted(cases,'case')} opened; ${counted(flagged,'line')} flagged (investigatory)". Recoupment: "A payer took back $18.00 from ${counted(n,'line')} of a case: ${n===1?'the line is':'the lines are'} reopened, and recovered (deposited) drops by that amount." Digest: "${counted(n,'case')} nearing ${n===1?'its':'their'} deadline". Exact replacements for the shots: "on 1 lines of a case: the lines are reopened" -> "from 1 line of a case: the line is reopened"; "1 cases nearing their deadline" -> "1 case nearing its deadline"; "1 cases;" -> "1 case opened;". Add a display.test.tsx case for count 1 of each kind. Then add a copy-lint rule (tools/brand-copy) that fails a template or JSX expression followed by a literal plural noun ("} lines", "} cases", "} claims", "} entries") so the class cannot come back. Apply counted() at every site listed in the evidence in the same change, not only the notifications.
- **Notes:** Confirms and extends the 2026-10-25 tour's "on 1 lines". components/monitoring/display.tsx is in PR #42's file list; the other sites are not, so they can land first and the notification half after #42 merges. The scan that found the sites was a regex over the source; a few hits (fixed constants, textarea rows) are not copy and are left out of the list. display.test.tsx is a new file that PR #42 adds.

### `words-practice-02`: "A payer took back -$18.00": a signed ledger amount printed inside a verb that already says the direction

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.home.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/notifications`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/monitoring/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/monitoring/display.test.tsx`
- **Evidence:** p.home.default.1280, Notifications card: "A payer took back -$18.00 on 1 lines of a case". "Took back minus eighteen dollars" reads as the payer paying $18.00. Cause: apps/web/src/components/monitoring/display.tsx:53 prints totals.recouped, which is the ledger's negative recouped entry, through usd() unchanged (display.tsx:21-24).
- **Rule:** PRD.md:256 (money from cents, read correctly) and BRAND.md:23 (exact; a ledger you trust); PRD 13 (recouped entries are negative, so the words must carry the sign once)
- **Best fix:** Print the magnitude where the verb carries the direction: "A payer took back $18.00 from 1 line of a case". In display.tsx use the absolute value of the recouped cents (a bigint negate, never a float) and keep the signed figure only in ledger tables, where the column is "Amount". Add a test that a recoupment notification never contains "-$".
- **Notes:** Same file as words-practice-01; land together. display.test.tsx is a new file that PR #42 adds.

### `words-practice-03`: Practice record shows the raw status code "active"

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.home.default.1280`, `p.home.empty.1280`, `p.home.error-last-run-failed.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Practice record card: "Status  active" in lower case, the database value. Cause: apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx:424 renders {practice.status} with no label map, while every other code on the page goes through a names map (VIA, JOB_NAMES, OUTCOME_NAMES at page.tsx:28, :43, :132).
- **Rule:** The brief's plain-language rule (no raw codes in what a person reads); apps/web/src/lib/copy/words.ts:22-26 (a code shown as a label in sentence case)
- **Best fix:** Map the status to words with the reason a person can act on: "active" -> "Active"; "suspended" -> "Suspended: new runs have stopped (see Billing)"; any other value through labelOf(). Use one PRACTICE_STATUS_TEXT map shared with the console's practice pages.

### `words-practice-04`: Form refusals name the field by its code key ("periodTo:", "contractVersionId:", "lineMateriality:") instead of its label

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.abstraction.error-tag.1280`, `p.abstraction-ledger.error-adjust-zero.1280`, `p.abstraction-version.error-amend.1280`, `p.cases.error.1280`, `p.case.error.1280`, `p.submissions.error.1280`, `p.claims.error.1280`, `p.contracts.error.1280`, `p.contract.error.1280`, `p.line.error.1280`, `p.plan.error.1280`, `p.pricing-runs.error.1280`, `p.roi.error.1280`, `p.correspondence.error.1280`, `p.defaults.error.1280`, `p.reconciliation.error-reverse.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions`, `/p/[practiceId]/claims`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/correspondence`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/field.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/packets/version-form.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/packets/export-status.tsx`, `tools/brand-copy/scan.ts`
- **Evidence:** Every refusal list item reads "<key>: <message>": "title: …", "amount: an adjustment changes the amount", "effectiveFrom: starts inside the amended version’s window", "lineMateriality: dollars and cents, such as 5.00", "text: say why for \"other\", and only for \"other\"", "amount: what the payer accepted, in dollars and cents", "payerClaimControlNumber: no claim of this practice has that payer claim control number", "effectiveFrom: versions of one contract never overlap…", "note: required", "contractVersionId: choose the signed-off contract version the plan is paid under", "toMonth: the same month as the first, or later", "periodTo: the period ends on or after its first day", "name: no @ here: an email goes in its own field", "staffRatePerHour: the pursuit cost needs the staff rate and both times together, or none of them", "reason: say why the match is reversed". Only Pricing settings shows a label ("Billing ZIP: …", p.pricing-settings.error.1280). Cause: apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx:76 prints `labels[issue.field] ?? issue.field`, and only 2 of the 112 <ActionForm> uses pass `labels` (both in apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/pricing/page.tsx:97 and :112). apps/web/src/components/packets/version-form.tsx:155 and export-status.tsx:93 print issue.field with no label lookup at all.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:256 (every error shown with a next step, in words the person can act on); the brief's plain-language rule (no raw field keys); WCAG 2.1 SC 3.3.1 Error Identification (the item in error is identified and described in text)
- **Best fix:** Make a raw key impossible to render. (1) <Field> registers its name and visible label with the enclosing ActionForm through a small FormLabels context, so every refusal is named by the label the person sees, with no per-form map to forget; controls not built with <Field> pass `labels` explicitly. (2) When a key has no label, ActionForm shows the message alone, never the key. (3) Use the same lookup in version-form.tsx and export-status.tsx. (4) Add an ActionForm test and a copy-lint rule that fail on a camelCase token followed by ":" in a rendered refusal. Exact replacements for the shots: "periodTo:" -> "Last day:"; "toMonth:" -> "Last month:"; "contractVersionId:" -> "Contract version:"; "lineMateriality:" -> "Per line (dollars):"; "payerClaimControlNumber:" -> "Payer claim control number:"; "effectiveFrom:" -> "Effective from:" (contracts, contract) and "Amendment effective from:" (abstraction version); "title:" -> "Title of a new contract:"; "amount:" -> "Change in dollars:" (ledger) and "Amount accepted:" (submissions); "text:" -> "Why:"; "note:" -> "Note:"; "name:" -> "Name or office:"; "staffRatePerHour:" -> "Staff rate per hour:"; "reason:" -> "Why the match is reversed:".
- **Notes:** The message wording itself is words-practice-07. WCAG 3.3.1 also wants the message beside the field (today only aria-describedby points at the summary); that placement belongs to the accessibility lens and lands in the same ActionForm change. The <Field> component's path is assumed from its use in settings/pricing/page.tsx:100; confirm the file name when building.

### `words-practice-05`: "Check the fields marked below." sits under the fields it points to

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.abstraction.error-tag.1280`, `p.abstraction-version.error-amend.1280`, `p.cases.error.1280`, `p.case.error.1280`, `p.submissions.error.1280`, `p.claims.error.1280`, `p.contracts.error.1280`, `p.contract.error.1280`, `p.line.error.1280`, `p.plan.error.1280`, `p.pricing-runs.error.1280`, `p.roi.error.1280`, `p.correspondence.error.1280`, `p.defaults.error.1280`, `p.pricing-settings.error.1280`, `p.reconciliation.error-reverse.1280`, `p.abstraction-ledger.error-adjust-zero.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/**`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts`
- **Evidence:** In every refusal shot the "Not done" box with "Check the fields marked below." is rendered after the form's fields, just above the submit button (for example p.correspondence.error.1280: Name or office is at the top of the form and the box is at its foot; p.abstraction.error-tag.1280 and p.contract.error.1280: the box sits beside or under the marked field). Cause: the sentence is fixed in apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts:162 (and :180 for sign-in), while apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx:66-84 renders {children} first and the Alert after them.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:256 (every error with a next step: the step must be true); BRAND.md:23 (exact)
- **Best fix:** Render the refusal summary at the top of the form, before its fields (action-form.tsx: move the Alert above {children}), which makes "marked below" true and puts the summary where the eye lands after submitting, and keep each field's own message under the field. If the summary must stay at the foot of a form, the sentence becomes "Check the fields marked above."; never "below" for a box that is below. Add an ActionForm test that the summary precedes the first control in document order.
- **Notes:** Placement overlaps the accessibility lens; the words fix is the same change.

### `words-practice-06`: After a refusal the form shows other values than the ones refused, so the message contradicts what is on screen and a retry can record the wrong choice

- **Flags:** blocking; size M
- **Screens:** `p.roi.error.1280`, `p.cases.error.1280`, `p.pricing-settings.error.1280`, `p.submissions.error.1280`, `p.case.error.1280`, `p.contracts.error.1280`, `p.contract.error.1280`, `p.pricing-runs.error.1280`, `p.correspondence.error.1280`, `p.defaults.error.1280`, `p.abstraction-version.error-amend.1280`, `p.claims.error.1280`, `p.abstraction-ledger.error-adjust-zero.1280`, `p.reconciliation.error-reverse.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/pricing`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/correspondence`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/field.tsx`
- **Evidence:** p.roi.error.1280: "periodTo: the period ends on or after its first day" beside First day 07/01/2026 and Last day 09/30/2026, a valid period. p.cases.error.1280: "lineMateriality: dollars and cents, such as 5.00" beside a field reading 5.00. p.pricing-settings.error.1280: "Billing ZIP: five digits, or ZIP+4" beside 27601. p.submissions.error.1280: the Response select reads "Accepted" after a "Partial" response was refused, so fixing the amount and pressing Record records Accepted. p.case.error.1280: the write-off reason is back to "Window passed" after "Other" was refused while the message talks about "other". p.contracts.error.1280: Contract is back to "A new contract with this payer" under a message about overlapping versions of an existing contract. Cause: apps/web/src/components/action-form.tsx:32 uses useActionState with uncontrolled fields; React 19 resets a form after its action, so each control returns to its defaultValue (the stored value, or the first option), and ActionState (apps/web/src/lib/http/next.ts:124-129) carries no submitted values back.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:256 (every error with a next step; no screen depends on client-side state that a refresh loses); BRAND.md:23 (exact); WCAG 2.1 SC 3.3.1 and 3.3.3 (the error is described against what the person entered)
- **Best fix:** Return the submitted values with every refusal (ActionState gains `values`, taken from the FormData in runAction, never including file fields or passwords) and have ActionForm and <Field> prefer them over the stored defaults, so the refused value stays on screen beside its message and every other choice survives. Add a test per form kind (date, month, select, checkbox, money) that a refusal keeps the entered value. Then each message can quote the value ("Last day 06/30/2026 is before the first day 07/01/2026"; see words-practice-07).
- **Notes:** Blocking because two forms (payer response, line write-off) then record a different status than the person chose if they retry after fixing only the named field. The flows and states lenses will likely raise the same cause; one fix serves all.

### `words-practice-07`: Field messages state a rule instead of what to do, and some give a step the role cannot take

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.abstraction-ledger.error-adjust-zero.1280`, `p.abstraction-version.error-amend.1280`, `p.case.error.1280`, `p.claims.error.1280`, `p.contract.error.1280`, `p.contracts.error.1280`, `p.correspondence.error.1280`, `p.defaults.error.1280`, `p.line.error.1280`, `p.plan.error.1280`, `p.pricing-runs.error.1280`, `p.reconciliation.error-reverse.1280`, `p.roi.error.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/claims`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/routes/reconciliation.ts`, `packages/contracts/src/services.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/pricing.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/schemas.ts`, `packages/triage/src/cases.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/settings.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/reports.ts`
- **Evidence:** "an adjustment changes the amount" (apps/web/src/lib/routes/reconciliation.ts:136); "starts inside the amended version’s window" (packages/contracts/src/services.ts:845), which reads as a description of the refused date; "versions of one contract never overlap: amend the version in force, or supersede it" (services.ts:259) shown to a billing manager, who can do neither (the contract page itself says the contract specialist drafts amendments); "the same month as the first, or later" (apps/web/src/lib/routes/pricing.ts:315); "the period ends on or after its first day"; "required" (apps/web/src/lib/routes/schemas.ts:19); "say why for \"other\", and only for \"other\"" (packages/triage/src/cases.ts:111, straight quotes and lower-case "other" while the option reads “Other”); "the pursuit cost needs the staff rate and both times together, or none of them".
- **Rule:** PRD.md:256 and PRD.md:260 (every error state with a next step); docs/brand/BRAND.md:23 (serious, exact, warm)
- **Best fix:** Write each message as an instruction that names the value and the way out, in the role's reach. Replacements: "an adjustment changes the amount" -> "Enter a change other than 0.00: a minus sign lowers the entry, such as -5.00."; "starts inside the amended version’s window" -> "Choose a date after version 1 starts (2026-01-01) and before it ends; an amendment starts inside the version it amends."; for the billing manager's tag and add forms, "versions of one contract never overlap: amend the version in force, or supersede it" -> "Version 1 already covers this date (2026-01-01 through 2026-12-31). Choose a date after 2026-12-31, or ask the contract specialist to draft an amendment from this document (Help and support)." (keep the specialist's wording on the abstraction screens); "the same month as the first, or later" -> "Choose a last month the same as the first month or later, or leave it blank for one month."; "the period ends on or after its first day" -> "Choose a last day on or after the first day."; "required" -> "Write the note before adding it." (each requiredText caller passes its own sentence); "say why for \"other\", and only for \"other\"" -> "Say why when the reason is “Other”; leave Why blank for any other reason."; "the pursuit cost needs the staff rate and both times together, or none of them" -> "Fill in the staff rate and both times, or leave all three blank to clear the pursuit cost.". Dates in these sentences come from the record, through the shared date formatter (words-practice-10). Also: "no @ here: an email goes in its own field" -> "Put an email address in Email; this field is for a name or an office."; "say why the match is reversed" -> "Say why the match is reversed: the ledger keeps the reason with the reversing entry." A search that finds nothing is not a field error: "payerClaimControlNumber: no claim of this practice has that payer claim control number" under "Check the fields marked below." -> title "No claim found" and "No claim of this practice has payer claim control number SYNCCN123. Check the number on the remittance, or filter the table below by payer and service month."
- **Notes:** settings.ts (the pursuit and correspondence messages) is in PR #42's list; the rest are not. The route db tests that assert the old strings (for example apps/web/src/lib/routes/contracts-routes.db.test.ts:473) change with them. The ROI period message's file is assumed to be lib/routes/reports.ts; confirm.

### `words-practice-08`: The permission state repeats the role, quotes the permission's internal description and says "do this here" instead of naming the page

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.abstraction.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.abstraction-version.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.abstraction-regulations.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.abstraction-ledger.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.packet-review.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.packet-reviews.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.packet.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.report-review.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.access-log.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.support-access.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.digest.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.contracts.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.home.error-forbidden.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/**`
- **Files:** `packages/ui/src/states/permission-state.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/**/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** p.abstraction-regulations.error-forbidden.1280: "As Billing manager, you cannot do this here (Billing manager cannot edit rate records)." on a page about confirming a regulation. p.packet-review.error-forbidden.1280: "As Billing manager, you cannot do this here (Billing manager cannot validate audit findings against contracts and sign the attestation)." p.support-access.error-forbidden.1280: "(Billing manager cannot read the practice’s access log)" on a support-grant page. The page itself is never named (no heading). Causes: packages/ui/src/states/permission-state.tsx:19 renders "As {role}, you cannot {cannot}."; apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx:361 passes cannot=`do this here (${message})`; the message is packages/db/src/authz/require.ts:29 "${roleNames(roles)} cannot ${what}" with what = the permission's description (packages/db/src/authz/permissions.ts:55-96), so the role is named twice and the words describe the permission the route checks, not the page.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:260 (a permission state that explains what the role cannot do); PRD.md:256 (every error with a next step); the brief's plain-language rule
- **Best fix:** Give Forbidden a page-specific `cannot` from the page (each page already passes a section; add a short `what` such as "confirm a Medicare Advantage regulation", "attest a packet's export", "read a support grant's record") and drop the permission description from what a person reads (keep it in the audit event). PermissionState then reads, with a heading naming the page: title "Regulations to confirm" and body "Billing managers cannot confirm a regulation. The contract specialist confirms it under a support grant; you see the result on the packet." Replacements for the shots: "As Billing manager, you cannot do this here (Billing manager cannot edit rate records)." -> "Billing managers cannot edit a contract's terms." (abstraction, abstraction version) / "Billing managers cannot confirm a regulation." (regulations); "As Billing manager, you cannot do this here (Billing manager cannot validate audit findings against contracts and sign the attestation)." -> "Billing managers cannot attest a packet's export."; "As Billing manager, you cannot do this here (Billing manager cannot read the practice’s access log)." -> "Billing managers cannot open a support grant's record."; "As Contract specialist, you cannot do this here (Contract specialist cannot read dashboards, reports, the ledger and ROI statements)." -> "Contract specialists cannot open this practice's digests."
- **Notes:** shell.tsx is in PR #42's file list, so this lands after #42. The missing page heading is also an accessibility point (WCAG 2.4.6); the words fix supplies it.

### `words-practice-09`: "Ask the practice's administrator for access." is the next step for internal staff and for things no administrator can grant

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.home.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.contracts.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.digest.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.packet.error-forbidden.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`, `/p/[practiceId]/digests/[digestId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/contracts/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/digests/[digestId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** A contract specialist (internal role, specialist rail visible) on the practice home, Contracts and a digest is told "Ask the practice's administrator for access." (p.home.error-forbidden.1280, p.contracts.error-forbidden.1280, p.digest.error-forbidden.1280). A practice's administrator cannot grant an internal role anything: internal roles reach a practice only through a support grant (packages/db/src/authz/permissions.ts:110-111, support_grant_required). p.packet.error-forbidden.1280 tells a practice administrator to "open the case to see its state" with no link to it. Cause: 32 pages hard-code nextStep="Ask the practice's administrator for access." and 7 settings pages "Ask the practice administrator." (for example apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx:153, contracts/page.tsx, digests/[digestId]/page.tsx:21).
- **Rule:** PRD.md:256 and :260 (every error and permission state with a true next step)
- **Best fix:** Choose the next step in Forbidden from who is refused, not per page: an internal role gets "Work on this practice from the Specialist queue, under the ticket's support grant." with a link to the queue; a customer role gets "Your role is set by the practice's administrators. Ask one of them if your work needs this page." with a link to Settings, Users and roles where the viewer can see who they are; a page whose work belongs to another named role keeps its specific sentence and adds its way back ("Open the case" as a link on the packet page). Replace the two variant sentences ("Ask the practice's administrator for access." and "Ask the practice administrator.") with that one rule.
- **Notes:** Same change as words-practice-08 (shell.tsx, after #42).

### `words-practice-10`: Straight and curly apostrophes mixed, often in one message

- **Flags:** polish; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.home.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.contracts.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.home.empty.1280`, `p.abstraction.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.abstraction-ledger.default.1280`, `p.notifications.empty.1280`, `p.submissions.empty.1280`, `p.coverage.default.1280`, `p.releases.default.1280`, `p.case.error.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/**`
- **Files:** `tools/brand-copy/scan.ts`, `tools/brand-copy/scan.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/**/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/shell.tsx`, `packages/triage/src/cases.ts`
- **Evidence:** p.contracts.error-forbidden.1280: "(Contract specialist cannot open the practice’s workspace)." (curly) above "Ask the practice's administrator for access." (straight). p.home.empty.1280: "The practice's workspace … each file's receipt" beside "this practice's data". p.abstraction-ledger.default.1280: "Correct an entry's amount … the entry's evidence", "The practice's ledger", "a payer's remittance" (straight) on a page whose other copy is curly. p.coverage.default.1280: "the specialist's question"; p.releases.default.1280: "The release's notes (RELEASE.md)"; p.case.error.1280: 'say why for "other"' (straight double quotes). The app's copy holds about 620 curly apostrophes and about 70 straight ones (60 in double-quoted JSX props, 10 &apos;), for example apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx:186, abstraction/ledger/page.tsx:99,115,125,129, notifications/page.tsx:47, cases/[caseId]/submissions/page.tsx:296,368, and every Forbidden nextStep. The copy lint (tools/brand-copy/scan.ts:21-36) has no punctuation rule.
- **Rule:** The brief's typography rule (curly against straight apostrophes); BRAND.md:23 (exact); the house style the copy already follows in ~90% of strings
- **Best fix:** Replace every straight apostrophe and straight double quote in product copy with ’ and “ ”, and add a copy-lint rule (tools/brand-copy) that fails a straight ' between letters or a straight " inside read text, with the existing exemption comment for code samples. Examples: "Ask the practice's administrator for access." -> "Ask the practice’s administrator for access."; "Correct an entry's amount" -> "Correct an entry’s amount"; 'say why for "other"' -> "say why for “Other”".
- **Notes:** Touches pages in #42's list (support-access page, shell.tsx); the lint rule itself does not.

### `words-practice-11`: Materiality and pursuit settings show raw cents as dollars ("$500 per line", "$25000 per case"), so saving the form unchanged sets 100 times the threshold

- **Flags:** blocking; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.defaults.default.1280`, `p.defaults.error.1280`, `p.cases.empty.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/routes/settings.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** p.defaults.default.1280 (NORTH, billing manager): "Now $500 per line and $25000 per case." with Per line (dollars) prefilled 500 and Per case (dollars) prefilled 25000. The same practice's Cases screen (p.cases.empty.1280) reads "per-line threshold ($5.00) … per-case threshold ($250.00)" with fields 5.00 and 250.00. Cause: apps/web/src/lib/routes/settings.ts:292 `const text = (cents: Cents) => centsToString(cents)` returns the integer cents as text ("500"), which apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults/page.tsx:40 prints after a typed "$" and :48-49 put in the dollars fields; the save parses those fields as dollars (settings.ts:274 'dollars and cents, such as 5.00'). The pursuit line has the same fault: page.tsx:62 `$${pursuit.staffRateCentsPerHour} an hour` and :70 defaultValue={pursuit?.staffRateCentsPerHour}. The figures also miss the thousands separator and the mono face.
- **Rule:** CLAUDE.md rule 2 and PRD.md:256 (every money value rendered from cents, in the practice's locale); .claude/rules/brand.md (every money figure in the mono font with tabular numerals); severity per the brief: misleads about money
- **Best fix:** In settings.ts:292 return dollars text with formatCents ("5.00", "250.00", "45.00") for the form fields, and render the description's figures with <Money/> (formatUsd, en-US): "Now $500 per line and $25000 per case." -> "Now $5.00 per line and $250.00 per case."; "$4500 an hour" -> "$45.00 an hour". Add a route test that the defaults page returns "5.00" for 500 cents and an e2e check that saving the untouched form leaves materiality at 500 cents. Then make the two materiality forms one (Cases and Settings now word and save the same setting differently: "Save the thresholds" against "Save materiality"), keeping it on Settings with a link from Cases.
- **Notes:** settings.ts is in PR #42's file list. Given the severity, land it first after #42 merges, or ask Lane A to fold the one-line formatter fix into #42.

### `words-practice-12`: Build references shown to customers: "(milestone 12)", "(golden question Q34)", "verified to level L2", "(CON-02)", "DEADLINE_UNVERIFIED", "for now"

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.agreements.empty.1280`, `p.data.default.1280`, `p.abstraction-regulations.default.1280`, `p.abstraction-version.default.1280`, `p.abstraction-version.error-amend.1280`, `p.packet.error-export-blocked.1280`, `p.contract-version.default.1280`, `p.contract-version.error-page-missing.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/settings/agreements`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/data`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/regulations`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/agreements/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/data/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/packets/view.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/versions/[versionId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/contracts/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/contracts/display.ts`, `tools/brand-copy/scan.ts`
- **Evidence:** p.agreements.empty.1280: "A billing firm reaches this practice only through a grant you approve (milestone 12)." (settings/agreements/page.tsx:90). p.data.default.1280: "The export and deletion workflows arrive in a later release (milestone 12)." (settings/data/page.tsx:31). p.abstraction-regulations.default.1280 and the packet's deadline: "… is a product-wide question (golden question Q34) that the contract specialist is answering … even where its Medicare window record is verified to level L2" (apps/web/src/lib/packets/view.ts:108, :127, :129; cases/page.tsx:102-105 adds "DEADLINE_UNVERIFIED"; packet/page.tsx:283-284 "verified to level L2"). p.abstraction-version.default.1280: "(CON-02)", "(CON-03)" (abstraction/versions/[versionId]/page.tsx:414, :437); components/contracts/display.tsx:111 "(CON-04)". p.contract-version.default.1280: "The product counts a contract’s dispute window only as calendar days after the payment date for now" (apps/web/src/lib/contracts/display.ts:116). The copy lint (tools/brand-copy/scan.ts) has no rule for these.
- **Rule:** The brief's plain-language rule (no internal jargon or codes in what a person reads); BRAND.md:23 (serious, exact)
- **Best fix:** Remove every build reference from read text and say what the person can rely on. Replacements: "A billing firm reaches this practice only through a grant you approve (milestone 12)." -> "No billing firm can reach this practice. A firm reaches it only through a grant you approve here."; card title "Not built yet" -> "Export and deletion, by request" and "The export and deletion workflows arrive in a later release (milestone 12)." -> "Until these screens are built, PaidWhole’s security official carries out an export or a deletion by hand, under the same policy." (the page's next paragraph already links to support); "(golden question Q34)" -> delete; "verified to level L2" -> "verified against Medicare’s published rule"; "(…, DEADLINE_UNVERIFIED)" -> delete the code; "(CON-02)", "(CON-03)", "(CON-04)" -> delete. Add a copy-lint rule that fails on /\bmilestone \d+|golden question|\bQ\d{2}\b|\b[A-Z]{3}-\d{2}\b|\bL[0-9]\b|\b[A-Z]+(_[A-Z]+)+\b/ in read text (the last one catches a code in capitals and underscores), with the usual exemption comment.
- **Notes:** The Q34 sentence is also long (an 80-word notice); shortening it is part of the same edit: "No packet of a Medicare Advantage non-contracted line is exported yet: PaidWhole is confirming which dispute route such lines take. Until then, dispute them through the plan’s own payment dispute policy, outside the product." The data page's legal wording (retention, certificate) is unchanged; only the milestone reference goes.

### `words-practice-13`: Unpriced reasons and methods shown as raw codes (NO_AGREEMENT, FIXED_SCHEDULE, NO_MAPPING) where a billing manager reads them

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.claims.default.1280`, `p.claims.error.1280`, `p.pricing-run.default.1280`, `p.compare.default.1280`, `p.coverage.default.1280`, `p.coverage.empty.1280`, `p.line.default.1280`

- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/claims/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/pricing/diff.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/coverage/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/copy/words.ts`
- **Evidence:** Claims and lines: every unpriced row's Status reads "Unpriced" over "NO_AGREEMENT" (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/claims/page.tsx:212). Pricing run: Method or reason column "FIXED_SCHEDULE" (pricing-runs/[runId]/page.tsx:266). Compare: Second run "NO_AGREEMENT" (apps/web/src/components/pricing/diff.tsx:37). Coverage alert: "Lines outside every mapping’s dates are left unpriced with NO_MAPPING, one by one." Line page: "Method FIXED_SCHEDULE" (lines/[lineId]/page.tsx:74) and the unpriced reason at :90. The words exist and are unused: METHOD_NAMES (apps/web/src/lib/contracts/display.ts:43) and UNPRICED_REASON_TEXT (apps/web/src/lib/contracts/remedies.ts:24-37).
- **Rule:** Combined audit #107 (unpriced reasons in words with their remedy); PRD.md:258 (Dollar figures carry their stage label; reasons in words); the brief's plain-language rule
- **Best fix:** Render every reason and method through the existing maps, keeping the code as secondary small text only where a person may need to quote it to support: Claims and lines "Unpriced / NO_AGREEMENT" -> "Unpriced: no agreement (out of network)" with the remedy link when there is one; pricing run "FIXED_SCHEDULE" -> "Fixed schedule"; compare "NO_AGREEMENT" -> "Unpriced: no agreement"; coverage "left unpriced with NO_MAPPING, one by one" -> "left unpriced as ‘no mapping on the service date’"; line page "FIXED_SCHEDULE" -> "Fixed schedule". Add one helper (reasonLabel, methodLabel) beside labelOf in apps/web/src/lib/copy/words.ts, and the copy-lint rule for a capitals-and-underscores code in read text (words-practice-12).
- **Notes:** Coverage's own Unpriced reasons table shows the code beside its meaning and remedy, which is acceptable as a reference table; this finding is about every other place the code stands alone. The line page's trace (raw JSON) is #38's and is words-practice-14.

### `words-practice-14`: Calculation steps read as code: integer cents ("Unit 5000 cents", "10000 cents to $100.00"), raw JSON trace, "FIXED_SCHEDULE" in the packet's contract citation

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; in flight: #38 every figure explained in words
- **Screens:** `p.packet-review.default.1280`, `p.packet-review.error-needs-downloads.1280`, `p.line.default.1280`, `p.line.error.1280`, `p.packet.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review`, `/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/packets/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Packet review Line schedule: Steps "Unit 5000 cents / Schedule 10000 cents / Base 10000 cents", Rounding "Half up to the cent: 10000 cents to $100.00"; Contract citation "Method FIXED_SCHEDULE" (apps/web/src/components/packets/display.tsx:105 prints citation.method unmapped, while :39 maps it elsewhere). Line page: "Expected allowed $100.00 (unrounded 10000 cents)", and every trace step as pretty-printed JSON ("timed_units_rule": "PER_CODE", "charge_cents": "15000"). The trace also numbers two sections "Step 2: the terms and method" and "Step 2: the fee schedule", the second reading "Not reached."
- **Rule:** PRD.md:256 (money rendered from cents, never as cents); CLAUDE.md rule 2 (keep the unrounded value beside the rounded one, which needs words a person reads); .claude/rules/brand.md (every money figure in mono, as money)
- **Best fix:** Inside #38's renderer: every amount in a step as dollars from cents, the unrounded value to the fraction of a cent it holds ("Unit $50.00 × 2 units = $100.00 (unrounded $100.0000); rounded half up to the cent: $100.00"), the citation's method through METHOD_NAMES ("FIXED_SCHEDULE" -> "Fixed schedule"), and "(unrounded 10000 cents)" -> "(unrounded $100.00)". Number the trace steps once each in order, and word a skipped step as why it was skipped ("Not reached." -> "Not used: a fixed schedule does not read the Medicare fee schedule").
- **Notes:** Recorded so #38's PR covers the packet citation's method and the cents wording, which its description (steps as plain sentences) does not name.

### `words-practice-15`: Earlier tour item confirmed: Actionable reads $0.00 on the home tile and $30.00 on the ROI statement for the same case, and the tile pairs $0.00 with "1 line"

- **Flags:** blocking; size S
- **Screens:** `p.home.default.1280`, `p.roi-statement.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/dashboard/queries.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx`, `apps/worker/src/reconcile/statements.ts`
- **Evidence:** LEDGER, same case: home tile "Actionable $0.00 / what the packets ask for: 1 line in cases still being worked" (p.home.default.1280) while the ROI statement reads "Actionable in open cases: $30.00" (p.roi-statement.default.1280). Causes: the tile sums ACTIONABLE_NOW (apps/web/src/lib/triage/queries.ts:354-357: lines still actionable and priced current) but counts lines with a different predicate (apps/web/src/lib/dashboard/queries.ts:93-97, no pricing check; ACTIONABLE_NOW_LINES at triage/queries.ts:360-363 is unused there); the ROI statement sums the case's stored c.actionable_cents (apps/worker/src/reconcile/statements.ts:73), which keeps lines that have since moved to payer approved.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:258 (dollar figures carry their stage label; a figure and its count describe the same lines); BRAND.md:23 (exact)
- **Best fix:** One definition of the actionable figure for every screen and document: the ROI statement takes ACTIONABLE_NOW (the line-stage and current-pricing rule) instead of the stored case column, and the tile counts with ACTIONABLE_NOW_LINES so its line count describes its dollars. Words: tile note "what the packets ask for: 1 line in cases still being worked" -> "what the packets ask for: 0 lines in cases still being worked" (through counted()); when lines drop out of the figure, add "(1 line has moved on to payer approved)". ROI "Actionable in open cases: $30.00" -> "Actionable in open cases: $0.00" for this data.
- **Notes:** Confirms and updates the 2026-10-25 tour item "Actionable $0.00 against $30.00 in open cases": still present, now between the home tile and the ROI statement. Blocking because two money figures for one stage disagree on screens a buyer audits. The ROI statement is printed by the worker; reading actionable through ACTIONABLE_NOW does not change any ledger entry or the recovered total (actionable is not a ledger stage), but the adversarial reviewer should confirm it sits outside the ledger freeze.

### `words-practice-16`: The headline's Recovered tile states no period beside a "this quarter" figure, and says "stage five"

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.abstraction-ledger.default.1280`, `p.case.default.1280`, `p.home.default.1280`, `p.home.error-last-run-failed.1280`, `p.reconciliation.default.1280`, `p.roi-statement.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/reconciliation/display.tsx`
- **Evidence:** p.home.default.1280: tile "Recovered (deposited) $12.00 / stage five: cash deposited, proved by the bank export, net of recoupments and reversals" and, lower on the page, "Recovered (deposited) this quarter $0.00 (2026-Q4, …)". Nothing says the tile is all time, so the two recovered figures read as a contradiction. "stage five" (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx:217; also the ledger totals tiles and the case summary) is the product's internal numbering of the five line stages; no screen tells a billing manager what stage five is. The Payer approved tile beside it says "what payers accepted, from the recovery ledger; not cash until it posts" (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx:212, fixed text) on a practice whose proof level is deposited, where posting alone does not make it recovered. ROI statement on the same deposited practice: "Payer approved, not yet at the proof level: $0.00 … Nothing approved is waiting to be posted." (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]/page.tsx:115) names posting where the proof is the deposit.
- **Rule:** PRD 13 (recovered for a case, a practice, a period: a figure names its period); PRD.md:258; .claude/rules/brand.md (Recovered always with posted or deposited, kept)
- **Best fix:** Name the period on every recovered figure and drop the stage number: tile note "stage five: cash deposited, proved by the bank export, net of recoupments and reversals" -> "all time: cash deposited, proved by the bank export, net of recoupments and reversals"; the same "stage five:" opening goes from the ledger totals tiles and the case summary ("stage five: cash posted in the practice’s payment posting…" -> "cash posted in the practice’s payment posting…"). Keep "Recovered (deposited) this quarter" as is. Word the Payer approved note from the proof level too: "not cash until it posts" -> "not counted as recovered until it is deposited" (deposited) or "… until it is posted" (posted). ROI empty line: "Nothing approved is waiting to be posted." -> "Nothing approved is waiting to be deposited." (from the proof level).
- **Notes:** The copy lint's recovered rule is unaffected (the parenthesised proof label stays). The ROI page carries #46 (the seal) in flight; this line is not the seal.

### `words-practice-17`: Signed-off contract versions still say "Publication is blocked … before the version can be approved" and "The version can still be approved"

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.contract-version.error-unresolved.1280`, `p.abstraction-version.default.1280`, `p.contract-version.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/versions/[versionId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/contracts/review.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/contracts/display.ts`
- **Evidence:** p.contract-version.error-unresolved.1280: "Pricing uses this version / Signed off…" above "Publication is blocked / 1 record is not checked against the cited page yet, or cite a page that no longer resolves. Every field of every record is approved against its cited page before the version can be approved." The version was approved and signed off, so the sentence describes a step already past; it also mixes subjects ("1 record is … or cite"). p.contract-version.default.1280 (signed off): dispute window "not checked yet" and, in red, "… The version can still be approved." Causes: apps/web/src/components/contracts/review.tsx:211-217 (no branch on status) and apps/web/src/lib/contracts/display.ts:116.
- **Rule:** BRAND.md:23 (exact); PRD.md:256 (every error with a true next step)
- **Best fix:** Word the alert by the version's status. Draft or under review: title "Not ready to approve", body "1 record is not yet checked against its cited page, or cites a page that no longer resolves. Every field is checked against its page before the version is approved." Signed off: title "A cited page no longer resolves", body "This version keeps pricing as signed off. Record 1’s cited page cannot be shown now; the contract specialist re-cites it on a corrected version if the document changed." For the dispute-window note on a signed-off version, drop "The version can still be approved." and use "Lines priced under this version wait for a verified deadline; keep the contract’s own deadline from its words."
- **Notes:** The data state (no worker read the pages at capture) is synthetic; the wording fault holds for any signed-off version whose page stops resolving.

Cause: apps/web/src/components/contracts/display.tsx:66-76 prints the requested page and the previous link whatever the count; the message is apps/web/src/lib/contracts/display.ts:79.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:256 (every error with a next step)
- **Best fix:** When the page is outside 1..pageCount, print "Document 0e0335da has 2 pages; page 99 is not one of them." with links "Show page 1" and "Show the cited page (page 2)", and no Previous/Next links.

### `words-practice-19`: A resolved case shows a deadline date beside "no window" on Cases, and "open" on its own page

- **Evidence:** Same case (PACKETS, Resolved): Cases list Deadline "2036-04-05" with a "no window" chip; the case page "Deadline (earliest open window) 2036-04-05" with an "open" chip. Cause: apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/page.tsx:279 passes state 'none' for resolved and written-off cases but keeps the date, and apps/web/src/components/triage/display.tsx:39-44 words 'none' as "no window", the words for a line with no dispute window at all; the case page (cases/[caseId]/page.tsx:95) passes the computed state.
- **Rule:** BRAND.md:23 (exact); the brief's consistent-terms rule
- **Best fix:** Give a closed case its own deadline wording on both screens: no date, chip "closed" ("2036-04-05 no window" -> "— closed"; case page "2036-04-05 open" -> "No deadline to meet: the case is resolved"). Keep "no window" only for a line or case that has no dispute window.

### `words-practice-20`: Timeline: "reopened from Actionable to Actionable, with a reason" and the reason not given

The first reads as nothing happening; neither gives the reason, which the line card above shows ("SYNTHETIC written off by mistake"). Cause: apps/web/src/lib/cases/timeline.ts:55 always prints from and to; :61 the line reopen.
- **Rule:** BRAND.md:23 (exact); PRD 7 (a reopen is explicit with a reason)
- **Best fix:** When the stage does not change, say what the reopen undid: "Line 1: reopened from Actionable to Actionable, with a reason" -> "Line 1: write-off undone; the line stays Actionable (reason on the line)"; and "Line 1: reopened into the case, with a reason" -> "Line 1: back in the case (reason on the line)". Show the reason text itself to the roles that read PHI, as Notes do.

### `words-practice-21`: Packet lists read "[4] an evidence item 1" and "Row 1: due —, paid 2026-04-08, —; interest not computed"

- **Flags:** polish; size S; in flight: #38 every figure explained in words
- **Screens:** `p.packet.default.1280`, `p.packet.error-export-blocked.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/packets/view.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/packets/display.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Numbered sources: "[4] an evidence item 1 [5] an evidence item 2 [6] an evidence item 3" (apps/web/src/lib/packets/view.ts:83 'an evidence item' plus the index, components/packets/display.tsx:397-398). Interest rows: "Row 1: due —, paid 2026-04-08, —; interest not computed" (components/packets/display.tsx:357), two dashes standing for unknown values inside a sentence.
- **Rule:** BRAND.md:23 (exact); PRD.md:256 (every error with its next step: here, why interest is not computed)
- **Best fix:** Sources: "[4] an evidence item 1" -> "[4] evidence 1: the contract document" (name the item's kind from EVIDENCE_KIND_TEXT). Interest rows without a due date: "Row 1: due —, paid 2026-04-08, —; interest not computed" -> "Row 1: paid 2026-04-08; interest not computed (the remittance does not say when the payer received the claim)", the reason taken from the row's evidence gap; with a due date keep "Row 1: due 2026-05-11, paid 2026-06-08, 28 days late; interest $1.23".
- **Notes:** Packet page wording in #38's scope; fold into that PR. The evidence-gap lines also cite the 835 segment code "(DTM 050)", which a billing manager will not know: "the remittance does not say when the payer received the claim (DTM 050)" -> drop the code on screen, keep it in the trace.

### `words-practice-22`: The live check names a wrong figure but not what to do about it

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.packet.error-live-check.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/packets/view.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/packets/version-form.tsx`
- **Evidence:** "Live check: 1 mismatch" then "Sentence 4: a figure that is not in the packet’s input records (amount) 1234.56". It says what is wrong in the verifier's terms ("input records") and gives no next step, while "Save as a new version" stays enabled. Cause: apps/web/src/lib/packets/view.ts:11 and :57-60.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:256 (every error with a next step); PRD 12 (a narrative figure not in the input blocks approval)
- **Best fix:** State the fix and the consequence: "Sentence 4: a figure that is not in the packet’s input records (amount) 1234.56" -> "Sentence 4: $1234.56 is not one of this packet’s figures. Use an amount from the line schedule or the contract citation, or remove it; a version with this sentence cannot be approved." Apply the same pattern to each MISMATCH_TEXT entry (for example sentence_without_citation -> "cites no numbered source: add one, such as [3] for the line schedule").
- **Notes:** Whether Save stays enabled is the flows lens's call; the words fix stands either way.

### `words-practice-23`: File sizes in KiB and GiB, mixed with "bytes", and documents named by lower-case kind

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.uploads.default.1280`, `p.uploads.error-era-system.1280`, `p.upload.default.1280`, `p.abstraction.default.1280`, `p.contracts.default.1280`, `p.contract.default.1280`, `p.contract-version.default.1280`, `p.abstraction-version.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/uploads`, `/p/[practiceId]/uploads/[documentId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/versions/[versionId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/uploads/receipt-labels.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/contracts/forms.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/contracts/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Receipts: "1000 bytes", "1.4 KiB", "9.5 KiB"; drop zones "up to 2.0 GiB each" (Uploads) and "up to 2 GiB each" (Contracts, apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/contracts/page.tsx:209); document labels "payer letter, 722 bytes, received …" and "contract, 1.9 KiB, received … (c28ff7c5)". Causes: apps/web/src/lib/uploads/receipt-labels.ts:207-216 (binary units, one decimal); apps/web/src/components/contracts/forms.tsx:18-19 (kind first, lower case).
- **Rule:** The brief's number-format and consistent-terms rules; BRAND.md:23 (exact, for a billing manager)
- **Best fix:** Use the units a billing manager's computer shows, consistently: "1000 bytes" -> "1 KB", "1.4 KiB" -> "1.4 KB", "2.0 GiB"/"2 GiB" -> "2 GB" (formatBytes with KB/MB/GB, no trailing .0). Start a document label with a capital: "contract, 1.9 KiB, received 2026-10-26 01:26 UTC (c28ff7c5)" -> "Contract, 1.9 KB, received 2026-10-26 01:26 UTC (c28ff7c5)".
- **Notes:** If the 2 GB limit is enforced as 2 GiB, keep the limit and change only its label to "2 GB" (the larger binary limit admits every 2 GB file).

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42

- **Evidence:** Access log rows: Through "ownership", What "read (case_review)" / "read (packet_build)", Record "service_line", "case_line", "case_activity", "packet_version" (mono, underlined, so the underscore reads as a space). Filtered state: "Who acted on one claim record." Support access: Record type "contract_version", "source_document", "rate_record"; Purpose "rate_record_review", "contract_evidence", "contract_workflow"; "Expiry notice sent: —". Causes: apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/access-log/page.tsx:62-75 print entry.via, entry.action, entry.purpose and entry.objectType as stored; apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/support-access/[supportGrantId]/page.tsx:58-60 the same; the practice home already words `ownership` as "your practice group" (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx:28-33), so the same value reads two ways.

- **Best fix:** One ACCESS_LOG_TEXT map (via, action, purpose, object type) shared by both pages and the home page's VIA: "ownership" -> "Your practice group"; "grant" -> "A grant you approved"; "support" -> "Support grant"; "read (case_review)" -> "Read, reviewing a case"; "read (packet_build)" -> "Read, building a packet"; "service_line" -> "Claim line"; "case_line" -> "Case line"; "contract_version" -> "Contract version"; "source_document" -> "Uploaded document"; "rate_record_review" -> "Reviewing a rate record"; unknown codes through labelOf(). "Expiry notice sent: —" -> "Expiry notice: not sent yet (it goes out when the grant ends)".
- **Notes:** support-access/[supportGrantId]/page.tsx is in PR #42's list; the access-log page is not, so the access-log half can land first.

Show "—" rather than "0" for Lines when a gap is not about lines.

### `words-practice-27`: The same agreement is "Terms of use" on Settings (Done) and "Terms of service" on Grants and agreements (Missing)

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.settings.default.1280`, `p.agreements.default.1280`, `p.agreements.empty.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/settings`, `/p/[practiceId]/settings/agreements`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/settings.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Getting started: "Done Terms of use / Accepted with their version and time." Grants and agreements: "Missing Terms of service". Two names, two states, one page apart, and the checklist's next item "Confirm the agreement chain" depends on the second. Causes: apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/page.tsx:15 ('Terms of use', the click-through a user accepts) and apps/web/src/lib/routes/settings.ts:72 ('Terms of service', the practice's signed agreement in the chain); the practice home calls the same chain item "Terms" (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx:37).
- **Rule:** The brief's consistent-terms rule; BRAND.md:23 (exact)

If they are in fact the same document, show one name and one state from one source.

### `words-practice-28`: "Check the ZIP code." is the only step offered for a ZIP that is right but missing from the release

- **Evidence:** Pricing location and the Getting started checklist: "Medicare’s ZIP code list in force does not include 27601. Check the ZIP code." 27601 is a real ZIP; the person has nothing to change, yet the only step offered is to recheck it. The ZIP+4 case already gives the right step ("If those four digits are right, tell us and we check Medicare’s list; until then, months that need it wait."). Cause: packages/contracts/src/location.ts:64-65 (zip_unknown).
- **Rule:** PRD.md:256 (every error with a next step)
- **Best fix:** "Medicare’s ZIP code list in force does not include 27601. Check the ZIP code." -> "Medicare’s ZIP code list in force does not include 27601. If 27601 is right, tell us through Help and support and we check Medicare’s list; until then, months that need it wait." (the same pattern as zip_plus_four_unknown), with the support link the page already has.

### `words-practice-29`: Compare two runs says "Not priced by this run" for lines the run never held, "Only one run priced the line" when neither did, and "0 of 0 lines both runs priced are priced differently"

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.compare.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/compare`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/pricing/diff.tsx`
- **Evidence:** March against April (different months, so no line is in both): summary "0 of 0 lines both runs priced are priced differently; 3 only in the first run; 32 only in the second. Expected allowed over the lines both priced: $0.00 before, $0.00 after." Each April row: First run "Not priced by this run", Second run "NO_AGREEMENT", Cause "Only one run priced the line"; each March row: Second run "Not priced by this run". The April lines were left unpriced by the second run, and they were never in the first run at all, so both cells misstate what happened. Causes: apps/web/src/components/pricing/diff.tsx:33 (side === null means the line is not in that run), :58 (summary), :152 (no causes).
- **Rule:** BRAND.md:23 (exact); PRD 7 (compare lists exactly the lines that differ, with the cause)
- **Best fix:** Word the three facts apart: a missing side -> "Not in this run" ("Not priced by this run" -> "Not in this run"); an unpriced side -> its reason in words ("NO_AGREEMENT" -> "Unpriced: no agreement"); the cause for a one-sided line -> "Only one run covers this line" ("Only one run priced the line" -> "Only one run covers this line"). When no line is in both runs, replace the summary with "These runs share no line: they price different months (2026-03 and 2026-04). 3 lines are only in the first run and 32 only in the second."; otherwise "0 of 0 lines both runs priced are priced differently" -> "Of the N lines in both runs, M are priced differently" through counted().
- **Notes:** Comparing two different months is allowed by the form; the flows lens may want the form to warn. The words fix stands either way.

### `words-practice-30`: One quantity, five names: variance, underpaid, short, shortfall, validated variance

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.claims.default.1280`, `p.pricing-runs.default.1280`, `p.pricing-run.default.1280`, `p.case.default.1280`, `p.packet.default.1280`, `p.notifications.default.1280`, `p.digest.default.1280`, `p.abstraction-ledger.default.1280`, `p.monthly.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/claims`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/notifications`, `/p/[practiceId]/digests/[digestId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/monthly`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/claims/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/pricing/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/packets/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/monitoring/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger/page.tsx`, `docs/brand/BRAND.md`
- **Evidence:** Expected allowed less observed allowed is labelled "Short (variance)" (Claims and lines column), "Underpaid (validated)" (pricing runs list and run page, apps/web/src/components/pricing/display.tsx:152, pricing-runs/page.tsx:169), "Variance" (run lines, case lines "variance $30.00", report tables), "Variance (Actionable)" (packet line schedule), "validated variance" (notifications, digest), "a shortfall of $90.00" (packet narrative); meanwhile "Shortfalls" on the abstraction ledger means something else (a payer paying less than its response accepted). In code: "variance" ~206 times, "shortfall" ~69, "underpaid" ~22 across apps/web/src.
- **Rule:** The brief's consistent-terms rule; .claude/rules/brand.md and BRAND.md section 7 ("expected, paid and short" in that order; the status token is named Short, "paid below the contract")
- **Best fix:** Use the brand's word for the column and the figure everywhere a billing manager reads it: label "Short" (with the stage after it where a stage applies: "Short (validated)", "Short (actionable)"), and "shortfall" only in running sentences. Replacements: "Short (variance)" -> "Short"; "Underpaid (validated)" -> "Short (validated)"; "Underpaid lines" -> "Lines paid short"; "Variance" (column) -> "Short"; "Variance (Actionable)" -> "Short (actionable)"; "new validated variance $90.00" -> "new validated shortfall $90.00". Rename the abstraction ledger's other meaning to "Approved but paid less" so "shortfall" means one thing. Record the vocabulary in a short glossary in docs/brand/BRAND.md section 7 so the copy lint can hold it later.
- **Notes:** components/monitoring/display.tsx is in PR #42. The packet's printed narrative and PDF wording belong to #38 and the packet PDF lens; this finding covers the screens. Rules are silent on the word for the column beyond the brand's "short"; this is the recommendation. Claims and lines also orders the columns Expected allowed, Observed allowed, Short, Paid, where .claude/rules/brand.md asks for expected, paid and short in that order; the brand lens owns the order.

### `words-practice-31`: Coverage and mapping state vacuous facts on an empty practice: "0 of 0 lines (0%)", "every line counts as unpriced", "Pricing can run", "Every line has a mapping"

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.coverage.empty.1280`, `p.mapping.empty.1280`, `p.mapping.default.1280`
