- **Evidence:** Seeded practice with no lines: "Priced: 0 of 0 lines (0%); $0.00 of $0.00 billed (0%)", "No pricing run has priced this practice’s lines yet, so every line counts as unpriced.", alert "Pricing can run / Every plan with lines has a mapping."; mapping "0 lines on 0 claims: $0.00 billed, $0.00 paid. Every line has a mapping on its service date." Also "Every plan with lines has a mapping on their service dates" (plan … their). Percentages are "0%" here, "0.0%" and "100.0%" on pricing runs and the digest. Causes: apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/coverage/page.tsx:119, :144-145; apps/web/src/components/coverage/share.tsx:22-23.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:260 (every empty state says what to do next); BRAND.md:23 (exact)
- **Best fix:** When the practice holds no lines, show one empty state in place of the figures: "No lines yet. Coverage counts start once 835 files load and a pricing run prices them." with "Go to Uploads", and hide the "Pricing can run" alert and the mapping sentence. Format every share with one helper and one decimal ("0.0%", "8.6%", "100.0%"). "Every plan with lines has a mapping on their service dates" -> "Every plan with lines has a mapping for its service dates".

### `words-practice-32`: Coverage's Unpriced reasons speak in rules-reference shorthand: "Claim status 2 or 3", "Deferred release", "Specialist resolves scope", "Operator alert", and a lead-in that contradicts itself

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.coverage.default.1280`, `p.coverage.empty.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/coverage`
- **Files:** `docs/product/unpriced-reasons.md`, `apps/web/src/lib/contracts/remedies.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/contracts/remedies.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/coverage/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Lead-in: "Lines the runs in effect priced, each by the reason its pricing in effect left it unpriced." (priced … unpriced; apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/coverage/page.tsx:234). Meanings and remedies: SECONDARY_PAYER "Claim status 2 or 3" / "Deferred release"; AMBIGUOUS_RATE "Specialist resolves scope"; UNSUPPORTED_METHOD "None; reported"; MUE_EXCEEDED "Units above the MUE with NCCI on"; ENGINE_ERROR "Unexpected input" / "Operator alert". These are copied verbatim from the rules reference (apps/web/src/lib/contracts/remedies.ts:24-37, held equal by remedies.test.ts).
- **Rule:** Combined audit #107 (unpriced reasons in words with their remedy); docs/product/unpriced-reasons.md is the closed list of reasons (the codes), not a ban on plain wording; the brief's plain-language rule
- **Best fix:** Add a customer wording to each reason in docs/product/unpriced-reasons.md and remedies.ts (keep the reference text for specialists), and use it on Coverage, Claims and lines and the line page. Lead-in: "Lines the runs in effect priced, each by the reason its pricing in effect left it unpriced." -> "The lines the pricing runs in effect could not price, by reason, with what raises each." Replacements: "Claim status 2 or 3" / "Deferred release" -> "The payer paid as secondary" / "Not priced yet: secondary claims come in a later release of PaidWhole"; "Specialist resolves scope" -> "The contract specialist decides which rate applies"; "None; reported" -> "Nothing to do: the contract pays this way and PaidWhole cannot price it"; "Units above the MUE with NCCI on" -> "More units than Medicare allows for the code in a day (MUE)"; "Unexpected input" / "Operator alert" -> "PaidWhole could not read the line" / "PaidWhole has been alerted; nothing for you to do".
- **Notes:** Pairs with words-practice-13 (the codes elsewhere). Changing docs/product wording is a product-doc edit, not content/releases.

### `words-practice-33`: Case and packet summaries pair a figure with a count of other lines: "Actionable $0.00 (3 of 3 lines not written off)", "Written off $0.00 (0 lines, struck through below)"

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.case.default.1280`, `p.case.error.1280`, `p.case.empty.1280`, `p.packet.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Resolved case (PACKETS): "Actionable $0.00 (3 of 3 lines not written off)" reads as three actionable lines worth nothing; "Written off $0.00 (0 lines, struck through below)" points at struck-through lines that do not exist; "Payer approved $60.00" of a $90.00 shortfall with nothing on the page saying what became of the other $30.00 on a case marked Resolved. Packet subtitle (p.packet.default.1280): "Actionable: $0.00 on 0 of the 3 lines not written off (the rest are out of the figure: their pricing is no longer current, or they moved past actionable), as of 2026-10-26." Causes: apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/page.tsx:77 and :80; apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx:182-186.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:258 (dollar figures carry their stage label; the words beside a figure describe that figure); BRAND.md:23 (exact; never a figure the product cannot explain)
- **Best fix:** Count the lines each figure sums, and say where the rest went, through counted(): "Actionable $0.00 (3 of 3 lines not written off)" -> "Actionable $0.00 (no line is still actionable: 3 lines moved on to payer approved)"; "Written off $0.00 (0 lines, struck through below)" -> "Written off: none" (and "$30.00 (1 line, struck through below)" only when there is one). For a partial acceptance, add one line under Payer approved: "The payer accepted $60.00 of the $90.00 asked; $30.00 was not accepted (partially accepted on 2026-10-26)." Packet subtitle -> "Paid below the contract rate. Actionable now: $0.00 (0 of 3 lines; 3 have moved past actionable), as of 2026-10-26."
- **Notes:** Whether a partially accepted case should read Resolved at all is combined audit #89's (one case status, PR 8/13); this finding is the words beside the figures, which stand whatever the status becomes. The packet subtitle sits on a #38 page, but it is a summary line, not a calculation. The timeline then marks all three lines "Payer approved" after a partial acceptance of $60.00 of $90.00; whether a line accepted in part is at stage payer approved is PRD 13 / audit #12 territory (in flight, PR #51); the words here only need to show the part not accepted.

### `words-practice-34`: The response log says "(Payer approved once the recovery ledger approves it)" after the ledger has already recorded it

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.submissions.default.1280`, `p.submissions.error.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Submission log: "2026-10-26: Partially accepted, $60.00 accepted (Payer approved once the recovery ledger approves it) (with its document)" while the case page shows Payer approved $60.00 from the ledger and the timeline "Line 1: Payer approved (the recovery ledger)". The ledger does not approve anything (PRD 13: an approved entry is created when a payer response accepts an amount); two parentheses in a row; "Payer approved" capitalised mid-sentence. Cause: apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions/page.tsx:419 and :424.
- **Rule:** PRD 13 (the approved entry is created when a payer response accepts an amount; the words must use "approved" exactly so); the brief's honest-claims rule
- **Best fix:** Word it from the ledger's state: when the approved entry exists, "2026-10-26: Partially accepted, $60.00 accepted (with its document). Recorded as payer approved in the recovery ledger."; while it does not yet, "… The recovery ledger records it as payer approved once the payer’s letter is matched." Pass the entry's existence from the page's query; never a fixed parenthesis.

### `words-practice-35`: "No approved packet version of this practice has this reference." gives no next step

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.by-reference.empty.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/by-reference`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/by-reference/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** No-match card: title "Reference ZZZZZ-ZZZZZ", body "No approved packet version of this practice has this reference." and nothing else.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:256 and :260 (every error and empty state with a next step)
- **Best fix:** "No approved packet version of this practice has this reference." -> "No approved packet of this practice has reference ZZZZZ-ZZZZZ. Check it against the payer’s letter (two groups of five, such as SK6CS-K6CSK), or switch practice if the letter is for another of yours." Title: "Reference ZZZZZ-ZZZZZ" -> "No packet found".

### `words-practice-36`: Dates and times: ISO dates and UTC clock times on screen beside browser date pickers in mm/dd/yyyy

- **Flags:** should_fix; size L
- **Screens:** `p.submissions.default.1280`, `p.roi.default.1280`, `p.contracts.default.1280`, `p.home.default.1280`, `p.uploads.default.1280`, `p.notifications.default.1280`, `p.reconciliation.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/**`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/format.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/uploads/receipt-labels.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/pricing/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Every displayed date is ISO ("2026-10-26", "File by 2036-04-05") and every time UTC to the minute ("2026-10-26 01:26 UTC", components/format.ts:1-11), while every date input renders the browser's US format ("10/26/2026", "mm/dd/yyyy"; Submitted on, First day / Last day, Effective from). An upload at 9:26 p.m. Eastern shows as the next day, 01:26 UTC. components/format.ts:1 reads PRD 6 as "every date stored and shown in UTC", but PRD.md:256 says stored in UTC, with service dates kept as calendar dates; it is silent on display. ADR 0061 already notes the cost (an approval after 7 or 8 p.m. US time prints the next day) and defers a practice time zone to PR 12.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:256 (money from cents in the practice's locale; dates stored in UTC: the rule is silent on displayed dates, so this is a recommendation); the brief's date-format rule
- **Best fix:** One date formatter used by every screen, in the practice's locale: calendar dates as "Oct 26, 2026" (unambiguous, and how a US billing manager reads a payer letter), times in the practice's time zone with the zone named ("Oct 25, 2026, 9:26 PM ET"), UTC kept in audit-log exports and file hashes' records. Store the practice's time zone in practice.settings (derived from the billing ZIP at onboarding, editable on Pricing settings), so no migration is needed. Keep ISO in CSV exports.
- **Notes:** Printed approval dates on the payer's copy are ADR 0061's PR 12 item and stay out of this change. If the founder prefers ISO for its precision, keep ISO dates but still show times in the practice's zone: a UTC clock time is the part that misleads.

### `words-practice-37`: ROI statement prints "Pursuit hours logged: 0.00" though no time is logged anywhere

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.roi-statement.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]/page.tsx`, `apps/worker/src/reconcile/statement-html.ts`, `apps/worker/src/reconcile/statements.ts`
- **Evidence:** "Pursuit hours logged: 0.00 / Hours the cases’ time log records for the period; no time log is kept yet." A figure of zero states that nobody spent time pursuing; the product cannot know that, because it records no time at all (apps/worker/src/reconcile/statements.ts:23 "none is kept yet, so none are logged"). Rendered by apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]/page.tsx:142 and printed in the PDF by apps/worker/src/reconcile/statement-html.ts:118, the document a practice shows its owner.
- **Rule:** The brief's honest-claims rule (no figure the product cannot prove); PRD 13 (ROI statement: pursuit hours logged); BRAND.md:23 (exact)
- **Best fix:** Print no figure where nothing is measured: "Pursuit hours logged: 0.00" -> "Pursuit hours: not tracked yet" with the line "PaidWhole does not log time on cases yet, so this statement states no pursuit hours." on screen and in the PDF; print a number only once a time log exists. The same rule for the fees line when billing records nothing (it already says so in words; keep it).
- **Notes:** ROI pages carry #46 (the Verified seal) in flight; this line is outside the seal's scope. The stored pursuit_hours column stays; only what is printed for it changes, so no ledger or schema change.

### `words-practice-38`: The packet review's attested state does not say who attested, then prints "I attest that I downloaded…" to a reviewer who downloaded nothing

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S
- **Screens:** `p.packet-review.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/packets/review.ts`
- **Evidence:** Viewing reviewer (reviewer@synthetic.test): Exported files say "You have not downloaded it yet." for both files; the Attestation region reads "Attested 2026-10-26 01:26 UTC over these files: …" (no name), the seven items, then "I attest that I downloaded and reviewed this packet’s exported PDF and CSV against the checklist above." The first-person statement under an unnamed attester reads as the viewer's own attestation. Cause: apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review/page.tsx:248 adds " by you" for the viewer and nothing otherwise; :265 prints PACKET_ATTESTATION_TEXT as plain text. The audit report's checklist names the signer ("Signed … by the reimbursement reviewer", reports/[reportId]/review/page.tsx:71).
- **Rule:** The brief's honest-claims rule (an attestation is attributed exactly); PRD 12 (two-person rules: who did what is recorded and shown)
- **Best fix:** Name the attester and quote the statement as theirs: "Attested 2026-10-26 01:26 UTC over these files:" -> "Attested 2026-10-26 01:26 UTC by packet-reviewer@synthetic.test (reimbursement reviewer) over these files:" (or "by you"), and introduce the statement: "They signed this statement: “I attest that I downloaded and reviewed this packet’s exported PDF and CSV against the checklist above.”" Use the same pattern on the audit report checklist.
- **Notes:** Showing the attester's email to another internal reviewer is not PHI; if the rule is to show roles only, "by another reimbursement reviewer" is the minimum.

### `words-practice-39`: Export histories credit PaidWhole's reviewer downloads to "a member of the practice"

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.report.default.1280`, `p.contract.default.1280`, `p.contract-version.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/versions/[versionId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/reports/queries.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/reports/documents.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/contracts/contract-queries.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/contracts/mapping-queries.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/remittances/queries.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/reconciliation/queries.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Audit report export history: "PDF by a member of the practice" and "line CSV by a member of the practice" beside "PDF by reports-manager@synthetic.test"; the unnamed downloads are the reimbursement reviewer's (the checklist requires them before signing), an internal PaidWhole person, not a practice member. Contract and version pages: "Signed off: a member of the practice". Cause: the fallback 'a member of the practice' wherever the joined user's email is not visible to the practice scope (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/page.tsx:148, reports/page.tsx:126,257,312, reports/documents/[documentId]/page.tsx:45, roi/[statementId]/page.tsx:45; apps/web/src/lib/contracts/contract-queries.ts:283,494; lib/contracts/mapping-queries.ts:485; lib/remittances/queries.ts:733; lib/reconciliation/queries.ts:229; the download query lib/reports/queries.ts:212-213).
- **Rule:** PRD 6 (audit log and the practice-visible access report: who did what, exactly); the brief's honest-claims rule
- **Best fix:** Resolve the actor's kind with the event and word each kind truthfully: an internal person -> "PaidWhole’s reimbursement reviewer" (or "PaidWhole support", "PaidWhole’s contract specialist", by their support grant's role); a firm user under a grant -> "SYNTHETIC BILLING FIRM (billing firm)"; a removed user -> "a former member of the practice" (the wording triage already uses, apps/web/src/lib/triage/queries.ts:397). Replace the scattered literal with one actorLabel() helper and a test that an internal actor never reads as a practice member.
- **Notes:** The contract sign-off case may be a user the practice scope cannot read for another reason; the helper covers both. Whether the actor's kind is readable in the practice scope without a new definer function needs checking; if it needs one, it goes to Lane A.

### `words-practice-40`: Internal system names in customer copy: "engine phase-two/2", "the reconcile job", "the reconcile reads it", "The database refuses your approval"

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.abstraction-ledger.default.1280`, `p.case.default.1280`, `p.case.error.1280`, `p.compare.default.1280`, `p.home.error-last-run-failed.1280`, `p.line.default.1280`, `p.monthly.default.1280`, `p.packet-review.default.1280`, `p.packet.default.1280`, `p.packet.error-live-check.1280`, `p.pricing-run.default.1280`, `p.pricing-runs.default.1280`, `p.proof-level.empty.1280`, `p.reconciliation.default.1280`, `p.report-document.default.1280`, `p.reports.default.1280`, `p.reports.empty.1280`, `p.roi-statement.default.1280`, `p.roi.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review`, `/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/compare`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/review/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/compare/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/proof-level/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/reconciliation/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/reports/documents.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/cases/timeline.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/packets/export-attempts.ts`
- **Evidence:** "Release 900001, engine phase-two/2; created …" (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/page.tsx:160; also compare/page.tsx:36, lines/[lineId]/page.tsx:115); "matched by the reconcile job", "Written by: the reconcile job" (reconciliation/page.tsx:54, :126; abstraction/ledger/page.tsx:74; reports/roi/page.tsx:92); "once it is stored, the reconcile reads it with the practice’s template" (reconciliation/page.tsx:261). Packet page: "You drafted or last edited this packet / The database refuses your approval: a second person who approves packets approves it." (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/page.tsx:388) and "The export is held to the deadline as the database derives it" (packet/page.tsx:284; review/page.tsx:146 "The deadline as the database derives it now"); export refusal "The database refused to record the export: one of its rules no longer holds for this version." (apps/web/src/lib/packets/export-attempts.ts:65). Reconciliation and the ledger tiles: Recouped "taken back by PLB WO or a reversal", recoupment "PLB WO take-back", evidence "PLB adjustment" (835 provider-level adjustment code WO). (apps/web/src/components/reconciliation/display.tsx:35, :78; apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/page.tsx:75) Practice home failed-run alert: "The operator has been alerted (job reference 1d574871)." (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx:177). ROI statement Files: "The PDF is printed by the worker and stored sealed with the practice’s key" (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/[statementId]/page.tsx:65); monthly summary "Each carries the audit flag (the single-user gap)". Reports: "The report job triages the run on today’s date, then prints the report to PDF and writes the line CSV". Proof level: "The next reconcile, queued when you save, makes the change." and the heading "Now: posted" (the stored value, lower case). Case timeline actors: "(triage)", "(the export job)", "(the recovery ledger)" (apps/web/src/lib/cases/timeline.ts eventActor).
- **Rule:** The brief's plain-language rule (no internal jargon)
- **Best fix:** Say who acts in the customer's terms and keep version strings for the trace and support: "engine phase-two/2" -> drop from the run lists and the compare cards (keep it on the line trace's release step, labelled "Pricing engine version"); "the reconcile job" -> "PaidWhole (automatic match)"; "the reconcile reads it with the practice’s template and matches its rows to remittances, to the cent" -> "PaidWhole reads it with the practice’s posting template and matches each row to a remittance, to the cent". "The database refuses your approval: a second person who approves packets approves it." -> "You cannot approve a packet you drafted or edited. Ask another person who approves packets: use Request approval below."; "as the database derives it" -> "as PaidWhole computes it"; "The database refused to record the export: one of its rules no longer holds for this version." -> "The export was not recorded: this version no longer meets the export checks. Reload the page to see which one." "taken back by PLB WO or a reversal" -> "taken back by the payer in a later remittance, or by a reversed claim"; "PLB WO take-back" -> "the payer took it back from a later payment (recoupment, PLB WO)"; evidence "PLB adjustment" -> "Recoupment in a later remittance". "The operator has been alerted (job reference 1d574871)." -> "PaidWhole support has been alerted (reference 1d574871; quote it if you contact support)." "The PDF is printed by the worker" -> "PaidWhole prints the PDF"; "Each carries the audit flag (the single-user gap)" -> "Each is marked in the audit log as approved without a second person." "The report job triages the run on today’s date" -> "PaidWhole judges the run’s lines as of today, then prints the report to PDF with its line CSV". "The next reconcile, queued when you save, makes the change." -> "PaidWhole applies the change to the ledger within a few minutes of saving."; "Now: posted" -> "Now: Posted". Timeline actors: "(triage)" -> "(PaidWhole, automatic)"; "(the export job)" -> "(PaidWhole, on export)".
- **Notes:** The packet page sits in #38/#48 scope, but these sentences are approval and export states, not figures or drafts. The raw JSON trace on the line page is #38's (words-practice-14).

### `words-practice-41`: Monthly summary puts the link text "the ledger’s" where a dollar figure belongs, and months are written three ways

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.monthly.default.1280`, `p.monthly.empty.1280`, `p.report-document.default.1280`, `p.home.default.1280`, `p.anomalies.default.1280`

- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/reports/documents.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/monthly/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/documents/[documentId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/format.ts`
- **Evidence:** By stage, Dollars column: Payer approved "the ledger’s", Recovered (posted or deposited) "the ledger’s" (apps/web/src/components/reports/documents.tsx:145-150): a link whose text is a possessive with no noun, read aloud as "link, the ledger’s". Months: "Monthly variance summary, September 2026" (monthly page) against "Monthly variance summary, 2026-03" (the printed document) and "No line has a service date in 2026-09." on the September page; quarters as "2026-Q4" (home) and "2026-Q2" (anomalies).
- **Rule:** The brief's date-format and consistent-terms rules; WCAG 2.4.4 Link Purpose (in context)

Write every month as "September 2026" and every quarter as "Q4 2026 (Oct–Dec)" in screen text through the shared formatter (words-practice-36); keep ISO months in CSVs and URLs. "Monthly variance summary, 2026-03" -> "Monthly variance summary, March 2026"; "No line has a service date in 2026-09." -> "No line has a service date in September 2026."

### `words-practice-42`: The fee-schedule letter's refusal is a raw JSON page with the field key "codes"

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.coverage.error-letter-codes.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/coverage`, `/p/[practiceId]/coverage/letter`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/coverage/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/coverage.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/http/handler.ts`
- **Evidence:** After "Download the letter" with codes "ABC", the browser shows only {"status":"invalid","issues":[{"field":"codes","message":"procedure codes of five letters or digits, separated by commas"}]}: no page, no words for a person, no way back but the browser's Back. Causes: the form is a plain GET to the download route (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/coverage/page.tsx:347), and downloadHandler returns a refusal as the JSON outcome (apps/web/src/lib/http/handler.ts:272-273); the message is apps/web/src/lib/routes/coverage.ts:189.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:256 (every error shown with a next step); PRD 7 (every screen has an error state with a next step)
- **Best fix:** Check the codes on the Coverage page before the download (a server action that validates and then redirects to the download, or the page re-rendered with ?letterError=codes), and show the refusal beside the field in words: "procedure codes of five letters or digits, separated by commas" -> "Procedure codes: “ABC” is not a procedure code. Enter five-character codes separated by commas, such as 97110, 97530, or leave the field blank to ask for the whole schedule." A download route that refuses a person's browser request redirects back to its form with the message, never answering with JSON.
- **Notes:** The flows and states lenses will likely raise the dead end; the words fix is the message and where it shows.

### `words-practice-43`: Settings overview tells a billing manager nothing, and its "admin" badges are unexplained

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.settings.empty.1280`, `p.settings.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/settings`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/settings/nav.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Billing manager: heading, "SYNTHETIC PHYSICAL THERAPY NORTH. Your role: Billing manager.", eleven section tabs, seven carrying a lower-case "admin" badge, and nothing below. The badge does not say whether the section is hidden, read-only or editable for this role (Materiality and pursuit has no badge and the billing manager edits it).
- **Rule:** PRD.md:260 (every screen's empty state says what to do next); the brief's plain-language rule
- **Best fix:** Add one line under the tabs for roles without the checklist: "Sections marked Admin are changed by the practice administrator; you can open Notification preferences, Materiality and pursuit and Pricing settings." Badge text "admin" -> "Admin" with an accessible name "changed by the practice administrator".
- **Notes:** Which sections a billing manager can open is taken from the screens (the Access log refuses them); confirm the list against the routes when writing the sentence.

### `words-practice-44`: Confirmation checkboxes end in a bare command ("Write it off.", "Reverse it.", "Adjust it.") instead of saying what the person confirms

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.case.error.1280`, `p.reconciliation.error-reverse.1280`, `p.abstraction-ledger.error-adjust-zero.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`, `/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** The required checkbox's label is a long consequence paragraph followed by an imperative: "…The totals and the line’s stage follow the ledger. Adjust it." (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/ledger/page.tsx:39), "…Recovered (posted or deposited) and the line’s stage move back with it. Reverse it." (reconciliation/page.tsx:46), "…while its dispute window is open. Write it off." (cases/[caseId]/page.tsx:222). Ticking a box labelled with a command reads oddly, and a screen reader announces the whole 80-word paragraph as the checkbox's name.
- **Rule:** The brief's plain-language rule; WCAG 2.5.3 / 1.3.1 (a control's name says what it does, kept short, with the explanation described)
- **Best fix:** Label the box with what the person confirms and describe it by the consequence: checkbox label "Adjust it." -> "I understand this writes a new ledger entry in my name"; "Reverse it." -> "I understand this reverses the match and moves recovered back"; "Write it off." -> "I understand the line leaves the actionable total". Keep the consequence paragraph as visible text above the box, tied to it with aria-describedby.

### `words-practice-45`: The empty practice home says uploads wait for the whole agreement chain while Uploads is already open

- **Flags:** polish; size S; touches #42
- **Screens:** `p.home.empty.1280`, `p.uploads.empty.1280`, `p.agreements.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/uploads`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/routes/practice.ts`
- **Evidence:** NORTH (BAA and engagement letters on file; terms of service and data-rights addendum missing): home empty state "… Uploads open once the agreement chain below is on file." with "Go to Uploads", while the Uploads page shows an open drop zone. Uploads are gated on the BAA alone (apps/web/src/lib/routes/uploads.ts:110 baaOnFile), and the home page's agreement table lists only signed documents, so "the agreement chain below" is neither the gate nor what Settings calls the chain (four documents). Cause: the fixed sentence at apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/page.tsx:186.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:260 (an empty state says what to do next, truthfully); the brief's honest-claims rule
- **Best fix:** Choose the sentence from the gate the page already knows: BAA on file -> "Upload your first 835 files. Each file’s receipt follows it from received to loaded, and this page counts what it finds."; BAA missing -> "Uploads open once the business associate agreement is on file. A platform administrator records it." (no Go to Uploads link in that case).
- **Notes:** The home page's data comes from lib/routes/practice.ts (in #42's list) if the BAA fact must be added to its payload; if the page already holds it, only page.tsx changes.

### `words-practice-46`: Units print as "1.000" and "2.000" on Claims and lines, "2" on the case and packet, and "x" against "×"

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.claims.default.1280`, `p.claims.error.1280`, `p.case.default.1280`, `p.packet.default.1280`, `p.compare.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/claims`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/compare`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/copy/words.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/claims/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/anomalies/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/packets/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/pricing/diff.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Claims and lines Units column: "1.000", "2.000" (the numeric column's text, apps/web/src/lib/remittances/queries.ts:574 `l.units::text`, printed as is at apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/claims/page.tsx:190). The same lines read "97110 x 2" on the case page (cases/[caseId]/page.tsx:167, a letter x) and "2" in the packet's line schedule; the compare page writes "97110 × 1" (components/pricing/diff.tsx:134).
- **Rule:** The brief's number-format and consistent-terms rules; BRAND.md:94 (every unit count in mono, tabular)
- **Best fix:** One units formatter in apps/web/src/lib/copy/words.ts: whole units without decimals ("1.000" -> "1", "2.000" -> "2"), fractional units to the decimals the 835 sent ("1.5"), and "×" for the multiplication sign everywhere ("97110 x 2" -> "97110 × 2"). Use it on Claims and lines, the case, the packet, the anomalies table and compare.
- **Notes:** Formatting the text of a numeric value, never parsing it to a float.

### `words-practice-47`: Plans are named by their raw remittance key ("CE:SYNPLAN-FIX-ONE") on screens, while the payer's copy already words them ("plan identifier SYNPLAN-FIX-ONE")

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.cases.default.1280`, `p.case.default.1280`, `p.case.empty.1280`, `p.packet.default.1280`, `p.submissions.default.1280`, `p.contract.default.1280`, `p.contracts.default.1280`, `p.anomalies.default.1280`, `p.plan.default.1280`, `p.pricing-run.default.1280`, `p.report.default.1280`, `p.packet-pdf.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/submissions`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/anomalies`, `/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/lib/triage/queries.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/mapping/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/mapping/[planId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/coverage/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/abstraction/regulations/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]/page.tsx`, `packages/packets/src/payer.ts`
- **Evidence:** Page titles and tables read "SYNTHETIC PAYER Q, CE:SYNPLAN-FIX-ONE" (case, packet, submissions headings), "CE:SYNPLAN-COMM-C" (contracts, contract), "CE:SYNPLAN-OON-CAS" (anomalies); "CE:" is the 835's REF qualifier (class of contract), and a plan known only by its claim filing indicator would show as "CLP06:12". The packet PDF for the same case prints "plan identifier SYNPLAN-FIX-ONE" through planName()/planText() (packages/packets/src/payer.ts:108-144), so the screen and the payer's copy name the plan differently. Causes: `coalesce(pl.name, pl.plan_key) AS plan` (apps/web/src/lib/triage/queries.ts:149, :366) and `plan.planName ?? plan.planKey` or `{plan.planKey}` (mapping/page.tsx:127, mapping/[planId]/page.tsx:74, coverage/page.tsx:212, contracts/[contractId]/page.tsx:103, abstraction/regulations/page.tsx:83, lines/[lineId]/page.tsx:208).
- **Rule:** The brief's plain-language rule (no internal codes) and consistent-terms rule (one name for one thing across screens and the payer's copy)
- **Best fix:** Name every plan on screen with the payer's-copy helper, moved to a shared place (packages/packets planName() is pure): "SYNTHETIC PAYER Q, CE:SYNPLAN-FIX-ONE" -> "SYNTHETIC PAYER Q, plan SYNPLAN-FIX-ONE"; a filing-indicator plan "CLP06:12" -> "plan type Preferred Provider Organization (PPO)". Keep the raw key only on the plan's mapping page, labelled "Remittance key CE:SYNPLAN-FIX-ONE" for support.
- **Notes:** The PDF wording is the reference and is not changed. The audit report's tables (apps/worker/src/report) should take the same helper.

### `words-practice-48`: Contract calendar says "No dates ahead" for a signed-off version that ends on 2026-12-31, and implies sign-off would have added dates

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.contracts.default.1280`, `p.contract.default.1280`, `p.contracts.empty-filtered.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/contracts/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/contracts/[contractId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/contracts/contract-queries.ts`, `packages/contracts/src/calendar.ts`
- **Evidence:** Contracts lists "Version 1 (in force) 2026-01-01 through 2026-12-31, Signed off", and the calendar beneath reads "No dates ahead. Signing off a version records its anniversary, notice deadline, fee-schedule year and amendment dates here." (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/contracts/page.tsx:146); the contract page "Calendar / No dates ahead." (contracts/[contractId]/page.tsx:114). The version has been signed off and nothing was recorded, because each derived date must fall inside the version's window (packages/contracts/src/calendar.ts:72-97: the anniversary 2027-01-01 is outside it). The one date a billing manager most needs, the version's own end (after which lines go unpriced as "no signed-off version covers the service date"), is not on the calendar at all.
- **Rule:** The brief's honest-claims rule; PRD 7 (Contracts: the calendar); PRD.md:260 (an empty state says what to do next)
- **Best fix:** Show each signed-off version's end date in the calendar, read from the version record at page time (no stored event, no migration): "2026-12-31: Version 1 of SYNTHETIC PAYER C AGREEMENT ends. Lines after it are not priced until an amendment or renewal is signed off: upload it here." And word the empty state truthfully: "No dates ahead. Signing off a version records its anniversary, notice deadline, fee-schedule year and amendment dates here." -> "No dates ahead in the versions signed off. Anniversaries, notice deadlines and fee-schedule years show here when they fall inside a version’s term."
- **Notes:** If the founder prefers the end date as a stored calendar event (new contract_calendar_event kind), that needs a migration and goes to Lane A; the read-time line does not.

### `words-practice-49`: Records named by UUID and 835 segment codes where a name exists: "Mapping d1cb53de-…", "rate record 86f15cfd-…", "Code HC:97110", "AMT B6", adjustments "CO 45" with no words

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M; in flight: #38 every figure explained in words
- **Screens:** `p.line.default.1280`, `p.line.empty-not-priced.1280`, `p.line.error.1280`, `p.pricing-run.default.1280`, `p.pricing-run.empty-filtered.1280`, `p.contract-version.default.1280`, `p.packet.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/pricing-runs/[runId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts/versions/[versionId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/pricing/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/packets/display.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/contracts/display.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Line page Pricing card: "Mapping d1cb53de-ae70-4b15-9966-dcff0a09cec0", "Contract version 11d8ad16-4cc6-…" (a link whose text is a UUID), "Rate record 86f15cfd-762c-…" (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/lines/[lineId]/page.tsx:93-110). The line as paid: "AMT B6 $70.00" as a row label and "Observed allowed $70.00 (AMT B6 (the payer’s allowed amount))" (page.tsx:184), "Code HC:97110" (:200), "Plan key CE:SYNPLAN-FIX-ONE" (:207); Adjustments "CO 45 $80.00", "PR 2 $14.00" with no reason words, although the rule release carries the CARC table. Pricing run: "Priced under … rate record 86f15cfd-762c-48b2-8f25-18ca01903de3" (apps/web/src/components/pricing/display.tsx:177). Packet citation and version review: "Document 5338e48f-5d2c-f3d2-7a8e-e6823a330e21, page 2".
- **Rule:** The brief's plain-language rule (no internal identifiers or codes in what a person reads); PRD 7 (Claims and lines: reasons in words)
- **Best fix:** Name each record the way its own page titles it, and keep the ID only as a copyable detail for support: "Mapping d1cb53de-…" -> "Plan SYNPLAN-FIX-ONE mapped to SYNTHETIC FIXED SCHEDULE AGREEMENT from 2026-01-01"; "Contract version 11d8ad16-…" -> "SYNTHETIC FIXED SCHEDULE AGREEMENT, version 1" (same link); "Rate record 86f15cfd-…" -> "Record 1: 97110, $50.00 per unit"; "rate record 86f15cfd-…" under Priced under -> "record 1 (97110)"; "Document 5338e48f-…, page 2" -> "Contract, uploaded 2026-10-26, page 2". 835 fields in words with the code after: "AMT B6" row -> "Payer’s allowed amount (AMT B6)" and "Observed allowed $70.00 (AMT B6 (the payer’s allowed amount))" -> "Observed allowed $70.00, the payer’s allowed amount"; "Code HC:97110" -> "Procedure code 97110 (CPT/HCPCS)"; adjustments "CO 45" -> "Contractual obligation (CO) 45: charge exceeds the fee schedule" from the release’s CARC records.
- **Notes:** The line page and packet are #38's screens; the pricing run's "Priced under" UUID and the contract version page are outside #38 and can land on their own. The remittance page's columns were not checked here beyond its shot.

### `words-practice-50`: Remittance and upload pages speak X12: "The 835 transaction set 0001 of interchange 050080001", "Trace (TRN02)", "ST02 0001 (segment 3)", "Receipt fcd812dc"; and "Patient responsibility" here is "Patient share" everywhere else

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.remittance.default.1280`, `p.upload.default.1280`, `p.uploads.default.1280`, `p.remittances.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/remittances/[remittanceId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/uploads/[documentId]`, `/p/[practiceId]/uploads`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/remittances/[remittanceId]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/uploads/[documentId]/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** Remittance: card description "The 835 transaction set 0001 of interchange 050080001." (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/remittances/[remittanceId]/page.tsx:63), "Trace (TRN02)" (:79), "Payee 9950828159" with no word for what the number is (:88), Claims column "Patient responsibility" (:155) while Claims and lines, the line page and the packet say "Patient share" (claims/page.tsx:153). Receipt: heading "Receipt fcd812dc" (uploads/[documentId]/page.tsx:243) and Parse report row "ST02 0001 (segment 3)" (:102); the receipts table's Document column shows only an 8-character ID.
- **Rule:** The brief's plain-language and consistent-terms rules
- **Best fix:** Lead with the words a billing manager uses and keep the X12 references as secondary detail: "The 835 transaction set 0001 of interchange 050080001." -> "One payment from the 835 file received 2026-10-26 (envelope 050080001, set 0001)."; "Trace (TRN02)" -> "Check or EFT trace number"; "Payee 9950828159" -> "Payee NPI 9950828159" (or "Payee tax ID" by the qualifier); "Patient responsibility" -> "Patient share"; "Receipt fcd812dc" -> "835 remittance received 2026-10-26 01:25 UTC" with "Document fcd812dc" under it; "ST02 0001 (segment 3)" -> "Payment 1 (transaction set 0001)".
- **Notes:** Uploads keep no original filename (PHI may sit in it; CLAUDE.md Never list), so a kind and a time are the honest name for a document.

### `words-practice-51`: Upload refusals name the file by position ("File 1 is empty.") and give no next step

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.reconciliation.error-empty-file.1280`, `p.uploads.error-era-system.1280`, `p.template.error.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation`, `/p/[practiceId]/uploads`, `/p/[practiceId]/reconciliation/template`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/components/uploads/uploader.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/reconciliation/template-mapper.tsx`
- **Evidence:** "Nothing was uploaded / File 1 is empty." (apps/web/src/components/uploads/uploader.tsx:189; the size refusal at :191 has the same "File N"). In a batch of 40 the person cannot tell which file is meant, and the message stops at the fault. The uploader already keeps file names in the browser and shows them while a batch uploads (uploader.tsx:15-16), so naming the file sends nothing anywhere. The template mapper calls an empty file "That file is not a CSV with a header row." (apps/web/src/components/reconciliation/template-mapper.tsx:76), a different fault for the same file.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:256 (every error shown with a next step)
- **Best fix:** Name the file as the browser knows it and say what to do: "File 1 is empty." -> "remit-june.csv is empty (0 bytes). Export it again from the practice-management system and choose it again; the other files were not sent either."; size: "File 2 is larger than 2.0 GiB; split it or zip it." -> "remit-2025.zip is larger than 2 GB. Split it into smaller files or zip it, then choose it again." Template mapper, for a 0-byte file: "That file is not a CSV with a header row." -> "That file is empty. Choose a sample export with its header row and a few rows."
- **Notes:** File names stay in the browser (no PHI leaves it): the message is built client-side, as the uploader already does.

### `words-practice-52`: The payer's copy cover asks to "reprocess underpaid claims" without saying how much, for which claims, or who to answer

- **Flags:** should_fix; size S; in flight: #38 every figure explained in words
- **Screens:** `p.packet-pdf.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases/[caseId]/packet/[versionId]/pdf`
- **Files:** `apps/worker/src/packets/html.ts`
- **Evidence:** Page 1 of the exported PDF: practice name, kicker "PAYMENT DISPUTE", heading "Request to reprocess underpaid claims", then "To SYNTHETIC PAYER Q (payer ID SYNPAYERQ), plan identifier SYNPLAN-FIX-ONE / Dispute level 1; prepared 2026-10-26 / Reference MKYAA-PE6N2", and nothing else on a full page. The amount asked ($90.00), the claim and line count (3 claims, 3 lines), the service dates (2026-03-05 to 2026-03-07) and the correspondence contact are all in the version's content but appear only on later pages. Cause: the cover meta is fixed at apps/worker/src/packets/html.ts:236-240.
- **Rule:** BRAND.md:23 (exact; the claim says what it is); PRD 12 (the packet presents the pattern once and the lines as a schedule); CLAUDE.md rule 1 (the figures come from the version's records, never typed)
- **Best fix:** Add one summary line to the cover from the version's own figures (code-computed, already in content.pattern and the payer's copy): "Amount in dispute: $90.00 on 3 lines of 3 claims, service dates 2026-03-05 to 2026-03-07, paid 2026-04-08." and "Reply to: SYNTHETIC BILLING OFFICE, (919) 555-0100 ext. 12, disputes@synthetic-practice.test; quote reference MKYAA-PE6N2." The verifier already treats these figures as the version's inputs, so they need no new source.
- **Notes:** Changing the printed payer's copy is a new packet format version if the format is versioned (ADR 0061 notes format versions); confirm with #38's owner. Not legal text.

### `words-practice-53`: A queued report's checklist reads "windows judged on —" and tells the reviewer to read a PDF and CSV that do not exist yet

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.report-review.empty.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/review`
- **Files:** `apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/review/page.tsx`
- **Evidence:** "Service 2026-04-01 to 2026-04-30; windows judged on —. Read the report, its PDF and its line CSV (line-level PHI: the download is recorded), check each finding against the practice’s contracts, then sign." above "The report is not generated yet; the checklist opens once it is ready." The PDF and line CSV links lead to files that are not generated. Cause: apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/[reportId]/review/page.tsx:42-51 renders the intro and links whatever the report's state.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:260 (an empty state says what to do next); BRAND.md:23 (exact)
- **Best fix:** While the report is queued, replace the intro with one sentence and no file links: "Service 2026-04-01 to 2026-04-30; windows judged on —. Read the report, its PDF and its line CSV …" -> "Service 2026-04-01 to 2026-04-30. The report is being generated; its PDF, line CSV and this checklist open here once it is ready. Reload in a few minutes."

(apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/reports/roi/page.tsx:61) with the Generate form directly above; "No audit report yet / A billing manager or analyst generates one from the pricing run in effect for its period." (reports/page.tsx:112) with the Generate form above. The person who can act reads a description of someone else.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:260 (an empty state says what to do next); BRAND.md:23 (warm: speak to the person)

Choose a period above and generate one; the quarter’s statement is written automatically once the quarter ends." and "No audit report yet. Choose a pricing run above and generate the report."; to a role that cannot -> keep naming who does it ("A billing manager or analyst generates one…").

### `words-practice-55`: "No pricing run in effect yet: a complete run, or a re-run once it is accepted." defines a term instead of giving the step, with no link

- **Flags:** polish; size S
- **Screens:** `p.cases.empty.1280`, `p.reports.empty.1280`, `p.contracts.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/cases`, `/p/[practiceId]/reports`, `/p/[practiceId]/contracts`

- **Evidence:** Cases (Triage a run card) and Reports (Generate an audit report card) on a practice with no run: "No pricing run in effect yet: a complete run, or a re-run once it is accepted." (apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/cases/page.tsx:70; reports/page.tsx:91). The sentence explains what "in effect" means but never says what to do, and links nowhere; the practice home's version of the same state links to Pricing runs (page.tsx:290-294). Contracts' coverage cell says "No pricing run in effect yet" (contracts/page.tsx:131) under a signed-off version.
- **Rule:** PRD.md:256 and :260 (every empty and error state with a next step)
- **Best fix:** "No pricing run in effect yet: a complete run, or a re-run once it is accepted." -> "No pricing run has priced this practice yet. Create one on Pricing runs; once it completes, you can triage it here." (Reports: "…; once it completes, you can generate its audit report here.") with "Pricing runs" as a link, as the practice home already does.

- **Flags:** should_fix; size M
- **Screens:** `p.notifications.default.1280`, `p.plan.error.1280`, `p.contracts.error-forbidden.1280`, `p.agreements.empty.1280`, `p.claims.default.1280`, `p.defaults.default.1280`
- **Routes:** `/p/[practiceId]/**`
- **Files:** `tools/brand-copy/scan.ts`, `tools/brand-copy/scan.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/action-form.test.tsx`
- **Evidence:** tools/brand-copy/scan.ts:21-36 fails only on ProofPack, AI, intelligence, "independent verification platform", an unlabelled "recovered", the screening's owed/underpaid/recovered, and the brand's words typed out (FORBIDDEN_TERMS :111-116, SCREENING_TERMS :119-123, BRAND_WORDS :126). It passes every fault in this review: a count before a fixed plural ("1 cases", words-practice-01), a refusal that prints a field key (words-practice-04, which is runtime, so the lint needs an ActionForm test beside it), straight apostrophes and quotes (words-practice-10), build references "(milestone 12)", "golden question Q34", "(CON-02)" (words-practice-12), codes in capitals and underscores "NO_AGREEMENT", "FIXED_SCHEDULE", "DEADLINE_UNVERIFIED" in read text (words-practice-13), and money typed as "$" plus a cents string (words-practice-11: `$${materiality.lineCents}` in apps/web/src/app/p/[practiceId]/settings/defaults/page.tsx:40). CO-11's own required words are present where they apply on these screens (the Verified definition beside the seal on the ROI statement; every "recovered" labelled posted or deposited), so the CO-11 rules hold.
- **Rule:** CO-11 acceptance (a copy lint); the brief: "read its rules and say what it misses"; PRD.md:256
- **Best fix:** Add six rules to tools/brand-copy/scan.ts, each with a scan.test.ts case and the existing exemption comment: (1) plural-after-count: a JSX expression or template placeholder followed by a fixed plural noun (lines, cases, claims, entries, files, days, payers, records, patients) unless wrapped in counted(); (2) straight-quote: a straight ' between letters or a straight " inside read text; (3) build-reference: /\bmilestone \d+|golden question|\bQ\d{2}\b|\((?:CON|CAS|LED|PKT|SEC|SCR)-\d{2}\)|\blevel L[1-3]\b/; (4) code-in-copy: a token in capitals with an underscore (/\b[A-Z]{2,}(?:_[A-Z0-9]+)+\b/) in read text; (5) dollar-literal: a "$" in read text immediately followed by a template placeholder (money goes through <Money/> or formatUsd); (6) camel-key: a camelCase token followed by a colon in read text. Pair (6) with an ActionForm test that renders a refusal for every route's schema keys and asserts no key text appears.
- **Notes:** Land the lint rules in the same PR as the fixes they guard (or with exemptions listing the open findings), so pnpm lint:copy stays green. action-form.test.tsx is a new file.
